# Brahmavaivarta-purāṇa 3

## Chapter 1

## \|\| 1.1 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde prathamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Offering salutation to the back of the humans, Narayana, Sarasvati, the goddess of speech as well as Vyasa, reciting the slogan of victory, the recitation of the Puranas should be started.

## \|\| 1.2-5 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*śrutaṃ prakṛti khaṇḍaṃ tadamṛtārṇavamuttamam \|*  
*sarvotkṛṣṭamabhīṣṭaṃ ca mūḍhānāṃ jñānavardanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*adhunā śrīgaṇeśasya khaṇḍaṃ śrotumihāgataḥ \|*  
*tajjanma caritaṃ nṝṇāṃ sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*kathaṃ jajñe suraśreṣṭhaḥ pārvatyā udare śubhe \|*  
*devī kena prakāreṇa cālabhattādṛśaṃ sutam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*sa cāṃśaḥ kasya devasya kathaṃ janma lalābha saḥ \|*  
*ayonisambhavaḥ kiṃ vā kiṃ vā'sau yonisambhavaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

2-5. Narada said – I have listened to the Prakritikhanda which is like nectar and the best of all and bestows knowledge even to the foolish people. Now I intend to listen to the Ganeshakhanda because the birth of Ganesha provides prosperity and all welfare. How was the best of gods Ganesha born from the womb of Parvati? How did the goddess achieve him as a son? Of which god does he belong to be the part and how was he bom? Was he born with human contact or without it.

## \|\| 1.6-9 \|\|

*kiṃ vā tadbrahmatejo vā kiṃ tasya ca parākramaḥ \|*  
*kā tapasyā ca ki jñānaṃ kiṃ vā tannirmmalaṃ yaśaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*kathaṃ tasya puraḥpūjā viśveṣu nikhileṣu ca \|*  
*sthite nārāyaṇe śambhau jagadīśe ca dhātari \|\| 7 \|\|*

*purāṇeṣu nigūḍhaṃ ca tajjanma parikīrttitam \|*  
*kathaṃ vā gajavaktro'yamekadanto mahodaraḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*etatsarvaṃ samācakṣva śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama \|*  
*suvistīrṇaṃ mahābhāga tadatīva manoharam \|\| 9 \|\|*

6-9. What is the extent of his divine glory, his prowess, the tapas, his intelligence and the spotless popularity? How was he considered to be adored first of all inspite of the presence of Narayana, Shiva and Brahma in the universe? In the Puranas, his birth is treated to be quite mysterious. How did he get the face of an elephant, one tusk and the protruding belly? О virtuous one, you kindly tell me the whole story in detail because it is quite pleasant and I feel quite anxious to know about it.

## \|\| 1.10-11 \|\|

*śrīnārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu nārada vakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*pāpasantāpaharaṇaṃ sarvavighnavināśanam \|\| 10 \|\|*

*sarvamaṅgaladaṃ sāraṃ sarvaśrutimanoharam \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣabījaṃ ca pāpamūlanikṛntanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. Narayana said – О Narada I am revealing the astonishing secret you to and you listen to it. The one who removes mental agony because of the commission of sin, is the remover of all obstructions, grants all welfare, is the essence of all, is quite pleasant to listen, bestower of pleasure, the cause of moksa and the destroyer of sins.

## \|\| 1.12 \|\|

*daityārditānāṃ devānāṃ tejorāśisamudbhavā \|*  
*devī saṃhṛtya daityaughāndakṣakanyā babhūva ha \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. When the gods were troubled by the demons, the goddess emerged from the tejas of the gods and she destroyed the demons. Thereafter she was born as a daughter of Daksha.

## \|\| 1.13 \|\|

*sā ca nāmnā satī devī svāmino nindayā purā \|*  
*dehaṃ saṃtyajya yogena jātā śailapriyodare \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. She was known as Sati there. In the earlier times the same Sati protesting against the denouncement of Shiva, ended her life and was reborn as Parvati from the womb of Mena.

## \|\| 1.14 \|\|

*śaṅkarāya dadau tāṃ ca pārvatīṃ parvato mudā \|*  
*tāṃ gṛhītvā mahādevo jagāma vijanaṃ vanam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Himalaya the king of the mountains pleasantly gave away Parvati in marriage to Shiva. Lord Mahadeva accompanied by Parvati retired to a secluded forest.

## \|\| 1.15 \|\|

*śayyāṃ ratikarīṃ kṛtvā puṣpacandanacarcitām \|*  
*sa reme narmadātīre puṣpodyāne tayā saha \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Reaching the bank of the Narmada they came across an orchard where they prepared a flowery bed and both of them enjoyed the conjugal pleasures there.

## \|\| 1.16 \|\|

*sahasravarṣaparyyantaṃ daivamānena nārada \|*  
*tayorbabhūva śṛṃgāro viparītādiko mahān \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О Narada, they continued to enjoy the conjugal pleasures using various means of make up or -decoration of the body up to a thousand divine years.

## \|\| 1.17 \|\|

*durgāṃgasparśamātreṇa madanānmūrcchitaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*mūrcchitā sā śivasparśādbubudhe na divāniśam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. With the very touch of the limbs of Parvati, Shiva was infatuated with passion and fainted. Similar was the case with Parvati. While indulging in love-sport they lost the sense of day and night.

## \|\| 1.18-20 \|\|

*haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇe puṃskokilarutākule \|*  
*nānāpuṣpavikāsāḍhye bhramaradhvaniguñjite \|\| 18 \|\|*

*sugandhikusumāśleṣivāyunā surabhīkṛte \|*  
*atīva sukhade tatra sarvajantuvivarjite \|\| 19 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā tayostacchṛṅgāraṃ cintāṃ prāpuḥ surāḥ parām \|*  
*brahmāṇaṃ ca puraskṛtya yayurnārāyaṇāntikam \|\| 20 \|\|*

18-20. In the forest there echoed the sweet voice of geese, ducks and other birds like cuckoos. Several types of flowers blossomed there, the black-wasps emitted the hissing sound and fragrant breeze pervaded the entire forest. It was a quite pleasant and charming place which was devoid of wild animals. Finding both of them so indulging in conjugal pleasures, the gods felt concerned. They went to Brahma and taking him with them they reached the abode of Vishnu.

## \|\| 1.21 \|\|

*taṃ natvā kathayāmāsa brahmā vṛttāntamīpsitam \|*  
*saṃtasthurdevatāḥ sarvāścitraputtalikā yathā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Brahma offered his salutation to him and he narrated the entire story of lord Vishnu, while other gods stood there like puppets.

## \|\| 1.22 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*sahasravarṣaparyyantaṃ devamānena śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*ratau rataśca niśceṣṭo na yogī virarāma ha \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Brahma said – Lord Shiva is inactive because of his indulging in the conjugal pleasures for a thousand years. The great yogi is not detracting himself from the love-sport.

## \|\| 1.23 \|\|

*maithunasya virāme ca dampatyorjagadīśvara \|*  
*kimbhūtaṃ bhavitā'patyaṃ tathyaṃ kathitumarhasi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. О lord of the universe, what type of child will they produce after the end of this love-sport you kindly tell me?

## \|\| 1.24 \|\|

*śrībhagavānuvāca \|*  
*cintā nāsti jagaddhātaḥ sarva bhadraṃ bhaviṣyati \|*  
*mayi ye śaraṇāpannāsteṣāṃ dukhaṃ kuto vidhe \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The lord said – О creator of the universe, don’t get worried about this. Everything will be well done because those who take refuge with me can never face trouble.

## \|\| 1.25-26 \|\|

*yenopāyena tadvīryaṃ bhūmau patati niścitam \|*  
*tatkuruṣva prayatnena sārddhaṃ devagaṇena ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

*yadā ca śambhorvīryyaṃ tatpārvatyā udare patet \|*  
*tato'patyaṃ ca bhavitā surāsuravimardakam \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. You do something by which the semen of lord Shiva falls on earth. You should accomplish this with the help of all the gods because, in case the semen of Shiva is implanted in the womb of Parvati, in that case a son will be born to her who will be the destroyer of all the gods and the demons.

## \|\| 1.27-28 \|\|

*tataḥ śakrādayaḥ sarve surā nārāyaṇājñayā \|*  
*prayayurnarmmadātīraṃ yayau brahmā nijālayam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*tatraiva parvatadroṇībahirdeśe surāḥ parāḥ \|*  
*viṣaṇṇavadanāḥ sarve babhūvurbhayakātarāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. There after Indra accompanied by other gods, at the command of Narayana, reached the bank of Narmada. Thereafter Brahma retired to his respective abode. The gods feeling upset in their mind stood in the valley.

## \|\| 1.29-30 \|\|

*śakro rājā kuberaṃ ca kubero varuṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*samīraṇaṃ ca varuṇo yamaṃ caiva samīraṇaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*hutāśanaṃ yamaścaiva bhāskaraṃ ca hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*candraṃ tathā bhāskaraśca tvīśānaṃ candra eva ca \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. There after Indra said to Kubera and Kubera spoke to Varuna, Varuna spoke to Vayu, Vayu spoke to Agni, Agni spoke to Surya, Surya spoke to Chandrama and Chandrama spoke to Ishana.

## \|\| 1.31-32 \|\|

*evaṃ devāḥ prerayanti devāṃśca ratibhañjane \|*  
*haraśṛṃgāra bhaṃgaṃ ca kurvityuktvā parasparam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*dvāri sthito vakraśirāḥ śakraḥ prāha maheśvaram \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. Thus the gods were conversing among themselves to detach Shiva from his conjugal pleasure inciting one another to obstruct the love-sport of lord Shiva. Indra then stood at the entrance gate, turning his face and spoke to lord Shiva.

## \|\| 1.33-36 \|\|

*indra uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karoṣi mahādeva yogīśvara namo'stu te \|*  
*jagadīśa jagadbīja bhaktānāṃ bhayabhañjana \|\| 33 \|\|*

*harirjagāmetyuktvā tamājagāma ca bhāskaraḥ \|*  
*uvāca bhīto dvārastho bhayārtto vakracakṣuṣā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*śrīsūryya uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karoṣi mahādeva jagatāṃ paripālaka \|*  
*suraśreṣṭha mahābhāga pārvatīśa namo'stu te \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ityevamuktvā śrīsūryyaḥ sa jagāma bhayāttataḥ \|*  
*ājagāma tathā candra avocadvakrakandharaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

33-36. Indra said – О Mahadeva, О yoglsvara, I offer my salutation to you. О lord of the universe you are the cause of the universe and remove the danger of the devotees. What is all this that you are doing? Thus speaking Indra left the place; thereafter the sun-god stood at the gate and feeling panicky in his mind he said. Surya said – О Mahadeva, О preserver of the universe, О best of the gods, О virtuous one, О lord of Parvati, I bow in reverence to you. What is all this that you are doing? Thus speaking Surya feeling panicky left the place; thereafter Chandrama arrived and turning his face to the other direction he also spoke.

## \|\| 1.37 \|\|

*candra uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karoṣi trilokeśa trilocana namo'stu te \|*  
*ātmārāma svayaṃpūrṇa puṇyaśravaṇakīrttana \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Chandrama said – О lord of the three worlds, О three-eyed lord, I offer my salutation to you. You pervade the soul. You are complete in yourself, your voice is auspicious for the ear; what is all this being done by you?

## \|\| 1.38 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā bhītaśca virarāma niśāpatiḥ \|*  
*samīraṇo'pi dvārasthassaṃvīkṣyovāca sādaram \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thus speaking Chandrama the lord of the night kept quiet feeling panicky in his mind; thereafter the wind-god standing at the gate also spoke similarly.

## \|\| 1.39 \|\|

*pavana uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karoṣi jagannātha jagadbandho namo 'stu te \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ bījarūpa sanātana \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The wind god said – O Lord of the universe, O friend of the universe, I bow in reverence to you, you are the seed for dharma, artha, kama and moksa and you are eternal; what is all this being done by you?

## \|\| 1.40 \|\|

*ityevaṃ stavanaṃ śrutvā yogajñānaviśāradaḥ \|*  
*tyaktukāmo na tatyāja śṛṅgāraṃ pārvatī bhayāt \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Shiva who was completely well-versed in the yogic practices, on hearing the prayer could not discard the conjugal pleasure getting afraid of Parvati though he wanted to discontinue the same.

## \|\| 1.41 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā surānbhayārttaśca punaḥ stotuṃ samudyatān \|*  
*vijahau sukhasambhogaṃ kaṇṭhalagnāṃ ca pārvatīm \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Feeling panicky Shiva saw that the gods were starting to offer prayer again and as such he discarded the conjugal pleasure and separated himself from the embrace of Parvati.

## \|\| 1.42 \|\|

*uttiṣṭhato maheśasya trāsalajjāyutasya ca \|*  
*bhūmau papāta tadvīryyaṃ tataḥ skando babhūva ha \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Feeling remorseful from fear and shame, Shiva dropped the semen on the earth from which Karttikeya was born.

## \|\| 1.43 \|\|

*paścāttāṃ kathayiṣyāmi kathāmatimanoharām \|*  
*skandajanmaprasaṅge ca sāmprataṃ vāñchitaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Thereafter the pleasant story of the same will be recited but currently you listen to the story relating to the birth of Karttikeya.

## Chapter 2

## \|\| 2.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*tyaktvā ratiṃ mahādevo dadarśa purataḥ surān \|*  
*palāyadhvamiti prāha kṛpayā pārvatībhayāt \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – Lord Mahadeva advised the gods after leaving the conjugal pleasure to run away from the place. He said: "You at once flee from his place.”

## \|\| 2.2 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nārada nārāyaṇasaṃvāde dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The gods who were terrified from the curse of Parvati fled away from the place and lord Shiva also started trembling for fear of Parvati.

## \|\| 2.3 \|\|

*talpādutthāya sā durgā na ca dṛṣṭvā purassurān \|*  
*samutthitaṃ kopavahniṃ stambhayāmāsa dehataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Durga got up from the bed and after doing so she did not find the gods there. At once she felt enraged but she contained her anger in the body itself.

## \|\| 2.4-7 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti te devā vyarthavīryyā bhavantviti \|*  
*śaśāpa devī tāndevānatiruṣṭā babhūva ha \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tataḥ śivaḥ śivāṃ dṛṣṭvā krodhasaṃraktalocanām \|*  
*rudantīṃ namravadanāṃ likhantīṃ dharaṇītalam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*śivastāṃ duḥkhitāṃ dṛṣṭvā krodhasaṃraktalocanām \|*  
*haste gṛhītvā deveśo vāsayāmāsa vakṣasi \|\| 6 \|\|*

*atīva bhītaḥ saṃtrasta uvāca madhuraṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ ruṣṭā giriśreṣṭhakanye dhanye manohare \|\| 7 \|\|*

4-7. But getting immensely annoyed Parvati pronounced a curse on the gods, that their semen should become infructuous and they shall no able to produce children thereafter. Shiva looked at Parvati who was standing there with her eyes turned red and was crying at the same time. She stood there still. Shiva, finding Parvati in anger and her eyes turned red was disturbed at heart. He then held her hand and embraced her; getting terrified he spoke to her in a sweet voice.

## \|\| 2.8-10 \|\|

*mama saubhāgyarūpe ca prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛdevate \|*  
*kiṃ te 'bhīṣṭaṃ kariṣyāmi vada māṃ jagadambike \|\| 8 \|\|*

*brahmāṇḍasaṃghe nikhile kimasādhyamihāvayoḥ \|*  
*aho niraparādhaṃ māṃ prasannā bhava sundari \|\| 9 \|\|*

*daivādajñātadoṣasya śāntiṃ me karttumarhasi \|*  
*tvayā yuktaḥ śivo'haṃ ca sarvaṣāṃ śivadāyakaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

8-10. Shiva said – О daughter of the mountains, you are quite graceful and attract the mind, you are my fortune and the goddess of my heart, О mother of the universe, you let me know your desire. I am sure to fulfil the same. There is nothing beyond our reach in this universe.

## \|\| 2.11 \|\|

*tvayā vinā hīśvaraśca śavatulyo'śivaḥ sadā \|*  
*prakṛtistvaṃ ca buddhistvaṃ śaktistvaṃ ca kṣamā dayā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Therefore, О beautiful one, you forgive me, since I am quite innocent. I have committed a crime unknowingly. You please forgive me, I am Shiva only in union with you and can bestow welfare to all.

## \|\| 2.12-13 \|\|

*tuṣṭistvaṃ ca tathā puṣṭiḥ śāntistvaṃ kṣāntireva ca \|*  
*kṣuttvaṃ chāyā tathā nidrā tandrā śraddhā sureśvari \|\| 12 \|\|*

*sarvādhārasvarūpā tvaṃ sarvabījasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*smitapūrvaṃ vada vacaḥ sāmprataṃ sarasaṃ śive \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Without you I am like a dead body and become the cause of miseries, О best of the goddess, you are Prakriti, intelligence, prowess, forgiveness, mercy, satisfaction, support, sleep, peace, anger, shadow, drowsiness and faith.

## \|\| 2.14 \|\|

*tvatkopaviṣasaṃdagdhaṃ drutaṃ jīvaya māṃ mṛtam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. О Siva, you are the base for all, the seed for all; therefore you speak in a pleasant voice, wearing a smile on your face.

## \|\| 2.15 \|\|

*śaṃkarasya vacaḥ śrutvā kṣamāyuktā ca pārvatī \|*  
*uvāca madhuraṃ devī hṛdayena vidūyatā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Because of the poison of your anger I have been burnt alive. You bring me back to life. On hearing the words of Shiva, Parvati who always believed in forgiveness, spoke the pleasant words.

## \|\| 2.16 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*kiṃ tvā'haṃ kathayiṣyāmi sarvajñaṃ sarvarūpiṇam \|*  
*ātmārāmaṃ pūrṇakāmaṃ sarvadeheṣvavasthitam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Parvati said – What shall I talk to you? You are all knowledgeable, the form of everything, the one who pervades the soul, accomplish all deeds and pervades the body of everyone.

## \|\| 2.17 \|\|

*kāminī mānasaṃ kāmamaprajñaṃ svāminaṃ vadet \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ hṛdayajñaṃ ca hṛdīṣṭaṃ kathayāmi kim \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. A damsel reveals her mind to her husband but you are the one who knows the minds of all; therefore what can I speak to you since you know everything?

## \|\| 2.18 \|\|

*sugopyaṃ sarvanārīṇāṃ lajjājananakāraṇam \|*  
*akathyamapi sarvāsāṃ maheśa kathayāmi te \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O Mahesa, I am telling you something which is quite secretive, shameful and beyond expression.

## \|\| 2.19 \|\|

*sukheṣu madhye strīṇāṃ ca vibhaveṣu sureśvara \|*  
*satpuṃsā saha sambhogo nirjaneṣu paraṃ sukham \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О best of the gods, it is a matter of great pleasure for ladies to enjoy the company of their husbands in secluded places.

## \|\| 2.20-22 \|\|

*tadbhaṃgena ca yadduḥkhaṃ tatsamaṃ nāsti ca striyāḥ \|*  
*kāntānāṃ kāntavicchedaśokaḥ paramadāruṇaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇapakṣe yathā candraḥ kṣīyamāṇo dine dine \|*  
*tathā kāntaṃ vinā kāntā kṣīṇā kānta kṣaṇe kṣaṇe \|\| 21 \|\|*

*cintājvaraśca sarveṣāmupatāpaśca vāsasām \|*  
*sādhvīnāṃ kāntavicchedasturagānāṃ ca maithunam \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. And there is no other mental agony for a lady when she is deprived of it because the separation from her husband is quite unbearable for the ladies. О lord, as the moon goes on reducing during the black fortnight, similarly a lady without the husband goes on reducing. Worry is the cause of mental agony for all, for the costumes the summer season is unbearable and for the chaste lady, the separation from husband is quite painful and for the horses, the love making is painful.

## \|\| 2.23 \|\|

*ratibhaṃgo duḥkhamekaṃ dvitīyaṃ vīryyapātanam \|*  
*duḥkhātireki duḥkhaṃ ca tṛtīyamanapatyatā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The disturbance in conjugal pleasure and the second painful experience is the fall of your semen on the ground and the third painful experience is that I have no son.

## \|\| 2.24 \|\|

*trailokyakāntaṃ kāntaṃ tvāṃ labdhvā'pi na ca me sutaḥ \|*  
*yā strī putravihīnā ca jīvanaṃ tannirarthakam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. I don’t have a son inspite of achieving you the lord of the three worlds as my husband. Such of the ladies who have no sons, their life is said to be of no consequence.

## \|\| 2.25 \|\|

*janmāntarasukhaṃ puṇyaṃ tapodānasamudbhavam \|*  
*sadvaṃśajātaputraśca paratreha sukhapradaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The merit earned by performing tapas and charity provides pleasure after many births but a son born in the high race, provides pleasure in this world and even beyond.

## \|\| 2.26 \|\|

*suputraḥ svāminoṃ'śaśca svāmitulyasukhapradaḥ \|*  
*kuputraśca kulāṅgāro manastāpāya kevalam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. A son born from the husband who provides pleasure is like a husband and if he happens to be a wicked son, he serves like a burning flame. He creates mental agony.

## \|\| 2.27 \|\|

*svāmī svāṃśena sustrīṇāṃ garbhe janma labheddhruvam \|*  
*sādhvīstrī mātṛtulyā ca satataṃ hitakāriṇī \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The lords of the noble ladies appear from their womb by uniting with their husbands and the chaste lady always thinks of welfare of all like a mother.

## \|\| 2.28 \|\|

*asādhvī vairitulyā ca śaśvatsantāpadāyinī \|*  
*mukhaduṣṭā yoniduṣṭā cāsādhvīti tridhā smṛtā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. An unchaste woman produces sons who provide mental agony like an enemy. The wicked women are of three types viz., those who speak unchaste words, those who indulge in unholy contacts and those who are unchaste by nature.

## \|\| 2.29-30 \|\|

*kimupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi vada yogīśvareśvara \|*  
*upāyasindho tapasāṃ sarveṣāṃ ca phalaprada \|\| 29 \|\|*

*ityuktvā pārvatī devī namravaktrā babhūva ha \|*  
*prahasya śaṃkaro devo bodhayāmāsa pārvatīm \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. О lord of the yogis, you are the ocean of remedies and provide the result of the tapas. Therefore, you tell me what I should do. Thus speaking Parvati cast her head downwards and Shiva smilingly consoled her.

## \|\| 2.31 \|\|

*satputrabījaṃ sukhadaṃ tāpanāśanakāraṇam \|*  
*mitaṃ snigdhaṃ suruciraṃ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Lord Shiva started speaking pleasant words which removed her mental agony. His eyes were filled with love, quite pleasant and appeared to be the cause of having a noble son.

## Chapter 3

## \|\| 3.1-2 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu pārvati vakṣyāmi tava bhadraṃ bhaviṣyati \|*  
*upāyataḥ kāryyasiddhirbhavatyeva jagattraye \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sarvavāṃchitasiddhestu bījarūpaṃ sumaṅgalam \|*  
*manasaḥ prītijanakamupāyaṃ kathayāmi te \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Mahadeva said – О Parvati listen to me, I am telling you something which will provide welfare to you. One gets success by attending to the remedies against adversities in the universe. I am going to tell you a remedy which will be the cause of meeting with success. It shall bestow success, welfare and love in one's mind.

## \|\| 3.3-8 \|\|

*harerārādhanaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ kuru varānane \|*  
*vrataṃ ca puṇyakaṃ nāma varṣamekaṃ kariṣyasi \|\| 3 \|\|*

*mahākaṭhorabījaṃ ca vāñchākalpataruṃ param \|*  
*sukhadaṃ puṇyadaṃ sāraṃ putradaṃ sarvasaukhyadam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*nadīnāṃ ca yathā gaṃgā devānāṃ ca hariryathā \|*  
*vaiṣṇavānāṃ yathāhaṃ ca devīnāṃ tvaṃ yathā priye \|\| 5 \|\|*

*varṇānāṃ ca yathā viprastīrthānāṃ puṣkaraṃ yathā \|*  
*puṣpāṇāṃ pārijātaṃ ca patrāṇāṃ tulasī yathā \|\| 6 \|\|*

*yathā puṇyapradānāṃ ca tithirekādaśī smṛtā \|*  
*ravivāraśca vārāṇāṃ yathā puṇyapradaḥ śive \|*  
*māsānāṃ mārgaśīrṣaścāpyṛtūnāṃ mādhavo yathā \|\| 7 \|\|*

*saṃvatsaro vatsarāṇāṃ yugānāṃ ca kṛtaṃ yathā \|*  
*vidyāpradaśca pūjyānāṃ gurūṇāṃ jananī yathā \|\| 8 \|\|*

3-8. О damsel, by adoring the lord you must perform a Punyaka-vrata for a year, this is quite difficult and forms of the seed, is kalpavrksa and wish fulfilling, besides being quite pleasant, meritorious, the essence, bestower of son and rivers, Vishnu is best of all the gods, I myself in the Vaishnavas, you yourself in the goddesses, Brahmana in the varnas, Puskara in the holy places, Parijata in the flowers, Tulasi among the leaves, Ekadasi which grants merit and Sunday is the best of all the days.

## \|\| 3.9-31 \|\|

*sādhvī patnī yathāptānāṃ viśvastānāṃ mano yathā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*yathā dhanānāṃ ratnaṃ ca priyāṇāṃ ca yathā patiḥ \|*  
*yathā putraśca bandhūnāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ kalpapādapaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*phalānāṃ vai cūtaphalaṃ varṣāṇāṃ bhārataṃ tathā \|*  
*vṛndāvanaṃ vanānāṃ ca śatarūpā ca yoṣitām \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yathā kāśī purīṇāṃ ca sūryyastejasvināṃ yathā \|*  
*yathenduḥ sukhadānāṃ ca sundarāṇāṃ ca manmathaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*śāstrāṇāṃ ca yathā vedāḥ siddhānāṃ kapilo yathā \|*  
*hanūmānvānarāṇāṃ ca kṣetrāṇāṃ brāhmaṇānanam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*yaśodānāṃ yathā vidyā kavitā ca manoharā \|*  
*ākāśo vyāpakānāṃ ca hyaṃgānāṃ locanaṃ yathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*vibhavānāṃ harikathā sukhānāṃ haricintanam \|*  
*sparśānāṃ putrasaṃsparśo hiṃsrāṇāṃ ca yathā khalaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*pāpānāṃ ca yathā mithyā pāpināṃ puṃścalī yathā \|*  
*puṇyānāṃ ca yathā satyaṃ tapasāṃ harisevanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*yathā ghṛtaṃ ca gavyānāṃ yathā brahmā tapasvinām \|*  
*amṛtaṃ bhakṣyavastūnāṃ sasyānāṃ dhānyakaṃ yathā \|\| 17 \|\|*

*puṇyadānāṃ yathā toyaṃ śuddhānāṃ ca hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*suvarṇaṃ taijasānāṃ ca miṣṭānāṃ priyabhāṣaṇam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*garuḍaḥ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva hastināmindravāhanam \|*  
*yogināṃ ca kumāraśca devarṣīṇāṃ ca nāradaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*gandharvāṇāṃ citraratho jīvo buddhimatāṃ yathā \|*  
*sukavīnāṃ yathā śukraḥ kāvyānāṃ ca purāṇakam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*srotasvatāṃ samudraśca yathā pṛthvī kṣamāvatām \|*  
*lābhānāṃ ca yathā muktirharibhaktiśca sampadām \|\| 21 \|\|*

*pavitrāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavāśca varṇānāṃ praṇavo yathā \|*  
*viṣṇumantraśca mantrāṇāṃ bījānāṃ prakṛtiryathā \|\| 22 \|\|*

*viduṣāṃ ca yathā vāṇī gāyatrī chandasāṃ yathā \|*  
*yathā kubero yakṣāṇāṃ sarpāṇāṃ vāsukiryathā \|\| 23 \|\|*

*yathā pitā te śailānāṃ gavāṃ ca surabhiryathā \|*  
*vedānāṃ sāmavedaśca tṛṇānāṃ ca yathā kuśaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*sukhadānāṃ yathā lakṣmīrmano vai śīghragāminām \|*  
*akṣarāṇāmakāraśca yathā tāto hitaiṣiṇām \|\| 25 \|\|*

*śālagrāmaśca mūrtīnāṃ parśūnāṃ viṣṇupañjaraḥ \|*  
*catuṣpadānāṃ pañcāsyo mānavo jīvināṃ yathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*yathā svāntaṃ cendriyāṇāṃ mandāgniśca rujāṃ yathā \|*  
*balināṃ ca yathā śaktirahaṃ śaktimatāṃ tathā \|\| 27 \|\|*

*mahānvirāṭ ca sthūlānāṃ sūkṣmāṇāṃ paramāṇukaḥ \|*  
*yathendra āditeyānāṃ daityānāṃ ca baliryathā \|\| 28 \|\|*

*yathā dadhīcirdātṝṇāṃ prahrādaścaiva sādhuṣu \|*  
*brahmāstraṃ ca yathā'strāṇāṃ cakrāṇāṃ ca sudarśanam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*nṛṇāṃ rājā rāmacandro dhanvināṃ lakṣmaṇo yathā \|*  
*sarvādhāraḥ sarvasevyaḥ sarvabījaṃ ca sarvadaḥ \|*  
*sarvasāro yathā kṛṣṇo vratānāṃ puṇyakaṃ yathā \|\| 30 \|\|*

*vrataṃ kuru mahābhāge triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|*  
*sarvaśreṣṭhaśca putraste vratādeva bhaviṣyati \|\| 31 \|\|*

9-31. О Shiva, Margas'Irsa is the best among the months, spring among the seasons, Samvatsara among the years, krtayuga among the yugas, the teacher among the adorable and mother among the teachers, while at the time of misfortune, the mind among the faithful, the gem among the riches, the husband among the beloved, the son among the relatives, kalpavrksa among the trees, mango among the fruits, the land of Bharata among the continents, Vrindavana among the forests, Satarupa among the ladies, KasI among the cities, Surya among those possessing lustre, Moon among the planets, Kamadeva among the beautiful ones, the Vedas in literature, Kapila among the siddhas, Hanuman among the monkeys, Brahmanamukha among the ksetras, knowledge among those who bestow glory and beautiful poetry, the sky among those who pervade everywhere, the eyes among all the limbs, the story of the lord among all the treasures, the adoration of lord of all the pleasures, the touch of the son among the things which provide pleasure by touch, the wicked among the terrorists, falsehood among the sins, the degraded woman among the sinners, truthfulness among the merits, the serving of Hari among the tapas, ghee among the fluids, Brahma among the tapasvls, nectar among the eatables, paddy among the cereals, the water among those which provide purity, Agni in the Shudras, gold among the articles which glitter, the sweet tongue among all the sweet things, Garuda among the birds, Airavata among the elephant, Kumara among the yogis, Narada among the royal priests, Chitraratha among the Gandharvas, Brihaspati among the intelligent people, Sukra among the poets, Puranas among the kavyas, ocean among the stores of water, the goddess earth among those who forgive, the devotion among the profits, the devotion of Hari among the faiths, Vaishnava among the purified person, Omkara among the letters, Vishnumantra among all the mantras, Prakriti among the seeds, Sarasvati among the intelligent people, Gayatri among the metres, Kubera among the Yaksas, Vasuki among the serpents, Himvan among the mountains, Surabhi among the cows, Samaveda among the Vedas, kusha among the grass, Lakshmi among the those v/ho provide pleasure, the mind out of the those which move with speed, the form in the letter, the father among the well-wishers, Sal a gram a among the images, Sudarsana-cakra among the weapons, lion among the quadrupeds, human among all the creatures, the mind among all the organ of senses, indigestion among the ailments, prowess among all the warriors; I myself among the valorous person, Mahavirat among the solid people, the atom among the smallest things, Indra among the sons of Aditi, Bali among the Daityas, Dadhici among those who perform the charity, Prahlada among the noble people, Brahmdstra among the weapons, Sudarsana among the cakras, the king Rama among the humans, Laksmana among the archers who is the base of all, served by all, the seed of all, the bestower of everything and his essence is lord Krishna. Similar is the case with Punyaka vrata. O virtuous one, you perform this vrata which is difficult to get in the three worlds. With the influence of this vrata you will achieve an influential son.

## \|\| 3.32 \|\|

*vratārādhyaśca vai kṛṣṇaḥ sarveṣāṃ vāñchitapradaḥ \|*  
*jano yatsevanānmuktaḥ pitṛbhiḥ koṭibhiḥ saha \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Lord Krishna happens to be the, main deity of this mantra who fulfils all the desires and by adoring whom one redeems crores of his generations.

## \|\| 3.33 \|\|

*uddhṛtya koṭipuruṣānvaikuṇṭhaṃ yāti niścitam \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇapārṣado bhūtvā sukhaṃ tatraiva modate \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Whosoever recites the mantra of the lord in the land of Bharata, his life meets with success. He also redeems crores of his generations and ultimately achieves Vaikuntha where he serves as a courtier of lord Krishna and spent his life conformably.

## \|\| 3.34-37 \|\|

*sahodarānsvabhṛtyāṃśca svabandhūnsahacāriṇaḥ \|*  
*svastriyaśca samuddhṛtya bhakto yāti hareḥ padam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*tasmādagṛhāṇa girije harer mantram sudurlabham \|*  
*japa mantra vrate tatra pitṛṇāṃ muktikāraṇam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ityuktvā śaṃkaro devo gatvā girijayā saha \|*  
*śīghraṃ ca jāhnavītīraṃ harermmantraṃ manoharam \|\| 36 \|\|*

*tasyai dadau ca saṃprītyā kavacaṃ stotrasaṃyutam \|*  
*pūjāvidhānaniyamaṃ kathayāmāsa tāṃ mune \|\| 37 \|\|*

34-37. Such a person after redeeming his brothers, servants, associates and the ladies of the household, proceeds to the house of the lord. О Parvati, therefore, you accept the mantra of the lord which is difficult to get and which redeems the manes; thereafter, lord Shiva went to the bank of Ganga together with Parvati and imparted the knowledge of the sacred mantra of the lord of Parvati. О sage, he also explained to her the kavacha, stotra and method of adoration to her.

## Chapter 4

## \|\| 4.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śrutvā vratavidhānaṃ ca durgā saṃhṛṣṭamānasā \|*  
*sarvaṃ vratavidhānaṃ ca saṃpraṣṭumupacakrame \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – After listening to the method of performing the Punyaka-vrata, the mind of Durga was delighted; she therefore enquired about the per forming of the vrata.

## \|\| 4.2-4 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*sarvaṃ vratavidhānaṃ māṃ vada vedavidāṃ vara \|*  
*he nātha karuṇāsindho dīnabandho parātpara \|\| 2 \|\|*

*kāni vratopayuktāni dravyāṇi ca phalāni ca \|*  
*saniyamaṃ sabhakṣyaṃ ca vidhānaṃ tatphalaṃ prabho \|\| 3 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde puṇyaka vratavidhānaṃ nāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. Parvati said – О best of the gods, О lord, О ocean of compassion, О beloved of the downtrodden, you are beyond everything and therefore, you kindly tell me what materials are required for this vrata and what is the reward, what is the time for it, the regulations, the eatables, the method and reward thereof? О lord, you also tell me about the best of the priests, the Brahmanas who adore with flowers and servants who collect the material.

## \|\| 4.5 \|\|

*anyāni copayuktāni mayā'jñātāni yāni ca \|*  
*sanniyojaya tatsarvaṃ strīṇāṃ svāmī ca sarvadaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. What else is required for this vrata, I am not aware of it. You kindly arrange for everything because the husband has to provide everything for the wife.

## \|\| 4.6 \|\|

*pitā kaumārakāle ca sadā pālanakārakaḥ \|*  
*bharttā madhye sutaḥ śeṣe tridhā'vasthā suyoṣitām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. During childhood, the woman is protected by the father, during youth the husband protects her and during old age, the son protects her. Therefore, there are three stages of a chaste woman.

## \|\| 4.7 \|\|

*tāto'śokaḥ prāṇatulyāṃ datvā satsvāmine sutām । svāmī nivṛttimāpnoti saṃnyasya svasute priyām \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The father gives away in marriage his daughter to the best of the husbands and feels satisfied thereafter and the husband feels satisfied after entrusting his wife to the care of the son.

## \|\| 4.8-9 \|\|

*bandhutrayayutā yā strī sā ca bhāgyavatī parā \|*  
*kiñcidvihīnā madhyā ca sarvahīnā'dhamā bhuvi \|\| 8 \|\|*

*eteṣāṃ ca samīpasthā praśaṃsyā sā jagattraye \|*  
*ninditā'nyeṣu saṃnyastā sarvametacchrutau śrutam \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. Thus a woman who deals with three types of these relatives is considered to be quite fortunate; the one who is with some deficiency, is considered to be mediocre and the one who is deprived of all the three is considered to be the degraded of all. Such a woman who remains with these relatives is considered to be the best everywhere. Such a woman, the care of whom is entrusted to anyone else earns denouncement. This is all prescribed in the Vedas.

## \|\| 4.10 \|\|

*sarvātmā bhagavāṃstvaṃ ca sarvasākṣī ca sarvavit \|*  
*dehi mahyaṃ putravaraṃ svātmanirvṛtihetukam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. You are the soul of all, the lord, the witness for all and you know everyone; therefore you kindly tell me everything to enable to me to achieve the best son.

## \|\| 4.11 \|\|

*svātmabodhānumānena mahātmani niveditam \|*  
*sarvāntarābhiprāyajñaṃ bodhajñaṃ bodhayāmi kim \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. I have spoken out to you, О great lord, everything and you are the one who knows the mind of everyone; what else can I speak to you?

## \|\| 4.12 \|\|

*ityuktvā pārvatī prītyā papāta svāminaḥ pade \|*  
*kṛpāsindhuśca bhagavānpravaktumupacakrame \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Thus speaking Parvati at once fell at the feet of the lord. Thereafter, lord Shiva started speaking to Parvati.

## \|\| 4.13 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi vidhānaṃ niyamaṃ phalam \|*  
*phalāni caiva dravyāṇi vratayogyāni yāni ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Mahadeva said – О goddess I am going to speak out to you the method for performing the vrata, its regulation, the reward and the things which are eatables and those who are prohibited from consumption for this vrata. You listen to it.

## \|\| 4.14 \|\|

*viprāṇāṃ śatakaṃ śuddhaṃ phalapuṣpopahārakam \|*  
*kiṃkarāṇāṃ ca śatakaṃ dravyāharaṇakārakam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. A hundred Brahmanas are needed for the collection of flowers and fruits and another hundred servants are required for bringing up other materials.

## \|\| 4.15-16 \|\|

*dāsīnāṃ śatakaṃ lakṣaṃ niyuktaṃ ca purohitam \|*  
*sarvavratavidhānajñaṃ vedavedāntapāragam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*pravaraṃ haribhaktānāṃ sarvajñaṃ jñānināṃ varam \|*  
*sanatkumāraṃ mattulyaṃ gṛhāṇa vratahetave \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. A crore of female slaves are required and the priest should be such as should be well-versed in the bringing of the material and well-versed in the Vedic and post-Vedic literature and should be the best of the devotees of lord Vishnu and the foremost of the intellectuals. Therefore for performing vrata you request Sanatkumara to function as a priest.

## \|\| 4.17 \|\|

*devi śuddhe ca kāle ca paraṃ niyamapūrvakam \|*  
*māghaśuklatrayodaśyāṃ vratārambhaḥ śubhaḥ priye \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. О goddess, О beloved, during the auspicious time you start this vrata in a proper manner. The bright thirteenth day of Magha shall be the most auspicious time.

## \|\| 4.18-25 \|\|

*gātraṃ sunirmalaṃ kṛtvā śirassaṃskārapūrvakam \|*  
*upoṣya pūrvadivase vastraṃ saṃśodhya yatnataḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*aruṇodayavelāyāṃ talpādutthāya suvratī \|*  
*mukhaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā snātvā vai nirmale jale \|\| 19 \|\|*

*ācamya yatnapūto hi harismaraṇapūrvakam \|*  
*dattvā'rghyaṃ haraye bhaktyā gṛhamāgatya satvaram \|\| 20 \|\|*

*dhaute ca vāsasī dhṛtvā hyupaviśyāsane śucau \|*  
*ācamya tilakaṃ dhṛtvā samāpya svāhnikaṃ punaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*ghaṭaṃ saṃsthāpya vidhivatsvastivācanapūrvakam \|*  
*purohitasya varaṇaṃ puraḥ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*saṅkalpaṃ vedavihitaṃ vratametatsamācaret \|*  
*vrate dravyāṇi nityāni copacārāstu ṣoḍaśa \|\| 23 \|\|*

*deyāni nityaṃ deveśi kṛṣṇāya paramātmane \|*  
*āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*snānīyaṃ madhuparkaṃ ca vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca \|*  
*sugandhi puṣpadhūpaṃ ca dīpanaivedyacandanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

18-25. After taking a bath including washing the hair of the head, one should clad oneself in clean garments and then resort to fasting, the next day, in the early morning one should get up from the bed and brush the teeth, should take a bath with clean water performing Acamana and with great effort recite the name of Hari and offer arghya to lord Krishna and proceed to his abode. One should clad oneself in two clean garments and be seated on the sacred seat. One should complete the daily routine taking Acamana (sipping of water), the application of sandal-paste and performing all other routines. Thereafter by making an effort, the priest should be selected who should consecrate the vase reciting the svasti-mantra. Thereafter, taking a samkalpa according to the provisions of the Vedas, one should complete the vrata. One should make all the sixteen offerings daily, while performing pUja. О goddess, these articles are offered to lord Krishna daily which include the seat, welcome padya, arghya, acamana, breathing, madhuparka, clothes, ornaments, fragrant flowers, essence, lamp, naivedya, sandal-paste, yajnopavita, camphor and fragrant betel.

## \|\| 4.26 \|\|

*yajñasūtraṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ karpūrādisuvāsitam \|*  
*dravyāṇyetāni pūjāyāścāṅgarūpāṇi sundari \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О beautiful one, all these articles are a part of the puja. О goddess, in case of any deficiency in the offerings, the performer is likely to lose her limbs.

## \|\| 4.27-28 \|\|

*devi kiñcidvihīnena cāṅgahāniḥ prajāyate \|*  
*aṃgahīnaṃ ca yatkarmma cāṅgahīno yathā naraḥ \|*  
*aṃgahīne ca kāryye ca phalahāniḥ prajāyate \|\| 27 \|\|*

*aṣṭottaraśataṃ puṣpaṃ pārijātasya viṣṇave \|*  
*deyaṃ pratidinaṃ durge svātmano rūpahetave \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. And the person is deprived of limbs with deficient performance. A deficient performance also results in the loss of merit. О Durga, a hundred and eight Parijata flowers are required to be offered to lord Krishna daily.

## \|\| 4.29-31 \|\|

*śvetacampakapuṣpāṇāṃ lakṣamakṣatamīpsitam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ haraye bhaktyā varṇasaundaryyahetave \|\| 29 \|\|*

*sahasrapatrapadmānāmakṣataṃ lakṣakaṃ tathā \|*  
*bhaktyā deyaṃ ca haraye mukhasaundaryyahetave \|\| 30 \|\|*

*amūlyaratnaracitaṃ darpaṇānāṃ sahasrakam \|*  
*deyaṃ nārāyaṇāyaiva netrayordīptihetave \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. And in order to increase the beauty, the white jasmine flowers are required to be offered besides a lakh of unbroken rice with devotion to lord Krishna, in order to increase the beauty of the face; a lotus with hundred petals and a lakh of unbroken flowers should be offered to the lord with devotion for the increase of the glory of the eyes. A lakh of mirrors made of invaluable gems should be offered of Narayana.

## \|\| 4.32 \|\|

*nīlotpalānāṃ lakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ kṛṣṇāya bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*vratāṅgabhūtaṃ deveśi cakṣuṣo rūpahetave \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. О goddess, for the increase in the beauty of the eyes, lord Krishna should be offered a lakh of blue lotus flowers with devotion which form part of the vrata.

## \|\| 4.33 \|\|

*himālayodbhavaṃ lakṣaṃ ruciraṃ śvetacāmaram \|*  
*pradeyaṃ keśavāyaiva keśasaundaryyahetave \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. For adding to the beauty of the hair on the head, a lakh of fly-whisks which are produced in the Himalaya should be offered to lord Kesava.

## \|\| 4.34 \|\|

*amūlyaratnaracitaṃ puṭakānāṃ sahasrakam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ gopikeśāya nāsāsaundaryahetave \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. For increasing the beauty of the nose, a lotus and beautiful boxes made of invaluable gems should be presented to Krishna the lord of the gopis.

## \|\| 4.35 \|\|

*bandhūkapuṣpalakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ rādheśvarāya ca \|*  
*saumyauṣṭhādharayoścaiva varṇasaundaryahetave \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. То increase the beauty of the lips, a lakh of Bandhukal flowers should be offered to Krishna, the lord of Radha.

## \|\| 4.36 \|\|

*muktāphalānāṃ lakṣaṃ ca dantasaundaryahetave \|*  
*deyaṃ golokanāthāya śailaje bhaktipūrvakam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O daughter of the mountain, for increase in the beauty of the teeth, lakh of pearls should be offered to the lord of the Goloka with devotion.

## \|\| 4.37 \|\|

*ratnagaṇḍūṣalakṣaṃ ca gaṇḍasaundaryyahetave \|*  
*maheśvarāya dātavyaṃ vrate śailendrakanyake \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О daughter of the mountain, in order to increase the beauty of the cheeks, a lakh of balls 1 made of gems should be offered to Maheshvara with devotion.

## \|\| 4.38-39 \|\|

*ratnapāśakalakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ brahmeśvarāya ca \|*  
*oṣṭhādhaḥ sthalarūpāya vratī prāṇeśi bhaktitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*karṇabhūṣaṇalakṣaṃ ca ratnasāravinirmmitam \|*  
*deyaṃ sarveśvarāyaiva karṇasaundaryyahetave \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. O lady of my life, for the increase of the beauty of the lower part of the lips, a lakh of gem-studded ornaments should be offered to lord Krishna with devotion; for increasing the beauty of ears, a lakh of ear ornaments should be given away in charity by the devotees to the lord of all.

## \|\| 4.40 \|\|

*mādhvīkakalaśānāṃ ca lakṣaṃ ratnavinirmmitam \|*  
*deyaṃ viśveśvarāyaiva svarasaundaryyahetave \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. For increasing the beauty of the voice, a lakh of kalasas filled with bassia letifolia should be offered to the lord of the universe.

## \|\| 4.41 \|\|

*sudhāpūrṇaṃ ca kumbhānāṃ sahasraṃ ratnanirmmitam \|*  
*deyaṃ kṛṣṇāya deveśi vākyasaundaryyahetave \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. О goddess, for the increase in the beauty of speech a thousand pitchers filled with nectar should be offered to lord Krishna, with devotion.

## \|\| 4.42 \|\|

*ratnapradīpalakṣaṃ ca gopaveṣavidhāyine \|*  
*deyaṃ kiśoraveṣāya dṛṣṭisaundaryya hetave \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. For increase in the beauty of the eyes, a lakh of lamps made of gems should be offered to the Balamukunda form of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 4.43-44 \|\|

*dhattūrakusumākāraṃ ratnapātrasahasrakam \|*  
*deyaṃ gorakṣakāyaiva balasaundaryyahetave \|\| 43 \|\|*

*sadratnasāraracitaṃ padmanālasahasrakam \|*  
*deyaṃ caṇḍakapālāya bāhusaundaryyahetave \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. To increase the beauty of the throat, a vase studded with a thousand gems, resembling the thorn-apple should be offered to lord who is the protector of the cows; for protecting the beauty of the arms a thousand lotuses with stalks should be offered to Chandakapala.

## \|\| 4.45 \|\|

*lakṣaṃ ca raktapadmānāṃ karasaundaryahetave \|*  
*deyaṃ gopāṃganeśāya nārāyaṇi harivrate \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. О Narayani, for increase in the beauty of the hands a lakh of red lotuses should be offered to the lord of the Gopis with devotion.

## \|\| 4.46 \|\|

*aṃgulīyakalakṣaṃ ca ratnasāravinirmmitam \|*  
*aṃgulīnāṃ ca rūpārthaṃ deyaṃ deveśvarāya ca \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. To increase the beauty of the fingers, a lakh of finger rings studded with gems should be offered to Deveshvara.

## \|\| 4.47 \|\|

*maṇīndrasāralakṣaṃ ca śvetavarṇaṃ manoharam \|*  
*deyaṃ munīndranāthāya nakhasaundaryyahetave \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. For the increase of the beauty of the nails the best of gems of white colour should be offered numbering a lakh of them to Munlndranatha.

## \|\| 4.48 \|\|

*sadratnasārahārāṇāṃ lakṣaṃ cātimanoharam \|*  
*deyaṃ madanamohāya vakṣaḥsaundaryyahetave \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. In order to increase the beauty of the chest, a lakh of beautiful garlands studded with gems should be offered to Madanamohana.

## \|\| 4.49 \|\|

*supakvaśrīphalānāṃ ca lakṣaṃ ca sumanoharam \|*  
*deyaṃ siddhendranāthāya stanasaundaryyahetave \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. For increasing the beauty of the breasts a lakh of ripe wood apples should be offered to lord Shiva.

## \|\| 4.50 \|\|

*sadratnavartulākārapatralaśaṃ manoharam \|*  
*deyaṃ padmālayeśāya dehasaundaryyahetave \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. For increase in the beauty of the body, a lakh of golden sheet studded with gems should be offered to lord Vishnu, with devotion.

## \|\| 4.51-52 \|\|

*sadratnasāraracitaṃ nābhīnāṃ ca sahasrakam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ padmanābhāya nābhisaundaryyahetave \|\| 51 \|\|*

*sadratnasāraracitaṃ rathacakrasahasrakam \|*  
*nitambasaundaryyārthaṃ ca deyaṃ vai cakrapāṇaye \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. For increasing the beauty of the navel, a thousand of navels made of the best of gold and studded with gems should be offered to Padmanabha. For increasing the beauty of the pelvic region, a thousand of Ratha-cakras made of the best of gems should be offered to lord Cakrapani.

## \|\| 4.53 \|\|

*suvarṇarambhāstambhānāṃ lakṣaṃ ca sumanoharam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ śrīnivāsāya śroṇisaundaryyahetave \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. For the increase in the beauty of the thighs a lakh of trunks of the banyana trees, made of gold should be offered to lord Srinivasa.

## \|\| 4.54 \|\|

*śatapatrasthalābjānāṃ lakṣamamlānamakṣatam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ padmanetrāya pādasaundaryyahetave \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. For increasing the beauty of the teeth, a lakh of spotless and unbroken lotuses should be offered to Kamala-nayana.

## \|\| 4.55 \|\|

*suvarṇaracitānāṃ ca khañjanānāṃ sahasrakam \|*  
*gatisaundaryyahetvarthaṃ deyaṃ lakṣmīśvarāya ca \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. For increase in the grace, a lakh of fire-flies made of gold should be offered to the lord of Lakshmi.

## \|\| 4.56 \|\|

*rājahaṃsasahasraṃ ca gajendrāṇāṃ sahasrakam \|*  
*suvarṇaracitaṃ deyaṃ haraye gatihetave \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. For preserving the speech, a thousand geese made of gold and a thousand elephants, made of gold should be offered to lord Gajendra.

## \|\| 4.57-58 \|\|

*suvarṇacchatralakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ nārāyaṇāya ca \|*  
*vicitraṃ ratnasāreṇa mūrddhasaundaryyahetave \|\| 57 \|\|*

*mālatīnāṃ ca kusumamakṣataṃ lakṣamīśvari \|*  
*deyaṃ vṛndāvaneśāya hāsyasaundaryyahetave \|\| 58\|\|*

57-58. In order to increase the beauty of the head a thousand umbrellas made of gold and studded with gems should be offered to Narayana, О goddess, to preserve the beauty in the smiling, a lakh of jasmine flowers and a lakh of unbroken Malati flowers should be offered to the lord of Vrindavana

## \|\| 4.59 \|\|

*amūlyaratnalakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ nārāyaṇāya vai \|*  
*suvrate vratapūrṇārthaṃ śīlasaundaryyahetave \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. О chaste lady, for the increase of the beauty of the character and for the successful completion of the vrata, a lakh of valuable gems, should be offered to Narayana.

## \|\| 4.60-61 \|\|

*svacchasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ maṇīndraśreṣṭhalakṣakam \|*  
*deyaṃ munīndranāthāya manassaundaryyahetave \|\| 60 \|\|*

*pravālasārasaṃkāśaṃ maṇisārasahasrakam \|*  
*deyaṃ kṛṣṇāya bhaktyā ca priyārāgavivṛddhaye \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. For the increase in the beauty of the mind, a lakh of crystal gems should be offered to lord Krishna.

## \|\| 4.62-63 \|\|

*māṇikyasāralakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ kṛṣṇāya yatnataḥ \|*  
*janmanaḥ koṭiparyyantaṃ svāmisaubhāgyahetave \|\| 62 \|\|*

*kūṣmāṇḍaṃ nārikelaṃ ca jambīraṃ śrīphalaṃ tathā \|*  
*phalānyetāni deyāni haraye putrahetave \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. In order to preserve the chastity of the lady and for the long life of her husband, a lakh of rubies should be offered to lord Krishna with the desire of having a son; a melon, a coconut citron and wood apple should also be offered to the lord.

## \|\| 4.64-65 \|\|

*ratnendrasāralakṣaṃ ca deyaṃ kṛṣṇāya yatnataḥ \|*  
*asaṃkhyajanmaparyyantaṃ svāmino dhanavṛddhaye \|\| 64 \|\|*

*vādyaṃ nānāprakāraṃ ca kāṃsyatālādikaṃ param \|*  
*vrate sampattivṛddhyartha śrīhariṃ śrāvayedvratī \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. For the increase of the fortune of the husband in innumerable births, a lakh of the best of the gems should be offered to lord Krishna; at the time of completion of the vrata, the devotee should arrange for a musical concert in honour of lord Hari.

## \|\| 4.66 \|\|

*pāyasaṃ piṣṭakaṃ sarpiḥ śarkarāktaṃ manoharam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ haraye bhaktyā svāmino bhogavṛddhaye \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Offering the delicious eatables made of ghee and sugar, besides the payasam and stuff made of a mixture should be offered to the lord.

## \|\| 4.67 \|\|

*sugandhipuṣpamālānāṃ lakṣamakṣatamīpsitam \|*  
*pradeyaṃ haraye bhaktyā haribhaktivivṛddhaye \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. For the increase in the devotion of the lord, the garland of a lakh of flowers should be offered with devotion to the lord.

## \|\| 4.68 \|\|

*naivedyāni ca deyāni svādūni madhurāṇi ca \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇaprītiprāptyarthaṃ durge nānāvidhāni ca \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. О Durga, in order to achieve the grace of lord Krishna, several types of delicious and sweet preparations may be offered as naivedya.

## \|\| 4.69 \|\|

*nānāvidhāni puṣpāṇi tulasīsaṃyutāni ca \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇaprītaye bhaktyā vrate deyāni suvrate \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. O chaste lady, in order to attract the grace of lord Krishna, one should offer the Tulasi leaves and several other flowers with devotion.

## \|\| 4.70 \|\|

*brāhmaṇānāṃ sahasraṃ ca pratyahaṃ bhojayedvratī \|*  
*svātmanaḥ sasyavṛddhyarthaṃ vrate janmani janmani \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. In order to increase the prosperity in several coming births, a thousand Brahmanas should be offered food daily.

## \|\| 4.71 \|\|

*puṣpāñjaliśataṃ deyaṃ nityaṃ pūrṇaṃ ca pūjane \|*  
*praṇāmaśatakaṃ devi kartavyaṃ bhaktivṛddhaye \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. О goddess, for the completion of this vrata, a hundred offerings of flowers should be made daily with devotion offering at the same time salutations, daily.

## \|\| 4.72 \|\|

*ṣaṇmāsāṃśca haviṣyānnaṃ māsānpañca phalādikam \|*  
*haviḥ pakṣaṃ jalaṃ pakṣaṃ vrate bhakṣecca suvrate \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. The person who performs this vrata should live on the left-over of the food of the sacrifice or on fruits for five months. The left-over of Hari for a fortnight and for a fortnight one should live on consuming water alone.

## \|\| 4.73 \|\|

*ratnapradīpaśatakaṃ vahniṃ dadyāddivāniśam \|*  
*rātrau kuśāsanaṃ kṛtvā nityaṃ jāgaraṇaṃ vrate \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. During the performing of the vrata, a hundred lamps of gems should be lighted and one should sleep on the bed of fa/sa-grass during the night and always remain awake.

## \|\| 4.74-79 \|\|

*jñanavṛddhirjāgaraṇe suvuddhirmūlabhojane \|*  
*lobhamohakāmakrodhabhayaścokavivādakam \|\| 74 \|\|*

*smaraṇaṃ kīrttataṃ keliḥ prekṣaṇaṃ guhyabhāṣaṇam \|*  
*saṃkalpo'dhyavasāyaśca kriyānirvṛttirityapi \|\| 75 \|\|*

*svapnamaithunakaṃ tyājyaṃ vratinā vrataśuddhaye \|*  
*kalahaśca parityājyo vrate krīḍāvivṛddhayev \|\| 76 \|\|*

*sampūrṇe ca vrate devi pratiṣṭhā tadanantaram \|*  
*triśataṃ vai ṣaṣṭyadhikaṃ rallakaṃ vastrasaṃyutam \|\| 77 \|\|*

*sabhojyaṃ sopavītaṃ ca sopahāraṃ dadātvayam \|*  
*triśataṃ vai ṣaṣṭyadhikasahasraṃ viprabhojanam \|\| 78 \|\|*

*deyā vratasamāptau ca dakṣiṇā vidhibodhitā \|*  
*anyāṃ samāptidivase kathayiṣyāmi dakṣiṇām \|\| 79 \|\|*

74-79. By remaining awake in Jagarana, there is increase in intelligence and by consuming fruits and roots, one develops noble wisdom. During this time, one should get oneself free from greed, illusion, passions, anger, fear, grief and unnecessary discussion. О goddess, for keeping chastity during this Punyaka-vrata, the worshipper should detract his attention from voluptuous thoughts, recitations, sports, gazing at something, secret talk, resolve for getting something, efforts for getting something, conjugal pleasures and various types of love-sports besides quarrelling should be discarded. At the completion of the vrata one should perform the ceremony of establishing it. One should collect three hundred and sixty blankets, clothes, food, yajnopavita and presents should be given away in charity, one should feed three hundred and sixty Brahmanas. One should make the offering of three hundred and sixty sea-same seeds and should also offer three hundred and sixty gold coins in daksina. This has been ordained by Brahma. О goddess, on the day of the completion of the vrata an other daksina has to be given which I am going to tell you.

## \|\| 4.80-82 \|\|

*etadvrataphalaṃ devi dṛḍhā bhaktirharau bhavet \|*  
*haritulyo bhavetputro vikhyāto bhuvanatraye \|*  
*saundaryaṃ svāmisaubhāgyamaiśvaryyaṃ vipulaṃ dhanam \|\| 80 \|\|*

*sarvavāñchitasiddhīnāṃ bījaṃ janmani janmani \|*  
*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ devi vrataṃ kuru maheśvari \|\| 81 \|\|*

*putraste bhavitā sādhvītyuktvā sa virarāma ha \|\| 82 \|\|*

80-82. Thus after completing this vrata one gets the reward of achieving immense devotion to the lord. One also gets a noble son who is well known in the three worlds and he attains enough of beauty and she gets fortune for the husband and enormous riches. In every birth she achieves all the desired riches. О Maheshvari, О goddess, thus I have spoken to you everything about the vrata and you better accomplish it. О Sadhavi, you will surely get a son. Thus speaking Shiva kept quiet.

## Chapter 5

## \|\| 5.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śrutvā vratavidhāne ca durgā saṃhṛṣṭamānasā \|*  
*punaḥ papraccha kāntaṃ sā divyāṃ vratakathāṃ śubhām \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – After listening to the method of performing the Punyaka-vrata, the mind of goddess Durga was fully delighted. Thereafter, she enquired about the sacred vrata from lord Shiva.

## \|\| 5.2 \|\|

*śrīpārvatyuvāca \|*  
*kimadbhutaṃ vrataṃ nātha vidhānaphalamasya ca \|*  
*adhikāṃ tatkathāṃ brūhi vrataṃ kena prakāśitam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Parvati said – О lord, this is an astonishing type of vrata. You kindly tell me the way of performing it, its result, its story and who started it.

## \|\| 5.3 \|\|

*atha puṇyakavratakathā \|*  
*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*śatarūpā manoḥ patnī putraduḥkhena duḥkhitā \|*  
*brahmaṇaḥ sthānamāgatya sā brahmāṇamuvāca ha \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Mahadeva said – Once Satarupa the wife of Manu, felt concerned because of the nonavailability of a son. She was desirous of the same and she went to Brahma and said.

## \|\| 5.4 \|\|

*śatarūpovāca \|*  
*brahmankena prakāreṇa vandhyāyāśca suto bhavet \|*  
*tanme brūhi jagaddhātaḥ sṛṣṭikāraṇakāraṇa \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Satarupa said – О Brahmana, you are the creator of the universe and the cause of the creation of the same. Therefore, you kindly tell me, how a barren woman can achieve a son.

## \|\| 5.5 \|\|

*iti śrībahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde puṇyakavratakathanaṃ nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Because, О Brahmana, the household in which there is no son, the life of the house-holder is of no consequence besides the riches and the fortune. The house is devoid of glory.

## \|\| 5.6 \|\|

*tapodānodbhavaṃ puṇyaṃ janmāṃtarasukhāvaham \|*  
*sukhado mokṣaḥ prītidātā putraśca putriṇām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The merit earned by performing tapas and charity is useful in the other worlds and the son provides pleasure to the parents besides salvation.

## \|\| 5.7-8 \|\|

*putrī putramukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā cāśvamedhaśatodbhavam \|*  
*phalaṃ punnāmanarakatrāṇahetuṃ labheddhruvam \|\| 7 \|\|*

*putrotpatterupāyaṃ vai vada māṃ tāpasaṃyutām \|*  
*tadā bhadraṃ na cedbhartrā saha yāsyāmi kānanam \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. A person having a son earns the merit of performing the Asvamedha sacrifice because his son protects him from falling in the hell named Purh. Therefore you kindly tell me, whose mind is filled with grief, a remedy for begetting a son or otherwise I shall retire to the forest with my husband.

## \|\| 5.9 \|\|

*gṛhāṇa rājyamaiśvaryya dhanaṃ pṛthvīṃ prajāvahām \|*  
*kimetenāvayostāta vinā putrairaputriṇoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. You take away our kingdom, fortune, riches and the land filled with people because, О father, in case we remain childless then what could we do with all these.

## \|\| 5.10 \|\|

*aputriṇo mukhaṃ draṣṭuṃ vidvānnotsahate 'śivam \|*  
*mukhaṃ darśayituṃ lajjāṃ samavāpnotyaputrakaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The intellectual people do not want to see the face of a childless person, who is considered inauspicious and the one having no son also feels shy of facing others.

## \|\| 5.11 \|\|

*athavā garalaṃ bhuktvā pravekṣyāmi hutāśanam \|*  
*aputrapautramaśivaṃ gṛhaṃ syātstrīvihīnakam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Or otherwise, I shall jump into the fire flame by consuming poison, because a household without sons, grandsons and housewife is considered to be inauspicious.

## \|\| 5.12 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā sā sākṣādbrahmaṇo'gre ruroda ha \|*  
*kṛpānidhiśca tāṃ dṛṣṭvā pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Thus speaking, she started crying before Brahma. Thereafter the merciful Brahma spoke looking at her.

## \|\| 5.13 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vatse pravakṣyāmi putropāyaṃ sukhāvaham \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyādibījaṃ ca sarvavāñchāpradaṃ śubham \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Brahma said – О daughter, I will tell you the pleasant way of begetting a son, which is quite auspicious, bestows riches and fulfils all the desires.

## \|\| 5.14 \|\|

*māghaśuklatrayodaśyāṃ vratametatsupuṇyakam \|*  
*kartavyaṃ śuklakāle ca kṛṣṇamārādhya sarvadam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. There is a vrata called Supunyaka which is performed on the bright thirteenth day of the moon in the month of Magha. This vrata should be performed with devotion in the auspicious time for lord Krishna who fulfils all the wishes.

## \|\| 5.15-16 \|\|

*saṃvatsaraṃ ca kartavyaṃ sarvavighnavināśanam \|*  
*dravyāṇi vedairuktāni vrate deyāni suvrate \|\| 15 \|\|*

*vrataṃ ca kāṇvaśākhoktaṃ sarvavāñchitasiddhidam \|*  
*kṛtvā putraṃ labha śubhe viṣṇutulyaparākramam \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. О lady, this vrata is the destroyer of all the obstructions and has to be completed in one full year. For that purpose, one should give away in charity all the things that are prescribed in the Vedas. О chaste lady, thus according to Kanvasakha, all the wishes are fulfilled by performing this vrata and with the completion of this vrata one gets a valorous son with the grace of lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 5.17-18 \|\|

*brahmaṇaśca vacaḥ śrutvā sā kṛtvā vratamuttamam \|*  
*priyavratottānapādau lebhe putrau manoharau \|\| 17 \|\|*

*vrataṃ kṛtvā devahūtirlebhe siddheśvaraṃ sutam \|*  
*nārāyaṇāṃśaṃ kapilaṃ puṇyakaṃ puṇyadaṃ śubham \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. On hearing the words of Brahma she performed that vrata as a result of which she got two sons named Priyavrata and Uttanpada. By performing the same auspicious Punyaka-vrata, Devahuti begot a son named Kapila who possessed the rays of Isvara and Narayana.

## \|\| 5.19 \|\|

*arundhatīdaṃ kṛtvā tu lebhe śaktisutaṃ śubhā \|*  
*śaktikāntā vrataṃ kṛtvā sutaṃ lebhe parāśaram \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Arundhatl on the other hand got a son named sakti, after performing this vrata and the wife of sakti after performing this vrata achieved Parashara as her son.

## \|\| 5.20 \|\|

*aditiśca vrataṃ kṛtvā lebhe vāmanakaṃ sutam \|*  
*śacī jayantaṃ putraṃ ca lebhe kṛtvedamīśvarī \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. By performing this vrata, Aditi got a son who happened to be the dwarf incarnation and IndranI, the goddess of all the gods, achieved a son named Jayanta by performing this vrata.

## \|\| 5.21-22 \|\|

*uttānapādapatnīdaṃ kṛtvā lebhe dhruvaṃ sutam \|*  
*kuberajāyā kṛtvedaṃ lebhe ca nalakūbaram \|\| 21 \|\|*

*sūryyapatnī manuṃ lebhe kṛtvedaṃ vratamuttamam \|*  
*atripatnī sutaṃ candraṃ lebhe kṛtvedamuttamam \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. The wife of Uttanapada got a son named Dhruva after performing this vrata and the wife of Kubera got two sons named Nala-Kubara. Sanjna, the wife of Atri got a son named Chandrama.

## \|\| 5.23-24 \|\|

*lebhe cāṃgirasaḥ patnī kṛtvedaṃ vratamuttamam \|*  
*bṛhaspatiṃ suraguruṃ putramasya prabhāvataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*bhṛgorbhāryyā vrataṃ kṛtvā lebhe daityaguruṃ sutam \|*  
*śukraṃ nārāyaṇāṃśaṃ ca sarvatejasvināṃ varam \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. The wife of Angira then got a son named Brihaspati who happened to be the priest of the gods. The wife of Bhrigu got a son named Sukra who happens to be the priest of the demons, who was born from the rays of Narayana and was quite illustrious.

## \|\| 5.25-26 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ devi vratānāṃ vratamuttamam \|*  
*tvamevaṃ kuru kalyāṇi himālayasute śubhe \|\| 25\|\|*

*sādhyaṃ rājendrapatnīnāṃ devīnāṃ ca sukhāvaham \|*  
*vratametanmahāsādhvi sādhvīnāṃ prāṇataḥ priyam \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. О goddess, I have, therefore, narrated to you the details about the best of the vratas. Therefore, О chaste lady, О daughter of Himalaya, О auspicious one, you also perform this vrata. О great ascetic, the chief queens can perform this vrata. It provides pleasure to the goddesses and is liked by the chaste ladies better then their lives.

## \|\| 5.27 \|\|

*vratasyāsya prabhāveṇa svayaṃ gopāṅganeśvaraḥ \|*  
*īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tava putro bhaviṣyati \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Because of the influence of this vrata, Krishna happens to be the lord of cowherds beside being lord of all the bMtas and will be your son also.

## \|\| 5.28 \|\|

*ityuktvā śaṃkarastatra virarāma ca nārada \|*  
*vrataṃ cakāra sā devī pradṛṣṭā śaṅkarājñayā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. О Narada, thus speaking Shiva kept quiet. Thereafter the goddess accepting the command of Shiva performed this vrata.

## \|\| 5.29 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ gaṇeśajanikāraṇam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Thus I have spoken everything to you which provides pleasure, salvation and is the cause for the birth of Ganesha; what else do you want to listen to from me.

## Chapter 6

## \|\| 6.1-2 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*nārāyaṇavacaḥ śrutvā nārado hṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*kiṃ papraccha punaḥ sādho tanme brūhi tapodhana \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*nārāyaṇavacaḥ śrutvā nārado hṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*vratārambhavidhānaṃ ca saṃpraṣṭumupacakrame \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Shaunaka said – О sage, О mendicant, on hearing the words of Narayana, Narada felt delightful and enquired about the performing of the vrata.

## \|\| 6.3 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*kṛtaṃ kena prakāreṇa vratametacchubhāvaham \|*  
*tanme brūhi muniśreṣṭha pārvatyā bharturājñayā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Narada said – О best of the sages accepting the command of the husband, how did Parvati perform the vrata, you kindly tell me.

## \|\| 6.4 \|\|

*lalābha janma bhūteśaḥ kṛte suvratayā vrate \|*  
*brahmankena prakāreṇa tannaḥ śaṃsitumarhasi \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О Brahmana after completion of the vrata by Parvati how could Krishna be born out of her womb you kindly tell me.

## \|\| 6.5-8 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kathayitvā kathāṃ divyāṃ vidhānaṃ ca vratasya ca \|*  
*svayaṃ vidhātā tapasāṃ jagāma tapase śivaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇeśakhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde vratājñāgrahaṇaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*paramānandapūrṇaśca jñānānandaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*divāniśaṃ na jānāti harimantraṃ bahiḥ smaran \|\| 7 \|\|*

*prahṛṣṭamanasā devī pārvatī bharturājñayā \|*  
*kiṅkarānprerayāmāsa viprāṃśca vratahetave \|\| 8 \|\|*

5-8. Narayana said – Shiva the creator of the tapas himself narrated the divine story about this vrata and the method of performing the same, after which he left the place, because he always remained anxious about performing the tapas of the lord. By taking to the form of the lord, he becomes Hari, he remains always engrossed in the devotion of Hari and as such he always adores Hari. He is blissful, all knowledgeable and eternal. By remaining engrossed in the reciting of the name of Hari, he becomes unmindful of day and night with the command of the husband. Parvati performed this vrata with a pleasant mind and she inspired her attendants and Brahmanas for the purpose.

## \|\| 6.9-11 \|\|

*ānīya sarvadravyāṇi vrate yogyāni yāni ca \|*  
*vrataṃ kartuṃ samārebhe śubhadā sā śubhe kṣaṇe \|\| 9 \|\|*

*sanatkumāro bhagavānājagāma vidheḥ sutaḥ \|*  
*mūrttimāṃstejasā rāśiḥ prajvalanbrahmatejasā \|\| 10 \|\|*

*brahmā''jagāma hṛṣṭaśca brahmalokātsabhāryyakaḥ \|*  
*atitrasto hi bhagavānājagāma maheśvaraḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

9-11. Parvati who always bestows fortunes summoned all the essentials for performing the vrata and started the same in an auspicious time. Sanatkumara, the son of Brahma, arrived there, who was shining with the divine lustre. Brahma also delightfully arrived there with his wife and the great lord also arrived there.

## \|\| 6.12-13 \|\|

*viṣṇuḥ kṣīrodaśāyī ca salakṣmīkaścaturbhujaḥ \|*  
*bhagavāñjagatāṃ pātā śāstā bharttā sapārṣadaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*vanamālādhara śyāmo bhūṣito ratnabhūṣaṇaiḥ \|*  
*tathā sambhṛtasambhāro ratnayānena nārada \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. The four-armed lord Vishnu who dwells in the ocean of milk also arrived there. He is the preserver of the universe and controls it. He arrived there with his courtiers and Lakshmi mounted on a plane. He was wearing a garland of forest flowers having the complexion of dark clouds, adorned with all the gem-studded ornaments and had all the materials with him.

## \|\| 6.14-20 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanandaśca kapilaśca sanātanaḥ \|*  
*āsuriśca kraturhaṃso voḍhuḥ pañcaśikho'ruṇiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*yatiśca sumatiścaiva vasiṣṭhaśca sahānugaḥ \|*  
*pulahaśca pulastyaścāpyatriśca bhṛguraṃgirāḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*agastyaśca pracetāśca durvāsāścyavanastathā \|*  
*marīciḥ kaśyapaḥ kaṇvo jaratkāruśca gautamaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*bṛhaspatirutathyaśca saṃvartaḥ saubharistathā \|*  
*jābālirjamadagniśca jaigīṣavyaśca devalaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*gokāmukho vakrarathaḥ pāribhadraḥ parāśaraḥ \|*  
*viśvāmitro vāmadeva ṛṣyaśṛṃgo vibhāṇḍakaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*mārkaṇḍeyo mṛkaṇḍuśca puṣkaro lomaśastathā \|*  
*kautso vatsaśca dakṣaśca bālāgniraghamarṣaṇaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*kātyāyanaḥ kaṇādaśca pāṇiniḥ śākaṭāyanaḥ \|*  
*śaṃkurāpiśaliścaiva śākalyaḥ śaṅkha eva ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

14-20. О Narada, thereafter, Sanaka, Sananda, Kapila, Sanatana, Asuri Kratu, Hansa, Vodhu, Pancasikha, Aruni, Yati, Sumati, Vasistha with his disciples, Pulaha, Pulastya, Atri, Bhrigu, Angira, Agastya, Praceta, Durvasa, Cyavana, Marlci, Kashyapa, Kanva, Jaratkaru, Gautama, Brihaspati, Utathya, Samvarta, Saubhri, Jabali, Jamadagni, Jaiglsavya, Devala, Gokamukha, Vakraratha, Paribhadra, Parasara, Vishvamitra, Vamadeva, Rsyashrnga, Vibhandaka, Markandeya, Mrkundu, Puskara, Lomasha, Kautsa, Vatsa, Daksha, Balagni, Aghamarsana, Katyayana, Kanada, Panini, Sakatayana, Sanku, Apisali, Sakalya and Saihkha also arrived there.

## \|\| 6.21 \|\|

*ete cānye ca bahavaḥ saśiṣyā munayo mune \|*  
*āvāṃ ca dharmmaputrau ca naranārāyaṇau samau \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. О sage, besides the above several other sages also arrived there together with their disciples. Both myself and the son of Dharma besides Nara-Narayana also arrived there.

## \|\| 6.22 \|\|

*dikpālāśca tathā devā yakṣagandharvakinnarāḥ \|*  
*ājagmuḥ parvatāḥ sarve sagaṇāḥ pārvatīvrate \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. In addition to the above the Dikpalas, the gods, the Yaksas, Gandharvas, Kinnaras and all the mountains also arrived there to participate in the vrata of Parvati.

## \|\| 6.23-27 \|\|

*himālayaḥ śailarājaḥ sāpatyaśca sabhāryakaḥ \|*  
*sagaṇaḥ sānugaścaiva ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*tathā sambhṛtasambhāro nānādravyasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*maṇi māṇikyaratnāni vrate yogyāni yāni ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

*nānāprakāravastūni jagatyāṃ durlabhāni ca \|*  
*lakṣaṃ ca gajaratnānāmaśvaratnaṃ trilakṣakam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*daśalakṣaṃ gavāṃ ratnaṃ śatalakṣaṃ suvarṇakam \|*  
*rucakānāṃ hīrakāṇāṃ sparśānāṃ ca tathaiva ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

*muktānāṃ ca caturlakṣaṃ kaustubhānāṃ sahasrakam \|*  
*susvādunānādravyāṇāṃ lakṣabhārāṇi kautukī \|*  
*anantaratnaprabhava ājagāma sutāvrate \|\| 27 \|\|*

23-27. Himalaya the king of the mountains arrived there together with his wife and children, adorned with all the ornaments and also carried various types of material like valuables, gems, rubies, jewels and several other things required for the vrata including a lakh of elephants, three lakhs of the best of horses, ten lakhs of cows, a hundred lakhs of gems and an equal number of gold, gold coins, gems, touch stones four lakhs of pearls, a thousand kaustubha-gems, besides delicious eatables which were a lakh in number. Thus Himalaya, carrying all the gems with him arrived there to participate in the vrata of his daughter.

## \|\| 6.28 \|\|

*brāhmaṇā manavaḥ siddhā nāgā vidyādharāstathā \|*  
*saṃnyāsino bhikṣukāśca bandinaḥ pārvatīvrate \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the vrata of Parvati many of the Brahmanas, sages, siddhas, Nagas, Vidyadharas, recluses, beggars and bards also arrived.

## \|\| 6.29 \|\|

*vidyādharī nartakī ca narttakā'psarasāṃ gaṇāḥ \|*  
*nānāvidhā vādyabhāṇḍā ājagmuḥ śivamandiram \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Vidyadharis, the female dancers, the male dancers, Apsaras and the persons who played on musical instruments, also arrived there.

## \|\| 6.30-31 \|\|

*kailāsarājamārgaṃ ca candanena susaṃskṛtam \|*  
*āmrapallavasūtrāḍhyaṃ kadalīstambhaśobhitam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*dūrvādhānyaphalaiḥ parṇalājapuṣpairvibhūṣitam \|*  
*nirmitaṃ padmarāgeṇa dadṛśuste gaṇā mudā \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. At that point of time the kings way of Kailasa was decorated with sandal trees, mango leaves, tied in cords, trunks of the banana trees, DUrva-grass, fried paddy, leaves and flowers, besides other scented materials. The invitees witnessed the same with interest.

## \|\| 6.32 \|\|

*uccaiḥ siṃhāsaneṣvete pūjitāḥ śaṅkareṇa ca \|*  
*kailāsavāsinaḥ sarve paramānandasaṃyutāḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Shiva himself adored everyone and lodged them on high pedestals. All the dwellers of Kailasa were filled with ecstasy.

## \|\| 6.33-35 \|\|

*dānādhyakṣaḥ śunāsīraḥ kuberaḥ kośarakṣakaḥ \|*  
*ādeṣṭā ca svayaṃ sūryyaḥ pariveṣṭā jalādhipaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

*dadhnāṃ nadyaḥ sahasrāṇi dugdhānāṃ ca tathaiva ca \|*  
*sahasrāṇi ghṛtānāṃ ca guḍānāṃ ca śatāni ca \|\| 34 \|\|*

*mādhvīkānāṃ sahasrāṇi tailānāṃ ca śatāni ca \|*  
*lakṣāṇi caiva takrāṇāṃ babhūvuḥ pārvatīvrate \|\| 35 \|\|*

33-35. In the vrata, Indra acted as the lord of charity, Kubera acted as the lord of riches, Sflrya issued all the commands, Varuna served the materials. There were a thousand streams of curd, a thousand of streams of milk and ghee, a hundred streams of raw-sugar (guda), a thousand streams of beverages, a hundred streams of butter milk.

## \|\| 6.36-37 \|\|

*pīyūṣāṇāṃ ca kumbhāni śatalakṣāṇi nārada \|*  
*miṣṭānnānāṃ śarkarāṇāṃ babhūvurlakṣarāśayaḥ \|*  
*yavagodhūmacūrṇānāṃ ghṛtāktānāṃ ca nārada \|\| 36 \|\|*

*svastikānāṃ ca pūrṇānāṃ babhūvurlakṣarāśayaḥ \|*  
*guḍasaṃskṛtalājānāṃ babhūvuḥ koṭirāśayaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. О Narada a hundred lakhs of vases of nectar, sweets and sugar were stored in a lakh of heaps. О Narada, the flour of wheat and barley was also collected in the same quantity. A lakh of fried sweets, soaked in ghee and a lakh of balls of fried paddy mixed with guda were made available.

## \|\| 6.38 \|\|

*śālīnāṃ pṛthukānāṃ ca rāśīnāṃ daśakoṭayaḥ \|*  
*varataṇḍularāśīnāṃ mune saṃkhyā na vidyate \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. There were ten crores of the preparation of fried rice. And the number of the rice preparations was beyond counting.

## \|\| 6.39-40 \|\|

*svarṇaraupyapravālānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca mahāmune \|*  
*babhūvuḥ parvatāstatra kailāse pārvatī vrate \|\| 39 \|\|*

*pāyasaṃ piṣṭakaṃ caiva śālyannaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*cakāra lakṣmīḥ pākaṃ ca vyañjanaṃ ghṛtasaṃskṛtam \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. О great sage, in the vrata of Parvati, there were mountains of gold, silver and gems, all the sweet preparation including payasam, cooked rice and other vegetables were prepared by Laks ml herself.

## \|\| 6.41 \|\|

*bubhuje devarṣigaṇaiḥ śivo nārāyaṇena ca \|*  
*babhūvurlakṣaviprāśca pariveṣaṇakārakāḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Lord Narayana was taking his food together with Shiva and all the gods, a lakh of Brahmanas were engaged in serving the food.

## \|\| 6.42 \|\|

*tāmbūlaṃ ca dadau tebhyaḥ karpūrādisuvāsitam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthebhyo vipralakṣāḥ sudakṣakāḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. A lakh of clever Brahmanas were offering betels to the invitees who were seated on their seats gracefully.

## \|\| 6.43-46 \|\|

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca viṣṇuṃ kṣīrodaśāyinam \|*  
*sevyamānaṃ pārṣadaiśca sasmitaiḥ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*ṛṣibhiḥ stūyamānaṃ ca siddhairdevagaṇaistathā \|*  
*vidyādharīṇāṃ nṛtyāni paśyantaṃ sasmitaṃ mudā \|\| 44 \|\|*

*gandharvāṇāṃ ca saṃgītaṃ śrutavantaṃ manoharam \|*  
*papraccha śaṅkaro brahmanbrahmeśaṃ prītipūrvakam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*brahmaṇā prerito yuktaṃ vrataṃ karttavyamīpsitam \|*  
*devarṣigaṇapūrṇāyāṃ sabhāyāṃ saṃpuṭāñjaliḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

43-46. О Brahmana, lord Vishnu, the dweller of the ocean of milk, was seated on the gem-studded lion-throne. The courtiers were moving the white fly-whisks on the lord. The sages and siddhas besides the gods were reciting the prayers, the blissful lord wore a serene smile on his face while the Vidyadharis kept on dancing accompanied by music. At that point of time Shiva at the instance of Brahma who was about to complete the vrata, asked Vishnu in the court, which was killed by gods and the sages.

## \|\| 6.47 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*madīyaṃ vacanaṃ nātha śrīnivāsa śṛṇu prabho \|*  
*tapassvarūpa tapasāṃ karmaṇāṃ ca phalaprada \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Mahadeva said – О lord, О Srinivasa, you are the form of tapas and also the one who bestows the result of the tapas. You kindly listen to my prayer.

## \|\| 6.48-49 \|\|

*vratānāṃ japayajñānāṃ pūjānāṃ sarvapūjita \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ bījarūpeṇa vāñchākalpataro hare \|\| 48 \|\|*

*supuṇyaṃ ca vrataṃ karttuṃ brahmannicchati pārvatī \|*  
*putrārthinī sā śokārttā hṛdayena vidūyatā \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. О lord, you are most adorable among the japam, yajnas and adorations. О Hari, you are a wish-fulfilling tree and the form of see for all. О Brahmana, Parvati is desirous of a son and as such she intends to perform a Punyaka-vrata with her heart filled with grief.

## \|\| 6.50 \|\|

*ratibhaṃge kṛte devairvyarthavīryyaśucārditā \|*  
*prabodhitā mayā sādhvī vividhairvacanāmṛtaiḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The semen of Shiva had been destroyed because of the disturbance caused by the gods. She was very much worried. Thereafter, I spoke many nectar-like sweet words to the chaste lady and pacified her.

## \|\| 6.51 \|\|

*satputraṃ svāmisaubhāgyaṃ suvratā yācate vrate \|*  
*tābhyāṃ vinā na santuṣṭā svaprāṇāṃstyaktumicchati \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. In this vrata she is desirous of having a son besides seeking for welfare of her husband. She cannot be happy without either of them. She is prepared to lay down her life.

## \|\| 6.52 \|\|

*purā tyaktvā svadehaṃ ca pitṛyajñe ca māninī \|*  
*mannindayā himavati punarjanma lalābha sā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. In earlier times, the proud lady ended her life in the yajha of her father because of my denouncement there and she was reborn in the house of Himalaya.

## \|\| 6.53 \|\|

*sarvaṃ jānāsi vṛttāntaṃ sarvajñaṃ tvāṃ vadāmi kim \|*  
*dīnāṃ tāṃ vada tattvajña pariṇāmaśubhapradām \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. О well-versed in the tattvas, you are all knowledgeable and are aware of all the stories; what can I speak to you? You let me know your command. You kindly speak out your command which will result in our welfare.

## \|\| 6.54-56 \|\|

*durnivāryyaśca sarveśa strīsvabhāvaśca cāpalaḥ \|*  
*dustyajyaṃ yogibhiḥ siddhairasmābhiśca tapasvibhiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

*jitendriyairjitakrodhaiḥ strīrūpaṃ mohakāraṇam \|*  
*sarvamāyākaraṇḍaṃ ca kāmavarddhanakāraṇam \|\| 55 \|\|*

*brahmāstraṃ kāmadevasya durbhedyaṃ jayakāraṇam \|*  
*sunirmitaṃ ca vidhinā sarvādyaṃ vidhipūrvakam \|\| 56 \|\|*

54-56. Because, О lord, of all the people, the nature of ladies is quite unstable and the mind of the ladies cannot be read properly by yogis, siddhas, Tapasvis, who have controlled their senses and anger. A woman is a cause of illusion and a bundle of passions. Brahma had in earlier times created her to establish victory over Kamadeva.

## \|\| 6.57-60 \|\|

*mokṣadvārakapāṭaṃ ca haribhaktinirodhanam \|*  
*saṃsārabandhanastambharajjurūpamakṛntanam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*vairāgyanāśabījaṃ ca śaśvadrāgavivarddhanam \|*  
*pattanaṃ sāhasānāṃ ca doṣāṇāmālayaṃ sadā \|\| 58 \|\|*

*apratyayānāṃ kṣetraṃ ca svayaṃ kapaṭamūrtimat \|*  
*ahaṅkārāśrayaṃ śaśvadviṣakumbhaṃ sudhāmukham \|\| 59 \|\|*

*sarvairasādhyamānaṃ ca durārādhyaṃ ca sarvadā \|*  
*svakāryyasādhyācārāḍhyaṃ kalahāṃkurakāraṇam \|\| 60 \|\|*

57-60. She happens to be the door to moksa, ar. obstruction in the adoration of the lord, a cord binding one to the worldly bondage, the destroyer of Vairagya, who increases the illusion always, the dwelling place for the courageous people, the one in whom all the ills reside, the field of deceitfulness, all deceitful, the refuge of arrogance. Her life is a vase of nectar with poison plastered on the neck of it. She is beyond the reach of everybody, beyond adoration, well-versed in establishing their own purpose and the seed for quarrel.

## \|\| 6.61 \|\|

*sarvaṃ niveditaṃ brahmankarttavyaṃ vaktumarhasi \|*  
*kāryyaṃ sarvaṃ parāmarśe pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. О Brahmana, I have spoken everything; now you kindly do something to accomplish my task, speaking the words which may be practical and result in pleasure.

## \|\| 6.62-63 \|\|

*śrīnārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*ityevamuktvā bhagavānnirīkṣya brahmaṇo mukham \|*  
*virarāma sabhāmadhye stutvā ca kamalāpatim \|\| 62 \|\|*

*śaṅkarasya vacaḥ śrutvā prahasya jagadīśvaraḥ \|*  
*hitaṃ ca nītivacanaṃ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. Narayana said – Shiva after thus speaking looked at the face of Brahma and praying in favour of lord Vishnu he kept quiet. On hearing the words of Shiva, lord Jagadlsvara smiled for a moment all. A spoke the words which were quite beneficial and appropriate.

## \|\| 6.64 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*supuṇyakavrataṃ sāraṃ satīsantānahetave \|*  
*svāmisaubhāgyabījaṃ ca patnī te kartumicchati \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Vishnu said – Your chaste wife intends to perform the Supunyaka-vrata for begetting a son who should be the essence of all.

## \|\| 6.65 \|\|

*sarvāsādhyaṃ durārādhyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|*  
*sukhadaṃ sukhasāraṃ ca mokṣadaṃ pārvatīśvara \|\| 65\|\|*

65\. О lord of Parvati, this vrata is very difficult to accomplish and could be performed with great trouble and devotion. It bestows the results of all the desires, is quite pleasant and is the essence of all the pleasures and bestows salvation.

## \|\| 6.66-72 \|\|

*sarvesri vrataparo vratārādhyo guṇātparaḥ। golokanātho bhagavānbrahma sanātanaḥ \|\| 66\|\|*

*ātmā sākṣisvarūpaśca jyotīrūpaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*nirāśrayaśca nirlipto nirupādhirnirāmayaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

*bhaktaprāṇaśca bhakteśo bhaktānugrahakārakaḥ \|*  
*durārādhyo hi yo'nyeṣāṃ bhaktānāmatisādhakaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

*bhaktyadhīno hi bhagavānsarvasiddho hi niṣphalaḥ \|*  
*te yasya ca kalāḥ puṃso brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

*mahānvirāḍyadaṃśaśca nirliptaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|*  
*avyayo nigrahaścogro bhaktānugrahavigrahaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*ugragraho grahāṇāṃ ca grahanigrahakārakaḥ \|*  
*trikoṭijanmamadhye ca na sādhyo bhavatā vinā \|\| 71 \|\|*

*labdhavā hi bhārate janma haribhaktiṃ labhennaraḥ \|*  
*sevanaṃ kṣudradevānāṃ kṛtvā saptasu janmasu \|\| 72 \|\|*

66-72. Shri Krishna is the lord of all, devoted to vratas and is adorable true vratas, beyond gunas, is lord of Goloka, complete Brahmana, everlasting, the great soul, witness of all, the form of flame, eternal, without any source, uninvolved, devoid of any degrees, spotless, life of the devotees, lord of the devotees and the one who always remains merciful towards the devotees. The things which 'are difficult to achieve by others, they can easily be achieved by his devotees. The lord is always controlled by his devotees. He accomplishes all the desires and is spotless. Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva happen to be the rays of the same lord. Mahavirat too happens to be his ray. He is uninvolved, beyond Prakriti, indestructible, all watchful, terrific, takes to any form to protect his devotees; he is the terrific of all the planets and controls them also. He cannot meet with success without you in three crores of births.

## \|\| 6.73-75 \|\|

*sūryyamantramavāpnoti kevalaṃ sa tadāśiṣā \|*  
*sūryyamantraṃ samārādhya triṣu janmasu bhārate \|\| 73 \|\|*

*prāpnoti śaivaṃ mantraṃ ca sarvadaṃ mānavo mudā \|*  
*saṃsevya parayā bhaktyā tvāmevaṃ saptajanmasu \|\| 74 \|\|*

*prāpnoti māyāmantraṃ ca tvatpadābjaprasādataḥ \|*  
*śataṃ janmasu cārādhya māyāṃ nārāyaṇīṃ parām \|\| 75 \|\|*

73-75. One achieves the devotion of the lord by taking birth in the sacred land of Bharata. By adoring small gods and getting their blessing one achieves the mantra of Surya.
