After adoring Sdrya-mantra for three births he achieves for the first time the mantra of lord Shiva. After serving at your feet for seven births and adoring you, he achieves the maya-mantra by the grace of your lotus-like feet. Thereafter he adores the Narayani-maya.

## \|\| 6.76 \|\|

*nārāyaṇakalāṃ sevyāṃ samavāpnoti mānavaḥ \|*  
*kalāṃ niṣevya varṣe'tra puṇyakṣetre sudurlabhe \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. In this sacred land of Bharata which is difficult to get, by serving the ray of Narayana, he achieves the devotion of lord Krishna which is achieved only by coming into contact with his devotees.

## \|\| 6.77-79 \|\|

*kṛṣṇabhaktimavāpnoti bhaktasaṃsargahaitukīm \|*  
*saṃprāpya bhaktiṃ niṣpakvāṃ bhrāmaṃbhrāmaṃ ca bhārate \|\| 77 \|\|*

*prāpnoti paripakvāṃ ca bhaktiṃ bhaktaniṣevayā \|*  
*tadā bhaktaprasādena devānāmāśiṣā śiva \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇamantraṃ prāpnoti nirvāṇaphaladaṃ param \|\| 78 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇavrataṃ kṛṣṇamantraṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|*  
*kṛṣṇatulyo bhavedbhaktaściraṃ kṛṣṇaniṣevayā \|\| 79 \|\|*

77-79. Achieving the upright devotion he moves around in the company of the devotees of the lord and gets maturity. О Shiva at that point of time with the grace of the devotees and the gods, he achieves salvation by receiving the Krishna-mantra.

## \|\| 6.80 \|\|

*mahati pralaye pātaḥ sarveṣāṃ vai suniścitam \|*  
*na pātaḥ kṛṣṇabhaktānāṃ sādhūnāmavināśinām \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. The vrata of Krishna as well as his mantra always bestows welfare. After serving the lord for a long time, he becomes like Krishna himself.

## \|\| 6.81 \|\|

*avināśini goloke modante kṛṣṇakiṃkarāḥ \|*  
*hasanti te suniścintā devānbrahmādikāñchiva \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. During the time of dissolution, all the people vanish but those who are devotees of lord Krishna they become eternal and do not face destruction.

## \|\| 6.82 \|\|

*tvaṃ saṃharttā ca sarveṣāṃ na bhaktānāṃ maheśvara \|*  
*māyā mohayate sarvānbhaktānna kṛpayā mama \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. О Shiva in this everlasting Goloka the attendants of lord Krishna always remain blissful and getting reassured they laugh at the gods like Brahma and others.

## \|\| 6.83 \|\|

*māyā nārāyaṇī mātā sarveṣāṃ kṛṣṇabhaktidā \|*  
*na kṛṣṇabhaktiṃ prāpnoti vinā māyāniṣevaṇam \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. О Maheshvara, you destroy all except the devotees of the lord; the illusion can influence everyone but by my grace it does not influence my devotees.

## \|\| 6.84 \|\|

*sā ca nārāyaṇī māyā mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī \|*  
*kṛṣṇapriyā kṛṣṇabhaktā kṛṣṇatulyā'vināśinī \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. The NarayanI illusion happens to be the mother of all and by serving the illusion one cannot achieve the devotion of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 6.85 \|\|

*sā ca tejassvarūpā ca svecchāvigrahadhāriṇī \|*  
*āvirbhūtā ca devānāṃ tejasā'suranigrahe \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. The same illusion of Narayana is given the name of MiilaPrakriti. Ishvari who is the beloved of lord Krishna, is devoted to him and is eternal like him.

## \|\| 6.86 \|\|

*nihatya daityasaṃghāṃśca dakṣapatnyāṃ ca bhārate \|*  
*lalābha dakṣastapasā janma cānekajanmanaḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. She is the form of tejas and takes to human form at will; she was born of the tejas of gods at the time of the war with the demons.

## \|\| 6.87 \|\|

*tyaktvā dehaṃ pituryajñe sā satī tava nindayā \|*  
*jagāma devī golokaṃ kṛṣṇaśaktiḥ sanātanī \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. After killing the demons, she was born in the house of Daksha who had performed great tapas for several births and she appeared as his daughter from his wife.

## \|\| 6.88 \|\|

*gṛhītvā vigrahaṃ tasyā guṇarūpāśraya param \|*  
*bhrāmaṃbhrāmaṃ bhārate tvaṃ viṣaṇṇo'bhūḥ purā hara \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Thereafter the chaste lady listened to the denouncement of her husband in the yajna of her father and ended her life. She then, as the eternal source of lord Krishna, went back to Goloka.

## \|\| 6.89 \|\|

*prabodhito mayā tvaṃ ca śrīśaileṣu sarittaṭe \|*  
*lalābha janma sā śailakāntāyāmacireṇa ca \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. О Shiva, you got upset and carrying the dead body of Sati on your shoulders wandered over the earth.

## \|\| 6.90 \|\|

*karotu puṇyakaṃ sādhvī suvratā suvrataṃ śivā \|*  
*rājasūyasahasrāṇāṃ puṇyaṃ śaṃkara puṇyake \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. Thereafter on the Shrishaila mountain, I made you realise your worth on the bank of the river. Then after a short time the goddess was reborn as the daughter of Himalaya from his wife Mena.

## \|\| 6.91 \|\|

*rājasūyasahasrāṇāṃ vrate yatra dhanavyayaḥ \|*  
*na sādhyaṃ sarvasādhvīnāṃ vratametattrilocana \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Therefore, О Shiva, the chaste lady and noble hearted Parvati should surely perform the Punyaka-vrata because after performing the same, one gets the merit of performing a thousand Rajasuya-yajnas.

## \|\| 6.92 \|\|

*svayaṃ bhūteśanāthastvaṃ puṇyakasya prabhāvataḥ \|*  
*pārvatīgarbhajātaśca varaḥ putro bhaviṣyati \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. О three-eyed Shiva, for performing a vrata if one has to spend wealth equivalent to performing of a thousand Rajasuya-yajnas, the performing of such a vrata will be beyond the reach of all chaste women.

## \|\| 6.93 \|\|

*svayaṃ devagaṇānāṃ sa yasmādīśaḥ kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*gaṇeśa iti vikhyāto bhaviṣyati jagattraye \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. With the influence of the Punyaka-vrata, lord Krishna will be born as your son from the womb of Parvati.

## \|\| 6.94-95 \|\|

*yasya smaraṇamātreṇa vighnanāśo bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*jagatāṃ hetunā'nena vighnanighnābhidho vibhuḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

*nānāvidhāni dravyāṇi yasmāddeyāni puṇyake \|*  
*bhuktvā lambodaratvaṃ ca tena lambodaraḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 95 \|\|*

94-95. О merciful one, since you yourself are the lord of Vaishnavas he will be known by the name of Ganesha in the three worlds, by remembering whom all the obstmctions will disappear. He will, therefore, be called Vighneshvara in the universe.

## \|\| 6.96 \|\|

*śanidṛṣṭyā śiraśchedādgajavaktreṇa yojitaḥ \|*  
*gajānanaḥ śiśustena sarveṣāṃ sarvasiddhidaḥ \|\| 96\|\|*

96\. In this Punyaka-vrata many things will have to be given in charity and because of consuming the same, he will be known as Lambodara.

## \|\| 6.97-98 \|\|

*dantabhaṅgaḥ paraśunā parśurāmasya vai yataḥ \|*  
*hetunā tena vikhyātaścaikadantābhidhaḥ śiśuḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

*pūjyaśca sarvadevānāmasmākaṃ jagatāṃ vibhuḥ \|*  
*sarvāgre pūjanaṃ tasya bhavitā madvareṇa vai \|\| 98 \|\|*

97-98. At the glance of Sani his head will be severed and the head of an elephant will be planted on the trunk and as such he will be known as Gajanana (elephant faced). One of his tusks will be cut-off by Parashurama and as such he will be known as Ekadanta (possessing one tusk only).

## \|\| 6.99-101 \|\|

*pūjāsu sarvadevānāmagre saṃpūjya taṃ janaḥ \|*  
*pūjāphalamavāpnoti nirvighnena vṛthā'nyathā \|\| 99 \|\|*

*gaṇeśaṃ ca dineśaṃ ca viṣṇuṃ śambhuṃ hutāśanam \|*  
*durgāmetānsanniṣevya pūjayeddevatāntaram \|\| 100 \|\|*

*gaṇeśapūjane vighnaṃ nirmūlaṃ jagatāṃ bhavet \|*  
*nirvyādhiḥ sūryyapūjāyāṃ śuciḥ śrīviṣṇupūjane \|\| 101 \|\|*

99-101. He will be adored by all the gods and the universe and because of my blessings he will be adored first of all. At the time of adoring the gods he will be adored first and whosoever does so he will be successful, otherwise he will have to face failure. Therefore after adoring Dinesa, Ganesha, Vishnu, Shiva, Agni and Durga, all other gods should be adored thereafter.

## \|\| 6.102-103 \|\|

*mokṣaśca pāpanāśaśca yaśaścaiśvaryamuttamam \|*  
*tattvajñānaṃ sutṛptīnāṃ bījaṃ śaṃkarapūjanāt \|\| 102 \|\|*

*svabuddhiśuddhijananaṃ kīrttitaṃ vahnipūjanam \|*  
*vidhisaṃskṛtavahnestu pūjāto jñānato mṛtiḥ \|\| 103 \|\|*

102-103. By adoring Ganesha all the obstructions of the universe disappear. By adoring Stirya one is freed from ailment, by adoring Vishnu one gets purified and by adoring Shiva one gets moksa and all his sins vanish. He achieves the best of glory, riches, divine knowledge and the seeds of beautiful tattvas.

## \|\| 6.104 \|\|

*dātā bhoktā ca bhavati śaṃkarāgniniṣevaṇāt \|*  
*haribhaktipradaṃ caiva paraṃ durgārccanaṃ śivam \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. It is treated that by adoring the lord Agni, the mind is purified. By adoring Agni properly, one achieves one's blissful end.

## \|\| 6.105 \|\|

*viparītaṃ trijagatāmeteṣāṃ pūjanaṃ vinā \|*  
*evaṃ kramo mahādeva kalpe kalpe'sti niścitam \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. By adoring Shiva and Agni a person becomes the giver of alms and enjoys pleasures and the blissful adoration of Durga bestows the worship of the lord.

## \|\| 6.106 \|\|

*ete śaśvadvidyamānā nityāḥ sṛṣṭiparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*āvirbhāvatirobhāvau caiteṣāmīśvarecchayā \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. In all the three worlds performing any pUja without the adoration of these gods will be of no consequence. О Mahadeva such a practice had been in vogue in all the kalpas.

## \|\| 6.107 \|\|

*ityuktvā śrīharistatra virarāma sabhātale \|*  
*prahṛṣṭā devatā viprāḥ pārvatyā saha śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. They are the gods of the earth and always remain present on earth. They appear at the command of the lord and thereafter they disappear. Thus speaking in the court, lord Hari kept quiet and listening to the discourse of lord Vishnu, all the gods, Brahmanas, Shiva and Parvati felt happy.

## Chapter 7

## \|\| 7.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*harerājñāṃ samādāya haraḥ saṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*uvāca pārvatīṃ prītyā harisaṃlāpamaṅgalam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – Lord Shiva bowed at the command of the lord and all the welfare ceremony was explained by him to Parvati.

## \|\| 7.2 \|\|

*śivājñāṃ ca samādāya śivā saṃhṛṣṭamānasā \|*  
*vādyaṃ ca vādayāmāsa maṃgalaṃ magalavrate \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Parvati accepting the command of lord Shiva, felt delighted and started the playing of welfare music, starting the vrata for prosperity.

## \|\| 7.3-4 \|\|

*susnātā sudatī śuddhā bibhratī dhautavāsasī \|*  
*saṃsthāpya ratnakalaśaṃ śukladhānyopari sthiram \|\| 3 \|\|*

*āmrapallavasaṃyuktaṃ phalākṣatasuśobhitam \|*  
*candanāgurukastūrīkuṃkumena virājitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. Parvati, having beautiful teeth, took a bath purifying herself, clad herself in beautiful garments and consecrated the pitcher of gems filled with water on the fried paddy which contained mango leaves, fruits, unbroken rice, sandal-paste, aguru, kasturl and vermilion.

## \|\| 7.5-7 \|\|

*ratnāsanasthā ratnāḍhyā ratnodbhavasutā satī \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthāṃśca saṃpūjya munipuṃgavān \|\| 5 \|\|*

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca saṃpūjya supurohitam \|*  
*candanāgurukastūrīratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde puṇyakavrate patidāne pārvatīkṛtaṃ śrīkṛṣṇastotrakathanaṃ nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

5-7. Parvati who was born in Himalaya in a bed of gems and jewels, adorning herself with gems, took her seat on the gem-studded seat; thereafter all the sages were made to sit on the gem-studded lion-thrones and were adored. The priest was also seated on the gem-studded lion-throne and offered sandal-paste, aguru, kastiiri, gem-studded ornaments and was offered adoration. The dikpalas were then consecrated on the gem-studded throne with devotion and all the gods, the humans and the Nagas were gracefully adored.

## \|\| 7.8 \|\|

*samarcya parayā bhaktyā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān \|*  
*candanāgurukastūrīkuṃkumena virājitān \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thereafter with devotion Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva, offered sandal-paste, aguru, kastiiri and vermilion.

## \|\| 7.9-11 \|\|

*vahniśuddhaissuvastraiśca sadratnairbhūṣaṇaistathā \|*  
*pūjādravyaiśca vividhaiḥ pūjitānpuṇyake mune \|\| 9 \|\|*

*samārebhe vrataṃ devī svastivācanapūrvakam \|*  
*āvāhyābhīṣṭadevaṃ taṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ maṃgale ghaṭe \|\| 10 \|\|*

*bhaktyā dadau krameṇaiva copacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*yāni vrate vidheyāni deyāni vividhāni ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

9-11. O sage, thus the purified garments having the lustre of fire, the gem-studded ornaments and several types of articles for worship were offered; thereafter for establishing the vrata, the Svastivacana was recited. Lord Krishna was invoked in the pitcher with the mind filled with devotion. Sixteen types of offering were made, while adoring the god. Whatever articles were required to be given in the adoration, were made available in abundance.

## \|\| 7.12-15 \|\|

*pradadau tāni sarvāṇi pratyekaṃ phaladāni ca \|*  
*vratoktamupahāraṃ ca durlabhaṃ bhuvanatraye \|\| 12 \|\|*

*tacca sarvaṃ dadau bhaktyā suvrate suvratā satī \|*  
*dattvā dravyāṇi sarvāṇi vedamantreṇa sā satī \|\| 13 \|\|*

*homaṃ ca kārayāmāsa trilakṣaṃ tilasarpiṣā \|*  
*brāhmaṇānbhojayāmāsa pūjayitvā'tithīṃstathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*bhojayāmāsa sā devī suvrate suvratā satī \|*  
*pratyahaṃ savidhānaṃ ca cakre sā pūrṇavatsaram \|\| 15 \|\|*

12-15. The chaste Parvati made all the valuable presents available in the three worlds and offered them with devotion to all of them. All the articles were given away in charity; thereafter Parvati at the reciting of the Vedic hymns poured the offerings of sea-same and ghee which counted three lakhs in number. During the performing of the noble vrata, the chaste Parvati offered food to the Brahmanas and other guests after adoring them; thus she continued the vrata for a complete one year.

## \|\| 7.16 \|\|

*samāptidivase viprastamuvāca purohitaḥ \|*  
*suvrate suvrate mahyaṃ dehi tvaṃ patidakṣiṇām \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. At the end of puja, the priest said to her, "O chaste lady, you give me your husband as daks ina as a result of my performing the vrata."

## \|\| 7.17 \|\|

*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā vilapya surasaṃsadi \|*  
*mūrcchāṃ prāpa mahāmāyā māyāmohitacetasā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Listening to these words of the priest, Parvati started wailing; thereafter Parvati who is the great illusion by herself, got infatuated with the illusion and fainted.

## \|\| 7.18 \|\|

*tāṃ ca te mūrcchitāṃ dṛṣṭvā prahasya munipuṃgavāḥ \|*  
*śaṅkaraṃ preṣayāmāsa brahmā viṣṇuśca nārada \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. О Narada, finding her fainted there, the chief of the sages and Vishnu and Shiva smilingly sent Brahma to him.

## \|\| 7.19 \|\|

*saṃprārthitaḥ sabhāsadbhiḥ śivāṃ bodhayituṃ tadā \|*  
*śivaḥ samudyamaṃ cakre pravaktuṃ vadatāṃ varaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. At that point of time all the people started persuading Shiva to bring Parvati back to her senses. Thereafter Shiva the best of the narrators, tried to convince Parvati.

## \|\| 7.20 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*uttiṣṭha bhadre bhadraṃ te bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*sāmprataṃ cetanaṃ kṛtvā madīyaṃ vacanaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Mahadeva said – "О noble one, you get up, you will surely meet with prosperity. You revive your consciousness and listen to my words."

## \|\| 7.21 \|\|

*śivaḥ śivāṃ tāmityuktvā śuṣkakaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukām \|*  
*vakṣasi sthāpayāmāsa kārayāmāsa cetanām \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. After thus speaking Shiva lifted up Parvati whose throat, lips and palate had dried up, embraced her and tried to revive her consciousness.

## \|\| 7.22 \|\|

*hitaṃ satyaṃ mitaṃ sarvaṃ pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|*  
*yaśaskaraṃ ca phaladaṃ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He started speaking beneficial, truthful, short but pleasant and glorious words to Parvati.

## \|\| 7.23 \|\|

*śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yadvedena nirūpitam \|*  
*sarvasammatamiṣṭaṃ ca dharmārthaṃ dharmmasaṃsadi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He said: "O goddess I am speaking to you the words that have been told to me in this connection by Dharma in the assembly, which are acceptable to all and which are according to Dharma; you please listen to them."

## \|\| 7.24 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ karmmaṇāṃ devi sārabhūtā ca dakṣiṇā \|*  
*yaśodā phaladā nityaṃ dharmiṣṭhe dharmakarmaṇi \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. О goddess, daksina is the essence of all the ceremonies and its bestows noble results for all the deeds.

## \|\| 7.25-30 \|\|

*daivaṃ vā paitṛkaṃ vā'pi nityaṃ naimittikaṃ priye \|*  
*yatkarmma dakṣiṇāhīnaṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet \|*  
*dātā ca karmmaṇā tena kālasūtraṃ vrajeddhruvam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*athānte dainyamāpnoti śatruṇā paripīḍitaḥ \|*  
*dakṣiṇā vipramuddiśya tatkālaṃ tu na dīyate \|\| 26\|\|*

*tanmuhūrte vyatīte tu dakṣiṇā dviguṇā bhavet \|*  
*caturguṇā dinātīte pakṣe śataguṇā bhavet \|\| 27 \|\|*

*māse pañcaśataghnā syātṣaṇmāse taccaturguṇā \|*  
*saṃvatsare vyatīte tu karmma tanniṣphalaṃ bhavet \|\| 28 \|\|*

*dātā ca narakaṃ yāti yāvadvarṣasahasrakam \|*  
*putrapautradhanaiśvaryyaṃ kṣayamāpnoti pātakāt \|\| 29 \|\|*

*dharmo naṣṭo bhavettasya dharmmahīne ca karmmaṇi \|\| 30 \|\|*

25-30. О dear one, all the deeds performed by the gods and the manes without the offering of daks ina become infructuous and because of that, the performer is thrown into the kalasUtra hell and thereafter he is tortured by his enemies in miserable conditions. Therefore, in case the Brahmana is not given the daksina, then after the expiry of the muhiirta, the amount of daksina is sure to be doubled. After the expiry of a day, it is escalated to four times and with the expiry of a fortnight it gets multiplied to a hundred times; with the expiry of a fortnight it multiplies to five hundred times. After expiry of six months it further multiplies to four times and after expiry of a year, the whole performance becomes of no consequence and the devotees fall into to the hell wherein he remains for a hundred years and because of that sin, he is deprived of sons, grandsons and wealth. His dharma is destroyed because of his performance without dharmas.

## \|\| 7.31 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*rakṣa svadharmaṃ dharmiṣṭhe dharmajñe dharmakarmaṇi \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ ca bhavedrakṣā svadharmaparipālane \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Vishnu said – О religious lady, in this religious performance you save your dharma because by following one’s own dharma everyone is protected.

## \|\| 7.32 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*yaśca kena nimittena na dharmaṃ parirakṣati \|*  
*dharme naṣṭe ca dharmajñe tasya kartā vinaśyati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Brahma said – О religious-minded lady, the one who does not follow his dharma due to any reason, such a performer gets destroyed together with his dharma.

## \|\| 7.33 \|\|

*dharma uvāca \|*  
*māṃ rakṣa yatnataḥ sādhvi pradāya patidakṣiṇām \|*  
*mayi sthite mahāsādhvi sarvaṃ bhadraṃ bhaviṣyati \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Dharma said – О chaste lady, you protect me by offering your husband in daksina. О great lady everything will be well done when I am there.

## \|\| 7.34 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*dharmaṃ rakṣa mahāsādhvi kuru pūrṇaṃ vrataṃ sati \|*  
*vayaṃ tava vrate pūrṇe kurmastvāṃ pūrṇamānasām \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The gods said – О great religious lady, protect the dharma and complete your vrata. With the successful completion of your vrata we shall all feel successful.

## \|\| 7.35 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*kṛtvā sādhvi pūrṇahomaṃ dehi viprāya dakṣiṇām \|*  
*sthiteṣvasmāsu dharmajñe kimabhadraṃ bhaviṣyati \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The sages said – О chaste lady, after completing the yajha you give away daksina to Brahmana. How can you meet with a misery on the earth when we are there.

## \|\| 7.36 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*śive śivaṃ dehi mahyaṃ na cedvrataphalaṃ tyaja \|*  
*suciraṃ saṃcitasyāpi svātmanastapasaḥ phalam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Sanatkumara said – О Shiva, you entrust lord Shiva to me otherwise be prepared to loose the merits of all the good deeds performed by you since long.

## \|\| 7.37 \|\|

*karmaṇyadakṣiṇe sādhvi yāgasyāhaṃ tu tatphalam \|*  
*prāpsyāmi yajamānasya saṃpūrṇaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О religious lady, in case the yajha remains without daksina, it becomes infructuous and the good result of the performing of the yajha will be available to me and not to you.

## \|\| 7.38 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karmmaṇā me deveśāḥ kiṃ me dakṣiṇayā mune \|*  
*kiṃ putreṇa ca dharmeṇa yatra bharttā ca dakṣiṇā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Parvati said – О lord of the gods, О sage, what do I care for karma or dharma. What shall I do with the son and dharma when my husband is going out of my hands in daksina.

## \|\| 7.39 \|\|

*vṛkṣārcane phalaṃ kiṃ vai yadi bhūmirna cārcyate \|*  
*gate ca kāraṇe kāryyaṃ kutaḥ sasyaṃ kutaḥ phalam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. In case the earth is not worshipped, what is the use of worshipping a tree; when the cause is not there how can you expect the result.

## \|\| 7.40 \|\|

*prāṇāstyaktāḥ svecchayā ceddehaissyātkiṃ prayojanam \|*  
*dṛṣṭiśaktivihīnena cakṣuṣā kiṃ prayojanam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. If the life departs willingly then what is the use of the body. After losing the eye sight, what is the use of the eyes?

## \|\| 7.41 \|\|

*śataputrasamaḥ svāmī sādhvīnāṃ ca sureśvarāḥ \|*  
*yadi bharttā vrate deyaḥ kiṃ vratena sutena vā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. О gods, for a chaste lady, the husband is better than a hundred sons. In case the husband is given away in vrata then what is the use of vrata and the son?

## \|\| 7.42 \|\|

*bharturvaṃśaśca tanayaḥ kevalaṃ bhartṛmūlakaḥ \|*  
*yatra mūlaṃ bhavedbhraṣṭaṃ tadvāṇijyaṃ ca niṣphalam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. A son happens to be a part of the husband who happens to be the cause of the same when the principal money is lost then the entire business is of no consequence.

## \|\| 7.43 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*putrādapi paraḥ svāmī dharmaśca svāminaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*naṣṭe dharme ca dharmiṣṭhe svāminā kiṃ sutena vā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Vishnu said – No doubt the husband is more important then the son but dharma is above husband but when the dharma is lodged what is the use of the husband and the son.

## \|\| 7.44 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*svāminaśca paro dharmo dharmātsatyaṃ ca suvrate \|*  
*satyaṃ saṃkalpitaṃ karma na tu bhraṣṭaṃ kuru vratam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Brahma said – О chaste lady, dharma is above the husband and truthfulness is above dharma, you had started this vrata based on truthfulness and therefore don’t desecrate it.

## \|\| 7.45-46 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*nirūpitaśca vedeṣu svaśabdo dhanavācakaḥ \|*  
*tadyasyāstīti sa svāmī vedajña śṛṇu madvacaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*tasya dātā sadā svāmī na ca svasvāmitāṃ labhet \|*  
*aho'vyavasthā bhavatāṃ vedajñānāmabodhataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Parvati said – О lord of the gods, you listen to my words. The words Sva is used to stand for riches in the Vedas and as such the one who possesses the same becomes the lord; therefore the one who gives away riches in charity is always called a Svdml but the lordship cannot be earned with the riches. Therefore I am afraid of your discussion which is beyond the provisions of the Vedas though you are all well-versed in the Vedic literature. I feel really surprised at the same.

## \|\| 7.47 \|\|

*dharmma uvāca \|*  
*patnī vinā'nyaṃ svaṃ sādhvi svāminaṃ dātumakṣamā \|*  
*dampatī dhruvamekāṅgau dvayordāne dvakau samau \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Dharma said – О chaste lady, a woman is unable to give away her husband in preference to the riches because both husband and wife are considered to be one, therefore both are equal in charity.

## \|\| 7.48 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*pitā dadāti jāmātre sa ca gṛhṇāti tatsutām \|*  
*na śrutaṃ viparītaṃ ca śrutau śrutiparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Parvati said – О sages well-versed in the scriptures, a father gives away in charity to his son-in-law and he accepts his daughter; nothing lias been heard in the Vedas against the same.

## \|\| 7.49-50 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*buddhisvarūpā tvaṃ durge buddhimanto vayaṃ tvayā \|*  
*vedajñe vedavādeṣu ke vā tvāṃ jetumīśvarāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*nirūpitā puṇyake tu vrate svāmī ca dakṣiṇā \|*  
*śrutau śruto yaḥ sa dharmo viparīto hyadharmakaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. The gods said – О Durga, О well-versed in the Vedas, you are the form of intelligence and we are intelligent because of you; therefore who can be competent enough to defeat you in the discussion on the Vedas? Therefore, whatever has been provided in the Vedas is dharma and whatever is against it is adharma.

## \|\| 7.51-52 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*kevalaṃ vedamāśritya kaḥ karoti vinirṇayam \|*  
*balavām̐llaukiko vedāllokācāraṃ ca kastyajet \|\| 51 \|\|*

*vede prakṛtipuṃsośca garīyānpuruṣo dhruvam \|*  
*nibodhata surāḥ prājñā bālāhaṃ kathayāmi kim \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. Parvati said – Who can decide on the basis of the Vedas, because the worldly practices have greater application than the Vedas; therefore who is competent enough to discard them? In the Vedas Purusa has been considered to be superior when compared to Prakriti and Purusa, О learned gods, you listen to what a girl like me has to speak?

## \|\| 7.53 \|\|

*bṛhaspatiruvāca \|*  
*na pumāṃsaṃ vinā sṛṣṭirna sādhvi prakṛtiṃ vinā \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇaśca dvayoḥ sraṣṭā samau prakṛtipūruṣau \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Brihaspati said – О chaste lady, there can be no creation without Purusa or Prakriti. Lord Krishna happens to be the creator of both Purusa and Prakriti which are equal to him.

## \|\| 7.54 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*sarvasraṣṭā ca yaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ soṃ'śena saguṇaḥ pumān \|*  
*pumāngarīyānprakṛtestathaiva na tataśca sā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Parvati said – Lord Krishna who happens to be the creator of all becomes Purusa by one of his rays. Therefore Purusa is considered to be better then Prakriti and similarly Prakriti cannot be better than Purusa.

## \|\| 7.55-56 \|\|

*etasminnantare devā munayastatra saṃsadi \|*  
*ratnendrasāraracitamākāśe dadṛśū ratham \|\| 55 \|\|*

*pārṣadaissaṃparivṛtaṃ yutaṃ śyāmaiścaturbhujaiḥ \|*  
*vanamālāparivṛtai ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaiḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. In the meantime the gods and the sages spotted a gem-studded chariot in the sky in which the dark complexioned lord Vishnu wearing a garland of forest flowers and gem-studded ornaments having four arms was surrounded by courtiers. Narayana descended from that chariot and delightfully reached the court.

## \|\| 7.57-60 \|\|

*avaruhya mudā yānādājagāma sabhātalam \|*  
*tuṣṭuvustaṃ surendrāste devaṃ vaikuṇṭhavāsinam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*śaṃkhacakragadāpadmadharamīśaṃ caturbhujam \|*  
*lakṣmīsarasvatīkāntaṃ śāntaṃ taṃ sumanoharam \|\| 58 \|\|*

*sukhadṛśyamabhaktānāmadṛśyaṃ koṭijanmabhiḥ \|*  
*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyaṃ koṭicandrasamaprabham \|\| 59 \|\|*

*amūlyaratnaracitacārubhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|*  
*sevyaṃ brahmādidevaiśca sevakaiḥ satataṃ stutam \|\| 60 \|\|*

57-60. All the gods started offering prayers to the lord of Vaikuntha who was holding shamkha, cakra and gada in his four hands. He was the lord of all having four arms and the husband of Lakshmi and Sarasvati, peaceful in nature, quite pleasant, pleasant to look at, who could not be seen by the sinners in crores of births, more beautiful than the crores of gods of love, having the lustre of crores of moons adorned with beautiful ornaments, served by Brahma and other gods and for whom the attendants were offering prayers.

## \|\| 7.61-63 \|\|

*tadbhāsā saṃparicchannairveṣṭitaṃ ca surarṣibhiḥ \|*  
*vāsayāmāsa taṃ te ca ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|\| 61 \|\|*

*taṃ praṇemuśca śirasā brahmaśaktiśivādayaḥ \|*  
*sampuṭāñjalayaḥ sarve pulakāṅgāśrulocanāḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

*sasmitastāṃśca papraccha sarvaṃ madhurayā girā \|*  
*prabodhitaḥ subodhajñaḥ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 63 \|\|*

61-63. His lustre pervaded everywhere. Thereafter Brahma, shakti and Shiva made him occupy the gem-studded lion-throne, who bowed before him in reverence at the same time. All were feeling emotional with their eyes wet with tears. Thereafter, the lord speaking sweet words asked about those who were present there. After coming to know of the facts, the lord started speaking.

## \|\| 7.64-65 \|\|

*śrīnārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*saha buddhyā buddhimanto na vaktumucitaṃ surāḥ \|*  
*sarve śaktyā yayā viśve śaktimanto hi jīvinaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

*brahmāditṛṇaparyyantaṃ sarvaṃ prākṛtikaṃ jagat \|*  
*satyaṃ satyaṃ vinā māṃ ca mayā śaktiḥ prakāśitā \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. Narayana said – It is not proper for the intelligent people to enter into a discussion with Parvati, who herself is the form of intelligence because all the people in the universe are alike or possessed prowess by her grace; that is why everything from Brahma to the straw is considered to be perishable; this is the truth and the total truth that I have lighted shakti without Purusa.

## \|\| 7.66 \|\|

*āvirbhūtā ca sā mattaḥ sṛṣṭau devī madicchayā \|*  
*tirohitā ca sā śeṣe sṛṣṭisaṃharaṇe mayi \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. In the universe she has appeared with my desire and from me and after the dissolution of the universe she merges in me.

## \|\| 7.67 \|\|

*prakṛtiḥ sṛṣṭikartrī ca sarveṣāṃ jananī parā \|*  
*mama tulyā ca manmāyā tena nārāyaṇī smṛtā \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Prakriti, because of the creation, is also considered to be the mother of all, my illusion; therefore it is like me and that is why she is called Narayani.

## \|\| 7.68 \|\|

*suciraṃ tapasā taptaṃ śaṃbhunā dhyāyatā ca mām \|*  
*tena tasmai mayā dattā tapasāṃ phalarūpiṇī \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. By adoring me Shiva performs tapas for a long time. As a result of the tapas I entrusted her care to him.

## \|\| 7.69 \|\|

*vrataṃ ca lokaśikṣārthamasyā na svārthameva ca \|*  
*svayaṃ vratānāṃ tapasāṃ phaladātrī jagattraye \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The supunyaka-vrata was properly finished by her and there was no selfish motive in the same because the reward of the vratas of all the three worlds is given by her alone.

## \|\| 7.70 \|\|

*māyayā mohitāḥ sarve kimasyā vāstavaṃ vratam \|*  
*sādhyamasya vrataphalaṃ kalpe kalpe punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. All of you have been influenced by illusion, otherwise, what is the form of her real vratal In each and every kalpa she receives the reward of the same vrata.

## \|\| 7.71 \|\|

*sureśvarā madaṃśāśca brahmaśaktimaheśvarāḥ \|*  
*kalāḥ kalāṃśarūpāśca jīvinaśca surādayaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. О Brahma, the lord of the gods, both sakti and Shiva happen to be my rays; and the gods and other creatures are all small parts of my rays.

## \|\| 7.72-73 \|\|

*mṛdā vinā ghaṭaṃ karttuṃ kulālaśca yathā'kṣamaḥ \|*  
*vinā svarṇaṃ svarṇakāraḥ kuṇḍalaṃ karttumakṣamaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

*vinā śaktyā tathā'haṃ ca svasṛṣṭiṃ kartumakṣamaḥ \|*  
*śaktipradhānā sṛṣṭiśca sarvadarśanasammatā \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. As the potter is unable to make a pot without the clay and the goldsmith is unable to make ornament or kundalas without the gold, similarly without sakti, I am unable to resort to creation. shakti is predominant in the universe; this is the opinion of all the scriptures.

## \|\| 7.74-75 \|\|

*ahamātmā hi nirlipto'dṛśyaḥ sākṣī ca dehinām \|*  
*dehāḥ prākṛtikāḥ sarve naśvarāḥ pāñcabhautikāḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

*ahaṃ nityaḥ śarīrī ca bhānuvigrahavigrahaḥ \|*  
*sarvādhārā sā prakṛtiḥ sarvātmā'haṃ jagatsu ca \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. I am the soul pervading in all the creatures of the universe but am unattached and invisible, all the bodies comprise of nature and lustre of the sun. I am the base of everyone in the universe, I am the soul of all.

## \|\| 7.76-77 \|\|

*ahamātmā mano brahmā jñānarūpo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*pañca prāṇāḥ svayaṃ viṣṇurbuddhiḥ prakṛtirīśvarī \|\| 76 \|\|*

*medhā nidrādayaścaitāḥ sarvāśca prakṛteḥ kalāḥ \|*  
*sā ca śailendrakanyaiṣā tviti vede nirūpitam \|\| 77\|\|*

76-77. I am the soul, Brahma is my mind, Shiva is my intelligence and Vishnu represents the five pranas. The Isvarl, Prakriti is the form of intelligence. Besides sleep and intelligence are the rays of Prakriti. The same Prakriti happens to be the daughter of Himalaya. This has been ordained in the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.78 \|\|

*ahaṃ golokanāthaśca vaikuṇṭheśaḥ sanātanaḥ \|*  
*gopīgopaiḥ parivṛtastatraiva dvibhujaḥ svayam \|*  
*caturbhujo'tra deveśo lakṣmīśaḥ pārṣadairvṛtaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. I am the lord of Goloka and Vaikuntha. I am eternal and getting surrounded by the cowherds and cowherdesses, I appear with two arms. With my four arms, I represent the lord of the gods and Lakshmi, surrounded by my courtiers.

## \|\| 7.79-80 \|\|

*ūrdhva paraśca vaikuṇṭhātpañcāśatkoṭiyojane \|*  
*mamāśrayaśca goloke yatrāhaṃ gopikāpatiḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

*vratārādhyassa dvibhujaḥ sa ca tatphaladāyakaḥ \|*  
*yadrūpaṃ cintayedyo hi tacca tatphaladāyakaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

79-80. My abode is in Goloka which is situated fifty crores of yajnas above Vaikuntha. I conduct myself there as the lord of the cowherdesses, a great god of the vratas with two arms and bestow the reward of the vratas, the one who remembers me in many forms, I bestow a similar reward to him.

## \|\| 7.81 \|\|

*vrataṃ pūrṇaṃ kuru śive śivaṃ dattvā ca dakṣiṇām \|*  
*punaḥ samucitaṃ mūlyaṃ dattvā nāthaṃ grahīṣyasi \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. Therefore, О Shiva, by giving away Shiva in daksina, you complete your vrata and after buying him in return with a suitable price you can get him back.

## \|\| 7.82 \|\|

*viṣṇudehā yathā gāvo viṣṇudehastathā śivaḥ \|*  
*dvijāya dattvā gomūlyaṃ gṛhāṇa svāminaṃ śubhe \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. Because, О beautiful one, as the cows are the body of Vishnu, similarly Shiva happens to the body of Vishnu. Therefore paying a suitable price you can get back your husband.

## \|\| 7.83 \|\|

*yajñapatnīṃ yathā dātuṃ kṣamaḥ svāmī sadaiva tu \|*  
*tathā sā svāminaṃ dātumīśvarīti śrutermatam \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. As the performer is unable to give away daks ina, similarly she is unable to give away to the lord in charity; this has been ordained in the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.84 \|\|

*ityuktvā sa sabhāmadhye tatraivāntaradhīyata \|*  
*hṛṣṭāste sā ca saṃhṛṣṭā dakṣiṇāṃ dātumudyatā \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Thus speaking, lord Vishnu disappeared from the court. All the gods felt delighted at the words of Vishnu; Parvati felt extremely satisfied and got herself ready for giving away daksina.

## \|\| 7.85 \|\|

*kṛtvā śivā pūrṇahomaṃ sā śivaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadau \|*  
*svastītyuktvā ca jagrāha kumāro devasaṃsadi \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. In the court of the gods, Parvati after making the final offerings, gave away Shiva in daksina. and kumara accepted him uttering svasti.

## \|\| 7.86 \|\|

*uvāca durgā saṃtrastā śuṣkakaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukā \|*  
*kṛtvāñjalipuṭā vipraṃ hṛdayena vidūyatā \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. At that point of time her throat, lips and palate dried up and she felt extremely grieved at heart and expressing her grief she said.

## \|\| 7.87 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*gomūlyaṃ matpatisamamiti vede nirūpitam \|*  
*gavāṃ lakṣaṃ prayacchāmi dehi matsvāminaṃ dvija \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. Parvati said – О Brahmana, the price of a cow is equivalent to that of my husband, this has been ordained in the Vedas; therefore I am giving you in return a lakh of cows and you kindly give me back my husband.

## \|\| 7.88 \|\|

*tadā dāsyāmi viprebhyo dānāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*ātmahīno hi dehaśca karmma kiṃ kartumīśvaraḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Thereafter I shall give away various types of riches in charity to the Brahmanas, otherwise a body without a soul is unable to perform.

## \|\| 7.89 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*gavāṃ lakṣeṇa me devi valgunā kiṃ prayojanam \|*  
*dattasyāmūlya ratnasya gavāṃ pratyarpaṇena ca \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Sanatkumara said – О goddess, I am not in need of a lakh cows, the invaluable gem given in charity cannot be returned in the form of cows.

## \|\| 7.90 \|\|

*svasya svasya svayaṃ dātā lokaḥ sarvo jagattraye \|*  
*kartturevepsitaṃ karma bhavetkiṃ vā parecchayā \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. In all the three worlds the people give away their own wealth in charity and the people who do so, do not achieve merit at the instance of others.

## \|\| 7.91 \|\|

*digambaraṃ puraḥ kṛtvā bhramiṣyāmi jagattrayam \|*  
*bālakānāṃ bālikānāṃ samūhasmitakāraṇam \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. I shall roam about in the entire world placing lord Shiva without robes, a head of me. All the children will jeer at him.

## \|\| 7.92 \|\|

*ityuktvā brahmaṇaḥ putro gṛhītvā śaṅkaraṃ mune \|*  
*sannidhau vāsayāmāsa tejasvī devasaṃsadi \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. О sage, the illustrious Sanatkumara the son of Brahma made Shiva to sit beside him after uttering these words.

## \|\| 7.93 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā śivaṃ gṛhyamāṇaṃ kumāreṇa ca pārvatī \|*  
*samudyatā tanuṃ tyaktuṃ śuṣkakaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukā \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. Parvati on the other hand holding Shiva, looking at Kumara resolved to her life. Her throat, lips and palate dried up.

## \|\| 7.94 \|\|

*vicintya manasā sādhvītyevameva duratyayam \|*  
*na dṛṣṭo'bhīṣṭadevaśca na ca prāptaṃ phalaṃ vrate \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. The chaste lady thought in her mind as to how difficult this vrata had been. She could neither meet lord Krishna nor could she achieve the reward for the same.

## \|\| 7.95-97 \|\|

*etasminnantare devāḥ pārvatīsahitāstadā \|*  
*sadyo dadṛśurākāśe tejasāṃ nikaraṃ param \|\| 95 \|\|*

*koṭisūryyaprabhordhvaṃ ca prajvalantaṃ diśo daśa \|*  
*kailāsaśailapurataḥ sarvadevādibhiryutam \|\| 96 \|\|*

*sarvāśrayaṃ gaṇācchannaṃ vistīrṇaṃ maṇḍalākṛtim \|*  
*tacca dṛṣṭvā bhagavatastuṣṭuvuste krameṇa ca \|\| 97 \|\|*

95-97. In the meantime she spotted a ball of lustre in the sky together with the gods. It was emitting the lustre of crores of suns and illumining in all the directions. It had all the gods in it and stood opposite to mount Kailasa. I was the refuge of everyone, infatuated with the ganas, quite vast and circular in shape. Witnessing the form of the lord, the gods started offering prayers.

## \|\| 7.98 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*brahmāṇḍāni ca sarvāṇi yallomavivareṣu ca \|*  
*so'yaṃ te ṣoḍaśāṃśaśca ke vayaṃ yo mahāvirāṭ \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. Vishnu said – The one in whose hair-pits the globes reside, the same Mahavirat is the sixteenth part of your body; how can we calculate about it.

## \|\| 7.99 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*vedopayuktaṃ dṛśyaṃ yatpratyakṣaṃ draṣṭumīśvara \|*  
*stotuṃ tadvarṇitumahaṃ śaktaḥ kiṃ staumi tatparaḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Brahma said – О lord, the visible scene which is described in the Vedas is difficult to be defined and the one who is beyond the same how can prayers be offered to him.

## \|\| 7.100 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*jñānādhiṣṭhātṛdevo'haṃ staumi jñānaparaṃ ca kim \|*  
*sarvānirvacanīyaṃ taṃ tvāṃ ca svecchāmayaṃ vibhum \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. Mahadeva said – I am the lord of intelligence but the one who is beyond intelligence, beyond description, the one who moves at will can hardly be described.

## \|\| 7.101 \|\|

*dharma uvāca \|*  
*adṛśyamavatāreṣu yaddṛśyaṃ sarvajantubhiḥ \|*  
*kiṃ staumi tejorūpaṃ tadbhaktānugrahavigraham \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. Dharma said – The invisible one, who can be seen by everyone after his incarnation on earth, the same form of lustre can hardly be eulogised who takes to definite form for the sake of the devotees.

## \|\| 7.102 \|\|

*devā ucuḥ \|*  
*ke vayaṃ tvatkalāṃśāśca kiṃ vā tvāṃ stotumīśvarāḥ \|*  
*stotuṃ na śaktā vedā yaṃ na ca śaktā sarasvatī \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. The gods said – The one who is beyond the prayer of Vedas and Sarasvati; we can hardly be competent enough to eulogise him.

## \|\| 7.103 \|\|

*munaya ucuḥ \|*  
*vedānpaṭhitvā vidvāṃso vayaṃ kiṃ vedakāraṇam \|*  
*stotumīśā na vāṇī ca tvāṃ vāṅmanasayoḥ param \|\| 103 \|\|*

103\. The sages said – The one who is the cause of the Vedas, is beyond description and even Sarasvati is unable to pray him; how can we eulogise him, simply by reciting of the Vedas.

## \|\| 7.104 \|\|

*sarasvatyuvāca \|*  
*vāgadhiṣṭhātṛdevīṃ māṃ vadante vedavādinaḥ \|*  
*kiñcinna śaktā tvāṃ stotumaho vāṅmanasoḥ param \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. Sarasvati said – Though the people well-versed in the Vedas describe me as the supreme goddess of speech, yet still I am unable to offer any prayer to you, because you are beyond the mind and the speech.

## \|\| 7.105 \|\|

*sāvitryuvāca \|*  
*vedaprasūrahaṃ nātha sṛṣṭvā tvatkalayā purā \|*  
*kiṃ staumi strīsvabhāvena sarvakāraṇakāraṇam \|\| 105 \|\|*

105\. Savitri said – О lord, no doubt I am the creator of the Vedas but I was created by your ray in the earlier times, but having the nature of a female, how can I eulogise you who happens to be the cause of all the causes.

## \|\| 7.106 \|\|

*lakṣmīruvāca \|*  
*tvadaṃśaviṣṇukāntāhaṃ jagatpoṣaṇakāriṇī \|*  
*kiṃ staumi tvatkalāsṛṣṭā jagatāṃ bījakāraṇam \|\| 106 \|\|*

106\. Lakshmi said – I have been born out of your own rays and I am beloved of lord Vishnu. I preserve the entire universe but I was born out of your own rays, therefore to eulogise you is beyond me, because you are the cause of the seed of the universe.

## \|\| 7.107 \|\|

*himālaya uvāca \|*  
*hasanti santo māṃ nātha karmaṇā sthāvaraṃ param \|*  
*stotuṃ samudyataṃ kṣudraḥ kiṃ staumi stotumakṣamaḥ \|\| 107 \|\|*

107\. Himalaya said – О lord, the sages laugh at me because of my being stationary; I am the degraded one. I am ready to eulogise but find myself unable to do so.

## \|\| 7.108-110 \|\|

*krameṇa sarve taṃ stutvā devā viraramurmune \|*  
*devyaśca munayaḥ sarve pārvatī stotumudyatā \|\| 108 \|\|*

*dhautavastrā jaṭābhāraṃ bibhratī suvratā vrate \|*  
*preritā paramātmānaṃ vratārādhyaṃ śivena ca \|\| 109 \|\|*

*jvaladagniśikhārūpā tejomūrtimatī satī \|*  
*tapasāṃ phaladā mātā jagatāṃ sarvakarmaṇām \|\| 110 \|\|*

108-110. О sage, when all the gods and goddesses kept quiet after eulogising, Parvati got up to offer prayers who was clad in the sanctified garments of the vratas. She was holding the locks of hair on the head which were quite sanctified. She was about to offer her prayer to lord Krishna who was considered to be the great lord Shiva himself. She was the form of burning flames and lustre. She was the chaste lady and the one who bestowed the reward of all the good deeds besides being the mother of the universe.

## \|\| 7.111 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*kṛṣṇa jānāsi māṃ bhadraṃ nāhaṃ tvāṃ jñātumīśvarī \|*  
*ke vā jānanti vedajñā vedā vā vedakārakāḥ \|\| 111 \|\|*

111\. Parvati said – О lord Krishna you know me well but I am unable to understand you; even those well-versed in the Vedas are not aware of your true form.

## \|\| 7.112 \|\|

*tvadaṃśāstvāṃ na jānanti kathaṃ jñāsyanti te kalāḥ \|*  
*tvaṃ cāpi tattvaṃ jānāsi kimanye jñātumīśvarāḥ \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. When you are unknown to your own race how can anyone else know about you; you are well aware of the tattvas but can others also know about them?

## \|\| 7.113 \|\|

*sūkṣmātsūkṣmatamo'vyaktaḥ sthūlātsthūlatamo mahān \|*  
*viśvastvaṃ viśvarūpaśca viśvabījaṃ sanātanaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. You are smaller than the smallest, invisible, greater than the greatest, you are the universe and the form of the universe, seed of the universe and eternal.

## \|\| 7.114-119 \|\|

*kāryyaṃ tvaṃ kāraṇaṃ tvaṃ ca kāraṇānāṃ ca kāraṇam \|*  
*tejassvarūpo bhagavānnirākāro nirāśrayaḥ \|\| 114 \|\|*

*nirlipto nirguṇaḥ sākṣī svātmārāmaḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*prakṛtīśo virāḍbījaṃ virāḍrūpastvameva ca \|\| 115 \|\|*

*saguṇastvaṃ prākṛtikaḥ kalayā sṛṣṭihetave \|*  
*prakṛtistvaṃ pumāṃstvaṃ ca vedā'nyo na kvacidbhavet \|\| 116 \|\|*

*jīvastvaṃ sākṣiṇo bhogī svātmanaḥ pratibimbakam \|*  
*karma tvaṃ karmabījaṃ tvaṃ karmaṇāṃ phaladāyakaḥ \|\| 117 \|\|*

*dhyāyanti yoginastejastvadīyamaśarīri yat \|*  
*keciccaturbhujaṃ śāntaṃ lakṣmīkāntaṃ manoharam \|\| 118 \|\|*

*vaiṣṇavāścaiva sākāraṃ kamanīyaṃ manoharam \|*  
*śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ pītāmbaraṃ param \|\| 119 \|\|*

114-119. You are the form of action, the cause of the action, cause of the causes, illustrious, the lord, spotless, without refuge, unattached, invisible, witness, cause of Virat and the form of Virat; you create the universe with the help of Prakriti. You are Prakriti yourself and also the Purusa because there is nothing else beyond you; you are the life, witness of all the actions, reflection of your own soul, you are action as well as the seed of action and the one who provides reward of one’s action, all the yogis meditate upon the lustre of your body; some people meditate upon the four armed Vishnu who is peaceful, the lord of Lakshmi and quite pleasant to look at, the Vais navas conceived him as visible, charming, fine, holding a shamkha, cakra, gada and padma in his four hands and clad in yellow lower garments; they adore such a lord.

## \|\| 7.120-121 \|\|

*dvibhujaṃ kamanīyaṃ ca kiśoraṃ śyāmasundaram \|*  
*śāntaṃ gopāṅganākāntaṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 120 \|\|*

*evaṃ tejasvinaṃ bhaktāḥ sevante santataṃ mudā \|*  
*dhyāyanti yogino yattatkutastejasvinaṃ vinā \|\| 121 \|\|*

120-121. The devotees also adore the lord having two arms, who is quite beautiful, of tender age, having a dark complexion, quite peaceful, the lord of the cowherdesses, adorned with gem-studded ornaments. The yogis on the other hand meditate upon the same lord who is illustrious.

## \|\| 7.122-123 \|\|

*tattejo bibhratāṃ deva devānāṃ tejasā purā \|*  
*āvirbhūtā surāṇāṃ ca vadhāya brahmaṇā stutā \|\| 122 \|\|*

*nityā tejastvarūpā'haṃ dhṛtvā vai vigrahaṃ vibho \|*  
*strīrūpaṃ kamanīyaṃ ca vidhāya samupasthitā \|\| 123 \|\|*

122-123. О god, you hold the same lustre and appeared at the prayer of Brahma in earlier times for the killing of the demons. О beautiful one, I am eternal as well as the form of lustre; I appeared there as a beautiful damsel.

## \|\| 7.124 \|\|

*māyayā tava māyā'haṃ mohayitvā'surānpurā \|*  
*nihatya sarvāñchailendramagamaṃ taṃ himālayam \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. Thereafter the one, who happens to be your illusion influenced the demons with my illusion and then went back to Himalaya.

## \|\| 7.125 \|\|

*tato'haṃ saṃstutā devaistārakākṣeṇa pīḍitaiḥ \|*  
*abhavaṃ dakṣajāyāyāṃ śivastrī bhavajanmani \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. The gods getting terrified from Tarakasura eulogised you; thereafter I became the daughter of Daksha and then became the wife of Shiva.

## \|\| 7.126 \|\|

*tyaktvā dehaṃ dakṣayajñe śivā'haṃ śivanindayā \|*  
*abhavaṃ śailajāyāyāṃ śailādhīśasya karmaṇā \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. I am Shiva therefore hearing the denouncement of Shiva in the yajha of Daksha I ended my life and took birth from the womb of Mena when Himalaya the lord of the mountains became my father.

## \|\| 7.127 \|\|

*anekatapasā prāptaḥ śivaścātrāpi janmani \|*  
*pāṇiṃ jagrāha me yogī prārthito brahmaṇā vibhuḥ \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. In this birth also Shiva in the form of a Brahmana performed many a tapas at the instance of Brahma; he accepted my hand and I became his wife.

## \|\| 7.128 \|\|

*śṛṅgārajaṃ ca tattejo nālabhaṃ devamāyayā \|*  
*staumi tvāmeva teneśa putraduḥkhena duḥkhitā \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. But О lord, getting deprived of his illusion I could not receive his semen. Therefore getting pained at heart I eulogised you.

## \|\| 7.129 \|\|

*vrate bhavadvidhaṃ putraṃ labdhumicchāmi sāmpratam \|*  
*devena vihitā vede sāṃge svasvāmidakṣiṇā \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. In this vrata I intended to achieve a son like you. And the gods took away my husband in the form of daksina.

## \|\| 7.130 \|\|

*śrutvā sarvaṃ kṛpāsindho kṛpāṃ me kartumarhasi \|*  
*ityuktvā pārvatī tatra virarāma ca nārada \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. Therefore, О ocean of mercy, you kindly be merciful towards me hearing the tale of my sufferings." О Narada, after thus speaking Parvati kept quiet.

## \|\| 7.131 \|\|

*bhārate pārvatīstotraṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti susaṃyataḥ \|*  
*satputraṃ labhate nūnaṃ viṣṇutulyaparākramam \|\| 131 \|\|*

131\. The one who listens with devotion to this stotra of Parvati, will beget a son as powerful as Vishnu himself.

## \|\| 7.132 \|\|

*saṃvatsaraṃ haviṣyāśī harimabhyarcya bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*supuṇyakavrataphalaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 132 \|\|*

132\. After consuming the food leftover after the yajha and adoring lord Shiva with devotion, a person surely gets the reward of the Punyaka-vrata. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 7.133-134 \|\|

*viṣṇustotramidaṃ brahmansarvasampattivaddharnam \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ svāmisaubhāgyavarddhanam \|\| 133 \|\|*

*sarvasaundaryyabījaṃ ca yaśorāśivivarddhanam \|*  
*haribhaktipradaṃ tattvajñānabuddhisukhapradam \|\| 134 \|\|*

133-134. О Brahmana, this stotra of Krishna increases all the riches, besides bestowing pleasure and moks a, the form of essence, increases the fortune of the husband, the cause of all the beauties, increases the glory and devotion to Hari, the best of intelligence and pleasures.

## Chapter 8

## \|\| 8.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*pārvatyāḥ stavanaṃ śrutvā śrīkṛṣṇaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ \|*  
*svarūpaṃ darśayāmāsa sarvādṛśyaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – On hearing the prayer of Parvati, Krishna the ocean of mercy appeared before her, who is invisible for all and difficult to get.

## \|\| 8.2-7 \|\|

*stutvā devī vrataparā kṛṣṇasaṃlagnamānasā \|*  
*dadarśa tejasāṃ madhye svarūpaṃ sarvamohanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*sadratnasāraracite hīrakeṇa pariṣkṛte \|*  
*yukte māṇikyamālābhī ratnapūrṇe manorame \|\| 3 \|\|*

*pītāṃśukaṃ vahniśuddhaṃ varaṃ vaṃśakaraṃ param \|*  
*vanamālāgalaṃ śyāmaṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*kiśoravayasaṃ citraveṣaṃ vai candanāṃkitam \|*  
*cārusmitāsyamīḍyaṃ tacchāradenduvinindakam \|\| 5\|\|*

*mālatīmālyasaṃyuktaṃ kekipicchāvacūḍakam \|*  
*gopāṃganāparivṛtaṃ rādhāvakṣasthalojjvalam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyalīlādhāma manoharam \|*  
*atīva hṛṣṭaṃ sarveṣṭaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 7 \|\|*

2-7. Goddess Parvati visualised the form of Krishna in her mind devoted toward him, in the form of the lustre which was quite surprising. He was wearing a garland studded with jewels, a beautiful garland of rubies, the yellow garments purified by fire, the best of all and bestower of progeny; his throat was adorned with the garland of forest flowers, had a dark complexion, was adorned with ornaments, was clad in garments studded with gems, was of tender age, was clad in astonishing costumes decorated with sandal-paste, a beautiful serene smile on the face, putting to shame the lustre of the moon of the winter season, wearing a garland of jasmine flowers, placing the feather of a peacock on the head, surrounded by the cowherdesses, illumining by embracing Radha, lowering the glory of crores of gods of love, quite pleasant to look at, joyful and the one who bestowed the grace on the devotees.

## \|\| 8.8-9 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇeśakhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde gaṇeśotpattivarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*varaṃ dattvā vareśastu yadyanmanasi vāñchitam \|*  
*dattvā'bhīṣṭaṃ surebhyaśca tattejo'ntaradhīyata \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. At the sight of the lord, the beautiful goddess Parvati desired in her mind to have a son like him. She was granted the boon at the same time, Krishna the lord of bestower of boons, was the form of lustre fulfilling the desire of all the gods and than disappeared from the same place.

## \|\| 8.10-12 \|\|

*kumāraṃ bodhayitvā tu devadevyai digambaram \|*  
*dadurnirupamaṃ tatra prahṛṣṭāyai kṛpānvitāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇebhyo dadau durgā ratnāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*suvarṇāni ca bhikṣubhyo bandibhyo viśvananditā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇānbhojayāmāsa devānvai parvatāṃstathā \|*  
*śaṃkaraṃ pūjayāmāsa copahārairanuttamaiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

10-12. The gods then convinced Sanatkumara who was all merciful and returned Shiva and Parvati. Thereafter Durga, the adorable wife of the lord of the universe, distributed gems among the Brahmanas besides gold to the beggars and the bards. She served the presents and adored lord Shiva.

## \|\| 8.13-15 \|\|

*dundubhiṃ vādayāmāsa kārayāmāsa maṃgalam \|*  
*saṃgītaṃ gāpayāmāsa harisambandhi sundaram \|\| 13 \|\|*

*vrataṃ samāpya sā durgā dattvā dānāni sasmitā \|*  
*sarvāṃśca bhojayitvā tu bubhuje svāminā saha \|\| 14 \|\|*

*tāmbūlaṃ ca varaṃ ramyaṃ karpūrādisuvāsitam \|*  
*kramātpradāya sarvebhyo bubhuje tena kautukāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

13-15. The drums were beaten; the welfare songs were sung and arrangements for the devotional music were made in honour of the lord. Thus completing the vrata and giving away valuables in charity, Durga served food to all with a serene smile on her face. Thereafter, she also took food herself together with Shiva. She also served the fragrant betels with camphor to all and also herself took the same.

## \|\| 8.16 \|\|

*payaḥphenanibhāṃ śayyāṃ ramyāṃ sadratnamañcake \|*  
*puṣpacandanasaṃyuktāṃ kastūrīkuṃkumānvitām \|*  
*rahasi svāminā sārddhaṃ suṣvāpa parameśvarī \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Thereafter, the supreme goddess reclined on the beautiful bed made of gems with a bed-sheet of pure white colour, quite charming, filled with flowers and sandal-paste, kastiln and kuthkuma, she retired to bed with Shiva.

## \|\| 8.17-18 \|\|

*kailāsasyaikadeśe ca ramye candanakānane \|*  
*sugandhikusumāḍhyena vāyunā surabhīkṛte \|\| 17 \|\|*

*bhramaradhvanisaṃyukte puṃskokilarutāśraye \|*  
*vyahārṣītsā surasikā tatra tena sahāmbikā \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. At that point of time in a part of Kailasa in the beautiful forest of sandal-wood, filled with fragrant flowers and a fragrant breeze, in which the hissing sound of black-wasps was being echoed and was the only place for the cuckoo to issue its sweet notes. Ambika went to the forest with Shiva.

## \|\| 8.19 \|\|

*retaḥpatanakāle ca sa viṣṇurviṣṇumāyayā \|*  
*vidhāya viprarūpaṃ tadājagāma ratergṛham \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. But at the time of the falling of the semen he was influenced by Vishnu who reached there in the form of a Brahmana at the gate of the pleasure house.

## \|\| 8.20 \|\|

*jaṭāvantaṃ vinā tailaṃ kucailaṃ bhikṣukaṃ mune \|*  
*atīvaśukladaśanaṃ tṛṣṇayā paripīḍitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. О sage, the beggar had the form of a Brahmana who had dishevelled hair on the head with tom clothes, white teeth and was upset with thirst.

## \|\| 8.21-22 \|\|

*atīva kṛśagātraṃ ca bibhrattilakamujjvalam \|*  
*bahukākusvaraṃ dīnaṃ dainyātkutsitamūrtimat \|\| 21 \|\|*

*ājuhāva mahādevamativṛddho'nnayācakaḥ \|*  
*daṇḍāvalambanaṃ kṛtvā ratidvāre'tidurbalaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. The lean and thin fellow had applied the shining tilaka on the head and spoke in a grief-stricken tone. He appeared in quite a miserable condition. He was desirous of food and was quite weak and old and was moving with the help of a staff. He accordingly reached the door of pleasure house and he called for lord Mahadeva.

## \|\| 8.23 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karoṣi mahādeva rakṣa māṃ śaraṇāgatam \|*  
*saptarātrivrate'tīte pāraṇākāṃkṣiṇaṃ kṣudhā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Brahmana said – О Mahadeva, what are you doing? You protect a person like me who has come to take refuge under you. I am hungry after performing vrata for seven nights and feel quite disturbed. I went to have food.

## \|\| 8.24 \|\|

*kiṃ karoṣi mahādeva he tāta karuṇānidhe \|*  
*paśya vṛddhaṃ jarāgrastaṃ tṛṣayā paripīḍitam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O Mahadeva, О lord, О ocean of mercy, what are you doing? I am quite old and thirsty because of being quite weak. You better look an old person like me.

## \|\| 8.25 \|\|

*mātaruttiṣṭha me'nnaṃ tvaṃ prayacchādya śivaṃ jalam \|*  
*anantaratnodbhavaje rakṣa māṃ śaraṇāgatam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. О mother, get up and give me the water of welfare besides food. О daughter of Himalaya, the abode of immense jewels, I have come to take refuge under you. You better protect me.

## \|\| 8.26 \|\|

*mātarmātarjaganmātarehi nāhaṃ sthito bahiḥ \|*  
*sīdāmi tṛṣayā kasmātsthitāyāmātmamātari \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О mother of the universe, come here, I am not out of the universe, I am suffering from lack of food and water inspite of my mother remaining there."

## \|\| 8.27 \|\|

*iti kākusvaraṃ śrutvā śivasyottiṣṭhato mune \|*  
*papāta vīryyaṃ śayyāyāṃ na yonau prakṛtestadā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. О sage thus after hearing the grief-stricken words, Shiva got up and his semen fell on the bed instead of in the womb of Durga.

## \|\| 8.28 \|\|

*uttasthau pārvatī trastā sūkṣmavastraṃ pidhāya ca \|*  
*ājagāma bahirdvāraṃ pārvatyā saha śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Thereafter the terrified Parvati also clad herself in fine garments and accompanied Shiva up to the door.

## \|\| 8.29-31 \|\|

*dadarśa brāhmaṇaṃ dīnaṃ jarayā paripīḍitam \|*  
*vṛddhaṃ lulitagātraṃ ca bibhrataṃ daṇḍamānatam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*tapasvinamaśāntaṃ ca śuṣkakaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukam \|*  
*kurvantaṃ parayā bhaktyā praṇāmaṃ stavanaṃ tayoḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*śrutvā tadvacanaṃ tatra nīlakaṇṭhaḥ sudhopamam \|*  
*uvāca parayā prītyā prasannastaṃ prahasya ca \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. Shiva looked at the Brahmana who was quite poor, old and disturbed. His body was trembling. He was an ascetic disturbed and could walk bending his back with the help of the staff. His throat, neck and tongue were dried up and inspite of that he was bowing in reverence with great devotion offering prayers. The blue throated Shiva, listening to his nectarlike words, spoke to him smilingly.

## \|\| 8.32 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*gṛhaṃ te kutra viprarṣe vada vedavidāṃ vara \|*  
*kinnāma bhavataḥ kṣipraṃ jñātumicchāmi sāmpratam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Shiva said – О Brahmana, О best of those well-versed in the Vedas, where do you live? What is your name? You tell me.

## \|\| 8.33 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*āgato'si kuto vipra mama bhāgyādupasthitaḥ \|*  
*adya me saphalaṃ janma brāhmaṇo madgṛhe'tithiḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Parvati said – О Brahmana, you have arrived here because of my fortune. Where from have you arrived, my life has been successful today, a Brahmana has arrived here as a guest.

## \|\| 8.34 \|\|

*atithiḥ pūjito yena trijagattena pūjitam \|*  
*tatraivādhiṣṭhitā devā brāhmaṇā guravo dvija \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О Brahmana, the one who adores the guest he himself adores in the three worlds, the gods, Brahmanas and the teachers at the same place.

## \|\| 8.35 \|\|

*tīrthānyatithipādeṣu śaśvattiṣṭhanti niścitam \|*  
*tatpādadhautatoyena miśritāni labhedgṛhī \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The sacred places reside in the feet of the guest, a householder by washing his feet achieves the merit of receiving the water of holy places.

## \|\| 8.36 \|\|

*sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ \|*  
*atithiḥ pūjito yena svātmaśaktyā yathocitam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Therefore, the one who adores the guest, according to his own competence, gets the merit of bathing in all the holy places and receives the diksha of all the yajnas.

## \|\| 8.37 \|\|

*mahādānāni sarvāṇi kṛtāni tena bhūtale \|*  
*atithiḥ pūjito yena bhārate bhaktipūrvakam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. He is taken to have performed all the great charities on earth. The one who adores the guest with devotion,

## \|\| 8.38 \|\|

*nānāprakārapuṇyāni vedoktāni ca yāni vai \|*  
*anye cātithisevāyāḥ kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. All the merits described in the Vedas do not compare even one sixteenth part of the merit of adoring the guest.

## \|\| 8.39-40 \|\|

*apūjito'tithiryasya bhavanādvinivarttate \|*  
*pitṛdevāgnayaḥ paścādguravo yāntyapūjitāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*yāni kāni ca pāpāni brahmahatyādikāni ca \|*  
*tāni sarvāṇi labhate nābhyarcyātithimīpsitam \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Therefore if a guest goes away from a house without adoration, his manes, gods, Agni and even teacher meet with disrespect and leave that place. He, who does not adore the desired guest, attracts the sin of Brahmahatya.

## \|\| 8.41-42 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*jānāsi vedānvedajñe vedoktaṃ kuru pūjanam \|*  
*kṣuttṛḍbhyāṃ pīḍito mātarvacanaṃ ca śrutau śrutam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*vyādhiyukto nirāhāro yadā vā'naśanavratī \|*  
*manorathenopahāraṃ bhoktumicchati mānavaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. Brahmana said – О well-versed in the Vedas, you are well aware of the Vedic practices; therefore you perform the puja as prescribed in the Vedas. О mother, I am suffering because of hunger and thirst. I have heard in the Vedas that the one who suffers from ailment, is hungry or resorts to fasting, decides to have things of his own liking.

## \|\| 8.43 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*bhoktumicchasi kiṃ vipra trailokye ca sudurlabham \|*  
*dāsyāmi bhoktuṃ tvāmadya majjanma saphalaṃ kuru \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Parvati said – О Brahmana, what would you like to eat? I will serve you anything which is difficult to get in the three worlds. I can serve you the same food. Let my life meet with success.

## \|\| 8.44 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*vrate suvratayā sarvamupahāraṃ samāhṛtam \|*  
*nānāvidhaṃ miṣṭamiṣṭaṃ bhoktuṃ śrutvā samāgataḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Brahmana said – О chaste lady, I have heard that for performing the vrata you have collected all types of the best of food. I have therefore arrived here to consume various types of sweets.

## \|\| 8.45 \|\|

*suvrate tava putro'hamagre māṃ pūjayiṣyasi \|*  
*dattvā miṣṭāni vastūni trailokye durlabhāni ca \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. О chaste lady, I am like your son, you adore me after giving away the sweets which are difficult to get in the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.46 \|\|

*tātāḥ pañcavidhāḥ proktā mātaro vividhāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*putraḥ pañcavidhaḥ sādhvi kathito vedavādibhiḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О ascetic lady, there are five types of fathers described in the scriptures and the number of mothers is innumerable. The sons however, are of five types. This has been ordained in the Vedas

## \|\| 8.47 \|\|

*vidyādātā'nnadātā ca bhayatrātā ca janmadaḥ \|*  
*kanyādātā ca vedoktā narāṇāṃ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The one who imparts knowledge, the giver of food, the one who protects others from danger, the one who gives birth and the one who gives away the daughter are the five types of fathers described in the Vedas.

## \|\| 8.48 \|\|

*gurupatnī garbhadhātrī stanadātrī pituḥ svasā \|*  
*svasā mātuḥ sapatnī ca putrabhāryyā'nnadāyikā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The list of mothers includes the wife of the teacher, the one who carries a child in the womb, the one who feeds with breast milk her child, the sister of the father, the sister of the mother, the step-mother, the wife of the son and the one who gives away food are called to be the mothers in the universe.

## \|\| 8.49-50 \|\|

*bhṛtyaḥ śiṣyaśca poṣyaśca vīryyajaḥ śaraṇāgataḥ \|*  
*dharmaputrāśca catvāro vīryyajo dhanabhāgiti \|\| 49 \|\|*

*kṣuttṛḍbhyāṃ pīḍito mātarvṛddho'haṃ śaraṇāgataḥ \|*  
*sāmprataṃ tava vandhyāyā anāthaḥ putra eva ca \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. There are five types of sons described in the scriptures which include the servant, the pupil, one who is brought up or adopted and the one who is born of one’s own semen and the one who arrives to take refuge. Out of these, four are as called Dharma-putras and the one who is bom of one’s own semen, he inherits the property and riches. О mother, I am suffering from hunger and thirst, am quite old and have come to take refuge with you. Currently, I am an orphan son of a barren lady like you.

## \|\| 8.51-55 \|\|

*piṣṭakaṃ paramānnaṃ ca supakvāni phalāni ca \|*  
*nānāvidhāni piṣṭāni kāladeśodbhavāni ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

*pakvānnaṃ svastikaṃ kṣīramikṣumikṣuvikārajam \|*  
*ghṛtaṃ dadhi ca śālyannaṃ ghṛtapakvaṃ ca vyañjanam \|\| 52 \|\|*

*laḍḍukāni tilānāṃ ca miṣṭānnaiḥ saguḍāni ca \|*  
*mamājñātāni vastūni sudhayā tulyakāni ca \|\| 53 \|\|*

*tāmbūlaṃ ca varaṃ ramyaṃ karpūrādisuvāsitam \|*  
*calaṃ sunirmalaṃ svādu dravyāṇyetāni vāsitam \|\| 54 \|\|*

*dravyāṇi yāni bhuktvā me cāru lambodaraṃ bhavet \|*  
*anantaratnodbhavaje tāni mahyaṃ pradāsyasi \|\| 55 \|\|*

51-55. You give me the fried round flour cakes, payasam, ripe frnit, many stuffs made of flour, stuffs made according to the time and the place, ripe cereals, svastika, milk, juice of sugar-cane and the stuffs made of it, besides ghee, curd boiled rice, the stuffs made or fried in ghee, the balls of sea-same, sweets of guda and all other stuffs which are unknown to me besides the sweets which are delicious like nectar and fragrant like camphor, in addition to the best of betels and spotless, clean and tasteful water. О daughter of the mountain, you make all these things available to me by eating which I should become a person with protmding belly.

## \|\| 8.56 \|\|

*svāmī te trijagatkarttā pradātā sarvasampadām \|*  
*mahālakṣmī svarūpā tvaṃ sarvaiśvaryyapradāyinī \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Your husband happens to be the lord of the three worlds and the creator of the three worlds, bestower of all the riches and you are yourself Maha-Lakshmi who can bestow all the fortunes.

## \|\| 8.57-58 \|\|

*ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ ramyamamūlyaṃ ratnabhūṣaṇam \|*  
*vahniśuddhāṃśukaṃ cāru pradāsyasi sudurlabham \|\| 57 \|\|*

*sudurlabhaṃ harermantraṃ harau bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ sati \|*  
*haripriyā hareḥ śaktistvameva sarvadā sthitā \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. You bestow on me the firm devotion of the lord Hari together with a beautiful gem-studded lion-throne, ornaments studded with gems and invaluable garments purified by fire, the mantra of the god which is difficult to get, because you are the beloved of the lord and always remain there as his shakti.

## \|\| 8.59-60 \|\|

*jñānaṃ mṛtyuñjayaṃ nāma dātṛśaktiṃ sukhapradām \|*  
*sarvasiddhiṃ ca kiṃ mātaradeyaṃ svasutāya ca \|\| 59 \|\|*

*manaḥ sunirmalaṃ kṛtvā dharme tapasi santatam \|*  
*śreṣṭhe savaṃ kariṣyāmi na kāme janma hetuke \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. You also give me the knowledge of overcoming death because you are the one who provides pleasure and all the siddhis. Q mother, you can give everything to your son. О best of the ladies, I shall devote myself to dharma and tapas purifying my mind but I shall never enslave myself with worldly desires.

## \|\| 8.61 \|\|

*svakāmātkurute karma karmaṇo bhoga eva ca \|*  
*bhogau śubhāśubhau jñeyau tau hetū sukhaduḥkhayoḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. One performs one’s deeds according to his desire and also has to face the result of the same. One has to face the reward of his deeds which are good as well as bad and result in pleasure and pain.

## \|\| 8.62 \|\|

*duḥkhaṃ na kasmādbhavati sukhaṃ vā jagadambike \|*  
*sarvaṃ svakarmaṇo bhogastena tadvirato budhaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. О goddess, one neither gets pain from anyone nor pleasure. Whatever one has to face is the result of one’s own deeds, therefore the learned people always remain unconcerned with the desire.

## \|\| 8.63 \|\|

*karma nirmūlayantyeva santo hi satataṃ mudā \|*  
*haribhāvanabuddhyā tattapasā bhaktasaṅgataḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. One who adores the lord with his utmost intelligence and tapas, gets pleased after coming into contact with the devotees of the lord which ultimately results in removing all the karmas.

## \|\| 8.64 \|\|

*indriyadravyasaṃyogasukhaṃ vidhvaṃsanāvadhi \|*  
*harisaṃlāparūpaṃ ca sukhaṃ tatsarvakālikam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Because the pleasure derived out of the combination of the pleasure of the organs of senses is short lived and the pleasure of the reciting of the name of the lord always remains present at all the times.

## \|\| 8.65 \|\|

*harismaraṇaśīlānāṃ nāyuryāti satāṃ satī \|*  
*na teṣāmīśvaraḥ kālo na ca mṛtyuñjayo dhruvam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. О chaste lady, the life of those who recite the name of the lord never gets wasted, the time cannot overpower them nor can lord Shiva do so.

## \|\| 8.66 \|\|

*ciraṃ jīvanti te bhaktā bhārate cirajīvinaḥ \|*  
*sarvasiddhiṃ ca vijñāya svacchandaṃ sarvagāminaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. In the land of Bharata, the devotees of the lord always have a long life and they achieved all the success and move about in all the places independently.

## \|\| 8.67 \|\|

*jātismarā harerbhaktā jānate koṭijanmanaḥ \|*  
*kathayanti kathāṃ janma labhante svecchayā mudā \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The devotees of the lord preserve the memory in earlier births; therefore they are well aware of the happening of crores of births who continue narrating these stories and they are reborn pleasantly according to their own wishes.

## \|\| 8.68 \|\|

*paraṃ punanti te pūtāstīrthāni svīyalīlayā \|*  
*puṇyakṣetre'tra sevāyai parārthaṃ ca bhramanti te \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. They are always pure at heart and by their performance they purify even the sacred places where they go on roaming about to serve others.

## \|\| 8.69-70 \|\|

*vaiṣṇavānāṃ padasparśātsadyaḥ pūtā vasundharā \|*  
*kālaṃ godohamātraṃ tu tīrthe yatra vasanti te \|\| 69 \|\|*

*gurorāsyādviṣṇumantraḥ śrutau yasya praviśyati \|*  
*taṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tīrthapūtaṃ pravadanti purāvidaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

69-70. In a sacred place where the Vaishnavas stay up to the evening, the land gets purified with the very touch of their feet because a person in whose ear the mantra of lord Vishnu enters from the mouth of the teacher, the people well-versed in the ancient scriptures consider him to be as sacred as a holy place.

## \|\| 8.71-72 \|\|

*puruṣāṇāṃ śataṃ pūrvamuddharanti śataṃ param \|*  
*līlayā bhārate bhaktyā sodarānmātaraṃ tathā \|\| 71 \|\|*

*mātāmahānāṃ puruṣāndaśa pūrvāndaśāparān \|*  
*mātuḥ prasūmuddharanti dāruṇādyamatāḍanāt \|\| 72 \|\|*

71-72. The devotees in the land of Bharata redeemed a hundred generations of their ancestors and a hundred coming generations without much effort. Similarly the brothers, the mother, maternal grandfather and ancestors, of ten generations are redeemed together with the ten generations of the maternal grand mother.

## \|\| 8.73-74 \|\|

*bhaktadarśanamāśleṣaṃ mānavāḥ prāpnuvanti ye \|*  
*te yātāḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitāḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

*na liptāḥ pātakairbhaktāḥ santataṃ harimānasāḥ \|*  
*yathā'gnayaḥ sarvabhakṣyā yathā dravyeṣu vāyavaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

73-74. Those of the people who had an audience with the devotees or embraced them, earn the merit of all the holy places and receive dlksa of all the yajnas. The devotees who always remain engrossed in the devotion of the lord and never get involved in any of the sins which are like fire which consumes everything but the wind remain unaffected by it.

## \|\| 8.75-76 \|\|

*trikoṭijanmanāmante prāpnoti janma mānavam \|*  
*prāpnoti bhaktasaṃgaṃ sa mānuṣe koṭijanmataḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

*bhaktasaṅgādbhavedbhakteraṅkuro jīvitaḥ satī \|*  
*abhaktadarśanādeva sa ca prāpnoti śuṣkatām \|\| 76 \|\|*

75-76. One gets the human birth after three crores of births and after having crores of births, a human being comes across the company of the devotees. О chaste lady, by coming across the devotees, the seed of devotion is sprouted which dries up at the sight of unbelievers.

## \|\| 8.77 \|\|

*punaḥ praphullatāṃ yāti vaiṣṇavālāpamātrataḥ \|*  
*aṃkuraścāvināśī ca varddhate pratijanmani \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. But it blossoms again after conversation with Vaishnavas because only such of the sprouting remains intact and grows in every birth.

## \|\| 8.78-79 \|\|

*tattarovarddhamānasya haridāsyaṃ phalaṃ sati \|*  
*pariṇāme bhaktipāke pārṣadaśca bhaveddhareḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

*mahati pralaye nāśo na bhavettasya niścitam \|*  
*sarvasṛṣṭeśca saṃhāre brahmalokasya vedhasaḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

78-79. О chaste lady, when this tree grows up a person achieves the slavehood of the lord and thereafter when the devotion is placed on firm footings, he becomes the courtier of the lord. Then such a person survives at the time of great dissolution when even Brahma and the entire globe vanishes. This is definite.

## \|\| 8.80 \|\|

*tasmānnārāyaṇe bhaktiṃ dehi māmambike sadā \|*  
*na bhavedviṣṇubhaktiśca viṣṇumāye tvayā vinā \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. О mother, therefore you bestow on earth always the devotion of the lord. О illusion of Vis nu, without your grace one cannot achieve the devotion of the lord.

## \|\| 8.81 \|\|

*tadvantaṃ lokaśikṣārthaṃ svatapastava pūjanam \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ phaladātrī tvaṃ nityarūpā sanātanī \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. People adore you or perform your tapas, vrata or puja for the sake of education, because you are the one who bestows the reward on all the people. Besides, you are everlasting and eternal.

## \|\| 8.82-84 \|\|

*gaṇeśarūpaḥ śrīkṛṣṇaḥ kalpe kalpe tavātmajaḥ \|*  
*tvatkroḍamāgataḥ kṣipramityuktvā'ntaradhīyata \|\| 82 \|\|*

*kṛtvā'ntardhānamīśaśca bālarūpaṃ vidhāya saḥ \|*  
*jagāma pārvatī talpaṃ mandirābhyantarasthitam \|\| 83 \|\|*

*talpasthe śivavīryye ca miśritaḥ sa babhūva ha \|*  
*dadarśa gehaśikharaṃ prasūte bālake yathā \|\| 84 \|\|*

82-84. In every kalpa lord Krishna happens to be your son in the form of Ganesha and he is shortly coming in your lap. Thus speaking the Brahmana disappeared from the scene. After disappearing from the scene the lord changed himself into the form of a child and he entered the bed of Parvati and was soaked in the semen of Shiva and he started looking like a just born infant and started looking at the peak of the palace.

## \|\| 8.85-89 \|\|

*śuddhacampakavarṇābhaḥ koṭicandrasamaprabhaḥ \|*  
*sukhadṛśyaḥ sarvajanaiścakṣūraśmivivarddhakaḥ \|\| 85 \|\|*

*atīva sundaratanuḥ kāmadevavimohanaḥ \|*  
*sukhaṃ nirupamaṃ bibhracchāradenduvinindakam \|\| 86 \|\|*

*sundare locane bibhraccārupadmavinindake \|*  
*oṣṭhādharapuṭaṃ bibhratpakvabimbavinindakam \|\| 87 \|\|*

*kapālaṃ ca kapolaṃ ca paramaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*nāsāgraṃ ruciraṃ bibhradvīndracañcuvinindakam \|\| 88 \|\|*

*trailokye vai nirupamaṃ sarvāṃgaṃ bibhraduttamam \|*  
*śayānaḥ śayane ramye prerayanhastapādakam \|\| 89 \|\|*

85-89. He had the complexion of pure jasmine flower, the lustre of crores of moons, pleasant to look at by all, the one who increases the eyesight, having extremely beautiful body which could put even the god of love in fallacy and had the face of the shining moon of the winter season. Both his eyes were beautiful and could put the lotus to shame. His beautiful lips could put the ripe wood-apple to shame; he had quite charming cheeks and his head and his nose could put the beak of the parrot to shame. Thus he was having beautiful limbs and was moving his hands and feet on the bed.

## Chapter 9

## \|\| 9.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*harau tirohite śarvāṇī durgā śaṃkarastadā \|*  
*brāhmaṇānveṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā babhrāma parito mune \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – О sage, after the disappearance of the lord from the scene, both Durga and Shiva went in search of the Brahmana in all directions.

## \|\| 9.2-4 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*aye viprendrātivṛddha kva gato'si kṣudhā''turaḥ \|*  
*he tāta darśanaṃ dehi prāṇānvai rakṣa me vibho \|\| 2 \|\|*

*śiva śīghraṃ samuttiṣṭha brāhmaṇānveṣaṇaṃ kuru \|*  
*kṣaṇamunmanasoreṣa gataḥ pratyakṣamāvayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*agṛhītvā gṛhātpūjāṃ gṛhiṇo'tithirīśvara \|*  
*yadi yāti kṣudhā''rttaśca tasya kiṃ jīvanaṃ vṛthā \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. Parvati said – О extremely old Brahmana, you were quite hungry; where have you gone? О father, О illustrious one, you appear before me and save my life. О Shiva, get up at once and search for the Brahmana. He appeared before us for a moment only. О lord, the householder from whose house a hungry and thirsty guest goes away without adoration, the life of the same house-holder becomes of no consequence.

## \|\| 9.5 \|\|

*pitarastanna gṛhṇanti piṇḍadānaṃ ca tarpaṇam \|*  
*tasyāhutiṃ na gṛhṇāti vahniḥ puṣpaṃ jalaṃ surāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Because the manes do not accept the offering the pindas and offerings made in fire from his hands. The gods also do not accept the flower and water offered from his hand.

## \|\| 9.6-8 \|\|

*havyaṃ puṣpaṃ jalaṃ dravyamaśuceśca surāsamam \|*  
*amedhyasadṛśaḥ piṇḍaḥ sparśanaṃ puṇyanāśanam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*etasminnantare tatra vāg babhūvāśarīriṇī \|*  
*kaivalyayuktā sā durgā tāṃ śuśrāva śucā''turā \|\| 7 \|\|*

*śāntā bhava jaganmātassvasutaṃ paśya mandire \|*  
*kṛṣṇaṃ golokanāthaṃ taṃ paripūrṇatamaṃ param \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. Не performing of yajna offering of flowers, water and other articles become impure beside the pindas. The merits vanish with the touch of such a person. In the meantime a divine voice was heard from the sky when Durga was feeling immensely grieved. It said, "O mother of the universe, be peaceful." You go to your son who is the lord of Goloka and is the form of lord Krishna completely and who happens to be the best of the humans.

## \|\| 9.9 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇa tṛtīye gaṇapati khaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde bālagaṇeśadarśanaṃ nāma navamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. He happens to be the result of the Supunyaka-vrata performed by you and whom the great yogis meditate upon always with a pleasant mind.

## \|\| 9.10 \|\|

*dhyāyanti vaiṣṇavā devā brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|*  
*yasya pūjyasya sarvāgre kalpe kalpe ca pūjanam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. He is the one who is adored first by the gods like Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva and Vaishnava gods in every kalpa.

## \|\| 9.11-12 \|\|

*yasya smaraṇamātreṇa sarvavighno vinaśyati \|*  
*puṇyarāśisvarūpaṃ ca svasutaṃ paśya mandire \|\| 11 \|\|*

*kalpe kalpe dhyāyasi yaṃ jyotīrūpaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*paśya tvaṃ muktidaṃ putraṃ bhaktānugrahavigraham \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. With the mere reciting of whose names all the obstructions disappear. You go to your abode and find your virtuous son, who has taken to the human form, showing his grace on the devotees.

## \|\| 9.13 \|\|

*tava vāñchāpūrṇabījaṃ tapaḥkalpataroḥ phalam \|*  
*sundaraṃ svasutaṃ paśya koṭikandarpanindakam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. He is the seed for the fulfilling of your desire and is the fruit of the kalpavrksa as a result of your tapas. You look at the beautiful son who surpasses the beauty of the crores of the gods of love.

## \|\| 9.14 \|\|

*nāyaṃ vipraḥ kṣudhā''rttaśca viprarūpī janārdanaḥ \|*  
*kiṃ vā vilāpaṃ kuruṣe kva vā vṛddhaḥ kva cātithiḥ \|*  
*sarasvatī tvevamuktvā virarāma ca nārada \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. He was not a hungry or a thirsty Brahmana but was lord Janardana turned as a Brahmana. Therefore why are you lamenting? Where is the old man and where is the guest?" О Narada, thus speaking the eternal voice was over.

## \|\| 9.15-16 \|\|

*trastā śrutvā''kāśavāṇīṃ jagāma svālayaṃ satī \|*  
*dadarśa bālaṃ paryyaṅke śayānaṃ sasmitaṃ mudā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*paśyantaṃ gehaśikharaṃ śatacandrasamaprabham \|*  
*svaprabhāpaṭalenaiva dyotayantaṃ mahītalam \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. The terrified goddess Durga listening to the divine voice from the sky rushed to her house and saw an infant placed on her bed, wearing a smile on his face. He was looking at the vase at the top of the house and was having the lustre of hundreds of moons. He was illuminating the earth with his lustre.

## \|\| 9.17 \|\|

*kurvantaṃ bhramaṇaṃ talpe paśyantaṃ svecchayā mudā \|*  
*umeti śabdaṃ kurvantaṃ rudantaṃ taṃ stanārthinam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He was rolling over the bed delightfully and was looking everywhere at will. He then started crying for sucking milk.

## \|\| 9.18 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tadadbhutaṃ rūpaṃ trastā śaṃkarasannidhim \|*  
*gatvā sovāca giriśaṃ sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. While crying, he uttered the word Uma. Gauri who bestows welfare to all, saw the infant with astonishing beauty and went to lord Shiva and then spoke to him.

## \|\| 9.19 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*gṛhamāgaccha sarveśa tapasāṃ phaladāyakam \|*  
*kalpe kalpe dhyāyasi yaṃ taṃ paśyāgatya mandiram \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Parvati said – О lord of all, you come home and find the one who bestows the reward of the tapas in all the kalpas and find him there.

## \|\| 9.20 \|\|

*śīghraṃ putramukhaṃ paśya puṇyabījaṃ mahotsavam \|*  
*punnāmanarakatrāṇakāraṇaṃ bhavatāraṇam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Soon you will see the face of your son, who is the cause of merit, the great festival and protects from the hell named Pumnama; he is the one who can redeem us from the ocean of the universe.

## \|\| 9.21 \|\|

*snānaṃ ca sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dakṣiṇam \|*  
*putrasaṃdarśanasyāsya kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The merit one earns by taking a bath in all the holy places of diksa for all the yajnas, will not compare even the one sixteenth ray of the glory of the son.

## \|\| 9.22 \|\|

*sarvadānena yatpuṇyaṃ kṣmāpradakṣiṇataśca yat \|*  
*putradarśanapuṇyasya kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The merit one earns by giving away everything in charity or by taking a bath in all the holy places does not compare even the sixteenth part of the merit earned by looking at the face of a son.

## \|\| 9.23 \|\|

*sarvaistapobhiryatpuṇyaṃ devānaśanairvrataiḥ \|*  
*satputrodbhavapuṇyasya kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The merit one earns by performing all the tapas, vratas and fasting does not compare with the merit one earns by looking at the face of a son.

## \|\| 9.24-26 \|\|

*yadviprabhojanaiḥ puṇyaṃ yadeva surasevanaiḥ \|*  
*satputraprāptipuṇyasya kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 24 \|\|*

*pārvatyā vacanaṃ śrutvā śivaḥ saṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*ājagāma svabhavanaṃ kṣipraṃ vai kāntayā saha \|*
