*dadarśa talpe svasutaṃ taptakāñcanasannibham \|\| 25 \|\|*

*hadayasthaṃ ca yadrūpaṃ tadevātimanoharam \|*  
*durgātalpātsamādāya kṛtvā vakṣasi taṃ sutam \|*  
*cucumbānandajaladhau nimagnā setyuvāca tam \|\| 26 \|\|*

24-26. The merit one earns by feeding the Brahmana and serving the gods does not compare even the sixteenth part of the merit one earns by looking at the face of the son. Shiva felt delighted and thereafter he went to the inner apartment with his wife and found the child having a fair complexion like gold who was quite pleasant to look at.

## \|\| 9.27-28 \|\|

*saṃprāpyāmūlyaratnaṃ tvāṃ pūrṇameva sanātanam \|*  
*yathā mano daridrasya sahasā prāpya saddhanam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*kānte suciramāyāte proṣite yoṣito yathā \|*  
*mānasaṃ paripūrṇaṃ ca babhūva ca tathā mama \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. Goddess Durga lifted up the son from the bed and took him in her arms. She was filled with ecstasy and kissed him. Thereafter she said, "As a mind of pauper is delighted at the sudden receiving of immense riches, similarly I have achieved a gem like you." You are completely eternal.

## \|\| 9.29 \|\|

*suciraṃ gatamāyātamekaputrā kathā sutam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭā yathā vatsa tathā'hamapi sāmpratam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. As a wife derives pleasure after the arrival of her husband from some other country after staying there, similarly my mind has been delighted.

## \|\| 9.30-31 \|\|

*sadratnaṃ suciraṃ bhraṣṭaṃ prāpya hṛṣṭo yathā janaḥ \|*  
*anāvṛṣṭau suvṛṣṭiṃ ca saṃprāpyāhaṃ tathā sutam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*yathā suciramandhānāṃ sthitānāṃ ca nirāśraye \|*  
*cakṣussunirmmalaṃ prāpya manaḥ pūrṇaṃ tathaiva me \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. As a mother feels delighted on the arrival of her son after a long time, similarly I feel immensely happy; as one gets suddenly the gem which was lost long back or a rainfall after a prolonged drought delights the people, similarly I have been delighted at the arrival of the son.

## \|\| 9.32 \|\|

*dustare sāgare ghore patitasya ca saṃkaṭe \|*  
*anaukasya prāpya naukāṃ manaḥ pūrṇaṃ tathā mama \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The pleasure a blind man obtains when he gets back his eye-sight, the same type of pleasure I have got by getting the son.

## \|\| 9.33 \|\|

*tṛṣṇayā śuṣkakaṇṭhānāṃ sucirācca suśītalam \|*  
*suvāsitaṃ jalaṃ prāpya manaḥ pūrṇaṃ tathā mama \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. As a man who is thrown into the difficult ocean without a support or a boat and during the time of misery if one gets a boat, the pleasure he derives can be compared with that of my pleasure.

## \|\| 9.34 \|\|

*dāvāgnipatitānāṃ ca sthitānāṃ ca nirāśraye \|*  
*niragnimāśrayaṃ prāpya manaḥ pūrṇaṃ tathā mama \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. A person whose throat is dried up due to thirst for long and he is delighted suddenly on receiving the cool and fragrant water, his mind is thus filled with pleasure, similarly my mind is feeling pleased.

## \|\| 9.35 \|\|

*ciraṃ bubhukṣitānāṃ ca vratopoṣaṇakāriṇām \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. A person who is surrounded by a forest fire all round and is suddenly rescued, the pleasure he derives in such an eventuality is comparable to my present pleasure.

## \|\| 9.36 \|\|

*sadannaṃ purato dṛṣṭvā manaḥ pūrṇaṃ tathā mama \|*  
*ityuktvā pārvatī tatra kroḍe kṛtvā svabālakam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. A person who suffers because of performing vrata for a long time and remains without food and water but finds the delicious food before him is pleased, similarly my mind is also feeling pleasure.

## \|\| 9.37-38 \|\|

*prītyā stanaṃ dadau tasmai paramānandamānasā \|*  
*kroḍe cakāra bhagavānbālakaṃ hṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*cucumba gaṇḍe vedoktaṃ yuyuje cāśiṣaṃ mudā \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. Thus speaking Parvati carrying the child in her lap, fed him from her breasts. Thereafter Shiva too held the child in his lap and delightfully kissed him, blessing him at the same time according to the Vedic rites.

## Chapter 10

## \|\| 10.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*tau dampatī bahirgatvā putramaṅgalahetave \|*  
*vividhāni ca ratnāni dvijebhyo dadaturmudā \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – The divine couple came at the door and distributed several gems to the Brahmanas in charity.

## \|\| 10.2-6 \|\|

*bandibhyo bhikṣukebhyaśca dānāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*nānāvidhāni vādyāni vādayāmāsa śaṅkaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*himālayaśca ratnānāṃ dadau lakṣaṃ dvijātaye \|*  
*sahasraṃ ca gajendrāṇāmaśvānāṃ ca trilakṣakam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*daśalakṣaṃ gavāṃ caiva pañcalakṣaṃ suvarṇakam \|*  
*muktāmāṇikyaratnāni maṇiśreṣṭhāni yāni ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

*anyānyapi ca dānāni vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca \|*  
*sarvāṇyamūlyaratnāni kṣīrodotpattikāni ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇebhyo dadau viṣṇuḥ kaustubhaṃ kautukānvitaḥ \|*  
*brahmā viśiṣṭadānāni viprāṇāṃ vāñchitāni ca \|*  
*sudurlabhāni sṛṣṭau ca brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 6 \|\|*

2-6. For the welfare of the child, the bards and beggars were also given various types of riches in charity. Shiva then made several musical instruments to be played upon. Himalaya distributed a lakh of gems in charity besides hundreds of elephants, three lakhs of horses, ten lakhs of gold coins, besides jewels, rubies, gems and several other precious stones, in addition to beautiful garments, ornaments, gems recovered from the ocean of milk and several other articles in charity. Vishnu on the other hand playfully gave away the kaustubha gem to the Brahmanas. Brahma on the other hand getting pleased gave away in charity to the Brahmanas their desired things, which were difficult to get in the universe.

## \|\| 10.7 \|\|

*dharmaḥ sūryyaśca śakraśca devāśca munayastathā \|*  
*gandharvāḥ parvatā devyo dadurdānaṃ krameṇa ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Thus Dharma, Surya and Indra besides other gods, the sages, Gandharvas, the mountains and the goddesses also have given away in charities to the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 10.8-14 \|\|

*tāpasānāṃ sahastrāṇi rucakānāṃ śatāni ca \|*  
*śatāni gsārāṇāṃ maṇīnadarāṇāṃ ca nārada \|\| 8 \|\|*

*māṇikyānāṃ sahasrāṇi ratnānāṃ ca śatāni ca \|*  
*śatāni kaustubhānāṃ ca hīrakāṇāṃ śatāni ca \|*  
*haridvarṇamaṇīndrāṇāṃ sahasrāṇi mudā'nvitāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*gavāṃ ratnāni lakṣāṇi gajaratnasahasrakam \|*  
*amūlyānyanyaratnāni śvetavarṇāni kautukāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

*śatalakṣaṃ suvarṇānāṃ vahniśuddhāṃśukāni ca \|*  
*brāhmaṇebhyo dadau brahmā tatra kṣīrodadhirmudā \|\| 11 \|\|*

*hāraṃ cāmūlyaratnānāṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|*  
*atīva nirmalaṃ sāraṃ sūryyabhānuvinindakam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*pariṣkṛtaṃ ca māṇikyairhīrakaiśca virājitam \|*  
*ramyaṃ kaustubhamadhyasthaṃ dadau devī sarasvatī \|\| 13 \|\|*

*trailokyasāraṃ hāraṃ ca sadratnagaṇanirmitam \|*  
*bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvāṇi sā sāvitrī dadau mudā \|\| 14 \|\|*

8-14. Brahma also gave away in charity thousands of jewels, a hundred gems, a hundred kaustubha gems, a hundred rubies, a thousand sapphires, a lakh of cows, a lakh of gems, a thousand elephants of the best quality, the invaluable white horses, a hundred lakhs of gold coins and the garments sanctified by the fire. The ocean gave away the garland of gems which was difficult to get in the three worlds, spotless, solid ornaments which put the ray of the sun to shame and were studded with gem and jewels having the kaustubha gem in the centre. The goddess Sarasvati gave away in charity the garland which was the essence of the universe and was made of the best of gems. Savitri gave away all the ornaments.

## \|\| 10.15 \|\|

*lakṣaṃ suvarṇaloṣṭānāṃ dhanāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*śatānyamūlyaratnānāṃ kuberaśca dadau mudā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Kubera delightfully gave away a lakh of gold coins besides several riches and invaluable gems in charity.

## \|\| 10.16 \|\|

*dānāni dattvā viprebhyaste sarve dadṛśuḥ śiśum \|*  
*paramānandasaṃyuktāḥ śivaputrotsave mune \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О sage, in the festivity of the birth of the son of Shiva, all the gods were filled with delight and had a look at the child.

## \|\| 10.17 \|\|

*bhāraṃ voḍhumaśaktāśca brāhmaṇā bandinastathā \|*  
*sthāyaṃsthāyaṃ ca gacchanto dhanāni pathi kātarāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. At that point of time the Brahmanas and the bards were walking quite slowly because of the heavy load of charities carried by them.

## \|\| 10.18 \|\|

*kathayanti kathāḥ sarve viśrāntāḥ pūrvadāyinām \|*  
*buddhāḥ śṛṇvanti muditā yuvāno bhikṣukā mune \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. О sage, those people while taking rest were also talking about the earlier people who gave away charities and all the old, the young and the beggars were listening to them attentively.

## \|\| 10.19-21 \|\|

*viṣṇuḥ pramuditastatra vādayāmāsa dundubhim \|*  
*saṃgītaṃ pāyayāmāsa kārayāmāsa nartanam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*vedāṃśca pāṭhayāmāsa purāṇāni ca nārada \|*  
*munīndrānānayāmāsa pūjayāmāsa tānmudā \|\| 20 \|\|*

*āśiṣaṃ dāpayāmāsa kārayāmāsa maṅgalam \|*  
*sārddhaṃ devaścai devībhirdadau tasmai śubhāśiṣaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. О Narada, Vishnu got delighted and he made the drums to be beaten, besides music and dance to continue. The Vedas and Puranas were recited, the pujas were performed by inviting the sages and all the gods blessed the child. Thereafter the gods and the goddesses also offered their blessings to the child.

## \|\| 10.22 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*śivena tulyaṃ jñānaṃ te paramāyuśca bālaka \|*  
*parākrame mayā tulyaḥ sarvasiddhīśvaro bhava \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Vishnu said – О child, you may acquire knowledge like Shiva and have a long life. You should have the prowess like me and you should be lord of all success.

## \|\| 10.23 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*yaśasā te jagatpūrṇaṃ sarvapūjyo bhavāciram \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ purataḥ pūjā bhavatvatisudurlabhā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Brahma said – Your glory should spread over the entire universe. Soon you will be adorable by all and you will be adored first of all.

## \|\| 10.24 \|\|

*dharmma uvāca \|*  
*mayā tulyaḥ sudharmiṣṭho bhavānbhavatu durlabhaḥ \|*  
*sarvajñaśca dayāyukto haribhakto hareḥ samaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Dharma said – You will be quite a noble person like me besides becoming all knowledgeable, compassionate, devoted to Hari and like the lord.

## \|\| 10.25 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*dātā bhava mayā tulyo haribhaktaśca buddhimān \|*  
*vidyāvānpuṇyavāñchānto dāntaśca prāṇavallabha \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Mahadeva said – О son, you are dearer to me than my life. You should perform charities like me, be devoted to Hari, possess great intelligence and be an intellectual, meritorious, peaceful and subjugate your enemies:

## \|\| 10.26-27 \|\|

*lakṣmīruvāca \|*  
*mama sthitiśca gehe te dehe bhavatu śāśvatī \|*  
*pativratā mayā tulyā śāntā kāntā manoharā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*sarasvatyuvāca \|*  
*mayā tulyā sukavitā dhāraṇāśaktireva ca \|*  
*smṛtirvivecanāśaktirbhavatvatitarāṃ suta \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Sarasvati said – О son, you will be able to compose beautiful poetry like me with immense power of absorption, wisdom and power of discussion.

## \|\| 10.28 \|\|

*sāvitryuvāca \|*  
*vatsāhaṃ vedajananī vedajñānī bhavāciram \|*  
*manmantrajapaśīlaśca pravaro vedavādinām \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Savitri said – О son, I am the mother of the Vedas; you will soon become well-versed in all the Vedas. By nature, you will recite my mantra and you will be the best of all those well-versed in the Vedas.

## \|\| 10.29 \|\|

*himālaya uvāca \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇe'timatiḥ śaśvadbhaktirbhavatu śāśvatī \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇatulyo guṇavānbhava kṛṣṇaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Himalaya said – You should always remain devoted to lord Krishna and you may achieve his eternal devotion. You will be virtuous like him and should always remain devoted to lord Krishna.

## \|\| 10.30 \|\|

*menakovāca \|*  
*samudratulyo gāmbhīryye kāmatulyaśca rūpavān \|*  
*śrīyuktaḥ śrīpatisamo dharmme dharmasamo bhava \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Menaka said – You will be as deep as the ocean, as beautiful as Kama, the god of love, be illustrious like Vishnu and quite religious like Dharma.

## \|\| 10.31 \|\|

*vasundharovāca \|*  
*kṣamāśīlo mayā tulyaḥ śaraṇyaḥ sarvaratnavān \|*  
*nirvighno vighnanighnaśca bhava vatsa śubhāśrayaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Vasundhara said – О son, you should be like me in forgiveness, protect the people who come to take refuge with you, should be adorned with all the gems, without obstructions and destroyer of all the obstructions representing welfare all the times.

## \|\| 10.32-33 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*tātatulyamahāyogī siddhaḥ siddhipradaḥ śubhaḥ \|*  
*mṛtyuñjayaśca bhagavānbhavatvativiśāradaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*ṛṣayo munayaḥ siddhāḥ sarve yuyujurāśiṣaḥ \|*  
*brāhmaṇā bandinaścaiva yuyujuḥ sarvamaṅgalam \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Parvati said – You will be a great yogi like your father, a siddha, a bestower of success, auspicious and full of fortune, over-power the death and quite intelligent. Thereafter the sages and the ascetics besides the siddhas variously blessed him. The Brahmanas and the bards showered welfare on him.

## \|\| 10.34 \|\|

*sarvaṃ te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalam \|*  
*gaṇeśajanmakathanaṃ sarvavighnavināśanam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О son, I have thus narrated to you the story of the birth of Ganesha which is the welfare of all the welfare and removes all the obstructions.

## \|\| 10.35 \|\|

*imaṃ sumaṃgalādhyakṣaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti sukhaṃ yataḥ \|*  
*sarvamaṃgalasaṃyuktaḥ sa bhavenmaṃgalālayaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The one who listens to this chapter filled with welfare, is bestowed with all the welfare and always meets with welfare and delight.

## \|\| 10.36-37 \|\|

*aputro labhate putramadhano labhate dhanam \|*  
*kṛpaṇo labhate sattvaṃ śaśvatsampatpradāyi ca \|\| 36 \|\|*

*bhāryyārthī labhate bhāryyāṃ prajārthī labhate prajām \|*  
*ārogyaṃ labhate rogī saubhāgye durbhagā labhet \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. A sonless person gets a son, a pauper gets the riches, a miser gets the sattva and the riches also. A person desirous to get a wife gets the same, the one who is desirous of people gets the people, the sick is relieved of all the ailments and an unfortunate person gets all the fortune.

## \|\| 10.38 \|\|

*bhraṣṭaputraṃ naṣṭadhanaṃ proṣitaṃ ca priyaṃ labhet \|*  
*śokāviṣṭaḥ sadā''nandaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thus one gets back his lost son, lost property and the beloved who might have gone to some other country. The one whose mind is filled with grief is relieved of the same. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 10.39-40 \|\|

*yatpuṇyaṃ labhate martyo gaṇeśākhyānakaśrutau \|*  
*tatphalaṃ labhate nūnamadhyāyaśravaṇānmune \|\| 39 \|\|*

*ayaṃ ca maṅgalādhyāyo yasya gehaṃ ca tiṣṭhati \|*  
*sadā maṅgalasaṃyuktaḥ sa bhavennātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. О sage, the merit one gets by listening to the story of the birth of Ganesha, the same merit is also achieved by listening to this chapter. Whosoever recites this chapter of welfare, he always meets with welfare; there is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 10.41 \|\|

*yātrākāle ca puṇyāhe yaḥ śṛṇoti samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sarvābhīṣṭaṃ sa labhate śrīgaṇeśaprasādataḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. At the time of travelling and at an auspicious time whosoever listens to it attentively, he achieves the desired reward by the grace of lord Ganesha.

## Chapter 11

## \|\| 11.1-3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*haristamāśiṣaṃ kṛtvā ratnasiṃhāsane vare \|*  
*devaiśca munibhiḥ sārddhamavasattatra saṃsadi \|\| 1 \|\|*

*dakṣiṇe śaṃkarastasya vāme brahmā prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*purato jagatāṃ sākṣī dharmo dharmavatāṃ varaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*tathā dharmasamīpe ca sūryyaḥ śakraḥ kalānidhiḥ \|*  
*devāśca munayo brahmannūṣuḥ śailāḥ sukhāsane \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Narayana said – Lord Vishnu occupied the gem-studded lion-throne after offering his blessing to the child. Shiva took his seat to his right side and Brahma was seated to his left. The religious Dharma also was present there. О Brahmana, Stirya, Indra, the moon, the gods, the sages, the mountains were also seated close to him.

## \|\| 11.4-6 \|\|

*nanartta narttakaśreṇī jagurgandharva kinnarāḥ \|*  
*śrutisāraṃ śrutisukhaṃ tuṣṭuvuḥ śrutayo harim \|\| 4 \|\|*

*etasminnantare tatra draṣṭuṃ śaṅkaranandanam \|*  
*ājagāma mahāyogī sūryaputraḥ śanaiścaraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*atyantanamravadana īṣanmudritalocanaḥ \|*  
*antarbahiḥ smarankṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇaikagatamānasaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. The apsaras were dancing while the Gandharvas and Kinnaras started singing from the Vedas, eulogising lord Vishnu, which was quite pleasant for the ear. In the meantime at the sight of Ganesha, the son of Surya also arrived there. He was looking downwards with half open eyes and was reciting the name of lord Krishna outside as well as inside.

## \|\| 11.7-8 \|\|

*tapaḥphalāśī tejasvī jvaladagniśikhopamaḥ \|*  
*atīva sundaraḥ śyāmaḥ pītāmbaradharo varaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*praṇamya viṣṇuṃ brahmāṇaṃ śivaṃ dharmaṃ raviṃ surān \|*  
*munīndrānbālakaṃ draṣṭuṃ jagāma tadanujñayā \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. He was quite illustrious who could enjoy the reward of the tapas, was like the burning flame and was clad in a beautiful yellow lower garment, he bowed in reverence to Vishnu, Brahma, Shiva, Dharma, Surya and other gods and thereafter he went to have a look at the child.

## \|\| 11.9 \|\|

*pradhānadvāramāsādya śivatulyaparākramam \|*  
*dvāḥsthaṃ vai śūlahastaṃ ca viśālākṣamuvāca ha \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On reaching the main-gate Saturn spoke to the gate-keeper, who had broad eyes and was valorous like Shiva.

## \|\| 11.10 \|\|

*śanaiścara uvāca \|*  
*śivājñayā śiśuṃ draṣṭuṃ yāmi śaṃkarakiṃkara \|*  
*viṣṇupramukhadevānāṃ munīnāmanurodhataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Saturn said – О servant of Shiva, with the permission of Vishnu, Shiva and other gods, I am going to have a look at the child.

## \|\| 11.11 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde śanipārvatīsaṃvāde śaneradhodṛṣṭau kāraṇakathanaṃ nāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Therefore, О intelligent one, you kindly permit me to go to Parvati. I shall come back soon after looking at the child because my mind always remains engrossed in worldly affairs.

## \|\| 11.12 \|\|

*viśālākṣa uvāca \|*  
*ājñāvaho na devānāṃ nāhaṃ śaṃkarakiṃkaraḥ \|*  
*mārgaṃ dātuṃ na śakto'haṃ vinā manmāturājñayā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Visalaksa said -I am neither the servant of the gods nor am I the servant of Shiva. I will not permit you to get in without the permission of my mother.

## \|\| 11.13 \|\|

*ityuktvā'bhyantarabhyetya preritaḥ sa śivājñayā \|*  
*dadau mārgaṃ graheśāya viśālākṣo mudā tataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus speaking he entered the house of with the permission of Parvati; he admitted Visalaksa inside the chamber.

## \|\| 11.14 \|\|

*śanirabhyantaraṃ gatvā cānamannamrakandharaḥ \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthāṃ ca pārvatīṃ sasmitāṃ mudā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Reaching inside he found Parvati moving her shoulders and smiling at the same time. He then bowed in reverence to her, who was seated on the gem-studded throne.

## \|\| 11.15-16 \|\|

*sakhībhiḥ pañcabhiḥ śaśvatsevitāṃ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|*  
*sakhīdattaṃ ca tāmbūlamupabhujya suvāsitam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*vahniśuddhāṃśukādhānāṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitām \|*  
*paśyantīṃ narttakīnṛtyaṃ putraṃ dhṛtvā ca vakṣasi \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Five of her maid-friends were swinging the white fly-whisks and serving Parvati. Parvati on her part was delightfully chewing the betel offered to her by her maid-friends. She was clad in garments purified by fire and adorned with all the ornaments. She held the child in her lap and was witnessing the dance by apsaras.

## \|\| 11.17 \|\|

*nataṃ sūryyasutaṃ dṛṣṭvā durgā saṃbhāṣya satvaram \|*  
*śubhāśiṣaṃ dadau tasmai pṛṣṭvā tanmaṃgalaṃ śubham \|\| 17\|\|*

17\. Finding Saturn standing there, casting his gaze downwards, she blessed him and enquired of his welfare.

## \|\| 11.18 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*kathamānamravaktrastvaṃ śrotumicchāmi sāmpratam \|*  
*kiṃ na paśyasi māṃ sādho bālakaṃ vā graheśvara \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Parvati said – О noble person, О lord of the planets, why are you casting your eyes downwards? I would like to listen. Why don’t you look at my son.

## \|\| 11.19 \|\|

*śaniruvāca \|*  
*sarve svakarmaṇā sādhvi bhuñjate tapasaḥ phalam \|*  
*śubhāśubhaṃ ca yatkarma koṭikalpairna lupyate \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Sani said – О chaste lady, all the people have to face the result of their deeds. Whatever good or bad deeds are done, they cannot be washed away even after completion of crores of kalpas.

## \|\| 11.20 \|\|

*karmaṇā jāyate janturbrahmendrāryyamamandire \|*  
*karmaṇā narageheṣu paśvādiṣu ca karmaṇā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The Jlva is born as Brahma, Indra and Surya because of his deeds and also is reborn as an animal because of his deeds.

## \|\| 11.21 \|\|

*karmaṇā narakaṃ yāti vaikuṇṭhaṃ yāti karmaṇā \|*  
*svakarmaṇā ca rājendro bhṛtyaścāpi svakarmaṇā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. One achieves hell because of his deeds an also Vaikuntha because of the same. He becomes the great king because of his own deeds and an ordinary servant because of his own deeds.

## \|\| 11.22 \|\|

*karmaṇā sundaraḥ śaśvadvyādhiyuktaḥ svakarmaṇā \|*  
*karmaṇā viṣayī mātarnnirliptaśca svakarmaṇā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He is born beautiful because of his own deeds and he becomes sick the same way. О mother, because of his own deeds he indulges into vices and by his own deeds he becomes detached from the world.

## \|\| 11.23 \|\|

*karmaṇā dhanavām̐lloko dainyayuktaḥ svakarmaṇā \|*  
*karmaṇā satkuṭumbī ca karmaṇā bandhukaṇṭakaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The people become rich because of their own deeds and because of their own deeds they become pauper and miserable. Because of the deeds one achieves high family and because of his own deeds one gets the brother who always indulges in pinpricks for him.

## \|\| 11.24-25 \|\|

*subhāryaśca suputraśca sukhī śaśvatsvakarmaṇā \|*  
*aputrakaśca kustrīko nistrīkaśca svakarmaṇā \|\| 24 \|\|*

*itihāsaṃ cātigopyaṃ śṛṇu śaṅkaravallabhe \|*  
*akathyaṃ jananīpārśve lajjājanakakāraṇam \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Because of his own deeds one gets the best wife and best son and he enjoys pleasure because of his own deeds. Because of his own deeds he remains without a son, gets a wicked wife or remains without a wife. О beloved of Shiva, I tell you a secret story. Because of it being shameful, it is not appropriate to be told to the mother.

## \|\| 11.26-27 \|\|

*ābālyātkṛṣṇabhakto'haṃ kṛṣṇadhyānaikamānasaḥ \|*  
*tapasyāsu rataśśaśvadviṣaye'pi rataḥ sadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*pitā dadau vivāhe tu kanyāṃ citrarathasya ca \|*  
*atitejasvinī śaśvattapasyāsu ratā satī \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. In my childhood I was a great devotee of lord Krishna and I was always devoted to him. I always recited his name and was also indulging in all types of vices. My father married me to the daughter of Chitraratha but I was always devoted to tapas.

## \|\| 11.28 \|\|

*ekadā sā tvṛtusnātā suveṣaṃ svaṃ vidhāya ca \|*  
*ratnālaṅkārasaṃyuktā munimānasamohinī \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Once after having a bath, after her period, she beautifully adorned herself. After adorning herself with all the ornaments, she became attractive and could attract the mind of the sages.

## \|\| 11.29-31 \|\|

*hareḥ pādaṃ dhyāyamānaṃ paśyantī madamohitā \|*  
*matsamīpaṃ samāgatya sasmitā lolalocanā \|\| 29 \|\|*

*śaśāpa māmapaśyantamṛtunāśācca kopataḥ \|*  
*bāhyajñānavihīnaṃ ca dhyānasaṃlagnamānasam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*na dṛṣṭā'haṃ tvayā yena na kṛtaṃ hyṛturakṣaṇam tvayā dṛṣṭaṃ ca yadvastu mūḍha sarvaṃ vinaśyati \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. Wearing a serene smile on her face, she with an unstable gaze came to me and asked me to look at her. At that point of time my mind was engrossed in meditation and I was unaware of what was happening outside. Therefore instead of looking at her I kept on performing the tapas. At this neglect, she became annoyed and feeling that her bath after the period has become infructuous, she pronounced a curse on me, "O foolish fellow you have not cared to look at me at this moment and you have not obliged me; therefore wherever you cast your glance it would be destroyed."

## \|\| 11.32 \|\|

*ahaṃ ca virato dhyānāttoṣayaṃstāṃ tadā satīm \|*  
*śāpaṃ moktuṃ na śaktā sā paścāttāpamavāpa ha \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Thereafter, getting relieved of the meditation I satisfied the chaste lady but she was unable to relieve me of the curse. She, therefore, repented in her mind of the same.

## \|\| 11.33 \|\|

*tena mātarna paśyāmi kiñcidvastu svacakṣuṣā \|*  
*tataḥ prakṛtinamrāsyaḥ prāṇihiṃsābhayādaham \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О mother, because of this I cannot cast my gaze at anything and in order to save the creatures from destruction I always cast my glance downwards.

## \|\| 11.34 \|\|

*śanaiścaravacaḥ śrutvā cāhasatpārvatī mune \|*  
*uccaiḥ prajahasuḥ sarvā narttakīkinnarīgaṇāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O sage, on hearing the words of Saturn, Parvati laughed and all the damsels also followed suit.

## Chapter 12

## \|\| 12.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*durgā tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sasmāra harimīśvaram \|*  
*īśvarecchāvaśībhūtaṃ jagadevetyuvāca ha \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – On hearing the words of Saturn, she recited the name of the lord and said to him, "The entire universe moves according to the wishes of the lord more than the move of destiny."

## \|\| 12.2 \|\|

*sā ca devī daivavaśā śaniṃ provāca kautukāt \|*  
*paśya māṃ macchiśumiti niṣekaḥ kena vāryyate \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Parvati playfully said to Saturn, you look at me and my child. Who can prevent the festivities of the birth.

## \|\| 12.3-6 \|\|

*pārvatyā vacanaṃ śrutvā śanirmene hṛdā svayam \|*  
*paśyāmi kiṃ na paśyāmi pārvatīsutamityaho \|*  
*yadi bālo mayā dṛṣṭastasya vighno bhaveddhruvam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*anyathā supraśastaṃ ca purataḥ svātmarakṣaṇam \|*  
*ityevamuktvā dharmiṣṭho dharmaṃ kṛtvā tu sākṣiṇam \|*  
*bālaṃ draṣṭuṃ manaścakre na tu tanmātaraṃ śaniḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*viṣaṇṇamānasaḥ pūrvaṃ śuṣkakaṇṭhauṣṭhatālukaḥ \|*  
*savyalocanakoṇena dadarśa ca śiśormukham \|\| 5 \|\|*

*śaneśca dṛṣṭimātreṇa cicchide mastakaṃ mune \|*  
*cakṣurnimīlayāmāsa tasthau namrānanaḥ śaniḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

3-6. On hearing the words of Parvati, Saturn thought in his mind, he was in a fix whether to look at the son of Parvati or not because he thought in case he looked at the child there would be an obstruction and if he did not do, how could he protect himself? Thus thinking Saturn decided to look at the child and not his mother. His mind was disturbed in the first instance and his throat, lips and palate got dried up. Therefore with the comer of the right eye he glanced at the child’s face.

## \|\| 12.7 \|\|

*tasthau ca pārvatīkroḍe tatsarvāṅgaṃ salohitam \|*  
*viveśa mastakaṃ kṛṣṇe gatvā golokamīpsitam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О sage, at the gaze of Saturn, the head of the child was cut-off and Saturn closed his eyes at once, looking downwards and stood there.

## \|\| 12.8-11 \|\|

*mūrcchā saṃprāpa sā devī vilapya ca bhṛśaṃ muhuḥ \|*  
*matteva ca pṛthivyāṃ tu kṛtvā vakṣasi bālakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*vismitāste surāḥ sarve citraputtalikā yathā \|*  
*devyaśca śailā gandharvāḥ sarve kailāsavāsinaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*tānsarvānmūrcchitāndṛṣṭvaivāruhya garuḍaṃ hariḥ \|*  
*jagāma puṣpabhadrāṃ sa cottarasyāṃ diśi sthitām \|\| 10 \|\|*

*puṣpabhadrānadītīre hyapaśyatkānane sthitam \|*  
*gajendraṃ nidritaṃ tatra śayānaṃ hastinīyutam \|\| 11 \|\|*

8-11. The head of the child who was lying in the lap of Parvati was severed and his body was soaked in blood. The severed head of the child went to Goloka and entered into the body of lord Krishna. Parvati started lamenting and carrying the child in her lap she fainted and fell down on earth like dead. All the gods were surprised and were flabbergasted. They stood there like puppets. All the gods, the goddesses, the mountains, Gandharvas and all the dwellers of Kailasa behaved similarly. Thereafter, finding all the people fainted Vishnu mounted on Garuda, went to the northern direction and reached the bank of Puspabhadra river.

## \|\| 12.12-14 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde śanikṛtagaṇeśadarśana tajjātagaṇeśaśiraḥpatana viṣṇukṛtagaṇeśaśiroyojana śaniśāpādikathanaṃ nāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*śīghraṃ sudarśanenaiva cicchide tacchiro mudā \|*  
*sthāpayāmāsa garuḍe rudhirāktaṃ manoharam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*gajacchinnāṅgavikṣepātprabodhaṃ prāpya hastinī \|*  
*śāvakānbodhayāmāsa cāśubhaṃ vadatī tadā \|*  
*ruroda śāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ sā vilapya śucāturā \|\| 14 \|\|*

12-14. Reaching the bank of Puspabhadra river, he found an elephant enjoying the company of cow elephants. He was resting after enjoying the company of the cow elephants, placing his head toward the north. The elephant-calves surrounded him. Lord Vishnu delightfully cut-off his head by using Sudarsana-cakra which was soaked in blood and kept it over Garuda and was quite pleasant to look at.

## \|\| 12.15-17 \|\|

*tuṣṭāva kamalākāntaṃ śāntaṃ sasmitamīśvaram \|*  
*śaṃkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ pītāmbaraṃ param \|*  
*garuḍasthaṃ jagatkāntaṃ bhrāmayantaṃ sudarśanam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*niṣekaṃ khaṇḍituṃ śaktaṃ niṣekajanakaṃ vibhum \|*  
*niṣekabhogadātāraṃ bhoganistārakāraṇam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*prabhustatstavanāttuṣṭastasyai vipra varaṃ dadau \|*  
*muṇḍātuṇḍaṃ pṛthakkṛtya yuyuje'nyagajasya ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

15-17. With the severing of the head of the elephant, the cow elephants were awakened and getting panicky made the young elephants to wake up. Thereafter the cow elephants with their progeny offered prayer to lord Vishnu, the husband of Kamala, who was peaceful, wearing a smile on his face holding sharhkha, cakra, gada and padma in his four hands, clad in yellow garments, mounted on Garuda, who was the lord of the entire universe and was wielding the Sudarsana-cakra.

## \|\| 12.18 \|\|

*jīvayāmāsa taṃ tatra brahmajñānena sarvavit \|*  
*sarvāṃge yojayāmāsa gajasya caraṇāmbujam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. She said, "You are competent to shatter the birth, the creator of the birth, glorious, bestower of pleasure, emanating from the birth and the one who relieves the people from the worldly pleasures, being the soul cause of the same."

## \|\| 12.19-21 \|\|

*tvaṃ jīvākalpaparyyantaṃ parivāraiḥ samaṃ gaja \|*  
*ityuktvā ca manoyāyī kailāsaṃ hyājagāma saḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*āhṛtya pārvatīhastādbālaṃ kṛtvā svavakṣasi \|*  
*ruciraṃ tacchirassamyagyojayāmāsa bālake \|\| 20 \|\|*

*brahmasvarūpo bhagavānbrahmajñānena līlayā \|*  
*jīvayāmāsa taṃ śīghraṃ huṃkāroccāraṇena ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. О Brahmana, lord Vishnu got pleased with the prayer and getting satisfied granted a boon. He lifted the head of the elephant from the trunk. With the application of the divine knowledge, he brought back to the dead elephant life. The lord who is all knowledgeable, touched the body of the elephant with his lotus-like feet and said, "0 elephant, you will remain alive with your family for a kalpa." Thus speaking lord Vishnu reached the peak of the mount Kailasa.

## \|\| 12.22 \|\|

*pārvatīṃ bodhayitvā tu kṛtvā kroḍe ca taṃ śiśum \|*  
*bodhayāmāsa tāṃ kṛṣṇa ādhyātmikavibodhanaiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He took out the trunk of the child from the hand of Parvati, kept him in his lap and joined the elephant head with the trunk of the child.

## \|\| 12.23-24 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*brahmādikīṭaparyyantaṃ phalaṃ bhuṅkte svakarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*jagadbuddhisvarūpā'si tvaṃ na jānāsi kiṃ śiva \|\| 23 \|\|*

*kalpakoṭiśataṃ bhogī jīvināṃ tatsvakarmaṇā \|*  
*upasthito bhavennityaṃ pratiyonau śubhāśubhaiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. The lord who has been the form of Brahman applying the divine knowledge uttered humkara and brought the child back to life. Thereafter Krishna after consoling Parvati variously placed the child in her lap and also imparted the divine knowledge to her.

## \|\| 12.25-26 \|\|

*indraḥ svakarmaṇā kīṭayonau janma labhetsati \|*  
*kīṭaścāpi bhavedindraḥ pūrvakarmaphalena vai \|\| 25 \|\|*

*siṃho'pi makṣikāṃ hantumakṣamaḥ prāktanaṃ vinā \|*  
*maśako hastinaṃ hantuṃ kṣamaḥ svaprāktanena ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. Vishnu said – All the creatures from Brahma to the small insect have to face the result of their deeds and you happen to be the form of wisdom. Therefore, О Parvati, are you not aware of the fact that all the people have to face the result of their deeds for the crores of kalpas and because of the good and bad deeds, one has to take to various births always?

## \|\| 12.27 \|\|

*sukhaṃ duḥkha bhayaṃ śokamānandaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam \|*  
*sukarmaṇaḥ sukhaṃ harṣamitare pāpakarmaṇaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Indra because of his deeds is also born as an insect and an insect also because of his good deeds could reach the position of an Indra.

## \|\| 12.28 \|\|

*ihaiva karmaṇo bhogaḥ paratra ca śubhāśubhaiḥ \|*  
*karmopārjanayogyaṃ ca puṇyakṣetraṃ ca bhāratam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Even the lion is unable to kill a fly because of his deeds and a mosquito because of the result of his deed can even kill the elephant.

## \|\| 12.29 \|\|

*karmaṇaḥ phaladātā ca vidhātā ca vidherapi \|*  
*mṛtyormṛtyuḥ kālakālo niṣekasya niṣekakṛt \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Therefore, the pleasure, pain, fear, grief and joy are the result of one’s own deeds. The good deeds result in pleasure and one has to suffer pain because of evil deeds.

## \|\| 12.30 \|\|

*saṃharturapi saṃharttā pātuḥ pātā parātparaḥ \|*  
*golokanāthaḥ śrīkṛṣṇaḥ paripūrṇatamaḥ svayam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Because of the good or bad deeds, one has to face pleasure and pain in this world and also in the world beyond. Bharata happens to be the sacred place for performing deeds.

## \|\| 12.31-32 \|\|

*vayaṃ yasya kalāḥ puṃso brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|*  
*mahāvirāḍyadaṃśaśca yallomavivare jagat \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kalāṃśāḥ ke'pi taddharme kalāśāṃśāśca kecana \|*  
*carācaraṃ jagatsarvaṃ tatra tasthau vināyakaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The one who bestows the reward happens to be superior to Brahma, death for the death and death for the time, origin for the origin or the originator, killer of the killer and protector of the protector, is lord Krishna himself who is complete in himself, is lord of Goloka and also beyond that.

## \|\| 12.33 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇorvacanaṃ śrutvā parituṣṭā ca pārvatī \|*  
*stanaṃ dadau ca śiśave taṃ praṇamya gadādharam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva beside myself are all his rays. Mahavirat is a part of him. The globes are enshrined in his hair-pits.

## \|\| 12.34-35 \|\|

*tuṣṭāva pārvatī tuṣṭā preritā śaṃkareṇa ca \|*  
*kṛtāñjalipuṭā bhaktyā viṣṇuṃ taṃ kamalāpatim \|\| 34 \|\|*

*āśiṣaṃ yuyuje viṣṇuḥ śiśuṃ ca śiśumātaram \|*  
*dadau gale bālakasya kaustubhaṃ ca svabhūṣaṇam \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. О goddess Durga, some people happen to be the rays of the same while others are parts of those rays. Thus this stable and unstable universe besides Vinayaka, is enshrined in him. On hearing the words of lord Krishna, Parvati felt delighted. She then bowed in reverence before the lord and started suckling the child.

## \|\| 12.36-37 \|\|

*brahmā dadau svamukuṭaṃ dharmo vai ratnabhūṣaṇam \|*  
*krameṇa devyo ratnāni daduḥ sarve yathocitam \|\| 36 \|\|*

*tuṣṭāva taṃ mahādevaścātyantaṃ hṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*devāśca munayaḥ śailā gandharvāḥ sarvayoṣitaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. With the inspiration of lord Shiva, Parvati delightfully folded her hands with devotion and started offering prayer to lord Krishna which pleased lord Vishnu who blessed the child with his mother and adorned the neck of the child with kaustubha gem.

## \|\| 12.38 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā śivāḥ śivā caiva bālakaṃ mṛtajīvitam \|*  
*brāhmaṇebhyo dadau tatra koṭiratnāni nārada \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Similarly Brahma offered his crown to him, Dharma offered gem-studded ornaments and the goddesses gave the child appropriate gems.

## \|\| 12.39 \|\|

*aśvānāṃ ca gajānāṃ ca sahasrāṇi śatāni ca \|*  
*bandibhyaḥ pradadau tatra bālake mṛtajīvite \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thereafter lord Shiva feeling delighted eulogised the lord. Similarly the gods, the sages, mountains, Gandharvas and all the ladies offered prayers to him.

## \|\| 12.40 \|\|

*himālayaśca saṃtuṣṭo hṛṣṭā devāśca tatra vai \|*  
*dadurdānāni viprebhyo bandibhyaḥ sarvayoṣita \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. On Narada finding the child of Shiva and Parvati alike, the Brahmanas distributed crores of gems in charity.

## \|\| 12.41 \|\|

*brāhmaṇānbhojayāmāsa kārayāmāsa maṅgalam \|*  
*vedāṃśca pāṭhayāmāsa purāṇāni ramāpatiḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. On regaining life by the child, the bards were given away a thousand horses and a hundred elephants in charity.

## \|\| 12.42 \|\|

*śaniṃ saṃlajjitaṃ dṛṣṭvā pārvatī kopaśālinī \|*  
*śaśāpa ca sabhāmadhye'pyaṅgahīno bhaveti ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Himalaya getting pleased and satisfied beside the gods and their wives gave away many things in charity to the bards.

## \|\| 12.43-44 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā śaptaṃ śaniṃ sūryyaḥ kaśyapaśca yamastathā \|*  
*te'tiruṣṭāḥ samuttasthurgāmukāḥ śaṅkarālayāt \|\| 43 \|\|*

*raktākṣāste raktamukhāḥ kopaprasphuritādharāḥ \|*  
*tāṃ dharmasākṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā viṣṇuṃ saṃśaptumudyatāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. Vishnu the lord of Yama finding the child regaining life fed the Brahmana and arranged for the recitation of the Vedas as well as the Puraqas. Saturn was filled with shame and stood there motionless. At the sight of Saturn, Parvati was enraged and she pronounced a curse on him saying, "You become deformed."

## \|\| 12.45 \|\|

*brahmā tānbodhayāmāsa viṣṇunā preritaḥ suraiḥ \|*  
*raktāsyāṃ pārvatīṃ caiva kopaprasphuritādharām \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Finding Saturn so cursed by Parvati, Surya, Kashyapa and Yama felt annoyed and they resolved to leave the abode of Shiva at once.

## \|\| 12.46 \|\|

*brahmāṇamūcuste tatra krameṇa samayocitam \|*  
*bhīravo devatāḥ sarve munayaḥ parvatāstathā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Their eyes and faces became red in anger and the lips started fluttering. They intended to pronounce a curse on Parvati and Vishnu, reciting Dharma as witness.

## \|\| 12.47 \|\|

*kaśyapa uvāca \|*  
*durdṛṣṭo'yaṃ prāktanena patnīśāpena sarvadā \|*  
*bālaṃ dadarśa yatnena tasya vai māturājñayā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Thereafter Brahma at the instance of Vishnu and other gods tried to convince Surya and other gods, whose faces had turned red and their lips were fluttering.

## \|\| 12.48 \|\|

*śrīsūryya uvāca \|*  
*taṃ dharmaṃ sākṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā sūnorvai māturājñayā \|*  
*yatputro'tiprayatnena hyapaśyatpārvatīsutam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. All of them then spoke appropriate words to Brahma that all the gods, the sages and the mountains are timid.

## \|\| 12.49 \|\|

*yathā niraparādhena matputraṃ sā śaśāpa ha \|*  
*tatputrasyāṃgabhaṃgaśca bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Kashyapa said – This Saturn has already achieved evil vision because of the curse from his wife but he cast a glance on the child at the instance of his mother.

## \|\| 12.50-51 \|\|

*yama uvāca \|*  
*pradāya svayamājñāṃ ca śaśāpeyaṃ svayaṃ katham \|*  
*vayaṃ śapāmaḥ ko'dharmo jighāṃsośca vihiṃsane \|\| 50 \|\|*

*brahmovāca \|*  
*śaśāpa pārvatī ruṣṭā strīsvabhāvācca cāpalāt \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ vacanenaiva kṣantumarhantu sādhavaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. Surya said – He looked at the child making Dharma as witness and with the permission of his mother. But still they have cursed my son inspite of his being innocent; therefore their son also will be deprived of a limb. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 12.52 \|\|

*durge dattvā tvamājñāṃ ca putradarśanahetave \|*  
*kathaṃ śapasi nirdoṣamatithiṃ tvadgṛhāgatam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Yama said – When she herself permitted Saturn to look at the child then why did she pronounce a curse? In such a situation if he also pronounced a curse then what would be wrong in it? Because there is nothing wrong in killing the killer.

## \|\| 12.53 \|\|

*ityuktvā śanimādāya bodhayitvā ca pārvatīm \|*  
*tāṃ taṃ samarpaṇaṃ cakre śāpa mocanahetave \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Brahma said – Parvati pronounced a curse getting irritated because of the female nature but the noble people are always merciful. Therefore all of you should forgive because of the insistence of all.

## \|\| 12.54 \|\|

*babhūva pārvatī tuṣṭā brahmaṇo vacanānmune \|*  
*śāntā babhūbuste tatra dineśayamakaśyapāḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Brahma spoke to Durga, "O Durga, you had asked Saturn to have a look at the child then why are you pronouncing a curse on an innocent invitee?"

## \|\| 12.55-56 \|\|

*uvāca pārvatī tatra santuṣṭā taṃ śanaiścaram \|*  
*prasāditā śivenaiva brahmaṇā parisevitā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*graharājo bhava śane madvareṇa haripriyaḥ \|*  
*cirajīvī ca yogīndro haribhaktasya kā vipat \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. Parvati said – О Saturn, with my blessing you will function as the lord of planets, beloved of the lord, have a long life and become Indra among the yogis. The devotees of the lord Hari do not have to face any miseries from today onwards from you. You will have uninterrupted and deep devotion in the lord, my curse cannot be infructuous and therefore you will remain lame throughout your life.

## \|\| 12.57 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti nirvighnā harau bhaktirdṛḍhā'stu te \|*  
*śāpo'moghastato me'dya kiñcitkhañjo bhaviṣyasi \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Parvati feeling pleased, uttered these words and blessings to Saturn took the child in her lap and was seated among the ladies.

## \|\| 12.58 \|\|

*ityuktvā pārvatī tuṣṭā bālaṃ dhṛtvā ca vakṣasi \|*  
*uvāsa yoṣitāṃ madhye tasmai dattvā śubhāśiṣaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. O Brahman, Saturn also felt delighted and bowed before Parvati in reverence with devotion and went to the gods.

## Chapter 13

## \|\| 13.1-2 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*atha viṣṇuḥ śubhe kāle devaiśca munibhiḥ saha \|*  
*pūjayāmāsa taṃ bālamupahārairanuttamaiḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sarvāgre tava pūjā ca mayā dattā surottama \|*  
*sarvapūjyaśca yogīndro bhava vatsetyuvāca tam \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narayana said – Lord Vishnu together with the other gods and the sages adored the child in an auspicious time and said, "O best of gods, I have adored you first of all, therefore, О son, you will be adorable by all and will attain the position of the lord of the yogis."

## \|\| 13.3 \|\|

*vanamālāṃ dadau tasmai brahmajñānaṃ ca muktidam \|*  
*sarvasiddhiṃ pradāyaiva cakārātmasamaṃ hariḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The lord also bestowed on him a garland of forest flowers, the divine knowledge which results in salvation besides all the siddhis, declaring him to be equal to himself.

## \|\| 13.4-6 \|\|

*dadau dravyāṇi cārūṇi copacārāṃśca ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*nāmabhiḥ stavanaṃ cakre munibhiśca samaṃ suraiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*vighneśaśca gaṇeśaśca herambaśca gajānanaḥ \|*  
*lambodaraścaikadantaḥ śūrpakarṇo vināyakaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*etānyaṣṭau ca nāmāni sarvasiddhipradāni ca \|*  
*āśiṣaṃ dāpayāmāsa cānayāmāsa tānmunīn \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. He offered beautiful materials and the sixteen types of offerings. Thereafter, he along with the gods and the sages started eulogising him. He said, "O remover of obstachies, Ganesha, Heramba, elephant faced, Lambodara, Ekdanta, Surpakama and Vinayaka; there are your eight names which bestows success. He then summoned the sages and asked them to shower their blessing on him."

## \|\| 13.7 \|\|

*siddhāsanaṃ dadau dharmastasmai brahmā kamaṇḍalum \|*  
*śaṅkaro yogapaṭṭaṃ ca tattvajñānaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Dharma offered him a seat for success. Brahma gave him kamandalu, Shiva gave him the divine garments besides the divine knowledge of tattva which is difficult to get.

## \|\| 13.8-10 \|\|

*ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ śakraḥ sūryyaśca maṇikuṇḍale \|*  
*māṇikyamālāṃ candraśca kuberaśca kirīṭakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*vahniśuddhaṃ ca vasanaṃ dadau tasmai hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*ratnacchatraṃ ca varuṇo vāyū ratnāṃgulīyakam \|\| 9 \|\|*

*kṣīrododbhavasadratnaracitaṃ valayaṃ varam \|*  
*mañjīraṃ cāpi keyūraṃ dadau padmālayā mune \|\| 10 \|\|*

8-10. Indra gave him the gem-studded lion-throne, the sun gave him the ear-ornaments of gems, Chandrama gave him the garland of pearls, Kubera gave him the kirlta crown, Agni gave him the garments purified by fire; Varuna gave him the umbrella of jewels, the wind god gave him gem-studded finger-rings. О sage, Lakshmi gave him the anklets and armlets which were produced from the ocean of milk.

## \|\| 13.11 \|\|

*kaṇṭhabhūṣāṃ ca sāvitrī bhāratī hāramujjvalam \|*  
*krameṇa sarvadevāśca devyaśca yautakaṃ daduḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Savitri gave him a necklace, Bharati gave him the illumining garland. Thereafter, all the gods and goddesses gave him several presents.

## \|\| 13.12 \|\|

*munayaḥ parvatāścaiva ratnāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*vasundharā dadau tasmai vāhanāya ca mūṣakam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Parvati and other sages gave him several types of gems and the goddess earth gave him the rat to serve as his vehicle.

## \|\| 13.13-14 \|\|

*krameṇa devā devyaśca munayaḥ parvatādayaḥ \|*  
*gandharvāḥ kinnarā yakṣā manavo mānavāstathā \|\| 13 \|\|*

*nānāvidhāni dravyāṇi svādūni madhurāṇi ca \|*  
*pūjāṃ cakruśca te sarve kramādvai bhaktipūrvakam \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. All the gods, the goddesses, sages, mountains, Gandharvas, Kinnaras, Yaksas, Manus and humans gave him several types of delicious and sweet presents with devotion.

## \|\| 13.15 \|\|

*pārvatī jagatāṃ mātā smerānanasaroruhā \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsane putraṃ vāsayāmāsa nārada \|\| 15\|\|*

15\. О Narada, wearing a serene smile on her lotuslike face, Parvati the mother of the universe placed her son on the gem-studded lion-throne.

## \|\| 13.16-19 \|\|

*sarvatīrthodakai ratnakalaśāvarjitaiḥ stutaiḥ \|*  
*snāpayāmāsa vedoktamantreṇa munibhistadā \|*  
*agniśuddhe ca vasane dadau tasmai satī mudā \|\| 16 \|\|*

*godāvaryudakaiḥ pādyamarghyaṃ gaṃgodakena ca \|*  
*dūrvābhirakṣatāpuṣpaiścandanena samanvitam \|\| 17 \|\|*

*puṣkarodakamānīya punarācamanīyakam \|*  
*madhuparkaṃ ratnapātrairāsavaṃ śarkarānvitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*snānīyaṃ viṣṇutailaṃ ca svarvaidyābhyāṃ vinirmitam \|*  
*amūlyaratnaracitaṃ cārubhūṣākadambakam \|\| 19 \|\|*

16-19. Thereafter all the sages bathed the child Ganesha with the sacred water of all the sacred places, filled in the pitcher of gems and reciting hymns from the Vedas. Sati offered him the two garments sanctified by fire. Thereafter padya was offered of the water of Godavari and the earth from the water of Ganga besides Diirva-grass, rice, flower and sandal-paste were also offered. Sending for the sanctified water from Puskara, he was given acamana and Madhuparka besides other beverages mixed with sugar were offered in the vases of gems. Asvinlkumara on his part prepared Vishnu oil for the purpose of his bathing.

## \|\| 13.20-27 \|\|

*pārijātaprasūnānāmanyeṣāṃ śatakāni ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

*mālatīcampakādīnāṃ puṣpāṇi vividhāni ca \|*  
*pūjārhāṇi ca patrāṇi tulasīsahitāni ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

*candanāgurukastūrī kuṃkumāni ca sādaram \|*  
*ratnapradīpanikaraṃ dhūpaṃ ca parito dadau \|\| 22 \|\|*

*naivedyaṃ tatpriyaṃ caiva tilalaḍḍukaparvatān \|*  
*yavagodhūmacūrṇānāṃ laḍḍukānāṃ ca parvatān \|\| 23 \|\|*

*pakvānnānāṃ parvatāśca susvādusumanoharān \|*  
*parvatānsvastikānāṃ ca susvāduśarkarānvitān \|\| 24 \|\|*

*guḍāktānāṃ ca lājānāṃ pṛthukānāṃ ca parvatān \|*  
*śālyannānāṃ piṣṭakānāṃ parvatānvyañjanaiḥ saha \|\| 25 \|\|*

*payobhṛtkalaśānāṃ ca lakṣāṇi pradadau mudā \|*  
*lakṣāṇi dadhipūrṇānāṃ kalaśānāṃ ca pūjane \|\| 26 \|\|*

*madhubhṛtkalaśānāṃ ca trilakṣāṇi ca sundarī \|*  
*sarpissuvarṇakumbhānāṃ paṃcalakṣāṇi sādaram \|\| 27 \|\|*

20-27. Besides other ornaments studded with gems, a hundred flowers of Parijata, Malati,1 Campaka2 and other flowers for worship besides Tulasi3 leaves, sandal-paste, aguru, kasturi and vermilion were offered to him. Several of the ornaments made of jewels besides the essence of gems were offered by all. His loving naivedya and heaps of the sea-same balls and balls of wheat, flour in heaps, delicious and pleasant fried cereals in heaps and the delicious svastika sweet in heaps, the fried paddy mixed with guda in heaps, the heaps of fried paddy, the paddy cereals an the ground stuffs in heaps and a lakh of pitchers filled with milk, a lakh of pitchers filled with curd and three lakhs of pitchers filled with sweets were offered to him. In addition to this, five lakh golden pitchers filled with ghee were offered to him with reverence.

## \|\| 13.28-31 \|\|

*dāḍimānāṃ śrīphalānāmasaṃkhyāni phalāni ca \|*  
*kharjūrāṇāṃ kapitthānāṃ jambūnāṃ vividhāni ca \|\| 28 \|\|*

*āmrāṇāṃ panasānāṃ ca kadalīnāṃ ca nārada \|*  
*phalāni nārikelānāmasaṃkhyāni dadau mudā \|\| 29 \|\|*

*anyāni paripakvāni kāladeśodbhavāni ca \|*  
*dadau tāni mahābhāga svādūni madhurāṇi ca \|\| 30 \|\|*

*svacchaṃ sunirmalaṃ caiva karpūrādisuvāsitam \|*  
*gaṅgājalaṃ ca pānārthaṃ punarācamanīyakam \|\| 31 \|\|*

28-31. The pomegranate, wood-apple and several other fruits including palm, rose-apple, mango, jack-fruit, banana and coconut were given in large numbers. О Narada, several other fhiits were offered which were available according to the time and season and were quite delicious and sweet. All these were offered with delight. For the purpose of sipping, the sacred water of Ganga.

## \|\| 13.32-33 \|\|

*tāmbūlaṃ ca varaṃ ramyaṃ karpūrādisuvāsitam \|*  
*suvarṇapātraśatakaṃ bhakṣyapūrṇaṃ ca nārada \|\| 32 \|\|*

*śailarājapriyā mātyāḥ pupūjuḥ śailajātmajam \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Mixed with camphor was also given. О Narada, the betels containing the fragrance of camphor and a hundred golden vases filled with delicious food were offered by Himalaya and his wife, his son and the courtiers to the goddess Parvati and her son.

## \|\| 13.34-36 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ gaṇeśvarāya brahmarūpāya cārave \|*  
*sarvasiddhipradeśāya viśveśāya namo namaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*ityanenaiva mantreṇa dattvā dravyāṇi bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*sarve pramuditāstatra brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*dvātriṃśadakṣaro mālāmantro'yaṃ sarvakāmada \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ phaladaḥ sarvasiddhidaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

34-36. … by reciting this mantra, delightfully Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva made their offerings with devotion to Ganesha. This garland mantra of thirty two letters fulfils all the desires bestowing dharma, artha, kama and moksa besides all the success.

## \|\| 13.37 \|\|

*pañcalakṣajapenaiva mantrasiddhistu mantriṇaḥ \|*  
*mantrasiddhirbhavedyasya sa ca viṣṇuśca bhārate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. By reciting this mantra five lakhs of times, one meets with success and whosoever meets with such a success, is equated with Vishnu in the land of Bharata.

## \|\| 13.38 \|\|

*vighnāni ca palāyante tannāmasmaraṇena ca \|*  
*mahāvāggmī mahāsiddhiḥ sarvasiddhisamanvitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. By the mere reciting of his name, the obstructions disappear and he himself becomes a great orator, meets with great success and becomes Mahasiddha.

## \|\| 13.39 \|\|

*vākpatirgurutāṃ yāti tasya sākṣātsuniścitam \|*  
*mahākavīndro guṇavānviduṣāṃ ca gurorguruḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He surely becomes equivalent to Brihaspati, best of the poets, the teacher of the learned people and the teacher of teachers.

## \|\| 13.40 \|\|

*saṃpūjyānena mantreṇa devā ānandasaṃplutāḥ \|*  
*nānāvidhāni vādyāni vādayāmāsurutsave \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The gods adoring Ganesha with the reciting of the mantra were filled with ecstasy and played on several musical instruments.

## \|\| 13.41 \|\|

*brāhmaṇānbhojayāmāsuḥ kārayāmāsurutsavam \|*  
*dadurdānāni tebhyaśca bandibhyaśca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. They distributed food to the Brahmanas, enjoyed festivities and also distributed charity to the Brahmanas and the bards.

## \|\| 13.42 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*atha viṣṇuḥ sabhāmadhye taṃ sampūjya gaṇeśvaram \|*  
*tuṣṭāva parayā bhaktyā sarvavighnavināśakam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Narayana said – Thereafter lord Vishnu adored Ganesha the lord of ganas in the presence of all the courtiers and started eulogising him.

## \|\| 13.43 \|\|

*īśa tvāṃ stotumicchāmi brahmajyotiḥ sanātanam \|*  
*naiva varṇayituṃ śakto'styanurūpamanīhakam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Vishnu said – О lord, I intend to offer prayers to you. You happen to be the eternal flame and eternal also. Therefore I cannot spell out from glory because you are beyond desire.

## \|\| 13.44-46 \|\|

*pravaraṃ sarvadevānāṃ siddhānāṃ yogināṃ gurum \|*  
*sarvasvarūpaṃ sarveśaṃ jñānarāśisvarūpiṇam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*avyaktamakṣaraṃ nityaṃ satyamātmasvarūpiṇam \|*  
*vāyutulyaṃ ca nirliptaṃ cākṣataṃ sarvasākṣiṇam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*saṃsārārṇavapāre ca māyāpote sudurlabhe \|*  
*karṇadhārasvarūpaṃ ca bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 46 \|\|*

44-46. You are the best of all the gods, teacher of all the siddhas and yogis, the form of everything, the lord of everything, the ocean of knowledge, invisible, indestructible, eternal, truthful, form of self, unattached like the wind, the witness for all and the ship for crossing the ocean of the universe. Being a divine sailor, you bestow your compassion on your devotees.

## \|\| 13.47-51 \|\|

*varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ varadānāmapīśvaram \|*  
*siddhaṃ siddhisvarūpaṃ ca siddhidaṃ siddhisādhanama \|\| 47\|\|*

*dhyānātiriktaṃ dhyeyaṃ ca dhyānāsādhyaṃ ca dhārmikam \|*  
*dharmasvarūpaṃ dharmajñaṃ dharmādharmaphalapradam \|\| 48 \|\|*

*bījaṃ saṃsāravṛkṣāṇāmaṃkuraṃ ca tadāśrayam \|*  
*strīpuṃnapuṃsakānāṃ ca rūpametadatīndriyam \|\| 49 \|\|*

*sarvādyamagrapūjyaṃ ca sarvapūjyaṃ guṇārṇavam \|*  
*svecchayā saguṇaṃ brahma nirguṇaṃ svecchayā punaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*svayaṃ prakṛtirūpaṃ ca prākṛtaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*tvāṃ stotumakṣamo'nantaḥ sahasravadanairapi \|\| 51 \|\|*

47-51. You are the best of all, bestower of the boons, the lord of boons and siddha besides the form of success, bestower of success, source of success, beyond comprehension, adorable, difficult for adoration, quite religious, form of dharma, well-versed in dharma, the one who bestows the reward of dharma and adharma, the seed of the tree of the universe, its branches and the sprouting of it, the women, the men and impotent, beyond the organ of the senses, the foremost of all, the foremost for adorable by all, virtuous, complete, BrShmana visible at will and Brahmana invisible at will. You yourself are the form of Prakpti, you are Prakfti and beyond Prakriti. Therefore even Ananta is unable to recite your glory with a thousand of his mouths.

## \|\| 13.52-53 \|\|

*na kṣamaḥ pañcavaktraśca na kṣamaścaturānanaḥ \|*  
*sarasvatī na śaktā ca na śakto'haṃ tava stutau \|\| 52 \|\|*

*na śaktāśca caturvedāḥ ke vā te vedavādinaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. Similarly Shiva with five faces, Brahma with four faces, Sarasvati and myself cannot recite your glory. Even the four Vedas are unable to do so, what to speak of those well-versed in the Vedas.

## \|\| 13.54 \|\|

*ityevaṃ stavanaṃ kṛtvā munīśasurasaṃsadi \|*  
*sureśaśca suraiḥ sārddhaṃ virarāma ramāpatiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Thus the lord of the gods, Vishnu the husband of Rama, after offering prayers to Ganesha with the gods and the sages kept quiet.

## \|\| 13.55-57 \|\|

*idaṃ viṣṇukṛtaṃ stotraṃ gaṇeśasya ca yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*sāyaṃ prātaśca madhyāhne bhaktiyuktaḥ samāhitaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

*tadvighnanāśaṃ kurute vighneśaḥ satataṃ mune \|*  
*varddhate sarvakalyāṇaṃ kalyāṇajanakaḥ sadā \|\| 56 \|\|*

*yātrākāle paṭhitvā yo yāti tadbhaktipūrvakam \|*  
*tasya sarvābhīṣṭasiddhirbhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

55-57. O sage, whosoever recites the stotra of Ganesha composed by Vishnu in the morning, noon and evening, all his obstructions vanish at the instance of lord Ganesha. There is an all-round increase in the welfare of such a person and he always meets with prosperity. The one who recites the stotra with devotion during the journey, always meets with success. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 13.58 \|\|

*tena dṛṣṭaṃ ca dussvapnaṃ susvapnamupajāyate \|*  
*kadā'pi na bhavettasya grahapīḍā ca dāruṇā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The bad dreams witnessed by him turn into good dreams and he never suffers from pain in the household.

## \|\| 13.59 \|\|

*bhavedvināśaḥ śatrūṇāṃ bandhūnāṃ cāpi varddhanam \|*  
*śaśvadvighnavināśaśca śaśvatsamyagvivarddhanam \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The enemies are destroyed. There is always an increase in the good relations, the obstructions are always destroyed and there is an increase in wealth.

## \|\| 13.60 \|\|

*sthirā bhavedgṛhe lakṣmīḥ putrapautravivarddhanam \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyamiha prāpya hyante viṣṇupadaṃ labhet \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Fortune always prevails in the house and there is an increase in the sons and grandsons. Such a person enjoying all the pleasures in this world proceeds on to the abode of Vishnu.

## \|\| 13.61 \|\|

*phalaṃ cāpi ca tīrthānāṃ yajñānāṃ yadbhaveddhruvam \|*  
*mahatāṃ sarvadānānāṃ tadgaṇeśaprasādata \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. With the grace of Ganesha he achieves the merit of visiting all the sacred places, performing of the yajhas and all the charities undoubtedly.

## \|\| 13.62 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*śrutaṃ stotraṃ gaṇeśasya pūjanaṃ ca manoharam \|*  
*kavacaṃ śrotumicchāmi sāmprataṃ bhavatāraṇam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Narada said – We have listened to the stotra of Ganesha and the method of his adoration. I would now like to listen to the kavacha of Ganesha which redeems everyone from the ocean of the universe.

## \|\| 13.63 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*pūjāyāṃ sunivṛttāyāṃ sabhāmadhye śanaiścaraḥ \|*  
*uvāca viṣṇuṃ sarveṣāṃ tārakaṃ jagatāṃ gurum \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Narayana said – After the completion of the worship, Saturn spoke to Vishnu, the teacher of the universe.

## \|\| 13.64 \|\|

*śanaiścara uvāca \|*  
*sarvaduḥkhavināśāya pāpapraśamanāya ca \|*  
*kavacaṃ vighnanighnasya vada vedavidāṃvara \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Saturn said – О best of those well-versed in the Vedas, you kindly enlighten us about the kavacha of Ganesha which removes all the obstructions, removes pain and destroys sins.

## \|\| 13.65 \|\|

*babhūva no vivādaśca śaktyā vai māyayā saha \|*  
*tadvighnapraśamārthaṃ ca kavacaṃ dhārayāmyaham \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. I have already developed a controversy with the great goddess, therefore I intend to hold the kavacha of Ganesha for the removal of obstructions.

## \|\| 13.66 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*vināyakasya kavacaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|*  
*sugopyaṃ ca purāṇeṣu durlabhaṃ cāgameṣu ca \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Lord Vishnu said – The kavacha of the Vinayaka is difficult to get in the three worlds. It is quite secret in the Puranas and is difficult to get in the scriptures.

## \|\| 13.67 \|\|

*uktaṃ kauthumaśākhāyāṃ sāmavede manoharam \|*  
*kavacaṃ vighnanāthasya sarvavighnaharaṃ param \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The kavacha of Ganesha which removes all the obstructions and is considered to be the best has been beautifully described in the kauthuml-shakha in the Samaveda.

## \|\| 13.68 \|\|

*rājyaṃ deyaṃ śiro deyaṃ prāṇā deyāśca sūryyaja \|*  
*evambhūtaṃ ca kavacaṃ na deyaṃ prāṇasaṅkaṭe \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. О son of Surya, the entire kingdom can be given away, the head can be given away and even the life can be given away hut the knowledge of such a kavacha cannot be imparted to anyone even when one's life is in danger.

## \|\| 13.69 \|\|

*āvirbhāvastirobhāvaḥ svecchayā yasya māyayā \|*  
*nityo'yamekadantaśca kavacaṃ cāsya vatsaka \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. О so, with the influence of whose illusion, one is relieved of everything, such a Ganesha having only one tusk is eternal and this kavacha belongs to him.

## \|\| 13.70 \|\|

*pūjā'sya nityā stotraṃ ca kalpe kalpe'sti santatam \|*  
*asya vai janmanaḥ pūrvaṃ munayaśca siṣevire \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. It is adored and eulogised always in such kalpa and even the sages adore it even before their birth.

## \|\| 13.71 \|\|

*yathā madavatāreṣu janmavigrahadhāraṇam \|*  
*tathā gaṇeśvarasyāpi janma śailasutodare \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. As I take to human form in every incarnation, similarly Ganesha is born from the womb of Parvati.

## \|\| 13.72-73 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā munayaḥ sarve jīvanmuktāśca bhārate \|*  
*niśśaṅkāśca surāḥ sarve śatrupakṣavimardakāḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

*kavacaṃ bibhratāṃ mṛtyurna bhiyā yāti sannidhim \|*  
*nāyurvyayo nāśubhaṃ ca brahmāṇḍe na parājayaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. In the land of Bharata, the sages are freed from life after holding this kavacha and the gods fearlessly destroy their enemies. Those who wear this kavacha, the death does not approach them feeling panicky. His life is not spent uselessly and he does not meet with any misfortune or defeat in the universe.

## \|\| 13.74-75 \|\|

*daśa lakṣajapenaiva siddhaṃ tu kavacaṃ bhavet \|*  
*yo bhavetsiddhakavaco mṛtyuṃ jetuṃ sa ca kṣamaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

*susiddhakavaco vāgmī ciraṃjīvī mahītale \|*  
*sarvatra vijayī pūjyo bhavedgrahaṇamātrataḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. By reciting this kavacha ten lakhs of times, one meets with success and whosoever gets success in the kavacha, becomes competent even to overpower death. With the achieving of the success with this kavacha, a person becomes truthful, enjoys a long life and becomes victorious on earth. He gets adorable with the earring of kavacha.

## \|\| 13.76 \|\|

*mālāmantramimaṃ puṇyaṃ kavacaṃ maṅgalaṃ śubham \|*  
*bibhratāṃ sarvapāpāni praṇaśyanti suniścitam \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. With the reciting of miila-mantra and with the holding of the auspicious kavacha all the sins of a person vanish.

## \|\| 13.77-78 \|\|

*bhūtapretapiśācāśca kūṣmāṇḍā brahmarākṣasāḥ \|*  
*ḍākinīyoginīyakṣavetālā bhairavādayaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

*bālagrahā grahāścaiva kṣetrapālādayastathā \|*  
*varmaṇaḥ śabdamātreṇa palāyante ca bhīravaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

77-78. The goblins, pretas, Pisacas, Kiismandas, Brahmaraksas, Dakinis, Yoginls, Yaksa, Vetalas, Bhairavas, Balagraha, the planet, Ksetra-palas and all other evil spirits run away in panic.

## \|\| 13.79 \|\|

*ādhayo vyādhayaścaiva śokāścaiva bhayāvahāḥ \|*  
*na yānti sannidhiṃ teṣāṃ garuḍasya yathoragāḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. As thus snakes cannot approach Garuda, similarly fears, grief and pain do not approach such a person.

## \|\| 13.80 \|\|

*ṛjave gurubhaktāya svaśiṣyāya prakāśayet \|*  
*khalāya paraśiṣyāya dattvā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. Therefore the knowledge of this kavacha should be imparted to a devoted pupil by the teacher but should not be imparted to a wicked pupil because, by doing so, one attracts death.

## \|\| 13.81-82 \|\|

*saṃsāramohakasyāsya kavacasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca bṛhatī devo lambodaraḥ svayam \|\| 81 \|\|*

*dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ kavacānāṃ ca sārabhūtamidaṃ mune \|\| 82 \|\|*

81-82. This is a kavacha which attracts the entire universe, has Prajapati as the Rsi, Brihatl as chanda (metre), Lambodara as the god and it

## \|\| 13.83 \|\|

*oṃ gaṃ huṃ śrīgaṇeśāya svāhā me pātu mastakam \|*  
*dvātriṃśadakṣaro mantro lalāṭaṃ me sadā'vatu \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. No translation

## \|\| 13.84 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ śrīṃ gamiti vai satataṃ pātu locanam \|*  
*tāluke pātu vighneśaḥ satataṃ dharaṇītale \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. No translation

## \|\| 13.85-89 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīmiti paraṃ santataṃ pātu nāsikām \|*  
*oṃ gauṃ gaṃ śūrpakarṇāya svāhā pātvadharaṃ mama \|\| 85 \|\|*

*dantāṃśca tālukāṃ jihvāṃ pātu me ṣoḍaśākṣaraḥ \|*  
*oṃ laṃ śrīṃ lambodarāyeti svāhā gaṇḍaṃ sadā 'vatu \|\| 86 \|\|*

*oṃ klīṃ hrīṃ vighnanāśāya svāhā karṇaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ gaṃ gajānanāyeti svāhā skandhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 87 \|\|*

*oṃ hrīṃ vināyakāyeti svāhā pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ klīṃ hrīmiti kaṅkālaṃ pātu vakṣaḥsthalaṃ param \|\| 88 \|\|*

*karau pādau sadā pātu sarvāṃgaṃ vighnanāśakṛt \|*  
*prācyāṃ lambodaraḥ pātu cāgneyyāṃ vighnanāyakaḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

85-89. No translation

## \|\| 13.86 \|\|

*dantāṃśca tālukāṃ jihvāṃ pātu me ṣoḍaśākṣaraḥ \|*  
*oṃ laṃ śrīṃ lambodarāyeti svāhā gaṇḍaṃ sadā 'vatu \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. this mantra should protect my cheeks.

## \|\| 13.87 \|\|

*oṃ klīṃ hrīṃ vighnanāśāya svāhā karṇaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ gaṃ gajānanāyeti svāhā skandhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. this mantra should protect my ears, this mantra should protect my shoulders, this mantra should protect my back, this mantra should protect my chest

## \|\| 13.88-92 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ vināyakāyeti svāhā pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ klīṃ hrīmiti kaṅkālaṃ pātu vakṣaḥsthalaṃ param \|\| 88 \|\|*

*karau pādau sadā pātu sarvāṃgaṃ vighnanāśakṛt \|*  
*prācyāṃ lambodaraḥ pātu cāgneyyāṃ vighnanāyakaḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

*dakṣiṇe pātu viśveśo nairṛtyāṃ tu gajānanaḥ paścime \|*  
*pārvatīputro vāyavyāṃ śaṃkarātmajaḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇasyāṃśaścottare ca paripūrṇatamasya ca \|*  
*aiśānyāmekadantaśca herambaḥ pātu cordhvataḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

*adho gaṇādhipaḥ pātu sarvapūjyaśca sarvataḥ \|*  
*svapne jāgaraṇe caiva pātu māṃ yogināṃ guruḥ \|\| 92 \|\|*

88-92. The mantra which removes the obstructions should protect my hands, feet, aid all the limbs always. Lambodara should protect the eastern directions, Vighnanayaka should protect the south-east, Vighnesa should protect the southeast and should be protected by Gajanana, the western direction should be protected by the son of Parvati, the north-western direction should be protected by the complete ray of Krishna. The north-east direction should be protected by Ekdanta, the upper regions should be protected by Heramba and the lower direction should be protected by the leader of ganas. All the directions should be protected by one adorable by all and the position of sleeping or remaining awake should be protected by the teacher of the yogis.

## \|\| 13.93 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvamantraughavigraham \|*  
*saṃsāramohanaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. О son, this is quite an astonishing kavacha which attracts the universe and I have told the same to you. This is the personification of all the kavachas.

## \|\| 13.94-95 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇena purā dattaṃ goloke rāsamaṇḍale \|*  
*vṛndāvane vinītāya mahyaṃ dinakarātmaja \|\| 94 \|\|*

*mayā dattaṃ ca tubhyaṃ ca yasmai kasmai na dāsyasi \|*  
*paraṃ varaṃ sarvapūjyaṃ sarvasaṃkaṭatāraṇam \|\| 95 \|\|*

94-95. О son of Surya, in the earlier times, in the Goloka of lord Krishna and the Msamandala in the Vridavana, this kavacha was given to a humble person like me, the knowledge of which I have imparted to you today. Therefore don’t part with the same to anyone. This is the best of all, adorable by all and protects one from all the miseries.

## \|\| 13.96 \|\|

*gurumabhyarcya vidhivatkavacaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|*  
*kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe bāhau so'pi viṣṇurna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. After adoring the teacher in a proper manner, whosoever ties this kavacha on the right arm, becomes like Vishnu, there is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 13.97 \|\|

*aśvamedhasahasrāṇi vājapeyaśatāni ca \|*  
*grahendra kavacasyāsya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. О lord of the planets, the performing of a thousand Asvamedha sacrifices and a hundred Vajpeya-yajnas cannot be compared with even the sixteenth ray of this kavacha.

## \|\| 13.98 \|\|

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā yo bhajecchaṃkarātmajam \|*  
*śatalakṣaprajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. Whosoever adores Ganesha without knowing about this kavacha, cannot achieve success even after reciting the same for a hundred lakh of time.

## \|\| 13.99 \|\|

*iti saṃsāramohanaṃ nāma kavacam \|*  
*dattvedaṃ sūryyaputrāya virarāma sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*paramānandasaṃyuktā devāstasthuḥ samīpataḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. The lord of the universe after imparting the knowledge of kavacha to Saturn, the son of Surya, the knowledge of this kavacha which could influence the entire universe, kept quiet and the gods felt delighted.

## Chapter 14

## \|\| 14.1-2 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*devāstasyāṃ sabhāyāṃ ca sarve saṃhṛṣṭamānasāḥ \|*  
*gandharvā munayaḥ śailāḥ paśyantaḥ sumahotsavam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*etasminnantare durgā smerānanasaroruhā \|*  
*uvāca viṣṇuṃ praṇatā deveśaṃ tatra saṃsadi \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narayana said – All the courtiers, the gods, the Gandharvas, the sages and the mountains, were watching the festivities gleefully. In the meantime the lotus-faced Durga wearing a serene smile on her face addressing lord Vishnu, spoke to him quite sweet and humble words.

## \|\| 14.3 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*tvaṃ pātā sarvajagatāṃ nātha nāhaṃ jagadbahiḥ \|*  
*kathaṃ matsvāmino vīryyamamoghaṃ rakṣitaṃ prabho \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Parvati said – О lord, you are the protector of the universe and I am not beyond it. Therefore, О lord, you kindly tell me the place were the semen of my lord has been preserved.

## \|\| 14.4-5 \|\|

*ratibhaṅge kṛte devairbrahmaṇā preritaistvayā \|*  
*bhūmau nipatitaṃ vīryaṃ kena devena vai hṛtam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*sarve devāstvatpuratastadanviṣyantu sādaram \|*  
*arājakaṃ kathamidaṃ tiṣṭhati tvayi rājani \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. At your instance, the gods and Brahma disturbed our love-sport, as a result of which the semen fell on the ground. I am not aware as to which of the gods took it. All the gods should search for it in your presence because this type of disturbance is not permissible in your kingdom.

## \|\| 14.6 \|\|

*pārvatīvacanaṃ śrutvā prahasya jagadīśvaraḥ \|*  
*uvāca devavarge ca munivarge ca tiṣṭhati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On hearing the words of Parvati, the lord of the universe, thought for a moment and , addressing the gods, spoke to them.

## \|\| 14.7-8 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*devāḥ śṛṇuta madvākyaṃ pārvatīvacanaṃ śrutam \|*  
*śivasyāmoghavīryyaṃ yattatpurā kena nirhṛtam \|\| 7 \|\|*

*sabhāmānayata kṣipraṃ na ceddaṇḍamihārhatha \|*  
*sa ko rājā na śāstā yaḥ prajābādhyaśca pākṣikaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. Vishnu said. – О gods, you listen to my words. You have to listen to the words of Parvati. Who has usurped the semen of Shiva which cannot be rendered infructuous? The semen has to be presented in this court at once, otherwise, you will become entitled to the punishment because the one who does not rule properly the people are troubled or acts one sided, such a king is to be denounced.

## \|\| 14.9 \|\|

*viṣṇostadvacanaṃ śrutvā samālocya parasparam \|*  
*ūcuḥ sarve śivāvākyaistrāsitāḥ purato hareḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О hearing the words of Vishnu, all the gods consulted among themselves and getting terrified with the words of Parvati, started speaking before lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 14.10 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*tadvīryaṃ nirhṛtaṃ yena puṇyabhūmau ca bhārate \|*  
*sa vañcito bhavatvatra puṇyāhe puṇyakarmaṇi \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Brahma said – Whosoever has stolen away your semen from the land of Bharata, he will be deprived of the noble merits of the noble days.

## \|\| 14.11 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*madvīryyaṃ nirhṛtaṃ yena puṇyabhūmau ca bhārate \|*  
*sa vañcito bhavatvatra sevane pūjane tava \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Mahadeva said – Whosoever has usurped my semen from the sacred land of Bharata, will be deprived of your adoration.

## \|\| 14.12 \|\|

*yama uvāca \|*  
*sa vañcito bhavatvatra śaraṇāgatarakṣaṇe \|*  
*ekādaśīvrate caiva tadvīryyaṃ yena nirhṛtam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Yama said – The one who has stolen the semen will be deprived of.the merit of providing protection to the one who takes refuge with him and the merit of the vrata of Ekadashi.

## \|\| 14.13 \|\|

*indra uvāca \|*  
*tadvīryaṃ nirhṛtaṃ yena pāpināṃ pāpamocane \|*  
*bhavatvatra yaśo luptaṃ tatpuṇyaṃ karma santatam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Indra said – Whosoever has stolen the semen, he will be unable to remove the sinners from the sins. Besides the glory and merit will also disappear.

## \|\| 14.14 \|\|

*varuṇa uvāca \|*  
*bhavatvatra kalau janma varṣe syādbhārate hare \|*  
*śūdrayājakapatnyāśca garbhe tadyena nirhṛtam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Varuna said – О lord Hari, whosoever has stolen the semen, will be turned as the performer of yajna of a Sildra in the age Kali and shall be born from the womb of a Shudra woman.

## \|\| 14.15 \|\|

*kubera uvāca \|*  
*nyāsahārī sa bhavatu viśvāsaghnaśca mitrahā \|*  
*satyaghnaśca kṛtaghnaśca tadvīryaṃ yena nirhṛtam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Kubera said – The person who has stolen away the semen, will earn the sin of misappropriating the trust, will be known as the betrayer of the faith, the killer of the friend, killer of the truth and an ungrateful person.

## \|\| 14.16 \|\|

*īśāna uvāca \|*  
*paradravyāpahārī ca sa bhavatvatra bhārate \|*  
*naraghātī gurudrohī tadvīryaṃ yena nirhṛtam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Isana said – Whosoever has stolen away the semen, will be treated in the land of Bharata as the one who steals away others' riches, the killer of human beings and the one who betrays his own teacher.

## \|\| 14.17 \|\|

*rudrā ūcuḥ \|*  
*te mithyāvādinaḥ santu bhārate pāradārikāḥ \|*  
*gurunindāratāḥ śaśvattadvīryyaṃ yaiśca nirhṛtam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Rudra said – The one who has stolen the semen will be treated in the land of Bharata as the one who speaks untruth, visiting others’ wives, wicked and the one indulging in the denouncing of the teacher.

## \|\| 14.18 \|\|

*kāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*kṛtvā pratijñāṃ yo mūḍho na sampālayate bhramāt \|*  
*bhājanaṃ tasya pāpasya sa bhavedyena taddhṛtam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Kamadeva said – Whosoever has stolen the semen will be treated like the one who does not fulfil his words and will earn sin.

## \|\| 14.19 \|\|

*svarvaidyāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*mātuḥ piturguroścaiva strīputrāṇāṃ ca poṣaṇe \|*  
*bhavetāṃ vañcitau tau ca yābhyāṃ vīryyaṃ ca taddhṛtam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Asvinikumara said – Those who have stolen the semen will be deprived of maintaining their parents, father, teacher, woman and son.

## \|\| 14.20 \|\|

*sarve devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*mithyāsākṣyapradātāro bhavantvatra ca bhārate \|*  
*aputriṇo daridrāśca yaiśca vīryyaṃ hi taddhṛtam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The gods said – Whosoever has usurped the semen, will earn the sin of giving false evidence and become a pauper.

## \|\| 14.21 \|\|

*devapatnya ūcuḥ \|*  
*tā nindaṃtu svabharttāro gacchantu parapūruṣam \|*  
*santu buddhivihīnāśca yābhirvīryyaṃ hi taddhṛtam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The wives of the gods said – Those laches who have usrnped the semen, will be treated like those women who denouncing their husband, enjoy the company of other men and are deprived of wisdom.

## \|\| 14.22-23 \|\|

*devānāṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā devīnāṃ ca harissvayam \|*  
*karmaṇāṃ sākṣiṇaṃ dharmaṃ sūryyaṃ candraṃ hutāśanam \|\| 22 \|\|*

*pavanaṃ pṛthivīṃ toyaṃ sandhye rātriṃ divaṃ mune \|*  
*uvāca jagatāṃ karttā pātā śāstā jagattraye \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. О sage, on hearing the word of the gods and the goddesses, lord Vishnu the creator of the universe and lord of the three worlds besides being the protector, looked at Dharma who stands witness for all besides, Sflrya, Chandrama, Agni, Vayu, the earth, water, both the Sandhayas besides the day and the night.

## \|\| 14.24-25 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*devairna nirhṛtaṃ vīryyaṃ tadetatkena nirhṛtam \|*  
*tadamoghaṃ bhagavato maheśasya jagadguroḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*yūyaṃ ca sākṣiṇo viśve satataṃ sarvakarmaṇām \|*  
*yuṣmābhirnirhṛta kiṃ vā kimbhūtaṃ vaktumarhatha \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. Vishnu said – In case the semen of lord Shiva had not been stolen by the gods then who else had done so. You happen to be the witness of all the deeds of earth. Therefore all of you have usurped the same or otherwise you tell me what happened to it.

## \|\| 14.26 \|\|

*īśvarasya vacaḥ śrutvā sabhāyāṃ kampitāśca te \|*  
*parasparaṃ samālocya krameṇocuḥ puro hareḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. At that point of time on hearing the words of the lord, all the people started trembling and after mutual consultation they spoke to the lord one by one.

## \|\| 14.27 \|\|

*śrīdharma uvāca \|*  
*rateruttiṣṭhato vīryyaṃ papāta vasudhātale \|*  
*mayā jñātamamoghaṃ tacchaṅkarasya prakopataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Dharma said – At that time of love-sport, when lord Shiva got up, his semen fell on the ground. This much is known to me.

## \|\| 14.28 \|\|

*kṣitiruvāca \|*  
*vīryyaṃ voḍhumaśaktā'haṃ tadvahnau nyakṣipaṃ purā \|*  
*atīva durvahaṃ brahmannabalāṃ kṣantumarhasi \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Ksiti said – О Brahmana, I had been unable to carry the terrific semen myself, therefore I had dropped the same in the fire. You therefore kindly forgive a helpless person like me.

## \|\| 14.29 \|\|

*agniruvāca \|*  
*vīryyaṃ voḍhumaśaktā'haṃ nyakṣipaṃ śarakānane \|*  
*durbalasya jagannātha kiṃ yaśaḥ kiṃ ca pauruṣam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Agni said – О lord of the universe I had been unable to carry the semen with me and I dropped it in the forest of canes because a weak person has no glory or strength.

## \|\| 14.30 \|\|

*vāyuruvāca \|*  
*śareṣu patitaṃ vīryyaṃ sadyo bālo babhūva ha \|*  
*atīva sundaro viṣṇo svarṇarekhānadītaṭe \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Vayu said – О Vishnu, the semen which fell in the forest of canes immediately turned into the form of a child who was extremely beautiful and went to the bank of the river Svarnarekha.

## \|\| 14.31 \|\|

*śrīsūryya uvāca \|*  
*rudantaṃ bālakaṃ dṛṣṭvā'gamamastācalaṃ prati \|*  
*preritaḥ kālacakreṇa niśi saṃsthātumakṣamaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Surya said – I saw the child who was crying and went for setting in the region beyond Astacala. Influenced by kalacakra I could not stay during the night.

## \|\| 14.32 \|\|

*candra uvāca \|*  
*rudantaṃ bālakaṃ prāpya gṛhītvā kṛttikāgaṇaḥ \|*  
*jagāma svālayaṃ viṣṇo gacchanbadarikāśramāt \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Chandrama said – О lord Vishnu, Krttikas moving from Badrikasrama found the crying child and took him to their abodes.

## \|\| 14.33 \|\|

*jalamuvāca \|*  
*amuṃ rudantamānīya stanaṃ dattvā stanārthine \|*  
*varddhayāmāsurīśasya taṃ tāḥ sūryyādhikaprabham \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The water said – The crying child was disturbed and was desirous of sucking milk. He possessed the lustre greater than the sun. Krttikas made the child suck the milk and brought him up.

## \|\| 14.34 \|\|

*sandhye ūcatuḥ \|*  
*adhunā kṛttikānāṃ ca ṣaṇṇāṃ tatpoṣyaputrakaḥ \|*  
*tannāma cakrustāḥ premṇā kārttikeya iti svayam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Both the Sandhayas said – Currently the son is being brought up by the Krttikas and lovingly they have given him the name of Karttikeya.

## \|\| 14.35 \|\|

*rātriruvāca \|*  
*na cakrurbālakaṃ tāśca locanānāmagocaram \|*  
*prāṇebhyo'pi premapātraṃ yaḥ poṣṭā tasya putrakaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Ratri said – Those Krttikas never allow the child to disappear from their eyes for a moment. They love him more than their own lives. The son belongs to the one who brings him up.

## \|\| 14.36 \|\|

*dinamuvāca \|*  
*yāni yāni ca vastūni trailokye durlabhāni ca \|*  
*praśaṃsitāni svādūni bhojayāmāsureva tam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The day said – The food stuffs which are difficult to get in the three worlds and which are quite delicious, are given to the child as food.

## \|\| 14.37-39 \|\|

*teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā santuṣṭo madhusūdanaḥ \|*  
*te sarve harimityūcuḥ sabhāyāṃ hṛṣṭamānasāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*putrasya vārttāṃ samprāpya pārvatī hṛṣṭamānasā \|*  
*koṭiratnāni viprebhyo dadau bahudhanāni ca \|\| 38 \|\|*

*dadau sarvāṇi viprebhyo vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca \|\| 39 \|\|*

37-39. Thus with a delightful mind the people said to the lord. The lord on hearing them felt delighted. Coming to know about the news of the son, Parvati felt happy and she gave away the Brahmanas again crores of gems and enormous riches. She gave away to Brahmanas various types of clothes.

## \|\| 14.40 \|\|

*lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī menā sāvitrī sarvayoṣitaḥ \|*  
*viṣṇuśca sarvadevāśca brāhmaṇebhyo dadurdhanam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Thereafter Lakshmi, Sarasvati, Mena, Savitri and all the ladies accompanied by Vishnu distribute enormous charities on the occasion.

## Chapter 15

## \|\| 15.1-5 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*putrasya vārttāṃ samprāpya pārvatyā saha śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*prerito viṣṇunā devairmunibhiḥ parvatairmune \|\| 1 \|\|*

*dūtānprasthāpayāmāsa mahābalaparākramān \|*  
*vīrabhadraṃ viśālākṣaṃ śaṅkukarṇaṃ kabandhakam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*nandīśvaraṃ mahākālaṃ vajradantaṃ bhagandaram \|*  
*godhāmukhaṃ dadhimukhaṃ jvaladagniśikhopamam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*lakṣaṃ ca kṣetrapālānāṃ bhūtānāṃ ca trilakṣakam \|*  
*vetālānāṃ caturlakṣaṃ yakṣāṇāṃ pañcalakṣakam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*kūṣmāṇḍānāṃ caturlakṣaṃ trilakṣaṃ brahmarākṣasān \|*  
*ḍākinīnāṃ caturlakṣaṃ yoginīnāṃ trilakṣakam \|\| 5 \|\|*

1-5.
