Narayana said – О sage, on coming to know about the news of their son both Shiva and Parvati at the instance of lord Vishnu, the gods and the sages besides the mountains, deputed some valorous messengers for bringing the child. The messengers included Virabhadra, Visalaksa, Sankukarna, Kabandhaka, Nandlshvara, Mahakala, Vajradanta, Bhagandara, Godhamukha, Dadhimukha, who was like burning flames, a lakh of Ksetra-palas, three lakhs of Bhutas, four lakhs of vetalas, five lakhs of Yaksas, four lakhs of kusmandas, three lakhs of Brahmaraksas, three lakhs of Dakinls and three lakhs of yoginis.

## \|\| 15.6 \|\|

*rudrāṃśca bhairavāṃścaiva śivatulyaparākramān \|*  
*anyāṃśca vikṛtākārānasaṃkhyānapi nārada \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. О Narada, the Rudras who were as valorous as Shiva himself, the Bhairavas and valorous other ganas with deformed limbs also proceeded for the purpose.

## \|\| 15.7 \|\|

*te sarve śivadūtāśca nānāśastrāstrapāṇayaḥ \|*  
*kṛttikānāṃ ca bhavanaṃ veṣṭayāmāsurujjvalam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. These messengers of Shiva holding weapons in their hands surrounded the palaces of Krttikas from all the four sides.

## \|\| 15.8 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tānkṛttikāḥ sarvā bhayavihvalamānasāḥ \|*  
*kārttikeyaṃ ca jagṛhurjvalantaṃ brahmatejasā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thereafter Krttikas felt disturbed at the sight of the messengers. They then addressed Karttikeya who possessed the divine lustre.

## \|\| 15.9 \|\|

*kṛttikā ūcuḥ \|*  
*vatsa sainyānyasaṃkhyāni veṣṭayāmāsurāyalam \|*  
*na jānīmo vayaṃ kasya karālāni ca bālaka \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Krttikas said – О son, О child, innumerable armies have surrounded our house from all the sides. We are not aware as to whom these armies belong?

## \|\| 15.10 \|\|

*kārttikeya uvāca \|*  
*bhayaṃ tyajata kalyāṇyo bhayaṃ kiṃ vo mayi sthite \|*  
*durnivāryyaḥ karmapāko mātaraḥ kena vāryyate \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Karttikeya said – О pleasant mother, don’t be afraid, why should you be afraid when I am there? О mothers, who can prevent result of the evil deeds.

## \|\| 15.11 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra senānīrnandikeśvaraḥ \|*  
*purataḥ kārttikeyasya tiṣṭhaṃstāsāmuvāca ha \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. At that point of time Nandlkeshvara who happened to be the commander of the armies spoke to Karttikeya.

## \|\| 15.12-14 \|\|

*nandikeśvara uvāca \|*  
*bhrātaḥ pravṛttiṃ śṛṇu me mātuścāpi śubhāvahām \|*  
*preṣitasya surendrasya saṃhartuḥ śaṃkarasya ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

*kailāse sarvadevāśca brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|*  
*sabhāyāṃ te vasantaśca gaṇeśotsavamaṅgalam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*śailendrakanyā taṃ viṣṇuṃ jagatāṃ paripālakam \|*  
*saṃbodhya kathayāmāsa tavānveṣaṇakāraṇam \|\| 14 \|\|*

12-14. Nandikeshvara said – О brother, you listen to the pleasant message of the mother and also of lord Shiva, who has sent us here. Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva besides other gods had collected at Kailasa in order to celebrate the festivities for the birth of Ganesha. At that very moment Parvati the daughter of Himalaya, the king of the mountains spoke to lord Vishnu who preserves the universe and desired the search for you to be made.

## \|\| 15.15 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇeśakhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde nandikārttikeyasaṃvādo nāma pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Thereafter lord Vishnu enquired from all the gods about your whereabouts and everyone gave the proper reply.

## \|\| 15.16-20 \|\|

*tvamatra kṛttikāsthāne kathayāmāsurīśvaram \|*  
*sarve dharmādayo devā dharmādharmasya sākṣiṇaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*yā babhūva rahaḥkrīḍā pārvatīśivayoḥ purā \|*  
*dṛṣṭasya ca suraiḥ śambhovīryyaṃ bhūmau papāta ha \|\| 17 \|\|*

*bhūmistadakṣipadvahnau vahniśca śarakānane \|*  
*tato labdhaḥ kṛttikābhiramūbhirgaccha sāmpratam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*tavābhiṣekaṃ viṣṇuśca kariṣyati suraiḥ saha \|*  
*śastraṃ labdhvā'khilaṃ deva tārakaṃ saṃhaniṣyati \|\| 19 \|\|*

*putrastvaṃ viśvasaṃharttustvāṃ goptuṃ na kṣamā imāḥ \|*  
*nāgniṃ goptuṃ yathā śaktaḥ śuṣkavṛkṣaḥ svakoṭare \|\| 20 \|\|*

16-20. Dharma the witness of all and other gods told the lord that you are staying with Krttikas. In the earlier times Shiva and Parvati had united in seclusion as a result of which the semen of Shiva fell on earth in full view of canes. From the same place Krttikas picked you up; therefore now you get along with us. О god, all the gods and Vishnu well, similarly by remaining in the house of Krttikas, you can never be graceful.

## \|\| 15.21 \|\|

*dīptimāṃstvaṃ ca viśveṣu tāsāṃ geheṣu śobhase \|*  
*yathā patanmahākūpe dvijarājo na rājate \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. No translation

## \|\| 15.22 \|\|

*karoṣi jagadālokaṃ nācchanno'syāṅgatejasā \|*  
*yathā sūryyaḥ karācchanno na bhavetpūruṣasya ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. You are illumining the entire universe with the lustre of your body but you cannot bear the glory of other gods at the same way as a person cannot withhold the sun in his palms.

## \|\| 15.23 \|\|

*viṣṇustvaṃ ca jagadvyāpī nāsāṃ vyāpyo'si śāmbhava \|*  
*yathā na keṣāṃ vyāpyaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ vyāpakaṃ nabhaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. О son of Shiva, you pervade the entire universe everywhere and cannot be concentrated at one place. Similarly you are not pervaded by these people.

## \|\| 15.24 \|\|

*yogīndro nānuliptastvaṃ bhogī ca paripoṣaṇe \|*  
*naiva lipto yathā''tmā ca karmma bhogeṣu jīvinām \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. You are the lord of the yogis and you also freed the universe without involving yourself in the time, as the soul does not get involved in the deeds of the bodies.

## \|\| 15.25 \|\|

*viśvādhārastvamīśaśca nāmṛte sambhavetsthitiḥ \|*  
*sāgarasya yathā nadyāṃ saritāmāśrayasya ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. You are the base of the entire universe besides being its lord. As the ocean is the final resort for all the rivers and as such it cannot have the base in the river; therefore your position cannot remain stable here.

## \|\| 15.26 \|\|

*nahi sarveśvarāvāsaḥ sambhavetkṛttikālaye \|*  
*garuḍasya yathā vāsaḥ kṣudre ca caṭakodare \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. As Garuda cannot be born out of the womb of a petty bird similarly the lord of the universe cannot stay in the house of Krttikas.

## \|\| 15.27 \|\|

*tvāṃ ca devā na jānanti bhaktānugraha vigraham \|*  
*guṇānāṃ tejasāṃ rāśiṃ yathā jñānamayoginaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. You' have taken to the human form for the sake of the devotees. You are the heap of the lustre and merits. The gods are unaware about your personality like the persons who is devoid of knowledge of the yogas is unaware about the soul.

## \|\| 15.28 \|\|

*tvāmanirvacanīyaṃ ca kathaṃ jānanti kṛttikāḥ \|*  
*yathā parāṃ harerbhaktimabhaktā mūḍhacetasaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. You are inexplicable and Krttikas are not aware about your birth in the same way as the one who is devoid of devotion is not aware of the true devotion of the lord.

## \|\| 15.29 \|\|

*bhrātarye yaṃ na jānanti te taṃ kurvantyanādaram \|*  
*nādriyante yathā bhekāstvekāvāsaṃ ca paṅkajam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Therefore, О brother, the one who is not aware of the true identity cf someone, he sometimes is sure to disrespect to him as the frog and the lotus live together but the frog does not show any respect to the letter.

## \|\| 15.30 \|\|

*kārttikeya uvāca \|*  
*bhrātaḥ sarvaṃ vijānāmi jñānaṃ traikālikaṃ ca yat \|*  
*jñānī tvaṃ kā praśaṃsā te yato mṛtyuñjayāśritaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Karttikeya said – О brother, I possessed the complete knowledge of all the three times and you are also well-versed in the scriptures because of your association with Shiva who has overpowered the death. Therefore I cannot praise you.

## \|\| 15.31 \|\|

*karmaṇā janma yeṣāṃ vā yāsu yāsu ca yoniṣu \|*  
*tāsu te nirvṛtiṃ bhrātarnāpnuvanti ca santatam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O brother, whosoever is born in difficult type of creatures is unable to get relieved of the effects of the environment.

## \|\| 15.32 \|\|

*ye yatra santi santo vā mūḍhā vā karmabhogataḥ \|*  
*te'pi taṃ bahumanyante mohitā viṣṇumāyayā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Because a noble person or the foolish person whosoever is born according to his deeds, is influenced by the illusion of Vishnu himself to be well-honoured in the same position.

## \|\| 15.33-34 \|\|

*sāmprataṃ jagatāṃ mātā viṣṇumāyā sanātanī \|*  
*sarvādyā viṣṇumāyā ca sarvadā viṣṇumaṅgalā \|\| 33 \|\|*

*śailendrapatnī garbhe sā cālabhajjanma bhārate \|*  
*dāruṇaṃ ca tapastaptvā samprāpacchaṅkaraṃ patim \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. Currently Parvati the mother of the universe who happens to be illusion of Vishnu, eternal, the form of all, who always provides welfare to all and is born from the womb of Mena the wife of Himalaya and has got Shiva as her husband after performing great tapas.

## \|\| 15.35-36 \|\|

*brahmāditṛṇaparyyantaṃ sarvaṃ mithyaiva kṛtrimam \|*  
*sarve kṛṣṇodbhavāḥ kāle vilīnāstatra kevalam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*kalpe kalpe jaganmātā mātā me pratijanmani \|*  
*yajjanmamāyayā baddho nityaḥ sṛṣṭividhāvaham \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. Everything right for a Brahma to a straw of perishable and artificial, all are born out of lord Krishna and ultimately merge in him. In every kalpa Parvati the mother of the universe also become my mother and at the time of creation influenced by illusion, I am born firm her.

## \|\| 15.37 \|\|

*prakṛterudbhavāssatyaṃ jagatyāṃ sarvayoṣitaḥ \|*  
*kāścidaṃśāḥ kalāḥ kāścitkalāṃśāṃśena kāścana \|\| 37\|\|*

37\. All the ladies of the universe are born out of Prakriti. This is the tmth someone is the part of Prakriti, someone is the part of the ray.

## \|\| 15.38 \|\|

*kṛttikā jñānavatyaśca yoginyaḥ prakṛteḥ kalāḥ \|*  
*stanaiśca saṃvarddhito'hamupahāreṇa santatam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Krttikas who are well-versed in the yogic practices or the rays of Prakriti they have brought me up by breast feeding me.

## \|\| 15.39-43 \|\|

*tāsāmahaṃ poṣyaputro madambāḥ poṣaṇādimāḥ \|*  
*tasyāśca prakṛteḥ putro gatastvatsvāmivīryyataḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*na garbhajo'haṃ śailendrakanyāyā nandikeśvara \|*  
*sā ca me dharmato mātā tathemāḥ sarvasammatāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*stanadātrī garbhadhātrī bhakṣyadātrī gurupriyā \|*  
*abhīṣṭadevapatnī ca pituḥ patnī ca kanyakāḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*sagarbhakanyā bhaginī putrapatnī priyāprasūḥ \|*  
*māturmātā piturmātā sodarasya priyā tathā \|\| 42 \|\|*

*mātuḥ pituśca bhaginī mātulānī tathaiva ca \|*  
*janānāṃ vedavihitā mātaraḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

39-43. I am the competent son of all of them and they happen to be my mothers. No doubt I am born of the semen of your lord and as such I happen to be the son of mother Parvati as well but, О Nandikeshvara, I am not the son born out of the womb of Parvati. She is my mother religiously. Similarly Krttikas are also my mothers according to the acceptable tradition. According to the acceptable tradition the one who feeds a child out of her breast, the one who gives birth out of the womb, the one who feeds, wife of the teacher, wife of the family god, wife of the father, daughter, pregnant daughter, sister, wife of the son, mother of the wife, mother of the mother, mother of the father, wife of the brother, sisters of mother and father, maternal aunt, are the sixteen types of mothers prescribed in the Vedas.

## \|\| 15.44 \|\|

*imāśca sarvasiddhijñāḥ paramaiśvaryyasaṃyutāḥ \|*  
*na kṣudrā brahmaṇaḥ kanyāstriṣu lokeṣu pūjitāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Therefore, the one who is aware of all the siddhis and who is bestowed with all the riches besides the daughter of Brahma cannot be considered as downgraded, that is why they are adored in all the three worlds.

## \|\| 15.45 \|\|

*viṣṇunā preritastvaṃ ca śambhoḥ putrasamo mahān \|*  
*gaccha yāmi tvayā sārddhaṃ drakṣyāmi surasaṃcayam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. You are also like the great son of Shiva and have been deputed by lord Vishnu; therefore accompanying you I shall also have an audience with all the gods.

## Chapter 16

## \|\| 16.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*ityevamuktvā taṃ śīghraṃ bodhayitvā ca kṛttikāḥ \|*  
*uvāca kiṃcidyuktaṃ ca vacanaṃ śaṅkarātmajaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – Karttikeya the son of Shiva thus spoke to Nandisvara, went to Krttikas and apprised them of the reality speaking the appropriate words.

## \|\| 16.2 \|\|

*kārtikeya uvāca \|*  
*yāsyāmi śaṃkarasthānaṃ drakṣyāmi surasañcayam \|*  
*mātaraṃ bandhuvargāṃścāpyājñāṃ me datta mātaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Karttikeya said – О mother, I am going to Shiva in order to meet the gods. I shall also meet my mother and other gods there. I, therefore, seek your kind permission to proceed.

## \|\| 16.3 \|\|

*daivādhīnaṃ jagatsarvaṃ janma karma śubhāvaham \|*  
*saṃyogaśca viyogaśca na ca daivātparaṃ balam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O mothers, don’t be afraid of anything; the birth and death in this universe, the good and bad deeds, meeting and separation are all in the hands of destiny; therefore there is no other strength greater then the strength of the destiny.

## \|\| 16.4 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāyattaṃ ca taddaivaṃ sa ca daivātparastataḥ \|*  
*bhajanti satataṃ santaḥ paramātmānamīśvaram \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. And the destiny changes according to the wishes of lord Krishna who is beyond the destiny; that is why all the people adore him always.

## \|\| 16.5 \|\|

*daivaṃ varddhayituṃ śaktaḥ kṣayaṃ karttuṃ svalīlayā \|*  
*na daivabaddhastadbhaktaścāvināśīti nirṇayaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. With his own divine play he can increase the destiny and also destroy it. His devotee is controlled by destiny who remains indestructible. This is the resolve of everyone.

## \|\| 16.6-7 \|\|

*tasmādbhajata govindaṃ mohaṃ tyajata duḥkhadam \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ janmamṛtyubhayāpaham \|\| 6 \|\|*

*paramānandajananaṃ mohajālanikṛntanam \|*  
*śaśvadbhajanti yatsarve brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Therefore shedding all the painful grief recite the name of Govinda, who provides all the pleasures, moksa is essence of all, destroyer of birth, death and fear, all blissful and destroys the net of illusion, he is adored by Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva always.

## \|\| 16.8 \|\|

*ko'haṃ bhavābdhau yuṣmākaṃ kā vā yūyaṃ mamātmikāḥ \|*  
*tatkarmasrotasāṃ sarvaṃ puñjībhūtaṃ ca phenavat \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. In this universe, you just think what are you to me and what am I to you? All are like the bubble or the foam of water.

## \|\| 16.9 \|\|

*saṃśleṣaṃ vā viyogaṃ vā sarvamīśvaracintayā \|*  
*brahmāṇḍamīśvarādhīnaṃ na svatantraṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The meeting and separation are in the hands of the lord. So much so that even the entire universe is in the hands of the lord. No one is independent. This has been ordained by the people of wisdom.

## \|\| 16.10-11 \|\|

*jalabudbudavatsarvamanityaṃ ca jagattrayam \|*  
*māyāmanitye kurvanti māyayā mūḍhacetasaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*santastatra na lipyante vāyuvatkṛṣṇacetasaḥ \|*  
*tasmānmohaṃ parityajya cājñaptiṃ datta mātaraḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. All the three worlds are perishable like the bubble of water. In this perishable universe, the people who are infatuated with illusion actually work for the illusion of the noble people who are devoted to lord Krishna and do not get involved in it like the wind which does not get involved in the body in anyway. Therefore, О mothers, leaving aside the false sense of attraction, you grant me the permission.

## \|\| 16.12 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā tā natvā sārddhaṃ śaṅkarapārṣadaiḥ \|*  
*yātrāṃ cakāra bhagavānmanasā śrīhariṃ smaran \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Thus reassuring them variously Karttikeya offered his salutations and reciting the name of Hari in his mind, he started his journey together with the messengers of Shiva.

## \|\| 16.13-14 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra dadarśa rathamuttamam \|*  
*viśvakarmakṛtaṃ ramyaṃ hīrakeṇa virājitam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*sadratnasāraracitaṃ māṇikyena virājitam \|*  
*pārijātaprasūnānāṃ mālājālaiśca śobhitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. In the meantime a beautiful chariot appeared on the scene which was built by Vishvakarma. It was quite beautiful studded with gems, jewels and rubies and adorned with the garland of Parijata flowers.

## \|\| 16.15 \|\|

*maṇīndradarpaṇaḥ śvetacāmarairatidīpitam \|*  
*krīḍārhamandirai ramyaiścitritaiścitritaṃ varam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The beautiful gems served as mirrors in the chariot. It was illumining with white fly-whisks. There were various types of shining fly-whisks and dancing halls of astonishing types. It was thus the best of all.

## \|\| 16.16 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde kārttikeyāgamanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. It was quite wide-spread and had a hundred wheels. It could move with the speed of the mind and was quite pleasant to look at. Parvati had sent it with several of her courtiers.

## \|\| 16.17 \|\|

*samāruhantaṃ yānaṃ tā hṛdayena vidūyatā \|*  
*sahasā cetanāṃ prāpya muktakeśyaḥ śucā''turāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The heart of Krttikas felt painful when Karttikeya mounted the chariot and they fainted. Suddenly on regaining consciousness, they dishevelled the hair and became upset.

## \|\| 16.18 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā ca svapuraḥ skandaṃ staṃbhitāścātiśokataḥ \|*  
*unmattā iva tatraiva vaktumārebhire bhiyā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. With their mind filled with grief they were completely upset. Krttikas looking at Skanda became hysterical and spoke in panic.

## \|\| 16.19 \|\|

*kṛttikā ūcuḥ \|*  
*kiṃ kurmaḥ kva ca yāsyāmo vayaṃ vatsa tvadāśrayāḥ \|*  
*vihāyāsmānkva yāsi tvaṃ nāyaṃ dharmmastavādhunā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Krttikas said – О son, we have been dependent on you; now where should we go and what should we do. Where are you going leaving us alone? It is not proper to become like this at the moment.

## \|\| 16.20 \|\|

*snehena varddhito'smābhiḥ putro'smākaṃ svadharmataḥ \|*  
*nāyaṃ dharmo mātṛvargānanuraktaḥ sutastyajet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. We have brought you up with great love and affection; you are our son according to dharma. This is not proper that a son getting stone-hearted should discard his mothers like this.

## \|\| 16.21-22 \|\|

*ityuktvā kṛttikāḥ sarvāḥ kṛtvā vakṣasi taṃ sutam \|*  
*punarmūrcchāmavāpustāḥ sutavicchedadāruṇam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*kumāro bodhayitvā tā adhyātmavacanena vai \|*  
*tābhiśca pārṣadaiḥ sārddhamāruroha rathaṃ mune \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. Thus speaking Krttikas embraced their son and fainted because the separation from the son is quite painful. О sage, thereafter Kumara imparted them the divine knowledge on Adhyatma. Thereafter he mounted the chariot together with his mothers.

## \|\| 16.23-26 \|\|

*pūrṇakumbhaṃ dvijaṃ veśyāṃ śukladhānyāni darpaṇam \|*  
*dadhyājyaṃ madhu lājāṃśca puṣpaṃ dūrvākṣatānsitān \|\| 23 \|\|*

*vṛṣaṃ gajendraṃ turagaṃ jvaladagniṃ suvarṇakam \|*  
*pūrṇaṃ ca paripakvāni phalāni vividhāni ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

*patiputravatīṃ nārīṃ pradīpaṃ maṇimuttamam \|*  
*muktāṃ prasūnamālāṃ ca sadyomāṃsaṃ ca candanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*dadarśaitāni vastūni maṅgalāni puro mune \|*  
*śṛgālaṃ nakulaṃ kumbhaṃ śavaṃ vāme śubhāvaham \|\| 26 \|\|*

23-26. О sage, during the time of the journey, they came across the things of welfare like a pitcher filled with water, a Brahmana, a prose, white paddy, mirror, curd, ghee, honey, fried paddy, flowers, Durvd, white rice, bull, elephant, horse, fire in flames, gold, ripe fruits, ladies having husbands and sons, lamp, best of gems, pearls, flower garland, fresh meat, sandal-paste and other things of prosperity. Similarly the jackal, the mangoose, the pitcher, the dead body were seen by them moving towards the left side which is considered to be quite auspicious.

## \|\| 16.27-28 \|\|

*rājahaṃsaṃ mayūraṃ ca khañjanaṃ ca śukaṃ pikam \|*  
*pārāvataṃ śaṅkhacillaṃ cakravākaṃ ca maṅgalam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇasāraṃ ca surabhiṃ camarīṃ śvetacāmaram \|*  
*dhenuṃ ca vatsasaṃyuktāṃ patākāṃ dakṣiṇe śubhām \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. They also found the geese, peacocks, wagtail bird, parrot, cuckoo, pigeon, sarhkha, vulture, the sheldrake (cakava), the black buck, Surabhi cow, spotted cow, white fly-whisks, cow with calf and banner moving to the right.

## \|\| 16.29-30 \|\|

*nānāprakāravādyaṃ cāpyaśrauṣīnmaṃgaladhvanim \|*  
*manoharaṃ ca saṃgītaṃ ghaṇṭāśaṃkhadhvaniṃ tathā \|\| 29 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā śrutvā maṅgalaṃ sa hyagamattātamandiram \|*  
*kṣaṇenānandayuktaśca manoyāyirathena ca \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Many musical instruments played the welfare tunes, besides music of Welfare. The sound of conch and the gong welcomed them. Kumara then delightfully moved on with the chariot and with the speed of the mind to the palace of his father.

## \|\| 16.31 \|\|

*kumāraḥ prāpya kailāsaṃ nyagrodhākṣayamūlake \|*  
*kṣaṇaṃ tasthau kṛttikābhiḥ pārṣadapravaraiḥ saha \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Reaching Kailasa, Karttikeya got down from the chariot together with Krttikas and the courtiers and in an instant they reached under the Aksyavata.

## \|\| 16.32-39 \|\|

*pārvatī maṃgalaṃ kṛtvā rājamārgaṃ manoharam \|*  
*padmarāgairindranīlaiḥ saṃskṛtaṃ paritaḥ puram \|\| 32 \|\|*

*rambhāstambhasamūhaiśca paṭṭasūtrāṃśukaistathā \|*  
*śrīkhaṇḍapallavairyuktaṃ pūrṇakumbhaiḥ suśobhitam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*pūrṇakumbhajalairvyāptaṃ siktaṃ candanavāribhiḥ \|*  
*asaṃkhyaratnadīpaiśca maṇirājairvirājitam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*naṭanarttakaveśyānāmutsavaiḥ saṃkulaṃ sadā \|*  
*bandibhirvipravargaiśca dūrvāpuṣpakarairyutam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*patiputravatībhiśca sādhvībhiśca samanvitam \|*  
*lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīṃ durgāṃ sāvitrīṃ tulasīṃ ratim \|\| 36 \|\|*

*arundhatīmahalyāṃ ca ditiṃ tārāṃ manoramām \|*  
*aditiṃ śatarūpāṃ ca śacīṃ sandhyāṃ ca rohiṇīm \|\| 37 \|\|*

*anasūyāṃ tathā svāhāṃ saṃjñāṃ varuṇakāminīm \|*  
*ākūtiṃ ca prasūtiṃ ca devahūtiṃ ca menakām \|\| 38 \|\|*

*tāmekapāṭalāmekaparṇāṃ mainākakāminīm \|*  
*vasundharāṃ ca manasāṃ puraskṛtya samāyayau \|\| 39 \|\|*

32-39. Parvati had decorated the entire king's way with rubies, sapphire and several types of the trunks of banana, silken garments, pitchers filled with Srikhanda leaves and water mixed with sandal-paste besides several lamps. There were lots of festivities in the city besides the dancing by the male and female dancers and apsaras. The ladies whose husbands and sons were alive carried Sh\>tfmz-grass and flowers together with Brahmanas and the bards on the way. Parvati, Lakshmi, Sarasvati, Durga, Savitn, Rati, Arundhati, Ahalya, Diti, beautiful Tara, Aditi, Satampa, IndranI, Sandhya, Rohini, Anusuya, Svaha, Sanjna, VarunI, Aktiti, Prasflti, Devahtiti, Menaka, a part of Mainaka, lady with a single costume, Vasundhara and Manasa reached there.

## \|\| 16.40-42 \|\|

*rambhā tilottamā menā ghṛtācī mohinī śubhā \|*  
*urvaśī ratnamālā ca suśīlā lalitā kalā \|\| 40 \|\|*

*kadambamālā surasā vanamālā ca sundarī \|*  
*etāścānyāśca bahavo viprendrāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*saṃgītanarttanaparāḥ sasmitā veṣasaṃyutāḥ \|*  
*karatālakarāḥ sarvā jagmurānandapūrvakam \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. О Brahmana, Rambha, Tilottama, Mena, GhrtacI, the pleasant Mohini, UrvasI, Ratnamala, Suslla, Lalita, Kala, Kadambamala, Suras a, the beautiful Vanamala and other beautiful apsaras clad in the best of garments started dancing wearing smiles on their faces. All the people holding karatalas in their hands playing on musical instruments were dancing, while moving forward.

## \|\| 16.43 \|\|

*devāśca munayaḥ śailā gandharvāḥ kinnarāstathā \|*  
*sarve yayuḥ pramuditāḥ kumārasyānumajjane \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. All the gods, the sages, the mountains, Gandharvas and Kinnaras, were moving joyfully to welcome Kumara.

## \|\| 16.44 \|\|

*nānāprakāravādyaiśca rudrairvā pārṣadaiḥ saha \|*  
*bhairavaiḥ kṣetrapālaiśca yayau sārddhaṃ maheśvaraḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Various types of musical instruments were played upon by Rudras, courtiers, Bhairavas, Ks etra-palas while moving forward with Shiva.

## \|\| 16.45-46 \|\|

*atha śaktidharo hṛṣṭo dṛṣṭvā ''rātpārvatīṃ tadā \|*  
*avaruhya rathāttūrṇaṃ śirasā praṇanāma ha \|\| 45 \|\|*

*taṃ padmāpramukhaṃ devīgaṇaṃ ca munikāminīm \|*  
*śivaṃ ca parayā bhaktyā sarvānsaṃbhāṣya yatnataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Thereafter the valorous Kumara felt delighted at finding Parvati approaching him. He got down from the chariot and bowed in respect to her, besides Lakshmi and other goddesses and the wives of the sages. He bowed in reverence to lord Shiva and started talking to him.

## \|\| 16.47-48 \|\|

*kārttikeyaṃ śivā dṛṣṭvā kroḍe kṛtvā cucumba ca \|*  
*śaṅkaraśca surāḥ śailā devyo vai śailayoṣitaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*pārvatīpramukhā devyastathā devaśca śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*śailāśca munayaḥ sarve dadustasmai śubhāśiṣaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. Finding Karttikeya there, Parvati lifted him up in her lap and kissed him. At that point of time Shiva, the gods, the mountains, the goddesses, wives of the mountains, Parvati, prominent goddesses, gods, the sages showered their blessing on Kumara.

## \|\| 16.49-51 \|\|

*kumāraḥ sa gaṇaiḥ sārddhamāgatya ca śivālayam \|*  
*dadarśa taṃ sabhāmadhye viṣṇuṃ kṣīrodaśāyinam \|\| 49 \|\|*

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|*  
*dharmabrahmendracandrārkavahnivāyvādibhiryutam \|\| 50 \|\|*

*īṣaddhāsyaṃ prasannāsyaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|*  
*stutaṃ munīndrairdevendraiḥ sevitaṃ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

49-51. Thereafter Kumara reached the abode of lord Shiva together with the gams and he found lord Vishnu the dweller of the ocean of milk, in the centre of the assembly hall, seated on a gem-studded lion-throne in Sukhasana. He was surrounded by Dharma, Brahma, Indra, the moon, the sun, the fire-god, the wind-god and others. He wore a serene smile on his face. He has been graceful to his devotees besides the best of the sages and the gods served as the fly-whisk bearers.

## \|\| 16.52 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā jagatāṃ nāthaṃ bhaktinamrātmakandharaḥ \|*  
*pulakānvitasarvāṃgaḥ śirasā praṇanāma ha \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Looking at Vishnu the lord of the universe, Kumara bowed in reverence to him and he felt sensational in the body.

## \|\| 16.53 \|\|

*vidhiṃ dharmaṃ ca devāṃśca munīndrāṃśca mudā'nvitān \|*  
*praṇanāma pṛthaktatra prāpa tebhyaśśubhāśiṣaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Thereafter he offered salutation to Brahma, Dharma, the gods and the sages and received their blessings individually.

## \|\| 16.54 \|\|

*pṛthaksaṃbhāṣya sarvāṃścāpyuvāsa kanakāsane \|*  
*dadau dhanāni viprebhyaḥ pārvatyā saha śaṅkaraḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. After individually talking to all of them, he took his seat. At that point of time both Shiva and Parvati distributed charities to the Brahmanas.

## Chapter 17

## \|\| 17.1-3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*atha viṣṇurjagatkānto hṛṣṭaḥ kṛtvā śubhakṣaṇam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsane ramye vāsayāmāsa ṣaṇmukham \|\| 1 \|\|*

*nānāvidhāni vādyāni kāṃsyatālādikāni ca \|*  
*nānāvidhāni yantrāṇi vādayāmāsa kautukāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

*vedamantrābhiṣiktaiśca sarvartīrthodapūrṇakaiḥ \|*  
*sadratnakumbhaśatakaiḥ snāpayāmāsa taṃ mudā \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Narayana said – Vishnu the lord of the universe was then pleased and in an auspicious time made the six-faced Karttikeya to occupy a gem-studded lion-throne playfully. Various types of musical instruments were played upon then. He was made to bathe with the pitchers of gems filled with the water of various sacred places.

## \|\| 17.4-5 \|\|

*sadratnasārakhacitaṃ kirīṭaṃ maṅgalāṅgade \|*  
*amūlyaratnakhacitaṃ bhūṣaṇāni bahūni ca \|\| 4 \|\|*

*vahniśuddhāṃśuke divye kṣīrodārṇavasambhavam \|*  
*kaustubhaṃ vanamālāṃ ca tasmai cakraṃ dadau mudā \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. He was then adorned with the kirlta crown, pleasant armlets and several other ornaments and the garments sanctified by fire. He was also offered the kaustubha gems which emerged out of the ocean of milk, besides a garland of forest flowers and the cakra.

## \|\| 17.6-7 \|\|

*brahmā dadau yajñasūtraṃ vedā vai vedamātaram \|*  
*sandhyāmantraṃ kṛṣṇamantraṃ stotraṃ ca kavacaṃ hareḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*kamaṇḍaluṃ ca brahmāstraṃ vidyāṃ vai vairimardinīm \|*  
*dharmo dharmamatiṃ divyāṃ sarvajīve dayāṃ dadau \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Brahma offered him yajnopavita, Gayatri, the Vedas, Sandhyd-mantra, Krishna-mantra, stotra of the lord, kavacha, kamandalu, Brahmastra and the knowledge of destroying one's enemies. Dharma bestowed the religious wisdom on him and the sense of mercy to be merciful towards all creatures.

## \|\| 17.8-12 \|\|

*paraṃ mṛtyuñjayaṃ jñānaṃ sarvaśāstrāvabodhanam \|*  
*śaśvatsukhapradaṃ tattvajñānaṃ ca sumanoharam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*yogatattvaṃ siddhitattvaṃ brahmajñānaṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*śūlaṃ pinākaṃ paraśuṃ śaktiṃ pāśupataṃ dhanuḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*saṃhārāstravinikṣepaṃ tatsaṃhāre dadau śivaḥ \|*  
*śvetacchatraṃ ratnamālāṃ dadau tasmai jaleśvaraḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*gajendraṃ ca hayendraṃ ca sudhākumbhaṃ sudhānidhiḥ \|*  
*manoyāyirathaṃ sūryyaḥ sannāhaṃ ca manoramam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yamadaṇḍaṃ yamaścaiva mahāśaktiṃ hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*nānāśastrāṇyupāyāni sarve devā dadurmudā \|\| 12 \|\|*

8-12. Shiva imparted the divine knowledge of overcoming the death, knowledge of tattvas which always provided welfare besides yogatattva, Siddhitattva and the divine knowledge of Brahmana, which is difficult to get, a shula (trident), a battle axe, shakti, Pasupata-bow, knowledge of using the weapons of destruction. The lord of waters provided him with a white umbrella, a garland of gems, elephants and the best of horses; the moon, who is the lord the nectar gave him a pitcher filled with nectar, while the sun gave him the chariot which could move with the speed of the mind and a kavacha. Yama gave him the Yamadanda, Agni gave him the mahasakti and other gods gave him many weapons.

## \|\| 17.13 \|\|

*kāmaśāstraṃ kāmadevo dadau tasmai mudā'nvitaḥ \|*  
*kṣīrodo'mūlyaratnāni viśiṣṭe ratnanūpure \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Kamadeva delightfully imparted to him the knowledge of Kamashastra and also gave him the beautiful anklets which emerged from the ocean of milk.

## \|\| 17.14-19 \|\|

*sāvitrī siddhavidyāṃ ca sarvāstāḥ kautukādaduḥ \|*  
*himālayo mayūraṃ ca vāhanārthaṃ ca mūkuṭam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*lakṣmīśca parameśva bhāratī hāramuttamam \|*  
*pārvatī sasmitā hṛṣṭā paramānandamānasā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*mahāvidyāṃ suśīlāṃ ca vidyāṃ medhāṃ dayāṃ smṛtim buddhiṃ sunirmalāṃ śāntiṃ tuṣṭiṃ puṣṭiṃ kṣamāṃ dhṛtim \|\| 16 \|\|*

*prajāpatirdevasenāṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitām \|\| 17 \|\|*

*dadau tasmai vedamantrairvivāhavidhinā svayam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*abhiṣicya kumāraṃ ca sarve devā yayurgṛham \|\| 19 \|\|*

14-19. Savitri imparted to him the knowledge of Siddhividya and the other gods playfully imparted to him various types of knowledge. Himalaya gave him the peacock as a vehicle besides the crown; Lakshmi gave him the great fortune and Sarasvati gave him the best of fortune. Parvati wearing a serene smile on her face and quite blissfully imparted to him the knowledge of Mahavidyd, MedhS, mercy, Smrti and the spotless wisdom besides peace satisfaction, nourishment, forgiveness, forbearance and devotion towards the lords. Prajapati gave him Devasena adorned with all the ornaments who was quite humble, good nature, pleasant and beautiful, while reciting the hymns from the Vedas performing all the rites for merits. The people of wisdom call her Mahasasthi who happens to be the goddess of children. Thus crowning Kumara, the gods retired to their own respective abodes.

## \|\| 17.20-23 \|\|

*nārāyaṇaṃ ca brahmāṇaṃ dharmaṃ tuṣṭāva śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*prītyā yayau ca śailendraḥ sagaṇaḥ śaṃkarārcitaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*paramānandasaṃyukto devyā saha maheśvaraḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*kālāntare ca tānsarvānpunarānīya śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*puṣṭiṃ dadau vivāhena gaṇeśāya mahātmane \|\| 23 \|\|*

20-23. О Narada, lord Shiva adored Narayana, Dharma and Brahma eulogising him at the same time. Thereafter, embracing Dharma he bowed in reverence to the lord. Thereafter, having been honoured by Shiva, the lord of the mountain, Himalaya gracefully retired to his abode. Thus all the people who had arrived there from different places dispersed joyfully. Thereafter Shiva was immensely pleased with PSrvati. After sometime, Shiva invited them again and gave away Push in marriage to Ganesha.

## \|\| 17.24-26 \|\|

*sutābhyāṃ sagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ pārvatī hṛṣṭamānasā \|*  
*siṣeve svāminaḥ pādapadmaṃ sā sarvakāmadam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kumārasyābhiṣecanam \|*  
*vivāhaḥ pūjanaṃ tasya gaṇeśasya vivāhakam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*pārvatīputralābhaśca devānāṃ ca samāgamaḥ \|*  
*kā te manasi vāñchā'sti kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 26 \|\|*

24-26. Thereafter, Parvati together with the gams of Shiva delightfully started serving at the lotus-like feet of Shiva who fulfils all the desires. Thus I have narrated to you the story about the crowning of kumara, his marriage and the adoration, in addition to the marriage of Ganesha and his marriage together with the assembly of gods. Now what do you want to listen to from me, you tell me?

## Chapter 18

## \|\| 18.1 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*nārāyaṇa mahābhāga vedavedāṃgapāraga \|*  
*pṛcchāmi tvāmahaṃ kiṃcidatisandehavānyataḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narada said – О Narayana, О virtuous one, О best of those well-versed in the Vedas and post-Vedic literature, I want to ask you a question because my mind is filled with doubt.

## \|\| 18.2-3 \|\|

*sutasya tridaśeśasya śaṃkarasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*vighnanighnasya yadvighnamīśvarasya kathaṃ prabho \|\| 2 \|\|*

*paripūrṇatamaḥ śrīmānparamātmā parātparaḥ \|*  
*golokanāthaḥ svāṃśena pārvatītanayaḥ svayam \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. О lord, how could Ganesha who happens to be the destroyer of all the obstructions himself to face the obstruction when he himself happens to be the son of Shiva. He is lord on to himself and is like the all powerful lord Krishna the great soul, eternal and lord of the Goloka and he himself became the son of Parvati from his own rays.

## \|\| 18.4 \|\|

*aho bhagavatastasya mastakacchedanaṃ vibho \|*  
*grahadṛṣṭyā graheśasya kathaṃ me vaktumarhasi \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О virtuous one, this is surprising that due to the movement of planet, even the head of the lord of the universe was severed. How could it happen you please tell me.

## \|\| 18.5 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*sāvadhānaṃ śṛṇu brahmannitihāsaṃ purātanam \|*  
*vighneśasya babhūvedaṃ vighnaṃ yena ca nārada \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Narayana said – О Brahmana, О Narada, I am going to tell you an ancient story as to how Ganesha the lord of obstruction could meet with an obstruction himself. You please listen to me.

## \|\| 18.6 \|\|

*ekadā śaṃkaraḥ sūryyaṃ jaghāna paramaṣṭadhā \|*  
*sumālimālihantāraṃ śūlena bahuvatsalaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Once Shiva in great anger killed Surya with the trident who had killed the demons named Mali and Sumall.

## \|\| 18.7 \|\|

*śrīsūryyo'moghaśūlenāśanitulyena tejasā \|*  
*jahau sa cetanāṃ sadyo rathācca nipapāta ha \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The trident was foil of lustre like vajra and infallible. With the striking of the trident, the sun fell down from chariot and fainted.

## \|\| 18.8 \|\|

*dadarśa kaśyapaḥ putraṃ mṛtamuttānalocanam \|*  
*kṛtvā vakṣasi taṃ śokādvilalāpa bhṛśaṃ muhuḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Kashyapa then looked at his son (Surya) who fell like dead. He picked him up in his lap and started lamenting again and again.

## \|\| 18.9-11 \|\|

*hāhākāraṃ surāścakrurvilepurbhayakātarāḥ \|*  
*andhībhūtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ babhūva tamasā vṛtam \|\| 9 \|\|*

*niṣprabhaṃ tanayaṃ dṛṣṭvā cāśapatkaśyapaḥ śivam \|*  
*tapasvī brahmaṇaḥ pautraḥ prajvanbrahmatejasā \|\| 10 \|\|*

*matputrasya yathā vakṣaśchinnaṃ śūlena te'dya vai \|*  
*tvatputrasya śiraśchinnaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

9-11. The gods also felt terrified and contributed to the grief of Kashyapa. At that point of time the entire universe was plunged in darkness. Kashyapa who happens to be the grandson of great tapas Brahma and was illumining with divine lustre. Finding his son falling pronounced a curse on Shiva that the way in which you have killed my son similarly the head of your son will also be shattered. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 18.12-13 \|\|

*śivaśca galitakrodhaḥ kṣaṇenaivāśutoṣakaḥ \|*  
*brahmajñānena tatpūrṇaṃ jīvayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt \|\| 12 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāmaṃśaśca triguṇātmakaḥ \|*  
*sūryyaśca cetanāṃ prāpya samuttasthau pituḥ puraḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. In a moment after being peaceful, Shiva got pleased and revived Surya back to life with his divine knowledge. Surya is said to have emerged from the rays of Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva and is thus the form of the Trigunas. On getting back his senses he sat down before his father.

## \|\| 18.14 \|\|

*nanāma pitaraṃ bhaktyā śaṃkaraṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*vijñāya śambhoḥ śāpaṃ ca kaśyapaṃ sa cukopa ha \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Surya then offered his devoted salutation to Shiva and was annoyed on coming to know of the curse pronounced by his father on Shiva.

## \|\| 18.15-16 \|\|

*viṣayānnaiva jagrāha kopenaivamuvāca ha \|*  
*viṣayāṃśca parityajya bhaje śrīkṛṣṇamīśvaram \|\| 15 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ tucchamanityaṃ ca naśvaraṃ ceśvaraṃ vinā \|*  
*vihāya maṅgalaṃ satyaṃ vidvānnecchedamaṃgalam \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. He then spoke to him in anger like this, "Discarding all this worldly pleasure I shall adore lord Krishna because without the lord, everything else is of no consequence, which is perishable and short lived. The people with wisdom do not accept miseries discarding the welfare."

## \|\| 18.17 \|\|

*devaiśca prerito brahmā samāgatya sasambhramaḥ \|*  
*bodhayitvā raviṃ tatra yuyoja viṣayeṣvajaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. At that very moment inspired by the gods, Brahma reached there and enlightening Surya variously attracted him again to the worldly affairs.

## \|\| 18.18 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde vighneśavighnakathanaṃnāmāṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thereafter Brahma and Shiva blessed Surya variously and retired to their respective abodes. Surya also retired to his proper place.

## \|\| 18.19-20 \|\|

*atha mālī sumālī ca vyādhigrastau babhūvatuḥ \|*  
*śvitrau galitasarvāṃgau śaktihīnau hataprabhau \|\| 19 \|\|*

*tāvuvāca svayaṃ brahmā yuvāṃ ca bhajatāṃ ravim \|*  
*sūryyakopena malinau yuvāmevaṃ hataprabhau \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Thereafter Mali and Sumali both suffered from ailment. They attracted the white leprosy and all their limbs developed septic tendency. Thereafter they became lustreless and lost strength. Brahma himself told them that they should better meditate upon Surya, because they have attracted leprosy because of the curse of Surya.

## \|\| 18.21-23 \|\|

*sūryyasya kavacaṃ stotraṃ sarvapūjāvidhiṃ vidhiḥ \|*  
*jagāma kathayitvā tau brahmalokaṃ sanātanaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*tatastau puṣkaraṃ gatvā siṣevāte raviṃ mune \|*  
*snātvā trikālaṃ bhaktyā ca japantau mantramuttamam \|\| 22 \|\|*

*tataḥ sūryyādvaraṃ prāpya nijarūpau babhūvatuḥ \|*  
*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ kiṃ bhayaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 23 \|\|*

21-23. Thereafter the eternal Brahma gave them the kavacha of Surya, his stotra and enlighted them on the method of his adoration. Thereafter he went back to his abode. Both the demons then went to the Puskara-ksetra and started adoring Sflrya with great devotion taking bath thrice a day. Thereafter getting a boon from Surya they were relieved of the ailment and the original glory. Thus I have narrated to you whatever was heard by me. What else do you want to listen to from me?

## Chapter 19

## \|\| 19.1 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ stotraṃ kavacaṃ nātha brahmaṇā lokasākṣiṇā \|*  
*dānavābhyāṃ purā dattaṃ sūryasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narada said – О lord in the earlier times Brahma who happens to be the witness of universe imparted to the demons the knowledge of the adoration of Surya and his stotra besides kavacha. You kindly give us the details of the same.

## \|\| 19.2 \|\|

*kiṃ vā pūjāvidhānaṃ vā kiṃ mantraṃ vyādhināśanam \|*  
*sarvaṃ cāsya mahābhāga tanme tvaṃ vaktumarhasi \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. О virtuous one, what is the method of his adoration and the mantra for the removal of ailment? You kindly tell me all this.

## \|\| 19.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā bhagavānkaruṇānidhiḥ \|*  
*stotraṃ ca kavacaṃ mantramūce tatpūjanakramam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Suta said – Listening to the words of the merciful Narada, Narayana started narrating to him the stotra, kavacha and mantra of Surya and the method of his adoration.

## \|\| 19.4 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu nārada vakṣyāmi sūryyapūjāvidheḥ kramam \|*  
*stotraṃ ca kavacaṃ sarvaṃ pāpavyādhivimocakam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Narayana said – О Narada, I am going to tell you the method of adoration of Surya and his stotra which relieves one of all the sins, besides the kavacha. You listen to it.

## \|\| 19.5 \|\|

*sumālimālinau daityau vyādhigrastau babhūvatuḥ \|*  
*vidhiṃ sasmaratuḥ stotuṃ śivamantrapradāyakam \|\| 5\|\|*

5\. When Mali and Sumall were suffering from an ailment, they adored Brahma to receive the Shiva-mantra from him.

## \|\| 19.6 \|\|

*brahmā gatvā ca vaikuṇṭhaṃ papraccha kamalāpatim \|*  
*śivaṃ tatraiva saṃpaśyanvasantaṃ harisannidhau \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thereafter Brahma went to Vaikuntha. On finding Shiva present there with Vishnu, he asked Vishnu the husband of Lakshmi.

## \|\| 19.7 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*sumālimālinau daityau vyādhigrastau babhūvatuḥ \|*  
*ka upāyo vada hare tayorvyādhivināśane \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Brahma said – О Shiva, the demons named Mali and Sumall are suffering from some ailment. You kindly tell me some way out to relieve them of the ailment.

## \|\| 19.8 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*kṛtvā sūryyasya sevāṃ ca puṣkare pūrṇavatsaram \|*  
*vyādhihanturmadaṃśasya tau ca muktau bhaviṣyataḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Vishnu said – Surya is born out of my rays and whosoever adores him in the sacred land of Pus kara for one year, he gets relieved of all the ailment.

## \|\| 19.9-10 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*sūryastotraṃ ca kavacaṃ mantraṃ kalpataruṃ param \|*  
*dehi tābhyāṃ jagatkānta vyādhihanturmahātmanaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*ārātsampatpradātārau sarvadātā hariḥ svayam \|*  
*vyādhihantā dinakaro yasya yo viṣayo vidhe \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Shiva said – О lord of the universe, you kindly enlighten me on the stotra, kavacha and mantra of Surya which are like kalpataru and removes all the ailments. О Brahma, both of us only bestow the riches but though everything is bestowed by Hari yet Surya alone can remove ailment. Because the one who is concerned with the particular subject, the relevant task is accomplished by him.

## \|\| 19.11 \|\|

*tayostu mantraṃ saṃprāpya yayau daityagṛhaṃ vidhiḥ \|*  
*tadā praṇamya taṃ dṛṣṭvā tasmai dadaturāsanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Thereafter, getting permission from both of them Brahma went to the house of the demons who bowed in reverence to the god of creation offering him a seat.

## \|\| 19.12 \|\|

*tāvuvāca svayaṃ brahmā rogagrastau dayānidhiḥ \|*  
*stabdhāvāhārarahitau pūyadurgandhasaṃyutau \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Brahma the ocean of mercy himself went to them surprising them at the same time and found the demons suffering from some ailment with pus oozing out of their wounds creating a bad smell. And they were without food also.

## \|\| 19.13 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*gṛhītvā kavacaṃ stotraṃ mantraṃ pūjāvidhikramam \|*  
*gatvā hi puṣkaraṃ vatsau bhajathaḥ praṇatau ravim \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Brahma said – О sons, you accept this kavacha, stotra, mantra and the method of adoration of Surya. Thereafter you go to Puskara-ksetra and adore the sun-god there offering salutations to him.

## \|\| 19.14 \|\|

*tāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*bhajāvaḥ kena vidhinā kena mantreṇa vā vidhe \|*  
*kiṃ stotraṃ kavacaṃ kiṃ vā tadāvābhyāṃ vadādhunā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Both of them said, "You kindly let us know the method by which we should adore him and recite his stotra. What is the stotra and kavachal You kindly tell us."

## \|\| 19.15-18 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*kṛtvā trikālaṃ snānaṃ ca mantreṇānena bhāskaram \|*  
*saṃsevya bhāskaraṃ bhaktyā nīrujau ca bhaviṣyatha \|\| 15 \|\|*

*oṃ hrīṃ namo bhagavate sūryyāya paramātmane svāhā \|*  
*ityanena ca mantreṇa sāvadhānaṃ divākaram \|\| 16 \|\|*

*saṃpūjya bhaktyā dattvā vai copahārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ yāvaddhruvaṃ muktau bhaviṣyathaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*apūrvaṃ kavacaṃ tasya yuvābhyāṃ pradadāmyaham \|*  
*yaddattaṃ guruṇā pūrvamindrāya prītipūrvakam \|\| 18 \|\|*

15-18. Brahma said – "Proceeding to Puskara-ksetra, you take a bath thrice a day and by reciting this mantra with devotion you will be freed from the ailments." sti it’ ЯФта WTR44 тзщ this mantra should be recited carefully with devotion and by giving away sixteen types of offerings you should adore the sun god. This adoration should continue for a year. You will surely be relieved of your ailment.

## \|\| 19.19 \|\|

*tatsahasrabhagāṅgāya śāpena gautamasya ca \|*  
*ahalyāharaṇenaiva pāpamuktāya saṅkaṭe \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. I am bestowing on you the knowledge of this kavacha the knowledge of which was lovingly imparted to Indra in earlier times.

## \|\| 19.20-21 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde vighnakāraṇakathanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*kavacaṃ bibhrato vyādhirna bhiyā yāti sannidhim \|*  
*yathā dṛṣṭvā vainateyaṃ palāyante bhujaṅgamāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. At the time Indra developed in his body a thousand vaginas and Indra was infatuated with the curse of the abduction of Ahalya. At that time Brihaspati had imparted the knowledge of him. Brihaspati said – О Indra I am going to enlighten you about the astonishing камаса by bearing which the ascetics are freed from the cycle of birth and death.

## \|\| 19.22 \|\|

*śuddhāya gurubhaktāya svaśiṣyāya prakāśayet \|*  
*khalāya paraśiṣyāya dattvā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. As the serpent flees away at the sight of Garuda similarly all the ailments disappear from the body of the person who recites the kavacha.

## \|\| 19.23 \|\|

*jagadvilakṣaṇasyāsya kavacasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca gāyatrī devo dinakaraḥ svayam \|*  
*vyādhipraṇāśe saundaryye viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Therefore the knowledge of the same should be imparted to a pure hearted pupil, devoted to the teacher, because if the knowledge of the same is imparted to a wicked person or an unintelligent pupil, one surely meets with his end. Prajapati happens to be Rsi of this astonishing kavacha, Gayatri is the metre, Surya is the god and achieving beauty.

*aṣṭādaśākṣaro mantraḥ kapālaṃ me sadā'vatu \|*

*pracaṇḍaḥ pātu gaṇḍaṃ me mārtaṇḍaḥ karṇameva ca \|*

*karau pātu sadā bradhnaḥ pātu pādau prabhākaraḥ \|*

24-29. ## \|\| 19.30-34 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa kavacaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*jagadvilakṣaṇaṃ nāma trijagatsu sudurlabham \|\| 30 \|\|*

*purā dattaṃ ca manave pulastyena tu puṣkare \|*  
*mayā dattaṃ ca tubhyaṃ tadyasmai kasmai na dehi bhoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*vyādhito mucyase tvaṃ ca kavacasya prasādataḥ \|*  
*bhavānarogī śrīmāṃśca bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*lakṣavarṣahaviṣyeṇa yatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ \|*  
*tatphalaṃ labhate nūnaṃ kavacasyāsya dhāraṇāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā yo mūḍho bhāskaraṃ yajet \|*  
*daśalakṣaprajapto'pi mantrasiddhirna jāyate \|\| 34 \|\|*

30-34. О son, thus I have narrated to you the details about the kavacha which is quite astonishing in the universe, quite pleasant and difficult to get in the three worlds. In the earlier times Pulastya had given this kavacha to Manu and I have given the same to you; but don’t part with its knowledge to everyone. By the grace of this kavacha, you will be relieved of the ailment and get back all the fortune. There is no doubt about it. The merit one achieves by consuming Havisya (the left on the sacrifice), for a lakh of years, the same merit is achieved by one, by holding on to this kavacha. If a foolish fellow worships Bhaskara without fully knowing about the kavacha, he cannot meet with success even after reciting the same for lakhs of times.

## \|\| 19.35-36 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*dhṛtvedaṃ kavacaṃ vatsau kṛtvā ca stavanaṃ raveḥ \|*  
*yuvāṃ vyādhivinirmuktau niścitaṃ tu bhaviṣyathaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*stavanaṃ sāmavedoktaṃ sūryyasya vyādhimocanam \|*  
*sarvapāpaharaṃ sāraṃ dhanārogyakaraṃ param \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. Brahma said – О son, by holding on this kavacha and by offering prayers to the sun, you will surely be relieved of your ailment, this is the stotra which removes all the ailments and has been prescribed in the Samaveda. It relieves one of all the sins, is the essence of all and bestows riches by removing all the ailments.

## \|\| 19.37 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*taṃ brahma paramaṃ dhāma jyotīrūpaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*tvāmahaṃ stotumicchāmi bhaktānugrahakārakam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Brahma said – I intend to adore the eternal Brahmana who is full of lustre, is everlasting and always is merciful towards his devotees.

## \|\| 19.38-43 \|\|

*trailokyalocanaṃ lokanāthaṃ pāpavimocanam \|*  
*tapasāṃ phaladātāraṃ duḥkhadaṃ pāpināṃ sadā \|\| 38 \|\|*

*karmānurūpaphaladaṃ karmabījaṃ dayānidhim \|*  
*karmarūpaṃ kriyārūpamarūpaṃ karmabījakam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāmaṃśaṃ ca triguṇātmakam \|*  
*vyādhidaṃ vyādhihantāraṃ śokamohabhayāpaham \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ bhaktidaṃ sarvakāmadam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*sarveśvaraṃ sarvarūpaṃ sākṣiṇaṃ sarvakarmaṇām \|*  
*pratyakṣaṃ sarvalokānāmapratyakṣaṃ manoharam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*śaśvadrasaharaṃ paścādrasadaṃ sarvasiddhidam \|*  
*siddhi svarūpaṃ siddheśaṃ siddhānāṃ paramaṃ gurum \|\| 42 \|\|*

*stavarājamidaṃ proktaṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ param \|*  
*trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhennityaṃ vyādhibhyassa pramucyate \|\| 43 \|\|*

38-43. He happens to be the eye of the three worlds, lord of the lokas, one who relieved one of all the sins, provides fruit of the tapas and is always troublesome for the sinners. He provides the reward according to one’s own deeds, is all merciful, is the form of deeds, the form of actions, formless, Brahmana, the seed of all the actions, the rays of Vishnu and Shiva, the form of trigunas, bestower of ailments and the remover of the ailments as well, the grief and the illusion besides the remover of the fear, bestower of pleasure and moksa, essence of all, the bestower of devotion, the one who fulfils all the desires, the lord of all, the form of everything, the witness of all the deeds, visible to all the people and invisible also, quite pleasant, the one who always extracts the essence, bestower of the essence, bestower of all success, the form of success, the lord of success and the teacher of all siddhas.

## \|\| 19.44 \|\|

*āndhyaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ ca dāridryaṃ rogaḥ śoko bhayaṃ kaliḥ \|*  
*tasya naśyati viśveśa śrīsūryyakṛpayā dhruvam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. I have imparted the knowledge of this stotra which is quite secretive in nature; whosoever recites the same thrice a day, will be relieved all the ailments, grief, fear and ill-effect of Kali. All these disappear with the grace of the sun-god.

## \|\| 19.45-46 \|\|

*mahākuṣṭhī ca galitaścakṣurhīno mahāvraṇī \|*  
*yakṣmagrasto mahāśūlī nānāvyādhiyuto'si vā \|\| 45 \|\|*

*māsaṃ kṛtvā haviṣyānnaṃ śrutvā'to mucyate dhruvam \|*  
*snānaṃ ca sarvatīrthānāṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. A person suffering from the terrific leprosy, septic ailments, blindness, wounds, consumption, stomach-ache and various other ailments can be relieved of the same if he consumes the left-over of the sacrificial food and listens to this stotra. He will also earn the merit of taking a bath in all the sacred places. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 19.47-49 \|\|

*puṣkaraṃ gacchataṃ śīghraṃ bhāskaraṃ bhajataṃ sutau \|*  
*ityevamuktvā sa vidhirjagāma svālayaṃ mudā \|\| 47 \|\|*

*tau niṣevya dineśaṃ taṃ nīrujau saṃbabhūvatuḥ \|*  
*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ vatsa kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 48 \|\|*

*sarvavighnaharaṃ sāraṃ vighneśaṃ vighnanāśanam \|*  
*stotreṇānena taṃ stutvā mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. Therefore, О sons, both of you should immediately proceed to Puskara and adore the sun-god." Thus speaking.Brahma left for his abode with a mind full of delight. О son, thus both of them by adoring the sun-god were relieved of the ailment. I have narrated the story to you, what more do you want to listen to from me? Surya happens to be the remover of all the obstructions, the essence of all and remover of all the ailments and obstructions. Whosoever adores him with this stotra is surely relieved of all the ailments.

24\. Because the one who is desirous of scented clean water could never desire for the dirty water; similarly the one who is desirous of the sandal-paste does not desire for the mud. The one who is desirous of the lotus, doesn't desire for the mud.

## \|\| 20.26-28 \|\|

*svargī ca narakaṃ necchetsubhogī duṣṭabhojanam \|*

They never like the persons with a wicked nature, old ones, weak in love-sport, miser and foolish people.

*nānāprakāraśṛṅgārādviparītādikānmune \|*

49-50. Не with great pleasure enjoyed the water-sport with her and then he came on to the bank of the river and again returned to the water enjoying the love-sport again and again. In the meantime the sage Durvasa arrived here who was moving from Vaikuntha to Kailasa.

51-52. Finding the sage Durvasa there, Indra was taken aback. He at once came and bowed to him in reverence. The sage pronounced his blessings on Indra. The sage Durvasa then handed over the Parijata flower to Indra which he had received from Narayana.

*dattvā puṣpaṃ mahābhāgastamuvāca kṛpānidhiḥ \|*

## \|\| 20.54 \|\|

54\. Durvasa said – О Davendra, this is the flower given by the lord which removes all the obstructions and the person on whose head it is placed, will be victorious all round.

## \|\| 20.55 \|\|

## \|\| 20.57 \|\|

57\. The degraded person who will not honour this flower or place it on his head, his fall from his caste is imminent and he will be deprived of all fortunes.

## \|\| 20.58-59 \|\|

*tatsa rambhāṃtike tiṣṭhaṃścikṣepa gajamastake \|\| 58 \|\|*

Finding Indra in such a pitiable condition, Rambha went back to the heaven because degraded women are unstable and are desirous of a person like her alone and not of anyone else.

60-62. The great elephant also left Indra and entered the forest and getting intoxicated he started enjoying the company of other cow elephants forcibly defeating other elephants. Being a female the cow elephant was overpowered by the elephant of Indra. He then produced many offshoots from that cow-elephant. Lord Vishnu cut off the head of the same elephant and fixed it on the head of Ganesha.

## \|\| 20.63 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ vatsa kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|*

63\. О son, I have narrated to you the story of fixing the elephant head which removes all the sins. What else do you want to listen to from me?

## Chapter 21

## \|\| 21.1-2 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*te devā brahmaśāpena niḥśrīkā kena vā prabho \|*  
*babhūvustadrahasyaṃ ca gopanīyaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kathaṃ vā prāpurete tāṃ kamalāṃ jagatāṃ prasūm \|*  
*kiṃ cakāra mahendraśca tadbhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narada said – О lord tell me the reason why Indra and other gods lost their lustre and fortune. This is a secret knowledge and difficult to get, but you kindly be graceful to reveal the same to me. You may also kindly tell me as to how Indra got back Lakshmi, the mother of the universe and what Indra did thereafter.

## \|\| 21.3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*gajendreṇa parābhūto rambhayā ca sumandadhīḥ \|*  
*bhraṣṭaśrīrdainyayuktaśca sa jagāmāmarāvatīm \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Narayana said – Indra the great god having been humiliated by Rambha and the elephant, lost Lakshmi and his condition became miserable. In that condition he went to Amaravati.

## \|\| 21.4 \|\|

*tāṃ dadarśa nirānando nirānandāṃ purīṃ mune \|*  
*dainyagrastāṃ bandhuhīnāṃ vairivargaiḥ samākulām \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. О sage, reaching there he found the city of Amaravati devoid of pleasure, filled with miseries, devoid of relatives and surrounded by the enemies.

## \|\| 21.5-6 \|\|

*iti śrutvā dūtamukhājjagāma gurumandiram \|*  
*tena devagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ jagāma brahmaṇaḥ sabhām \|\| 5 \|\|*

*gatvā nanāma taṃ śakraḥ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ tathā guruḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva vedavākyaiśca stotreṇāpi ca saṃyataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Listening to these words from the mouth of the messengers Indra took him and reached the abode of Brihaspati. Accompanied by Brihaspati and others gods, Indra reached Brahma. Indra and Brihaspati bowed in reverence before Brahma with all the gods offering many prayers to him.

## \|\| 21.7 \|\|

*pravṛttiṃ kathayāmāsa vākpatistaṃ prajāpatim \|*  
*śrutvā brahmā namravaktraḥ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Thereafter, Brihaspati narrated the entire story to Brahma hearing which Brahma lowered his head and said.

## \|\| 21.8 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*matprapautro'si devendra śaśvadrājañchriyā jvalan \|*  
*lakṣmīsamaśacībharttā parastrīlolupaḥ sadā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Brahma said – О Indra, you are my great grandson and you are always blessed by the royal fortune and you happen to be the husband of Shachi who is comparable to Lakshmi but still you always fall for the wives of others.

## \|\| 21.9 \|\|

*gautamasyābhiśāpena bhagāṃgaḥ surasaṃsadi \|*  
*punarlajjāvihīnastvaṃ parastrīratilolupaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Because of the curse of Gautama in the court of the gods you had developed Vaginas on your entire body. Inspite of that you are shameless and are always desirous of enjoying the company of others’ wives.

## \|\| 21.10 \|\|

*yaḥ parastrīṣu niratastasya śrī vā kuto yaśaḥ \|*  
*sa ca nindyaḥ pāpayuktaḥ śaśvatsarvasabhāsu ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The one who feels attracted towards the wives of others, is always deprived of fortune and glory. He is always considered a sinner and earns disgrace in all the assemblies.

## \|\| 21.11 \|\|

*naivedyaṃ śrīharereva dattaṃ durvāsasā ca te \|*  
*gajamūrdhni tvayā nyastaṃ rambhayā''hṛtacetasā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Durvasa had given you the garland of flowers as a gift of the lord which you threw away on the head of the elephant under the influence of Rambha.

## \|\| 21.12-13 \|\|

*kva sā rambhā sarvabhogyā kvādhunā tvaṃ śriyā hataḥ \|*  
*sarvasaukhyapradātrī tvāṃ gatā tyaktvā kṣaṇena sā \|\| 12 \|\|*

*veśyā saśrīkamicchantī niḥśrīkaṃ na ca cañcalā \|*  
*navaṃ navaṃ prārthayantī parinindya purātanam \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Now where is Rambha who was enjoyed by all and where are you having lost all your fortunes. Rambha who had pleased you for a moment left you in no time proceeding to some other place. The wicked women are of unstable mind and they remain so always.

## \|\| 21.14-15 \|\|

*yadgataṃ tadgataṃ vatsa niṣpannaṃ na nivarttate \|*  
*bhaja nārāyaṇaṃ bhaktyā padmāyāḥ prāptihetave \|\| 14 \|\|*

*ityuktvā taṃ jagatsraṣṭuḥ stotraṃ ca kavacaṃ dadau \|*  
*nārāyaṇasya mantraṃ ca nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. О son, whatever has happened cannot be undone. Therefore for getting back Lakshmi you adore Narayana. Thus speaking, Brahma the creator of the universe gave away to Indra the stotra, kavacha and mantra of the lord.

## \|\| 21.16-17 \|\|

*sa taiḥ sārddhaṃ ca guruṇā hyajapanmantramīpsitam \|*  
*gṛhītvā kavacaṃ tena paryaṣṭautpuṣkare harim \|\| 16 \|\|*

*varṣamekaṃ nirāhāro bhārate puṇyade śubhe \|*  
*siṣeve kamalākāntaṃ kamalāprāptihetave \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. The gods together with Brihaspati recited the mantra and held the kavacha. Indra going to the Puskara-ksetra adored the gods and performed tapas at that sacred place for one full year remaining without food. He adored lord Narayana for achieving Lakshmi.

## \|\| 21.18 \|\|

*āgatya taṃ haristasmai vāñchitaṃ ca varaṃ dadau \|*  
*lakṣmīstotraṃ ca kavacaṃ mantramaiśvaryyavarddhanam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thereafter, the lord appeared before him and granted him his desired boon besides Lakshmi-strotra, kavacha and the mantra which increased his fortune.

## \|\| 21.19 \|\|

*dattvā jagāma vaikuṇṭhamindraḥ kṣīrodameva ca \|*  
*gṛhītvā kavacaṃ stutvā prāpa padmālayāṃ mune \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. О sage, giving all these to Indra, the lord went back to Vaikuntha. Indra went on the other hand to the ocean of milk, wore the kavacha on his person and offering prayers, he got back Lakshmi also.

## \|\| 21.20 \|\|

*sureśvaro'riṃ jitvā vai hyalabhaccāmarāvatīm \|*  
*pratyekaṃ ca surāḥ sarve svālayaṃ prāpurīpsitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Indra then overpowered his enemies and got back Amaravati. He then restored all the gods to their respective places.

## Chapter 22

## \|\| 22.1 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*āvirbhūya haristasmai kiṃ stotraṃ kavacaṃ dadau \|*  
*mahālakṣmyāśca lakṣmīśastanme brūhi tapodhana \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narada said – О great tapas, what type of stotra and kavacha was given by Vishnu, the lord of Lakshmi to Indra, you kindly tell me?

## \|\| 22.2-4 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*puṣkare ca tapastaptvā virarāma sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatraiva kliṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭvā hariḥ svayam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*tamuvāca hṛṣīkeśo varaṃ vṛṇu yathepsitam \|*  
*sa ca vavre varaṃ lakṣmīmīśastasmai dadau mudā \|\| 3 \|\|*

*varaṃ dattvā hṛṣīkeśaḥ pravaktumupacakrame \|*  
*hitaṃ satyaṃ ca sāraṃ ca pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. Narayana said – Indra was performing tapas for the lord at the Puskara-ksetra. Finding him in a miserable condition the lord appeared before him. Lord Hrslkesa said to him, "You ask for a desired boon". He desired for Lakshmi and the same was provided to him. After so giving him the lord Rsikesa asked him a question which was quite beneficial, truthful, essence of all and was to result in pleasure.

## \|\| 22.5 \|\|

*śrīmadhusūdana uvāca \|*  
*gṛhāṇa kavacaṃ śakra sarvaduḥkhavināśanam \|*  
*paramaiśvaryyajanakaṃ sarvaśatruvimardanam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Madhusudana said – О Indra, you receive this kavacha which removes all the miseries, bestows all the fortunes and destroys all the enemies.

## \|\| 22.6 \|\|

*brahmaṇe ca purā dattaṃ viṣṭape ca jalaplute \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā jagatāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ sarvaiśvaryyayuto vidhiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. I imparted the knowledge of the same for the first time to Brahma at the time of deluge, by holding with Brahma, was bestowed with all the riches and became the best of all.

## \|\| 22.7-13 \|\|

*babhūvurmanavaḥ sarve sarvaiśvaryayutā yataḥ \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyapradasyāsya kavacasya ṛṣirvidhiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*paṅktiśchandaśca sā devī svayaṃ padmālayā varā \|*  
*siddhyaiśvaryyasukheṣveva viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*yaddhṛtvā kavacaṃ lokaḥ sarvatra vijayī bhavet \|*  
*mastakaṃ pātu me padmā kaṇṭhaṃ pātu haripriyā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*nāsikāṃ pātu me lakṣmīḥ kamalā pātu locane \|*  
*keśānkeśavakāntā ca kapālaṃ kamalālayā \|\| 10 \|\|*

*jagatprasūrgaṇḍayugmaṃ skandhaṃ sampatpradā sadā \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ kamalavāsinyai svāhā pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 11 \|\|*

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ padmālayāyai svāhā vakṣaḥ sadā'vatu \|*  
*pātu śrīrmama kaṅkālaṃ bāhuyugmaṃ ca te namaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ lakṣmyai namaḥ pādau pātu me saṃtataṃ ciram \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namaḥ padmāyai svāhā pātu nitambakam \|\| 13 \|\|*

7-13. All the Manus were adored with all the fortunes, this kavacha which bestows all these fortunes has Brahma as Rsi, Pankti as the metre, Kamala as the god and one has to take a resolve for achieving siddhi, fortune and pleasure. A person who holds this kavacha gets success everywhere. Padma should protect my head, Haripriya should protect my neck, Lakshmi should protect my nose, Kamala should protect both my eyes, Kesavakanta should protect my skull, the lord of the universe should protect my temples, Sampatprada should protect my shoulders, … this mantra should protect my back. зй ff ?ff ячшчт ЧЧ1ЧТ, this mantra should protect my chest and kamkala should protect both my arms. I bow in reverence to you.

## \|\| 22.14-15 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ mahālakṣmyai svāhā sarvāṅgaṃ pātu me sadā \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ mahālakṣmyai svāhā māṃ pātu sarvataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvasampatkaraṃ param \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyapradaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. … this mantra should protect my feet. … this mantra should protect my pelvic region, …. this mantra should protect all my limbs, this mantra should protect me from all sides.

## \|\| 22.16 \|\|

*gurumabhyarcya vidhivatkavacaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|*  
*kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe bāhau sa sarvavijayī bhavet \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. О son, I have thus narrated to you the entire kavacha which bestows all the fortunes and wealth.

## \|\| 22.17-18 \|\|

*mahālakṣmīrgṛhaṃ tasya na jahāti kadācana \|*  
*tasya cchāyeva satataṃ sā ca janmani janmani \|\| 17 \|\|*

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajellakṣmīṃ sa mandadhīḥ \|*  
*śatalakṣaprajāpe'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. The one who holds this kavacha on the right arm adoring his teacher, is always successful everywhere. The goddess of riches and fortunes never leaves his home and follows him like a shadow in all his births but the foolish without being fully aware of the kavacha cannot meet with success even after reciting the same for a lakh of times.

## \|\| 22.19-22 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*dattvā tasmai ca kavacaṃ mantraṃ vai ṣoḍaśākṣaram \|*  
*santuṣṭaśca jagannātho jagatāṃ hitakāraṇam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ namo mahālakṣmyai svāhā \|*  
*dadau tasmai ca kṛpayā cendrāya ca mahāmune \|\| 20 \|\|*

*dhyānaṃ ca sāmavedoktaṃ gopanīyaṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*siddhairmunīndrairduṣprāpyaṃ dhruvaṃ siddhipradaṃ śubham \|\| 21 \|\|*

*śvetacampakavarṇābhāṃ śatacandrasamaprabhām \|*  
*vahniśuddhāṃśukādhānāṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitām \|\| 22 \|\|*

19-22. Narayana said – О sage, after giving this mantra and kavacha to Indra, the lord also gave him the sixteen letter mantra which protects the interest of the entire universe. With this mantra, he enlighted him on the dhydnam which is quite secret, difficult to get, beyond the riches of siddhas and sages and bestowed always the success.

## \|\| 22.23-27 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde lakṣmīstavakavacapūjā kathanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*amūlyaratnaracitakuṇḍalojjvalabhūṣaṇam \|*  
*vigratī kavarībhāraṃ mālatīmālyaśobhitam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*sahasradalapadmasthāṃ svasthāṃ ca sumanoharām \|*  
*śāntāṃ ca śrīhareḥ kāntāṃ tāṃ bhajejjagatāṃ prasūm \|\| 25 \|\|*

*dhyānenānena devendro dhyātvā lakṣmīṃ manoharām \|*  
*bhaktyā saṃpūjya tasyai ca copacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa \|\| 26 \|\|*

*stutvā'nena stavenaiva vakṣyamāṇena vāsava \|*  
*natvā varaṃ gṛhītvā ca labhiṣyasi ca nirvṛtim \|\| 27 \|\|*

23-27. I adored the goddess who has the complexion of the white jasmine flower, the lustre of the moon, clad in garments sanctified by fire, adorned with gem-studded ornaments, wearing a serene smile on her face, the one who is graceful to all his devotees, seated on a thousand petalled lotus having sound health, is charming, peaceful, beloved of lord Hari and is the mother of the universe.

## \|\| 22.28 \|\|

*stavanaṃ śṛṇu devendra mahālakṣmyāḥ sukhapradam \|*  
*kathayāmi sugopyaṃ ca triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. О Devendra, with this dhyanam the pleasant Lakshmi should be adored with devotion making offering of sixteen types and then worshipped. О Indra, by reciting of this stotra and the stuti you will get a boon and pleasure thereafter. О Devendra, I am telling you the stotra of Maha-Lakshmi which bestows welfare, is quite secretive and is difficult to get. You please listen to it.

## \|\| 22.29-30 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*devi tvāṃ stotumicchāmi na kṣamāḥ stotumīśvarāḥ \|*  
*buddheragocarāṃ sūkṣmāṃ tejorūpāṃ sanātanīm \|*  
*anivāryyaguṇāḍhyāṃ ca ko vā nirvaktumīśvaraḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*svecchāmayīṃ nirākārāṃ bhaktānugrahavigrahām \|*  
*staumi vāṅmanasāpārāṃ kiṃ vā'haṃ jagadambike \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Narayana said – О goddess I intend to offer prayers to you though even the lord is unable to eulogise you, you are mysterious, the smallest of all, the form of lustre, everlasting and extremely inexplicable. Who can define you?

## \|\| 22.31-32 \|\|

*parāṃ caturṇāṃ vedānāṃ pārabījaṃ bhavārṇave \|*  
*sarvasasyādhidevīṃ ca sarvāsāmapi sampadām \|\| 31 \|\|*

*yogināṃ caiva yogānāṃ jñānānāṃ jñānināṃ tathā \|*  
*vedānāṃ vai vedavidāṃ jananīṃ varṇayāmi kim \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. But, О mother of the universe, you move at will, are formless but take to human from for the welfare of the devotees. You are beyond mind and speech, therefore what type of prayer can I offer to you? You are beyond all the Vedas, you are the only cause of crossing the ocean of the universe, you are the goddess of all the riches and agricultural produce and the mother of the yogis, yoga and knowledge, people well-versed in the knowledge of the Vedas. How can I destroy you?

## \|\| 22.33 \|\|

*yayā vinā jagatsarvamabījaṃ niṣphalaṃ dhruvam \|*  
*yayā stanaṃdhayānāṃ ca vinā mātrā sukhaṃ bhavet \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Without whom the entire universe becomes lifeless and without grace even an infant cannot get any pleasure without the mother.

## \|\| 22.34 \|\|

*prasīda jagatāṃ mātā rakṣāsmānatikātarān \|*  
*vayaṃ tvaccaraṇāmbhoje prapannāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. You are the mother of the universe, be pleased with me. You protect the helpless people like me; we take refuge under your lotus-like feet.

## \|\| 22.35 \|\|

*namaḥ śaktisvarūpāyai jaganmātre namo namaḥ \|*  
*jñānadāyai buddhidāyai sarvadāyai namo namaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. I bow in reverence again and again to the mother of the universe who happens" to be foremost shakti; you bestow knowledge, wisdom and everything else. I bow in reverence to you.

## \|\| 22.36 \|\|

*haribhaktipradāyinyai muktidāyai namo namaḥ \|*  
*sarvajñāyai sarvadāyai mahālakṣmyai namo namaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. You bestow the worship of the lord and salvation, I bow in reverence to you. You are all knowledgeable and are Maha-Lakshmi who bestows everything. I bow in reverence to you again and again.

## \|\| 22.37 \|\|

*kuputrāḥ kutracitsanti na kutrāpi kumātaraḥ \|*  
*kutra mātā putradoṣaṃ taṃ vihāya ca gacchati \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. There can be an evil son but an evil mother is unheard of. Can the mother depart leaving aside a sinful son?

## \|\| 22.38 \|\|

*stanandhayebhya iva me he mātardehi darśanam \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kuru kṛpāsindho tvamasmānbhaktavatsale \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Therefore, О mother, like an infant you appear before me. О ocean of mercy, you always show your grace on the devotees. Be graceful towards us.

## \|\| 22.39 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ vatsa padmāyāśca śubhāvaham \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ śubhadaṃ sampadaḥpradam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. О son, thus I have imparted you the knowledge of the pleasant stotra of Padma which bestows pleasure, salvation and the essence of everything, is quite full of welfare. It grants welfare and riches.

## \|\| 22.40 \|\|

*idaṃ stotraṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ pūjākāle ca yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*mahālakṣmīrgṛhaṃ tasya na jahāti kadācana \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The one who recites the stotra at the time of adoration, the goddess Maha-Lakshmi does not depart from that place.

## \|\| 22.41 \|\|

*ityuktvā śrīharistaṃ ca tatraivāntaradhīyata \|*  
*devo jagāma kṣīrodaṃ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ tadājñayā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Thus speaking, the lord disappeared from the scene and with his permission Indra and other gods proceeded to the ocean of milk.

## Chapter 23

## \|\| 23.1-3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*indraśca guruṇā sārddhaṃ suraissaṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*jagāma śīghraṃ padmāyai tīraṃ kṣīrapayonidheḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kavacaṃ ca gale baddhvā sadratnaguṭikānvitam \|*  
*manasā stavanaṃ divyaṃ smāraṃ smaraṃ punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*te sarve bhaktiyuktāśca tuṣṭuvuḥ kamalālayām \|*  
*sāśrunetrāśca dīnāśca bhaktinamrātmakandharāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Narayana said – Indra was immensely pleased and accompanied with the gods and Brihaspati, he went to the ocean of milk for getting back Lakshmi and he had worn the kavacha placing it in a small casket, round his neck and he started reciting the divine stotra again and again in his mind.

## \|\| 23.4 \|\|

*sā teṣāṃ stavanaṃ śrutvā sadyaḥ sākṣādbabhūva ha \|*  
*sahasradalapadmasthā śatacandrasamaprabhā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Similarly, all the people present there offered prayers to Lakshmi with their minds filled with devotion and lowering their heads.

## \|\| 23.5 \|\|

*jagadvyāptaṃ suprabhayā jaganmātraṃ yayā mune \|*  
*tānuvāca jagaddhātrī hitaṃ sāraṃ yathocitam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Thereafter, hearing the prayer of the people, Lakshmi appeared before them who was seated on a thousand petalled lotus having the lustre of hundreds of moons.

## \|\| 23.6-8 \|\|

*śrī mahālakṣmīruvāca \|*  
*vatsā necchāmi vo gehāngantuṃ naivaṃ kṣamā'dhunā \|*  
*bhraṣṭāndṛṣṭvā brahmaśāpādbibhemi brahmaśāpataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*prāṇā me brāhmaṇāḥ sarve śaśvatputrādhikaṃ priyāḥ \|*  
*vipradattaṃ ca yatkiñcidupajīvyaṃ sadaiva ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

*viprā bruvantu māṃ tuṣṭā yāsyāmi bhavadājñayā \|*  
*na me pūjāṃ dhruvaṃ kartuṃ kṣamāste ca tapasvinaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. О sage, her lustre pervaded the entire universe. The mother of the universe then spoke to the gods, these words which were beneficial and appropriate besides being the essence of her speech. Maha-Lakshmi said – О son, I do not want to go to you your place. I am unable to go to your abode at the moment because I am afraid of looking at the people who are cursed by the Brahmanas who happen to be my life and are dearer to me than my sons. Therefore I depend upon whatever is given to me by the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 23.9 \|\|

*gurubhirbrāhmaṇairdevairbhikṣubhirvaiṣṇavaistathā \|*  
*yadyabhāgyaṃ bhaveddaivātte śaptāḥ santi taissadā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In case the Brahmanas pleasantly ask me to 10, only in that case can I go; otherwise they are mable to adore me even for a moment.

## \|\| 23.10 \|\|

*nārāyaṇaśca bhagavānbibheti brahmaśāpataḥ \|*  
*sarvabījaṃ ca bhagavānsarveśaśca sanātanaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. On whomsoever misfortune appears it does so because of the move of destiny. He is thus cursed by his teachers, Brahmanas, gods and ascetics.

## \|\| 23.11 \|\|

*etasminnantare brahmanbrāhmaṇā hṛṣṭamānasāḥ \|*  
*ājagmuḥ sasmitāḥ sarve jvalanto brahmatejasā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Though lord Narayana happens to be the cause of all the causes, the lord of all and is everlasting yet even he feels panicky because of the curse of the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 23.12 \|\|

*aṃgirāśca pracetāśca kratuśca bhṛgureva ca \|*  
*pulahaśca pulastyaśca marīciścātrireva ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O Brahman, at that point of time a group of delightful Brahmanas arrived there, who were shining with divine lustre and wearing a serene smile on the faces.

## \|\| 23.13-16 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanandaśca tṛtīyaśca sanātanaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāro bhagavānsākṣānnārāyaṇātmakaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*kapilaścāsuriścaiva voḍhuḥ pañcaśikhastathā \|*  
*durvāsāḥ kaśyapo'gastyo gautamaḥ kaṇva eva ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

*aurvaḥ kātyāyanaścaiva kaṇādaḥ pāṇinistathā \|*  
*mārkaṇḍeyo lomaśaśca vasiṣṭho bhagavānsvayam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇā vividhairdravyaiḥ pūjayāmāsurīśvarīm \|*  
*devāścāraṇyanaivedyairupahāreṇa bhaktitaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

13-16. They included Angira, Praceta, Kratu, Bhrigu, Pulaha, Pulastya, Marlci, Atri, Sanaka, Sanandana, Sanatana, Sanatkumara, Kapila the form of Narayana, Asuri, Vodhu, Pancasikha, Durvasa, Kashyapa, Agastya, Gautama, Kanva, Aurva, Katyayana, Kanada, Panini, Markandeya, Lomasha and Vasistha.

## \|\| 23.17-18 \|\|

*stutvā munīndrāstāṃ bhaktyā cakrurārādhanaṃ mudā \|*  
*āgaccha devabhavanaṃ martyaṃ ca jagadambike \|\| 17 \|\|*

*teṣāṃ tadvacanaṃ śrutvā tānuvāca jagatprasūḥ \|*  
*parituṣṭā gāmukī ca nirbhayā brāhmaṇājñayā \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Thereafter all the Brahmanas adored goddess Lakshmi offering many presents. The gods in return offered presents and naivedya with devotion. The sages adored her with devotion and delightfully said, "O mother of the universe, you kindly be graceful to visit the houses of the gods and the humans." On hearing the words of the sages, Lakshmi the mother of the universe with the permission of the Brahmanas thought of coming on earth and said to Brahmanas.

## \|\| 23.19 \|\|

*śrīmahālakṣmīruvāca \|*  
*gṛhānyāsyāmi devānāṃ yuṣmākaṃ cājñayā dvijāḥ \|*  
*yeṣāṃ gehaṃ na gacchāmi śṛṇudhvaṃ bhārateṣu ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Maha-Lakshmi said – О Brahmanas I am going to the abode of gods with your permission, but I am going to tell you the details about the people whom I shall not visit in the land of Bharata. You please listen to me.

## \|\| 23.20 \|\|

*sthirā puṇyavatāṃ gehe sunītipathavedinām \|*  
*gṛhasthānāṃ nṛpāṇāṃ vā putravatpālayāmi tān \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. I shall firmly reside in the houses of the meritorious people and those who move on the noble path and whether they are kings or householders. Living with them, I shall take care of them like a mother.

## \|\| 23.21 \|\|

*yaṃ yaṃ ruṣṭo gururdevo mātā tātaśca bāndhavāḥ \|*  
*atithiḥ pitṛlokaśca yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. But I shall not go to the places of the people with whom the teachers, the gods, the parents, the brothers, the guests and the manes, are not happy.

## \|\| 23.22 \|\|

*mithyāvādī ca yaḥ śaśvadanadhyāyī ca yaḥ sadā \|*  
*sattvahīnaśca duśśīlo na gehaṃ tasya yāmyaham \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Those who always speak falsehood, the one who does not study, is untruthful and is of wicked nature, I shall never visit the houses of such people.

## \|\| 23.23 \|\|

*satyahīnaḥ sthāpyahārī mithyāsākṣyapradāyakaḥ \|*  
*viśvāsaghnaḥ kṛtaghno yo yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Those who are not truthful, betray the trust, give false evidence, are betrayers of the faith and are ungrateful, I do not visit the places of such people.

## \|\| 23.24 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde gaṇapatergajāsyatvakāraṇa lakṣmībrāhmaṇavirodhādi lakṣmīcaritrakathanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Those who are extremely worried, panicky, surrounded by the enemies, extremely beautiful, debtors, extremely miserly. I do not visit the houses of such sinners.

## \|\| 23.25-27 \|\|

*dīkṣāhīnaśca śokārtto mandadhīḥ strījitaḥ sadā \|*  
*na yāmyapi kadā gehaṃ puṃścalyāḥ patiputrayoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*puṃlyannamavīrānnaṃ yo bhuddhe kāmadaḥ sadā \|*  
*Rtra bhoje tadyājī tadehaṃ naivayāgyaham \|\| 26 \|\|*

*yo durvākkalahāviṣṭaḥ kaliḥ śaśvadyadālaye \|*  
*strī pradhānā gṛhe yasya yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 27 \|\|*

25-27. Those who are devoid of diksa, grief-stricken, foolish, defeated by woman, husbands or sons of wicked persons, I do not visit the houses of such people. Those who eat a food of the wicked woman, those who eat the food of a widow, having no son or husband, the one who eats the food of a Shudra and the one who performed yajna of a Shudra, I do not visit the houses of such people.

## \|\| 23.28 \|\|

*yatra nāsti hareḥ pūjā tadīyaguṇakīrttanam \|*  
*notsukastatpraśaṃsāyāṃ yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The one who speaks harsh words, is quarrelsome and the house in which Kali always resides, the one which is dominated by a lady, I never visit such houses.

## \|\| 23.29 \|\|

*kanyānnavedavikretā naraghātī ca hiṃsakaḥ \|*  
*narakāgārasadṛśaṃ yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The places were there is no adoration of gods and no recitation of his name and no prayers are offered to the lord, I do not visit such houses.

## \|\| 23.30-33 \|\|

*mātaraṃ pitaraṃ bhāryāṃ gurupatnīṃ guruṃ sutam \|*  
*anāthāṃ bhaginīṃ kanyāmananyāśrayabāndhavān \|\| 30 \|\|*

*kārpaṇyādyo na puṣṇāti sañcayaṃ kurute sadā \|*  
*tadgehānnarakāgārānyāmi tānna munīśvarāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*daśanaṃ vasanaṃ yasya samalaṃ rūkṣamastakam \|*  
*vikṛtau grāsahāsau ca yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 32 \|\|*

*mūtraṃ purīṣamutsṛjya yastatpaśyati mandadhīḥ \|*  
*yaḥ śete snigdhapādena yāmi tasya na mandiram \|\| 33 \|\|*

30-33. Those who sell away their daughters, food, Vedas, are killers of human beings, terrorists and such of the houses which are like hell, are never visited by me.
