*yogena yogināṃ sṛṣṭa svecchayā śaṃkareṇa ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

*śilpinā guruṇā svape na dṛṣṭhaṃ viścakarmaṇā \|*  
*janubhiravaṣaṭitaṃ brahamyogaduṣaṭaranirāmayaiḥ\|\| 10 \|\|*

*sarovaraśatardivayaiḥ padmarājīvirājitaiḥ \|*  
*puṣpodyānāyutairyuktaṃ sadā cātisuśobhitam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*maṇīndrasāraracitaiḥ śobhitairmaṇivedibhiḥ \|*  
*rājamārgaśatairdivyaiḥ sarvataḥ paribhūṣitam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*maṇīndrāsāranirmāṇaśatakoṭigṛhairyutam \|*  
*nānācitravicitrāḍhyairmaṇīndrakalaśojjvalaiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

7-13. It was surrounded by kalpavrksas which fulfil the desires of all, besides innumerable herds of Kamadhenu cows, innumerable forests of Parijata trees, infatuated with black-wasps always greedy for honey, filled with freest tree leaves and the sweet notes of cuckoos. It was built as per the desires of Shiva and the yogis. Such a type of construction had not been witnessed to even by Vishvakarma, the teacher of the architects. О Brahmana, Shivaloka was surrounded by yogis and healthy wild animals.

## \|\| 29.14 \|\|

*tanmadhyadeśe ramye ca dadarśa śaṅkarālayam \|*  
*maṇīndrasāraracitaprākāraṃ sumanoharam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. There were hundreds of divine pools with lines of lotus flowers and orchards, which added to the beauty of the place.

## \|\| 29.15-16 \|\|

*atyūrdhvamambarasparśi kṣīranīranibhaṃ param \|*  
*ṣoḍaśadvārasaṃyuktaṃ śobhitaṃ śatamandiraiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*amūlyaratnaracitai ratnasopānabhūṣitaiḥ \|*  
*ratnastambhakapāṭaiśca hīrakeṇa pariṣkṛtaiḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. There were many pedestals made of gems and the roads were also decorated with gems besides hundreds of dwelling places which were made of the best of precious gems and the astonishing type of decorated bases.

## \|\| 29.17 \|\|

*māṇikyajālamālābhiḥ sadratnakalaśojjvalaiḥ \|*  
*nānāvicitracitreṇa citritaiḥ sumanoharaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He found the abode of lord Shiva surrounded by beautiful buildings which were surrounded by a beautiful boundary wall.

## \|\| 29.18-19 \|\|

*ālayasya purastatra siṃhadvāraṃ dadarśa saḥ \|*  
*ratnendrasārakhacitakapāṭaiśca virājitaiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*śobhitaṃ vedikābhiśca bāhyābhyantarataḥ sadā \|*  
*racitābhiḥ padmarāgairmahāmarakatairgṛham \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. It was quite high almost touching the sky, white in colour like milk and had sixteen entrance gates besides other buildings. It had charming pillars and doors which were studded with gems.

## \|\| 29.20 \|\|

*nānāprakāracitreṇa citritaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*karālarūpāvadrākṣīd dvārapālau bhayaṃkarau \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. There were beds of fine decorations with sapphire gems, best of gems and many types of beautiful paintings.

## \|\| 29.21-26 \|\|

*mahākarāladantāsyau viraktau raktalocanau \|*  
*dagdhaśailapratīkāśau mahābalaparākramau \|\| 21 \|\|*

*vibhūtibhūṣitāṃgau ca vyāghracarmāmbarau varau \|*  
*piṅgalākṣau viśālākṣau jaṭilau ca trilocanau \|\| 22 \|\|*

*triśūlapaṭṭiśadharau jvalantau brahmatejasā \|*  
*tau dṛṣṭvā manasā bhītastrastaḥ kiñciduvāca ha \|\| 23 \|\|*

*vinayena vinītaśca durvinītau mahābalau \|*  
*ātmanaḥ sarvavṛttāntaṃ kathayāmāsa tatpuraḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*viprasya vacanaṃ śrutvā kṛpāyuktau babhūvatuḥ \|*  
*gṛhītvā''jñāṃ caradvārā śaṃkarasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*praveṣṭumājñāṃ dadaturīśvarānucarau varau \|*  
*bhṛgustadājñāmādāya praviveśa hariṃ smaran \|\| 26 \|\|*

21-26. He found again the main gates in front of the palace, which had a gate in which the best of the gems were studded. He then saw the palace having the pedestal decorated with gems and precious stones besides beautiful paintings. He found two terrific looking gatekeepers guarding the gate. Their teeth were protruding, were terrific to look at, with red and deformed eyes. They looked like a bumt-out mountain, were quite valorous and great warriors. They had applied ashes on their bodies, were clad in tiger skins, having broad pinkish eyes, wearing matted locks of hair on the head, having three eyes and held a strident and a pattisa in their hands and appeared like burning fire flames. The sight of those gate-keepers frightened Parashurama but inspite of that he spoke to them.

## \|\| 29.27 \|\|

*pratyekaṃ ṣoḍaśa dvāro dadarśa sumanoharāḥ \|*  
*dvārapālairniyuktāśca nānācitravicitritāḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Becoming meek and humble, appearing miserable, he narrated the pathetic story before the gate-keepers.

## \|\| 29.28-29 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tā mahadāścaryyādapaśyacchūlinaḥ sabhām \|*  
*nānāsiddhagaṇākīrṇāṃ maharṣigaṇasevitām \|\| 28 \|\|*

*pārijātasugandhāḍhyavāyunā surabhīkṛtām \|*  
*dadarśa tatra deveśaṃ śaṅkaraṃ candraśekharam \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. On hearing the story of the Brahmana both of them felt pity and getting permission from lord Shiva, they allowed him to have an audience with the lord. At the command of the lord Parashurama reciting his name entered the chamber of Shiva.

## \|\| 29.30 \|\|

*triśūlapaṭṭiśadharaṃ vyāghracarmāmbaraṃ param \|*  
*vibhūtibhūṣitāṃgaṃ taṃ nāgayajñopavītinam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Thus he crossed sixteen gates, all of which were quite charming to look at and were guarded by astonishing types of gate-keepers.

## \|\| 29.31-33 \|\|

*mahāśivaṃ śivakaraṃ śivabījaṃ śivāśrayam \|*  
*ātmārāmaṃ pūrṇakāmaṃ sūryyakoṭisamaprabham \|\| 31 \|\|*

*īṣaddhāsyaṃ prasannāsyaṃ bhaktānugrahakātaram \|*  
*śaśvajjyotissvarūpaṃ ca lokānugrahavigraham \|\| 32 \|\|*

*dhṛtavantaṃ jaṭājālaṃ dakṣakanyāsamanvitam \|*  
*tapasāṃ phaladātāraṃ dātāraṃ sarvasampadām \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. After witnessing all the gate-keepers he entered the court of lord Shiva which was surrounded by the siddha people and ascetics. The breeze carrying the fragrance of Parijata flowers pervaded the place. He found there Shiva, the lord of the gods, wearing a crescent on his head. He was holding a trident and pattisa, wore the skin of a tiger with all the limbs plastered with ashes, wearing the yajnopavita of nagas and was seated in sukhdsana on the gem-studded lion-throne, adorned with all the ornaments studded with gems.

## \|\| 29.34-44 \|\|

*śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ pañca vaktraṃ trilocanam \|*  
*guhyaṃ brahma pravocantaṃ śiṣyebhyastattvamudrayā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*stūyamānaṃ ca yogīndraiḥ siddhendraiḥ parisevitam \|*  
*pārṣadapravaraiśśaśvatsevitaṃ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*dhyāyamānaṃ paraṃ brahma paripūrṇatamaṃ param \|*  
*svecchāmayaṃ guṇātītaṃ jarāmṛtyuharaṃ param \|\| 36 \|\|*

*jyotīrūpaṃ ca sarvādyaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|*  
*dhyāyantaṃ paramānandaṃ pulakāñcitavigraham \|*  
*susvaraṃ sāśrunetraṃ tamudgāyantaṃ guṇārṇavam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*bhūtendrairvai rudragaṇaiḥ kṣetrapālaiśca veṣṭitam \|*  
*mūrdhnā nanāma paraśurāmo dṛṣṭvā tamādarāt \|\| 38 \|\|*

*tadvāme kārttikeyaṃ ca dakṣiṇe ca gaṇeśvaram \|*  
*nandīśvaraṃ mahākālaṃ vīrabhadraṃ ca tatpuraḥ \|*  
*aṃke dadarśa kāntāṃ tāṃ gaurīṃ śailendrakanyakām \|\| 39 \|\|*

*nanāma sarvānmūrdhnā ca bhaktyā ca parayā mudā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā haraṃ paraṃ toṣātstotuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 40 \|\|*

*sagadgadapadaṃ dīnassāśrunetro'tikātaraḥ \|*  
*kṛtāñjalipuṭaḥ śāntaḥ śokārtaḥ śokanāśanam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*īśa tvāṃ stotumicchāmi sarvathā stotumakṣamaḥ \|*  
*akṣarākṣayabījaṃ ca kiṃ vā staumi nirīhakam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*na yojanāṃ karttumīśo deveśaṃ staumi mūḍhadhīḥ \|*  
*vedā na śaktā yaṃ stotuṃ kastvāṃ stotumiheśvaraḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*vāgbuddhimanasāṃ dūraṃ sārātsāraṃ parātparam \|*  
*jñānamātreṇa sādhyaṃ ca siddhaṃ siddhairniṣevitam \|\| 44 \|\|*

34-44. He bestowed welfare, the seed of prosperity, refuge of welfare, the great soul, the one who fulfils all the desires, having the lustre of crores of suns. Wearing a serene smile on his face, he was having a delightful face. He is the one who is always merciful towards his devotees, always having the form of flame and the one who takes to the human forms for the welfare of all, wearing matted locks of hair, accompanied by Gauri. He is the result of tapas and bestower of all the riches, having the spotless complexion like a crystal gem, having five faces, three eyes and was imparting the divine knowledge to his pupils in tattva and the divine knowledge of Brahmana. He was adored by the yogis, served the siddhas all round, served by the attendants with white fly-whisks, form of flame and reciting the name of blissful lord Krishna, who is primeval and beyond Prakriti, meditated upon by people as MahaShiva, getting emotional. They were loudly reciting the name of the lord who happens to be oceans of virtues and the tears were flowing from their eyes. He was surrounded by bhiitas, Rudras and ksetra-palas. Thereafter Parashurama bowed in reverence to him; Kartikeya appeared to his left, Ganesha appeared to his right, while Nandikeshvara, Mahakala and Virbhadra were seated in front of him. Parvati the daughter of the king of mountains was seated in his lap. With his mind filled with devotion Paras'urama offered his salutation to lord Shiva bowing his head. Finding Shiva there, Parashurama was satisfied and he started offering prayers to him. He was feeling miserable, his eyes were filled with tears and he stood there with folded hands. He then started reciting the glory of lord Shiva with a choked voice.

## \|\| 29.45 \|\|

*yamākāśamivādyantamadhyahīnaṃ tathā'vyayam \|*  
*viśvatantramatantraṃ ca svatantraṃ tantrabījakam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Parashurama said – О lord, I intend to eulogise you but am unable to do so. What type of prayer can I offer to the one who is imperishable, eternal and without any desire?

## \|\| 29.46 \|\|

*dhyānāsādhyaṃ durārādhyamatisādhyaṃ kṛpānidhim \|*  
*trāhi māṃ karuṇāsindho dīnabandho'tidīnakam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. I cannot think about it but still I venture to do so being an utterly foolish person because the one whose glory is beyond the reach of the Vedas, then who else can recite your glory.

## \|\| 29.47 \|\|

*adya me saphalaṃ janma jīvitaṃ ca sujīvitam \|*  
*svapne'pyadṛṣṭaṃ bhaktaiścādhunā paśyāmi cakṣuṣā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. You are beyond speech, wisdom and the mind; you are the essence of the essence, beyond everything. You are the one who can be achieved only by divine knowledge. You are the siddha and are served by the siddhas.

## \|\| 29.48-49 \|\|

*śakrādayaḥ suragaṇāḥ kalayā yasya sambhavāḥ \|*  
*carācarāḥ kalāṃśena taṃ namāmi maheśvaram \|\| 48 \|\|*

*strīrūpaṃ klībarūpaṃ ca pauruṣaṃ ca bibharti yaḥ \|*  
*sarvādhāraṃ sarvarūpaṃ taṃ namāmi maheśvaram \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. You are without beginning, middle or end, like the sky, without destruction, tantra of the universe, beyond tantra, independent, the seed of tantra, difficult to achieve even by meditation, difficult to meditate upon and are most merciful. Therefore, О ocean of mercy, О lover of the down-trodden, I am in a miserable condition, therefore, you kindly protect me.

## \|\| 29.50 \|\|

*yaṃ bhāskarasvarūpaṃ ca śaśirūpaṃ hutāśanam \|*  
*jalarūpaṃ vāyurūpaṃ taṃ namāmi maheśvaram \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Му life has been a successful today because the one who is beyond the reach of the devotees even in dream, him I am visualising with my own eyes.

## \|\| 29.51 \|\|

*anantaviśvasṛṣṭīnāṃ saṃhartāraṃ bhayaṃkaram \|*  
*kṣaṇena līlāmātreṇa taṃ namāmi maheśvaram \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. From whose race, the gods like Indra have emerged and all the movable and immovable things on earth are his reflection alone. I bow in reverence to such a type of Maheshvara. I bow in reverence to Maheshvara who is having the form of a man, a woman, impotent, possessing great prowess and the base of all; I bow in reverence to Maheshvara.

## \|\| 29.52 \|\|

*yaḥ kālaḥ kālakālaśca kalirbījaṃ ca kālajaḥ \|*  
*ajaḥ prajaśca yaḥ sarvastaṃ namāmi maheśvaram \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The one who is the form of the sun, the moon, fire, water and the wind, I bow in reverence to the same Maheshvara.

## \|\| 29.53 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā sa bhṛguḥ papāta caraṇāmbuje \|*  
*āśiṣaṃ ca dadau tasmai suprasanno babhūva saḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The one who can destroy the entire universe in an instant, I bow in reverence to the same Maheshvara. Thus speaking Parashurama fell at the feet of Shiva who getting delighted blessed him. Whosoever recites the stotra composed by Parashurama, he is relieved of all sins and he ultimately achieves Shivaloka.

## Chapter 30

## \|\| 30.1 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*kastvaṃ vaṭo kasya putraḥ kva vāsaḥ stavanaṃ katham \|*  
*kiṃ vā te'haṃ kariṣyāmi vāñchitaṃ vada sāmpratam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Shiva said – "O child, who are you, who is your father and where do you live? Why are you eulogising me? You express your desire to me?

## \|\| 30.2 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*śokākulaṃ tvāṃ paśyāmi vimanaskaṃ suvismitam \|*  
*vayasā'tiśiśuṃ śāntaṃ guṇena guṇināṃ varam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Parvati said – "I find you filled with grief, sad and surprised, you resemble a child in age but because of your peaceful nature and other virtues, you are comparable to the virtuous people."

## \|\| 30.3-4 \|\|

*bhṛguruvāca \|*  
*jamadagnisuto'haṃ ca bhṛguvaṃśasamudbhavaḥ \|*  
*reṇukā'mbā me paraśurāmo'haṃ nāmataḥ prabho \|\| 3 \|\|*

*krīṇīhi māṃ dayāsindho vidyāpaṇyena kiṃkaram \|*  
*tvāmīśaḥ śaraṇāpannaṃ rakṣa māṃ dīnavatsala \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. Bhrigu said – "О lord, I am the son of Jamadagni and am born in the race of Bhrigu; the name of my mother is Renuka and my name is Parashurama. О lord of the universe, by imparting divine knowledge to me, you take me as your pupil. О lord, О uplifter of the down-trodden, I take refuge under you. Therefore, you protect me.

## \|\| 30.5-6 \|\|

*mṛgayāmāgataṃ bhūpaṃ pitā me copavāsinam \|*  
*cakārātithyamānīya kapilādattavastunā \|\| 5 \|\|*

*rājā taṃ kapilālobhādghātayāmāsa mandadhīḥ \|*  
*kapilā taṃ mṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā golokaṃ ca jagāma sā \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Finding the king who had come for hunting, hungry, my father welcomed him as a guest and served him with food provided by the Kapila cow. Thereafter, the foolish king became desirous of possessing the Kapila cow and he killed my father. Kapila on her part, finding my father dead, went back to Goloka.

## \|\| 30.7-9 \|\|

*mātā'nugamanaṃ cakre hyanātho'haṃ ca sāmpratam \|*  
*tvaṃ me pitā śivā mātā rakṣa māṃ putravatprabho \|\| 7 \|\|*

*mayā kṛtā pratijñā ca śokenaivātiduṣkarā \|*  
*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kariṣyāmi mahīmiti \|\| 8 \|\|*

*kārtavīryyaṃ haniṣyāmi samare tātaghātakam \|*  
*ityetatparipūrṇaṃ me bhagavānkartumarhati \|\| 9 \|\|*

7-9. The mother also accompanied the father and I am an orphan at the moment. Therefore, О lord, you are my father at the moment and Parvati happens to be my mother. You protect me like your own son. With my mind filled with grief I have taken a difficult vow that I shall rid the earth of Kshatriyas, twenty one times in the battlefield and shall also destroy Kartavirya who had killed my father. O lord you, enable me to fulfil this vow.

## \|\| 30.10 \|\|

*brāhmaṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā dṛṣṭvā durgāmukhaṃ haraḥ \|*  
*babhūvānamravaktraśca sā ca śuṣkauṣṭhatālukā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. On listening to the words of the Brahmana, Shiva looked at the face of Durga and the palate of Parvati dried up.

## \|\| 30.11-12 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*tapasvinvipraputrastvaṃ nirbhūpāṃ kartumicchasi \|*  
*trissaptakṛtvaḥ kopena sāhasaste mahānbaṭo \|\| 11 \|\|*

*hantumicchasi niḥśastraḥ sahasrārjunamīśvaram \|*  
*bhrūbhaṅgalīlayā yasya rāvaṇasya parājayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. Parvati said – "O tapasvi, because of your anger, you want to relieve the kings of the earth twenty one times. О ascetic this is a great courage of yours that you want to kill Sahasrarjuna without any weapon. He is the one by the side glance of whom even Ravana was defeated.

## \|\| 30.13-14 \|\|

*tasmai pradattaṃ dattena śrīhareḥ kavacaṃ baṭo \|*  
*śaktiravyartharūpā ca yayā te hiṃsitaḥ pitā \|\| 13 \|\|*

*harermmantraṃ saṃstavanaṃ yaḥ paṭhecca divāniśam \|*  
*ko vā śaknoti taṃ hantuṃ na paśyāmīha bhūtale \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. O ascetic, he has been provided with a kavacha of the lord by the Dattatreya and there is no let up in his prowess which never decreases because of which he has killed your father. He is the one who recites the name of the lord throughout day and night. Who can kill him on earth? I don’t have any such person known to me.

## \|\| 30.15 \|\|

*aye vipra gṛhaṃ gaccha kiṃ kariṣyati śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*anye bhūpāśca madbhṛtyāḥ kā bhīsteṣāṃ mayi sthite \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О Brahmana, you get back to your home. What shall Shiva be able to do in this case, the rest of the kings are my devotees and why should they be afraid when I am there."

## \|\| 30.16 \|\|

*kālyuvāca \|*  
*aye vipra baṭo jālma nirbhūpānkartumicchasi \|*  
*yathā hi vāmanaścandraṃ kareṇāhartumicchati \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Bhadrakali said – "O Brahmana ascetic, you are foolish that you intend to deprive the earth of her rulers. It is like the one who is dwarf but intends to catch hold of the moon.

## \|\| 30.17 \|\|

*kṛtayajñānmahāpuṇyānmahābalaparākramān \|*  
*digambarasahāyena madbhṛtyānhantumicchasi \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Do you intend to kill my devotees who are quite virtuous and have become meritorious with the help of Shiva."

## \|\| 30.18-20 \|\|

*sa tayorvacanaṃ śrutvā rurodoccaiśca śokataḥ \|*  
*sahasā puratasteṣāṃ prāṇāṃstyaktuṃ samudyataḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*viprasya rodanaṃ śrutvā śaṃkaraḥ karuṇānidhiḥ \|*  
*paśyandurgāṃ ca kālīṃ ca jñātvā''śayamatho vibhuḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*tayoranumatiṃ prāpya sarveśo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*jamadagnisutaṃ sadyaḥ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 20 \|\|*

18-20. Parashurama listening to the words of both of them started crying aloud and in their very presence he got ready to end his life. Hearing the cries of Parashurama the merciful lord Shiva looked at Kali and Durga both and with their permission, he started speaking to Parashurama.

## \|\| 30.21 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*adyaprabhṛti he vatsa tvaṃ me putrasamo mahān \|*  
*dāsyāmi mantraṃ guhyaṃ te triṣu lokeṣu durllabham \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Shiva said – "O son, from today onwards you will be like my son and I shall bestow on you the inaccessible secret mantra of the three world.

## \|\| 30.22 \|\|

*evaṃbhūtaṃ ca kavacaṃ dāsyāmi paramādbhutam \|*  
*līlayā matprasādena kārttavīryaṃ haniṣyasi \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. I will also give you an astonishing kavacha with the use of which you will be able to kill Kartavirya.

## \|\| 30.23-25 \|\|

*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kariṣyasi mahīṃ dvija \|*  
*jagatte yaśasā pūrṇaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*ityuktvā śaṃkarastasmai dadau mantraṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*trailokyavijayaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*stavaṃ pūjāvidhānaṃ ca puraścaraṇapūrvakam \|*  
*mantrasiddheranuṣṭhānaṃ yathāvanniyamakramam \|\| 25 \|\|*

23-25. О Brahmana, you will relieve the earth of the Kshatriyas twenty one times and the glory of your prowess will be spread in the universe. There is no doubt about it." Thus speaking lord Shiva gave him the inaccessible mantra called Trilokyavijaya the astonishing kavacha, stotra and the method of adoration, besides purascarana-mantra siddhi and the relevant regulations to be followed for making the same a success.

## \|\| 30.26 \|\|

*siddhisthānaṃ kālasaṃkhyaṃ kathayāmāsa nārada \|*  
*vedavedāṅgādikaṃ ca pāṭhayāmāsa tatkṣaṇam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О Narada, he then told him the name and time of success. He imparted to him the knowledge of the Vedas.

## \|\| 30.27-28 \|\|

*nāgapāśaṃ pāśupataṃ brahmāstraṃ ca sudurlabham \|*  
*nārāyaṇāstramāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ vāruṇaṃ tathā \|\| 27 \|\|*

*gāndharvaṃ gāruḍaṃ caiva jṛmbhaṇāstraṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*gadāṃ śaktiṃ ca paraśuṃ śūlamavyarthamuttamam \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. He also imparted to him the knowledge of Nagastra, Pasupata, inaccessible Brahmastra, Narayanastra, the fire weapon, the wind weapon, the water weapon, Gandharvastra Jrmbhanastra, besides the use of gada, shakti, pars'u and the indestructible trident.

## \|\| 30.29-31 \|\|

*nānāprakāraśastrāstramantraṃ ca vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*śastrāstrāṇāṃ ca saṃhāraṃ tūṇī cākṣayasāyakau \|\| 29 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde paraśurāmasya śivadattāstraśastrādiprāptivarṇanaṃ nāma triṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*rakṣaṇaṃ ca svasainyānāṃ parasainyavimardanam \|*  
*nānāprakāramatulamupāyaṃ raṇasaṃkaṭe \|*  
*saṃhāre mohinīṃ vidyāṃ dadau mṛtyuharāṃ haraḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. He also imparted the knowledge of the use of various types of weapons, mantras, the method of their use, the quivers and infallible arrows, the methods of protecting himself, for achieving success in the battle-field, many types of illusory wars, uttering of humkara, reciting of mantras, protection of his army and destruction of the enemy army, the method of protecting himself at the time of danger in the battle-field and the knowledge of over-coming death, besides the knowledge of illusion.

## \|\| 30.32 \|\|

*sthitvā ciraṃ gurorvāṃse sarvavidyāṃ vibodhya saḥ \|*  
*tīrthe kṛtvā mantrasiddhiṃ tāṃśca natvā jagāma saḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Remaining with the teacher for a long time, he fully acquainted himself with the use of all the weapons achieving the entire knowledge. Thereafter, bowing in reverence to everyone, Parashurama left the place.

## Chapter 31

## \|\| 31.1-2 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*bhagavañchrotumicchāmi kiṃ mantraṃ bhagavānharaḥ \|*  
*kṛpayā'dātparaśurāmāya stotraṃ ca varma ca \|\| 1 \|\|*

*ko vā'sya mantrasyārādhyaḥ kiṃ phalaṃ kavacasya ca \|*  
*stavanasya phalaṃ kiṃ vā tadbhavānvaktumarhati \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narada said – О lord, which one of the mantra, stotra and kavacha was given by the lord to Parashurama, who is prime god of the kavacha, what reward does that kavacha bestow on the devotee and what is the result of the kavacha you kindly let me know?"

## \|\| 31.3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*mantrārādhyo hi bhagavānparipūrṇatamaḥ svayam \|*  
*golokanāthaḥ śrīkṛṣṇo gopagopīśvaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Narayana said – Lord Krishna who is complete in himself and is the lord of the Goloka besides the cowherds and cowherdesses, himself happens to be the prime god of the kavacha.

## \|\| 31.4-6 \|\|

*trailokyavijayaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*stavarājaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ bhūtiyogasamudbhavam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*mantraṃ kalpataruṃ nāma sarvakāmaphalapradam \|*  
*dadau paraśurāmāya ratnaparvatasannidhau \|\| 5 \|\|*

*svayaṃprabhānadītīre pārijātavanāntare \|*  
*āśrame lokadevasya mādhavasya ca sannidhau \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. The extremely astonishing kavacha named Trailokyavijaya emerged with the combination of fortune and bestows great merit on one who recites it. Its mantra is kalpataru which fulfils all the desires. He gave it to Paras'urama in the forest of Parijata of Devaloka near the Madhavasrama located on the bank of Svayamprabha river.

## \|\| 31.7-10 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*vatsāgaccha mahābhāga bhṛguvaṃśasamudbhava \|*  
*putrādhiko'si premṇā me kavacagrahaṇaṃ kuru \|\| 7 \|\|*

*śṛṇu rāma pravakṣyāmi brahmāṇḍe paramādbhutam \|*  
*trailokyavijayaṃ nāma śrīkṛṣṇasya jayāvaham \|\| 8 \|\|*

*śrīkṛṣṇena purā dattaṃ goloke rādhikāśrame \|*  
*rāsamaṇḍalamadhye ca mahyaṃ vṛndāvane vane \|\| 9 \|\|*

*atiguhyataraṃ tattvaṃ sarvamantraughavigraham \|*  
*puṇyātpuṇyataraṃ caiva paraṃ snehādvadāmi te \|\| 10 \|\|*

7-10. Shiva said – О son, О virtuous one born in the race of Bhrigu, you come here and receive this kavacha, you will be dearer and more loveable to me than my own son. О Paras'urama, I am imparting to you the knowledge of the kavacha and Trailokyavijaya of Shri Krishna which is an astonishing one and provides victory in the universe. You listen to it. In the earlier times lord Krishna imparted the knowledge of the same to me in Goloka in the Rdsamandala of Radhikasrama, located in the Vrindavana forest. This is the essence of all the mantras and is quite meritorious. I am speaking it out to you because of my love for you.

## \|\| 31.11-12 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanāddevī mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī \|*  
*śumbhaṃ niśumbhaṃ mahiṣaṃ raktabījaṃ jaghāna ha \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yaddhṛtvā'haṃ ca jagatāṃ saṃharttā sarvatattvavit \|*  
*avadhyaṃ tripuraṃ pūrvaṃ durantamapi līlayā \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. By bearing it by a person or by reciting it, the Mula-Prakriti of the lord killed the demons like Sumbha, Nisumbha, Mahisasura and Raktabija. Possessing its knowledge I become aware of all the Tattavas and destroyed the entire universe. I had killed the terrific demon Tripurasura in no time.

## \|\| 31.13 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādbrahmā sasṛje sṛṣṭimuttamām \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā bhagavāñcheṣo vidhatte viśvameva ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. By holding it and reciting it Brahma created the best of the universe and by holding and reciting it, Shesha bears the load of the entire universe.

## \|\| 31.14 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā kūrmarājaśca śeṣaṃ dhatte hi līlayā \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā bhagavānvāyurviśvādhāro vibhuḥ svayam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. By hearing it the tortoise playfully carries the load of Shesha. By bearing it, the wind-god pervades it in the universe.

## \|\| 31.15 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā varuṇaḥ siddhaḥ kuberaśca dhaneśvaraḥ \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādindro devānāmadhipaḥ svayam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. By bearing it, Varuna became the siddhas, Kubera became the lord of riches and by holding and reciting it, Indra became the lord of god.

## \|\| 31.16 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā bhāti bhuvane tejorāśiḥ svayaṃ raviḥ \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanāccandro mahābalaparākramaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. By holding it Stirya gained the lustre illumining the universe. By holding it, the moon became valorous and strong.

## \|\| 31.17 \|\|

*agastyaḥ sāgarānsapta yaddhṛtvā paṭhanātpapau \|*  
*cakāra tejasā jīrṇaṃ daityaṃ vātāpisaṃjñakam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. By holding it Agastya drank the water of the seven oceans. By its glory, he also consumed the demon named Vatapi and destroyed him.

## \|\| 31.18 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanāddevī sarvādhārā vasundharā \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanātpūtā gaṅgā bhuvanapāvanī \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. By holding and reciting it, the goddess earth became the base of everything. By holding and reciting it Ganga was purified and she became the purifier of the universe.

## \|\| 31.19-21 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā jagatāṃ sākṣī dharmo dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*sarvavidyādhidevī sā yacca dhṛtvā sarasvatī \|\| 19 \|\|*

*yaddhṛtvā jagatāṃ lakṣmīrannadātrī parātparā \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādvedānsāvitrī sā suṣāva ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

*vedāśca dharmmavaktāro yaddhṛtvā paṭhanād bhṛgo \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanācchuddhastejasvī havyavāhanaḥ \|*  
*sanatkumāro bhagavānyaddhṛtvā jñānināṃ varaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. By holding it dharma became adorable by all the religious people and became the witness of the universe. By holding it, Sarasvati became the bestower of gems and riches and became the better of the best. By bearing and reciting it, Savitri created the Vedas which became the speakers for dharma. By bearing and reciting it, the fire-god became purified and glorious and by bearing it Sanatkumara became the best of the intellectuals.

## \|\| 31.22-25 \|\|

*dātavyaṃ kṛṣṇabhaktāya sādhave ca mahātmane \|*  
*śaṭhāya paraśiṣyāya dattvā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt \|\| 22 \|\|*

*trailokyavijayasyāsya kavacasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca gāyatrī devo rāseśvaraḥ svayam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*trailokyavijayaprāptau viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|*  
*parātparaṃ ca kavacaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|\| 24 \|\|*

*praṇavo me śiraḥ pātu śrīkṛṣṇāya namaḥ sadā \|*  
*pāyātkapālaṃ kṛṣṇāya svāhā pañcākṣaraḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

22-25. Therefore the knowledge of the same is imparted alone to the noble people and the devotees of lord Krishna. Because by parting its knowledge to the wicked people one has to face death. Prajapati happens to be the Rsi of the kavacha named Trailokyavijaya and lord Krishna happens to be its god. One has to take a resolve for getting victorious in the three worlds. This kavacha is beyond everything and is inaccessible in the three worlds, … this mantra should protect my head and the five letter mantra чяпя should protect my skull.

## \|\| 31.26 \|\|

*kṛṣṇeti pātu netre ca kṛṣṇa svāheti tārakam \|*  
*haraye nama ityevaṃ bhrūlatāṃ pātu me sadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Krishna should protect both my eyes, … should protect my eye-balls. … should always protect my eye-brows.

## \|\| 31.27 \|\|

*oṃ goviṃdāya svāheti nāsikāṃ pātu santatam \|*  
*gopālāya namo gaṇḍau pātu me sarvataḥ sadā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. … this mantra should protect my nose .. should always protect my cheeks.

## \|\| 31.28 \|\|

*oṃ namo gopāṅganeśāya karṇau pātu sadā mama \|*  
*oṃ kṛṣṇāya namaḥ śaśvatpātu me'dharayugmakam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. … should protect my ears and ... should protect both my lips.

## \|\| 31.29 \|\|

*oṃ govindāya svāheti dantaughaṃ me sadā'vatu \|*  
*pātu kṛṣṇāya dantādho dantordhvaṃ klīṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. … should protect my teeth. should protect my lower teeth and should protect my upper teeth.

## \|\| 31.30 \|\|

*oṃ śrīkṛṣṇāya svāheti jihvikāṃ pātu me sadā \|*  
*rāseśvarāya svāheti tālukaṃ pātu me sadā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. … should protect my tongue, should protect my palate.

## \|\| 31.31 \|\|

*rādhikeśāya svāheti kaṇṭhaṃ pātu sadā mama \|*  
*namo gopāṅganeśāya vakṣaḥ pātu sadā mama \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. … should protect my neck, should protect my chest.

## \|\| 31.32 \|\|

*oṃ gopeśāya svāheti skandhaṃ pātu sadā mama \|*  
*namaḥ kiśoraveṣāya svāhā pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. … should protect my shoulders, should protect my back.

## \|\| 31.33 \|\|

*udaraṃ pātu me nityaṃ mukundāya namaḥ sadā \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ kṛṣṇāya svāheti karau pātu sadā mama \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. … should protect my belly, should protect my hands.

## \|\| 31.34 \|\|

*oṃ viṣṇave namo bāhuyugmaṃ pātu sadā mama \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ bhagavate svāhā nakharaṃ pātu me sadā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. … should protect my arms, should protect my nails.

## \|\| 31.35 \|\|

*oṃ namo nārāyaṇāyeti nakharandhraṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ padmanābhāya nābhiṃ pātu sadā mama \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. … should protect the point of my nails, should protect my navel.

## \|\| 31.36 \|\|

*oṃ sarveśāya svāheti kaṅkālaṃ pātu me sadā \|*  
*oṃ gopīramaṇāya svāhā nitambaṃ pātu me sadā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. … should protect my bones, should protect my buttocks.

## \|\| 31.37 \|\|

*oṃ gopīramaṇanāthāya pādau pātu sadā mama \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ rasikeśāya svāhā sarvaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. … should protect my feet.

## \|\| 31.38 \|\|

*oṃ keśavāya svāheti mama keśānsadā'vatu \|*  
*namaḥ kṛṣṇāya svāheti brahmarandhraṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. … should protect my hair, should protect my Brahmarandhra.

## \|\| 31.39 \|\|

*oṃ mādhavāya svāheti me lomāni sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ rasikeśāya svāhā sarvaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. … should protect my hair-pits, should protect all.

## \|\| 31.40 \|\|

*paripūrṇatamaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ prācyāṃ māṃ sarvadā'vatu \|*  
*svayaṃ golokanātho māmāgneyyāṃ diśi rakṣatu \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The complete lord Krishna should protect me from the eastern side; the lord of Goloka should protect me from the south-eastern side.

## \|\| 31.41 \|\|

*pūrṇabrahmasvarūpaśca dakṣiṇe māṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*nairṛtyāṃ pātu māṃ kṛṣṇaḥ paścime pātu māṃ hariḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The form of Puma Brahman should protect me from the southern side and a south-east should be protected by lord Krishna himself. The western-side should be protected by Hari.

## \|\| 31.42 \|\|

*govindaḥ pātu māṃ śaśvadvāyavyāṃ diśi nityaśaḥ \|*  
*uttare māṃ sadā pātu rasikānāṃ śiromaṇiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The north-east side should be protected by Govinda and the north-side should be protected by the lord of all the gopis.

## \|\| 31.43 \|\|

*aiśānyāṃ māṃ sadā pātu vṛndāvanavihārakṛt \|*  
*vṛndāvanīprāṇanāthaḥ pātu māmūrdhvadeśataḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The north-east direction should be protected by the dweller of Vrindavana. The lord of Vrindavana should protect the upper region.

## \|\| 31.44 \|\|

*sadaiva mādhavaḥ pātu balihārī mahābalaḥ \|*  
*jale sthale cāntarikṣe nṛsiṃhaḥ pātu māṃ sadā \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The immensely valorous Madhava and Baliharl should always protect me. Nrsimha should protect me in the water, on land and in the sky.

## \|\| 31.45 \|\|

*svapne jāgaraṇe śaśvatpātu māṃ mādhavaḥ sadā \|*  
*sarvāntarātmā nirliptaḥ pātu māṃ sarvato vibhuḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. He should protect me always whether sleeping or awake Vibhu the soul of everyone who is unattached should protect me from all sides.

## \|\| 31.46 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvamantraughavigraham \|*  
*trailokyavijayaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O son, thus I have imparted to you the knowledge of the kavacha named Trailokyavijaya which happens to be the essence of all the mantras and is quite an astonishing one.

## \|\| 31.47-49 \|\|

*mayā śrutaṃ kṛṣṇavaktrātpravaktavyaṃ na kasyacit \|*  
*gurumabhyarcya vidhivatkavacaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe bāhau so'pi viṣṇurna saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*sa ca bhakto vasedyatra lakṣmīrvāṇī vasettataḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*yadi syātsiddhakavaco jīvanmukto bhavettu saḥ \|*  
*niścitaṃ koṭivarṣāṇāṃ pūjāyāḥ phalamāpnuyāt \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. I heard this from the mouth of Shri Krishna himself. Therefore, you do not part with the knowledge of this to anyone, by adoring the teacher, whosoever bears this kavacha around the neck or around the arm, he is turned like Vishnu. There is no doubt about it. Whenever such a devotee recites, Lakshmi and Sarasvati also reside there. In case one achieves success with this kavacha then he is relieved of the cycle of birth and death and he earns the merit of performing puja for crores of years.

## \|\| 31.50-51 \|\|

*rājasūyasahasrāṇi vājapeyaśatāni ca \|*  
*aśvamedhāyutānyeva naramedhāyutāni ca \|\| 50 \|\|*

*mahādānāni yānyeva prādakṣiṇyaṃ bhuvastathā \|*  
*trailokyavijayasyāsya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. The merit one earns by performing a thousand Rajasuya, a hundred Vdjapeya, ten thousand Ashvamedha, ten thousand Naramedha, yajnas, performing all the great charities, going round the entire universe, do not compare one sixteenth times of the merit of Trailokyavijaya.

## \|\| 31.52 \|\|

*vratopavāsaniyamaṃ svādhyāyādhyayanaṃ tapaḥ \|*  
*snānaṃ ca sarvatīrtheṣu nāsyārhanti kalāmapi \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Even the vratas, fasting, austerities, studies, self-studies, performing of tapas and bathing at the sacred places, do not compare even with the smallest ray of the same.

## \|\| 31.53-54 \|\|

*siddhatvamamaratvaṃ ca dāsatvaṃ śrīharerapi \|*  
*yadi syātsiddhakavacaḥ sarvaṃ prāpnoti niścitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

*sa bhavetsiddhakavaco daśalakṣaṃ japettu yaḥ \|*  
*yo bhavetsiddhakavacaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa bhaveddhruvam \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. Whosoever meets with success with this kavacha, achieves the position of siddhas everlasting slavehood of lord Hari and all other pleasures of life. The one who recites the same ten lakhs of times, he meets success with this kavacha.

## \|\| 31.55 \|\|

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajetkṛṣṇaṃ sumandadhīḥ \|*  
*koṭikalpaṃ prajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Whosoever adores the lord without being fully aware of the kavacha, such a foolish fellow cannot meet with success even after adoring it for crores of kalpas.

## \|\| 31.56 \|\|

*gṛhītvā kavacaṃ vatsa mahīṃ niḥkṣatriyāṃ kuru \|*  
*trissaptakṛtvo niśśaṃkaḥ sadānando hi līlayā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О son, by bearing this kavacha, you fearlessly move on earth and rid the earth of the Kshatriyas twenty one times enjoying all the pleasures of life.

## \|\| 31.57 \|\|

*rājyaṃ deyaṃ śiro deyaṃ prāṇā deyāśca putraka \|*  
*evaṃbhūtaṃ ca kavacaṃ na deyaṃ prāṇasaṃkaṭe \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. О son, you might give away the kingdom, the head or even your own life but do not part with the knowledge of this kavacha even if your life is in danger.

## Chapter 32

## \|\| 32.1-2 \|\|

*bhṛguruvāca \|*  
*samprāptaṃ kavacaṃ nāṣa śaśvatsarvāṅgarakṣaṇam \|*  
*sukhadaṃ mokṣadaṃ sāraṃ śatrusaṃhārakāraṇam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*adhunā bhagavanmantraṃ stotraṃ pūjāvidhiṃ prabho \|*  
*dehi mahyamanāthāya śaraṇāgatapālaka \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Bhrigu said – O lord you have imparted the knowledge of the kavacha which protects the entire body, which provides pleasure, moksa and eatables to one to overcome the enemy. О lord, now you bestow the knowledge of the mantra, stotra and the method of adoration because I am an orphan and have come to take refuge with you.

## \|\| 32.3 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ namaḥ śrīkṛṣṇāya paripūrṇatamāya svāhā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Mahadeva said – … reciting this mantra one should adore the lord of gopis.

## \|\| 32.4-9 \|\|

*mantreṣu mantrarājo'yaṃ mahānsaptadaśākṣaraḥ \|*  
*siddho'yaṃ pañcalakṣeṇa japena munipuṃgavaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*taddaśāṃśaṃ ca havanaṃ taddaśāṃśābhiṣecanam \|*  
*tarpaṇaṃ taddaśāṃśaṃ ca taddaśāṃśaṃ ca mārjanam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*suvarṇānāṃ ca śatakaṃ puraścaraṇadakṣiṇā \|*  
*mantrasiddhasya puṃsaśca viśvaṃ karatale mune \|\| 6 \|\|*

*śaktaḥ pātuṃ samudrāṃśca viśvaṃ saṃhartumīśvaraḥ pāñcabhautikadehena vaikuṇṭhaṃ gantumīśvaraḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*tasya saṃsparśamātreṇa padapaṅkajareṇunā \|*  
*pūtāni sarvatīrthāni sadyaḥ pūtā vasundharā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*dhyānaṃ ca sāmavedoktaṃ śṛṇu manmukhato mune \|*  
*sarveśvarasya kṛṣṇasya bhaktimuktipradāyi ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

4-9. This is the mantra of seven letters and could be called as the king of mantras. О best of the sages, one meets with its success by reciting it five lakhs of times, a tenth part of the number used for performing home, a tenth for consecration and the tenth of it for sprinkling of water. A hundred gold coins should be given in daksina for its completion. О sage, when one meets with success with this mantra he overpowers the universe and he attains strength even to destroy the entire universe; he also becomes entitled to reach Vaikuntha with the human body comprising of the five elements and with the touch of the dust of the feet of a person, all the holy places of the earth get sanctified.

## \|\| 32.10 \|\|

*navīnajaladaśyāmaṃ nīlendīvaralocanam \|*  
*śaratpārvaṇacandrāsyamīṣaddhāsyaṃ manoharam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. О sage, now you listen to the dhyanam of the lord Krishna as prescribed in the Samaveda which provides the devotion of the lord as well as salvation from the universe.

## \|\| 32.11-15 \|\|

*koṭikandarpalāvaṇyalīlādhāmamanoharam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ taṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*candanokṣitasarvāṃgaṃ pītāmbaradharaṃ varam \|*  
*vīkṣyamāṇaṃ ca gopībhiḥ sasmitābhiśca santatam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*praphullamālatīmālāvanamālā vibhūṣitam \|*  
*dadhataṃ kundapuṣpāḍhyāṃ cūḍāṃ candrakacarccitām \|\| 13 \|\|*

*prabhāṃ kṣipantīṃ nabhasaścandratārānvitasya ca \|*  
*ratnabhūṣitasarvāṃgaṃ rādhāvakṣaḥsthalasthitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*siddhendraiśca munīndraiśca devendraiḥ parisevitam \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇumaheśaiśca śrutibhiśca stutaṃ bhaje \|\| 15 \|\|*

11-15. The lord happens to possess the dark complexion of resembling a new cloud, both the eyes were like the blue lotus flowers, the face like the full moon of the winter season, wearing a serene smile, having the beauty of crore of gods of love, possessing great illusion seated on a gem-studded lion-throne, adorned with gem-studded ornaments having all the limbs plastered with sandal-paste, wearing a yellow lower garment and serene smile on the face, having been gazed at by the cowherdesses, wearing the garland of jasmine flowers as well as the forest flowers, adorning his body with lotus flower putting to disgrace the glory of the moon and the stars, possessing great lustre, having all the limbs adorned with all the gems, declining over the breasts of Radha and surrounded by siddhas, ascetics and the gods including Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva. I adore the lord who is placed in this position.

## \|\| 32.16 \|\|

*dhyānenānena taṃ dhyātvā copacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*dattvā bhaktyā ca saṃpūjya sarvajñatvaṃ labhetpumān \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Thus one should adore the lord making sixteen types of offerings for that devotion while adoring him.

## \|\| 32.17-19 \|\|

*arghyaṃ pādyaṃ cāsanaṃ ca vasanaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*gāmarghyaṃ madhuparkaṃ ca yajñasūtramanuttamam \|\| 17 \|\|*

*dhūpadīpau ca naivedyaṃ punarācamanīyakam \|*  
*nānāprakārapuṣpāṇi tāmbūlaṃ ca suvāsitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*manoharaṃ divyatalpaṃ kastūryagarucandanaiḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā bhagavate deyaṃ mālyaṃ puṣpāñjalitrayam \|\| 19 \|\|*

17-19. One should offer arghya, padya, the seed, costumes, ornaments, madhuparka, yajnopavlta, essence, lamp, eatables and again water for sipping should be offered. Thereafter the flowers, the fragrant betel, should be offered with a divine bed. The garlands with three handfuls of flowers should be offered to the lord.

## \|\| 32.20-21 \|\|

*tataḥ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ sampūjya paścātsa mpūjayedgaṇam \|*  
*śrīdāmānaṃ sudāmānaṃ vasudāmānameva ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

*haribhānuṃ candrabhānuṃ sūryyabhānuṃ subhānukam \|*  
*pārṣadapravarānsapta pūjayedbhaktibhā vataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Thereafter the adoration in six vases should be offered, besides the adoration of the ganas. Sudama, Sridama, Vasudama, Haribhanu, Chandrabhanu, Suryabhanu and Subhanu, all the seven courtiers of the lord should be adored.

## \|\| 32.22 \|\|

*gopīśvarīṃ rādhikāṃ ca mūlaprakṛtimīśvarīm \|*  
*kṛṣṇaśaktiṃ kṛṣṇapūjyāṃ pūjayedbhaktipūrvakam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Radhika the chief of the cowherdesses should be adored with devotion who happens to be the Mulaprabci, Isvari, shakti of lord Krishna and adorable by him.

## \|\| 32.23-24 \|\|

*gopagopīgaṇaṃ śāntaṃ māṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca pārvatīm \|*  
*lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīṃ pṛthvīṃ sarvadevaṃ sapārṣadam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*devaṣaṭkaṃ samabhyarcya punaḥ pañcopacārataḥ \|*  
*paścādevaṃ krameṇaiva śrīkṛṣṇaṃ pūjayetsudhīḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. Thereafter the cowherds, the cowherdesses, Brahma with peaceful faces, Parvati, Lakshmi, Sarasvati, PrthvI and the six gods should be adored making five types of offerings. Thereafter lord Krishna should also be adored like this.

## \|\| 32.25 \|\|

*gaṇeśaṃ ca dineśaṃ ca vahniṃ viṣṇuṃ śivaṃ śivām \|*  
*devaṣaṭkaṃ samabhyarcya ceṣṭadevaṃ ca pūjayet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Thereafter Ganesha, Surya, Agni, Vishnu, Shiva, Parvati, should also be adored together with the family god.

## \|\| 32.26-27 \|\|

*gaṇeśaṃ vighnanāśāya vyādhināśāya bhāskaram \|*  
*ātmanaḥ śuddhaye vahniṃ śrīviṣṇuṃ muktihetave \|\| 26 \|\|*

*jñānāya śaṅkaraṃ durgāṃ paramaiśva ryyahetave \|*  
*sampūjane phalamidaṃ viparītamapūjane \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Ganesha the remover of the obstruction, Surya the remover of the ailment, Agni for purification, Vishnu for salvation, Shiva for receiving wisdom and Parvati should be adored for achieving the fortune. By adoring them all, one gets the appropriate results and the things get in the opposite direction if one does not do so.

## \|\| 32.28 \|\|

*tataḥ kṛtvā parīhāramiṣṭadevaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*stotraṃ ca sāmavedoktaṃ paṭhedbhaktyā ca tacchṛṇu \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Thereafter the family gods should be adored with devotion and the stotra prescribed in the Samaveda should be recited which is being told by me.

## \|\| 32.29 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma paraṃ jyotiḥ sanātanam \|*  
*nirliptaṃ paramātmānaṃ namāmyakhilakāraṇam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Mahadeva said – You are the primeval Brahmana, enjoying the highest place, you are the eternal flame, ever-lasting, unattached, I bow in reverence to such a lord.

## \|\| 32.30 \|\|

*sthūlātsthūlatamaṃ devaṃ sūkṣmātsūkṣmatamaṃ param \|*  
*sarvadṛśyamadṛśyaṃ ca svecchācāraṃ namāmyaham \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. You are harder than the hardest, smaller than the smallest, visible as well as invisible and the great lord. I bow in reverence to you.

## \|\| 32.31 \|\|

*sākāraṃ ca nirākāraṃ saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ prabhum \|*  
*sarvādhāraṃ ca sarvaṃ ca svecchārūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. You are having a form as well as formless, have all the merits and are without merit. You are the base of everything and the one who moves at will, I adore the lord like this.

## \|\| 32.32 \|\|

*atīva kamanīyaṃ ca rūpaṃ nirupamaṃ vibhum \|*  
*karālarūpamatyantaṃ bibhrataṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. You are most beautiful having an extremely charming body while being the terrific one and the one who pervades everywhere. I bow in reverence to you.

## \|\| 32.33 \|\|

*karmmaṇaḥ karmarūpaṃ taṃ sākṣiṇaṃ sarvakarmaṇaḥ \|*  
*phalaṃ ca phaladātāraṃ sarvarūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. You are the form of all the actions, the witness of all actions and bestower of the result of one's own actions. I therefore, bow in reverence to the one having all the forms.

## \|\| 32.34 \|\|

*sraṣṭā pātā ca saṃhartā kalayā mūrtibhedataḥ \|*  
*nānāmūrtiḥ kalāṃśena yaḥ pumāṃstaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. You create, preserve and destroy the universe by your rays and with the same rays you take to many forms, I therefore bow in reverence to the divine Purusa.

## \|\| 32.35-36 \|\|

*svayaṃ prakṛtirūpaśca māyayā ca svayaṃ pumān \|*  
*tayoḥ paraṃ svayaṃ śaśvattannamāmi parātparam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*strīpunnapuṃsakaṃ rūpaṃ yo bibharti svamāyayā \|*  
*svayaṃ māyā svayaṃ māyī yo devastaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. The one who himself is the form of Prakriti and takes to the form of illusion, though he is beyond both of them. I bow in reverence to such a lord, the one who takes to the form of a woman, a man or impotent with his illusion, I bow in reverence to such a lord.

## \|\| 32.37 \|\|

*tārakaṃ sarvaduḥkhānāṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam \|*  
*dhārakaṃ sarvaviśvānāṃ sarvabījaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The one who relieves all of all the miseries, who happens to be the cause of all the causes, who maintains all the globes and is the form of the seed, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.38 \|\|

*tejasvināṃ raviryo hi sarvajātiṣu vāḍavaḥ \|*  
*nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca yaścandrastaṃ namāmi jagatprabhum \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Who is the sun among those possessing lustre, Brahmana among the castes, the moon among the constellations. I bow in reverence to such a lord of the universe.

## \|\| 32.39-40 \|\|

*rudrāṇāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca jñānināṃ yo hi śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*nāgānāṃ yo hi śeṣaśca taṃ namāmi jagatpatim \|\| 39 \|\|*

*prajāpatīnāṃ yo brahmā siddhānāṃ kapilaḥ svayam \|*  
*sanatkumāro muniṣu taṃ namāmi jagadgurum \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. The one who happens to be Shiva, Rudra, Vais nava in the intellectuals and Shesha among the Nagas, I bow in reverence to the lord of the universe, the one who is Brahma among Prajapatis, Kapila among the siddha, Sanatkumara among the ascetics, I bow in reverence to the lord who happens to be the teacher of the universe.

## \|\| 32.41 \|\|

*devānāṃ yo hi viṣṇuśca devīnāṃ prakṛtiḥ svayam \|*  
*svāyambhuvo manūnāṃ yo mānaveṣu ca vaiṣṇavaḥ \|*  
*nārīṇāṃ śatarūpā ca bahurūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The one who is Vishnu among the gods, Prakriti among the goddesses, Svayambhuva among the Manus, Vaishnavas among the humans and Satarilpa among the women, I bow in reverence to the lord who takes to many forms.

## \|\| 32.42 \|\|

*ṛtūnāṃ yo vasantaśca māsānāṃ mārgaśīrṣakaḥ \|*  
*ekādaśī tithīnāṃ ca namāmyakhilarūpiṇam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The one who happens to be spring among the seasons, Margas'Irsa among the months and Ekadashi among the dates, I bow in reverence to the eternal form of the lord.

## \|\| 32.43 \|\|

*sāgaraḥ saritāṃ yaśca parvatānāṃ himālayaḥ \|*  
*vasundharā sahiṣṇūnāṃ taṃ sarvaṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The one who happens to be the ocean among the rivers, Himalayas among the mountains, the earth among those possessing patience, I bow in reverence to the one who pervades the universe.

## \|\| 32.44 \|\|

*patrāṇāṃ tulasīpatraṃ dārurūpeṣu candanam \|*  
*vṛkṣāṇāṃ kalpavṛkṣo yastaṃ namāmi jagatpatim \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The one who is Tulasi leaf among the leaves, sandal-wood among the woods, kalpavrksha among the trees, I bow in reverence to such a lord of the world.

## \|\| 32.45 \|\|

*puṣpāṇāṃ pārijātaśca sasyānāṃ dhānyameva ca \|*  
*amṛtaṃ bhakṣyavastūnāṃ nānārūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The one who happens to be the Parijata among the flowers, paddy among the cereals, the nectar among the eatables, I bow in reverence to the lord who takes to many forms.

## \|\| 32.46-47 \|\|

*airāvato gajendrāṇāṃ vainateyaśca pakṣiṇām \|*  
*kāmadhenuśca dhenūnāṃ sarvarūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 46 \|\|*

*taijasānāṃ suvarṇaṃ ca dhānyānāṃ yava eva ca \|*  
*yaḥ kesarī paśūnāṃ ca vararūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 47\|\|*

46-47. The one who happens to be Airavata among the elephants, Garuda among the birds, Kamadhenu among the cows, I bow in reverence to the one who appears in all these forms. The one who happens to be gold among the articles possessing lustre, barley among the cereals, lion among the wild animals, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.48 \|\|

*yakṣāṇāṃ ca kubero yo grahāṇāṃ ca bṛhaspatiḥ \|*  
*dikpālānāṃ mahendraśca taṃ namāmi paraṃ varam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The one who happens to be the Kubera among the yaksas, Brihaspati among the planets and Mahendra among the dikpalas, I bow in reverence to the best of that form.

## \|\| 32.49 \|\|

*vedasaṃghaśca śāstrāṇāṃ paṇḍitānāṃ sarasvatī \|*  
*akṣarāṇāmakāro yastaṃ pradhānaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The one who happens to be the Veda in the scriptures, Sarasvati among the intellectuals, Omkara among the letters, I bow in reverence to such a chief of the gods.

## \|\| 32.50 \|\|

*mantrāṇāṃ viṣṇumantraśca tīrthānāṃ jāhnavī svayam \|*  
*indriyāṇāṃ mano yo hi sarvaśreṣṭhaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The one who happens to be Vishnu-mantra among the mantras, Ganga among the sacred rivers and the mind among the organs of senses, I bow in reverence to the best of the gods.

## \|\| 32.51 \|\|

*sudarśanaṃ ca śastrāṇāṃ vyādhināṃ vaiṣṇavo jvaraḥ \|*  
*tejasāṃ brahmatejaśca vareṇyaṃ taṃ namāmyaham \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The one who happens to be Sudarshana among the weapons, fever among the ailment, Brahmateja among the lustres, I bow in reverence to the same god.

## \|\| 32.52 \|\|

*balaṃ yo vai balavatāṃ mano vai śīghragāminām \|*  
*kālaḥ kalayatāṃ yo hi taṃ namāmi vilakṣaṇam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The one who happens to be the strength of the valorous people, the mind among those things which move and is the form of Kala in counting, I bow in reverence to such an astonishing lord.

## \|\| 32.53 \|\|

*jñānadātā gurūṇāṃ ca mātṛrūpaśca bandhuṣu \|*  
*mitreṣu janmadātā yastaṃ sāraṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The one who bestows knowledge among the teachers, mother among the relatives and the one who gives birth among the friends, the one who happens to be the essence of everyone, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.54 \|\|

*śilpināṃ viśvakarmā yaḥ kāmadevaśca rūpiṇām \|*  
*pativratā ca patnīnāṃ namasyaṃ taṃ namāmyaham \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The one who happens to be Vishvakarma among architects, the god of love among the beautiful persons, a chaste lady among the women, such a lord is adorable and I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.55 \|\|

*priyeṣu putrarūpo yo nṛparūpo nareṣu ca \|*  
*śālagrāmaśca yantrāṇāṃ taṃ viśiṣṭaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. The one who happens to be like the son of all the beloved, the king among the humans, Shalagmma among the images, I bow in reverence to such a lord.

## \|\| 32.56-57 \|\|

*dharmmaḥ kalyāṇabījānāṃ vedānāṃ sāmavedakaḥ \|*  
*dharmmāṇāṃ satyarūpo yo viśiṣṭaṃ taṃ namāmyaham \|\| 56 \|\|*

*jale śaityasvarūpo yo gandharūpaśca bhūmiṣu \|*  
*śabdarūpaśca gagane taṃ praṇamya namāmyaham \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. The one who happens to be the form of Dharma of all the welfare seeds, Samaveda among the Vedas and truth among the dharmas, I bow in reverence to the same lord. The one who is coolness of water, I bow in reverence to the lord who is adorable.

## \|\| 32.58-59 \|\|

*kratūnāṃ rājasūyo yo gāyatrī chandasāṃ ca yaḥ \|*  
*gandharvāṇāṃ citrarathastaṃ gariṣṭhaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 58 \|\|*

*kṣīrasvarūpo gavyānāṃ pavitrāṇāṃ ca pāvakaḥ \|*  
*puṇyadānāṃ ca yaḥ stotraṃ taṃ namāmi śubhapradam \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. The one who happens to be Rajasuya among the yajnas, Gayatri among the metres, Chitraratha among the Gandharvas, I bow in reverence to the great lord. The one who happens to be milk among the liquids, fire among the sanctified and happens to be the stotra among the sacred people, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.60 \|\|

*tṛṇānāṃ kuśarūpo yo vyādhirūpaśca vairiṇām \|*  
*guṇānāṃ śāntarūpo yaścitrarūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The one who happens to be enmity of the enemies, peaceful nature among the virtues, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.61 \|\|

*tejorūpo jñānarūpaḥ sarvarūpaśca yo mahān \|*  
*sarvānirvacanīyaṃ ca taṃ namāmi svayaṃ vibhum \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The one who is having the form of lustre, form of knowledge, form of all, is great and having an inexplicable form, I bow in reverence to the lord who pervades the universe.

## \|\| 32.62-63 \|\|

*sarvādhāreṣu yo vāyuryathā''tmā nityarūpiṇām \|*  
*ākāśo vyāpakānāṃ yo vyāpakaṃ taṃ namāmyaham \|\| 62 \|\|*

*vedānirvacanīyaṃ yanna stotuṃ paṇḍitaḥ kṣamaḥ \|*  
*yadanirvacanīyaṃ ca ko vā tatstotumīśvaraḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. The one who happens to be the wind among the bases, the form of soul among those having eternal form and the sky among those who pervade everywhere, I bow in reverence to the omnipotent lord. The one who is beyond the comprehension of even the Vedas and the one who is inexplicable who can eulogise him.

## \|\| 32.64 \|\|

*vedā na śaktā yaṃ stotuṃ jaḍībhūtā sarasvatī \|*  
*taṃ ca vāṅmanasāpāraṃ ko vidvānstotumīśvaraḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The one who cannot be fully adored by even the Vedas and Sarasvati also remains dumb folded before him, who can adore such a person who is beyond the mind and speech.

## \|\| 32.65 \|\|

*śuddhatejassvarūpaṃ ca bhaktānugrahavigraham \|*  
*atīva kamanīyaṃ ca śyāmarūpaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. I adore the lord who is spotless form of tejas takes to the human form for the sake of devotees is quite beautiful and of dark complexion, I adore such a lord.

## \|\| 32.66 \|\|

*dvibhujaṃ muralīvaktraṃ kiśoraṃ sasmitaṃ mudā \|*  
*śaśvadgopāṅganābhiśca vīkṣyamāṇaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. The one who has two arms, holds fruit in his hand, is of tender age, wears a serene smile, is surrounded by the cowherdesses who look at him with side glances, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.67 \|\|

*rādhayā dattatāmbūlaṃ bhuktavantaṃ manoharam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca tamīśaṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The one who takes the pleasant betel offered by Radha seated on a gem-studded lion-throne, I adore such a lord.

## \|\| 32.68 \|\|

*ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣāḍhyaṃ sevitaṃ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|*  
*pārṣadapravarairgopakumāraistaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The one who is adorned with gem-studded ornaments, is surrounded by the best of courtiers and the cowherds who move the white fly-whisks on the lord. I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.69 \|\|

*vṛndāvanāntare ramye rāsollāsasamutsukam \|*  
*rāsamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ namāmi rasikeśvaram \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The one who is always anxious to enjoy the Rasalila in Rasamandala being the lord of Radha, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.70 \|\|

*śataśṛṅge mahāśaile goloke ratnaparvate \|*  
*virajāpuline ramye praṇamāmi vihāriṇam \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The one who strolls on the great mountain of gems resembling Goloka and the bank of Viraja river, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 32.71 \|\|

*paripūrṇatamaṃ śāntaṃ rādhākāntaṃ manoharam \|*  
*satyaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ ca nityaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. The one who is complete in all respects, is peaceful, lord of Radha, pleasant to look at, truthful, the form of Brahmana, I bow in reverence to lord Krishna.

## \|\| 32.72 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇasya stotramidaṃ trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhennaraḥ \|*  
*dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ sa dātā bhārate bhavet \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. The one who recites this stotra of Krishna thrice a day, becomes the bestower of dharma, artha, kama and moksa in the land of Bharata.

## \|\| 32.73 \|\|

*haridāsyaṃ harau bhaktiṃ labhetstotraprasādataḥ \|*  
*iha loke jagatpūjyo viṣṇutulyo bhaveddhruvam \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. By the grace of this stotra one achieves the devotion of the lord Hari as well as his slavehood. He is equated with Vishnu in this loka and is adored everywhere.

## \|\| 32.74 \|\|

*sarvasiddheśvaraḥ śānto'pyante yāti hareḥ padam \|*  
*tejasā yaśasā bhāti yathā sūryyo mahītale \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. He becomes the lord of all the siddhas, peaceful and ultimately achieves the abode of the lord. He equates himself with Surya in glory and shines on earth.

## \|\| 32.75-77 \|\|

*jīvanmuktaḥ kṛṣṇabhaktaḥ sa bhavennātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*arogī guṇavānvidvānputravāndhanavānsadā \|\| 75 \|\|*

*ṣaḍabhijño daśabalo manoyāyī bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*sarvajñaḥ sarvadaścaiva sa dātā sarvasampadām \|*  
*kalpavṛkṣasamaḥ śaśvadbhavetkṛṣṇaprasādataḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

*ityevaṃ kathitaṃ stotraṃ vatsa tvaṃ gaccha puṣkaram \|*  
*tatra kṛtvā mantrasiddhiṃ paścātprāpsyasi vāñchitam \|\| 77 \|\|*

75-77. Such a devotee of lord Krishna is free from death. There is no doubt about it. He is relieved of all ailments, become virtuous, intelligent, possessing sons and riches, possesses all knowledge, prowess and moves with the speed of the mind. He becomes all knowledgeable, the giver of charities and riches and becomes like kalpavrksa with the grace of lord Krishna. О son, I have recited this stotra to you, now you move on to Puskara.

## \|\| 32.78 \|\|

*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kuru pṛthvīṃ yathāsukham \|*  
*mamāśiṣā muniśreṣṭha śrīkṛṣṇasya prasādataḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. After meeting success with the mantra you will achieve success in your desires. О best of the sages, with my blessings and the grace of lord Krishna, you will relieve the earth of the Ks atriyas twenty one times.

## Chapter 33

## \|\| 33.1-2 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śivaṃ praṇamya sa bhṛgurdurgāṃ kālīṃ mudā'nvitaḥ \|*  
*gatvā puṣkaratīrtha ca mantrasiddhiṃ cakāra ha \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sa babhūva nirāhāro māsaṃ bhaktisamanvitaḥ \|*  
*dhyāyankṛṣṇapadāmbhojaṃ vāyurodhaṃ cakāra saḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narayana said – Bhrigu bowed in reverence to Shiva, Durga and Kali. Thereafter he proceeded to Puskara-ksetra and started reciting the mantra there. With his devoted mind he performed a tapas without consuming anything for a month. He always devoted his mind at the lotus-like feet of lord Krishna and ultimately he stopped inhaling even the air.

## \|\| 33.3-7 \|\|

*dadarśa cakṣurunmīlya gaganaṃ tejasā vṛtam \|*  
*diśo daśa dyotayantaṃ samācchannadivākaram \|\| 3 \|\|*

*tejomaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ ratnayānaṃ dadarśa ha \|*  
*dadarśa tatra puruṣamatyantaṃ sundaraṃ varam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*īṣaddhāsyaprasannāsyaṃ bhaktānugrahakārakam \|*  
*praṇamya daṇḍavanmūrdhnā varaṃ vavre tamīśvaram \|\| 5 \|\|*

*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kariṣyāmi mahīmiti \|*  
*pādāravinde sudṛḍhāṃ tāṃ bhaktimanapāyinīm \|\| 6 \|\|*

*dāsyaṃ sudurllabhaṃ śaśvavatpādābje ca dehi me \|*  
*kṛṣṇastasmai varaṃ dattvā tatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 7 \|\|*

3-7. When he opened his eyes he found a fall of lustre in the sky which pervaded all the ten directions and even the sun. In that fall of lustre, he found a plane studded with gems, in which a beautiful person seated who was wearing a serene smile on his face, who was delightful and always showered his grace on his devotees. He prostrated before the lord lowering his head and prayed for a boon, "You bestow me strength enough to rid the earth of the Kshatriyas twenty one times. I should always remain devoted to your lotus-like feet and should achieve your slavehood." Lord Krishna granted him the boon and disappeared from the scene.

## \|\| 33.8-9 \|\|

*bhṛguḥ praṇamya bhavanaṃ tajjagāma parātparam \|*  
*paspanda dakṣiṇāṅgaṃ ca paraṃ maṅgalasūcakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*vāñchā pratītijananaṃ susvapnaṃ ca dadarśa ha \|*  
*manaḥ prasannaṃ sphītaṃ ca tadbabhūva divāniśam \|*  
*bhāṣya svajanaṃ sarvaṃ gṛhe tasthau mudā'nvitaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. Bhrigu also bowing in reverence to the eternal lord went to his abode. His right limbs then started fluttering which was indicative of his achieving success. He also witnessed the dream which pointed out towards achieving success. Since then Paras'urama always remained delighted in his mind. He narrated the entire story of his own people of the family and spent time delightfully.

## \|\| 33.10 \|\|

*svaśiṣyānpitṛśiṣyāṃśca bhrātṛvargāṃśca bāndhavān \|*  
*ānīyānīya vividhānmantrāṃśca sa cakāra ha \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Thereafter by collecting his own pupils, the pupils of his father, the brothers and other relatives, he started imparting to them the knowledge of the mantras.

## \|\| 33.11-12 \|\|

*paurvāparyyaṃ svavṛttāntaṃ tānevoktvā śubhakṣaṇe \|*  
*taireva sārddhaṃ balavānbabhūva gamanonmukhaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*dadarśa maṅgalaṃ rāmaḥ śuśrāva jayasūcakam \|*  
*bubudhe manasā sarvaṃ svajayaṃ vairisaṃkṣayam \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. At an auspicious moment all of them got ready to accompany Parashurama for waging the war. Parashurama at that point of time heard the sound of victory and welfare which reassured him and he thought, "I shall be victorious and the enemies will be destroyed."

## \|\| 33.13-14 \|\|

*yātrākāle ca purataḥ śuśrāva sahasā muniḥ \|*  
*hariśabdaṃ siṃhaśabdaṃ ghaṇṭādundubhivādanam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*ākāśavāṇīsaṃgītaṃ jayaste bhaviteti ca \|*  
*naveṅgitaṃ ca kalyāṇaṃ meghaśabdaṃ jayāvaham \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. At the time of starting on the journey, he suddenly listened to the sound of the moving of the horses, the blowing of conches, sounding of bells and big drums and also the divine voice from the sky saying, "You will be victorious." Many welfare signs became visible and thundering of the sound indicative of victory was also heard.

## \|\| 33.15-16 \|\|

*cakāra yātrāṃ bhagavāñchrutvaivaṃ vividhaṃ śubham \|*  
*dadarśa purato viprabandidaivajñabhikṣukān \|\| 15 \|\|*

*jvalatpradīpaṃ dadhatīṃ patiputravatīṃ satīm \|*  
*puro dadarśa smerāsyāṃ nānābhūṣaṇabhūṣitām \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Lord Parashurama thus continued to hear the various types of welfare sounds and he ultimately started journey. At that point of time he found in front the Brahmanas, fire, the astrologers, Samnyasi, burning lamps held in hands by chaste women whose husbands and sons were alive. These ladies wore a serene smile on the faces, were quite delightful and were adorned with several types of ornaments.

## \|\| 33.17 \|\|

*śivaṃ śivāṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ cāṣaṃ ca nakulaṃ tathā \|*  
*gacchandadarśa rāmaśca yātrāmaṃgalasūcakam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. While performing the journey Parashurama found the jackals, she-jackals, filled vases, Nllakantha, mangooses and other auspicious symbols.

## \|\| 33.18-28 \|\|

*kṛṣṇasāraṃ gajaṃ siṃhaṃ turagaṃ gaṇḍakaṃ dvipam \|*  
*camarīṃ rājahaṃsaṃ ca cakravākaṃ śukaṃ pikam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*mayūraṃ khañjanaṃ caiva śaṃkhacillaṃ cakorakam \|*  
*pārāvataṃ balākaṃ ca kāraṇḍaṃ cātakaṃ caṭam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*saudāmanīṃ śakracāpaṃ sūryyaṃ sūryāprabhāṃ śubhām \|*  
*sadyomāṃsaṃ sajīvaṃ ca matsyaṃ śaṅkhaṃ suvarṇakam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*māṇikyaṃ rajataṃ muktāṃ maṇīndraṃ ca pravālakam \|*  
*dadhi lājāñchukladhānyaṃ śuklapuṣpaṃ ca kuṅkumam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*parṇaṃ patākāṃ chatraṃ ca darpaṇaṃ śvetacāmaram \|*  
*dhenuṃ vatsaprayuktāṃ ca rathasthaṃ bhūmipaṃ tathā \|\| 22 \|\|*

*dugdhamājyaṃ tathā pūgamamṛtaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā \|*  
*śālagrāmaṃ pakvaphalaṃ svastikaṃ śarkarāṃ madhu \|\| 23 \|\|*

*mārjjāraṃ ca vṛṣendraṃ ca meṣaṃ parvatamūṣikam \|*  
*meghācchannasya ca raverudayaṃ candramaṇḍalam' \|\| 24 \|\|*

*kastūrīṃ vyajanaṃ toyaṃ haridrāṃ tīrthamṛttikām \|*  
*siddhārthaṃ sarṣapaṃ dūrvāṃ vipravālaṃ ca bālikām \|\| 25 \|\|*

*mṛgaṃ veśyāṃ ṣaṭpadaṃ ca karpūraṃ pītavāsasam \|*  
*go mūtraṃ gopurīṣaṃ ca godhūliṃ gopadāṅkitam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*goṣṭhaṃ gavāṃ vartma ramyāṃ gośālāṃ gogatiṃ śubhām \|*  
*bhūṣaṇaṃ devamūrtiṃ ca jvaladagniṃ mahotsavam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*tāmraṃ ca sphaṭikaṃ vandyaṃ sindūraṃ mālyacandanam \|*  
*gandhaṃ ca hīrakaṃ ratnaṃ dadarśa dakṣiṇe śubham \|\| 28 \|\|*

18-28. Again he found the black buck, elephant, lions, horses, gandaka, spotted cow, geese, ruddy birds, cuckoos, wagtail bird, samkhacilla bird, partridge bird, pigeon, sky-lark, green pigeon, cuckoo, the sparrows, lightning, the rainbow, sun and sun rays, fresh meat, fish which were alike, samkha, gold, jewels, silver, gems, coral, curd, fried paddy, white cereals, white flowers, saffron, palasa, banners, umbrella, mirror, white fly-whisks, cow with calf, king mounted on the chariot, milk, ghee, betel-nut, nectar, payasam, Shalagrama, ripe fruits, svastika, sugar, honey, cat, bull, sheep mountain-rat, the sun covered with clouds, disk of the moon, kasturl, fan, water, turmeric, earth of the holy places, mustard seeds, Brahmana boy, dtirva-grass, a girl child, deer, whore, black-wasps, camphor, yellow garment, urine of cow, cowdung, prints of cow's hoofs, rising dust of the evening, the abode of cow, their path-way, gosald, the moving of the cows, ornaments, images of the gods, fire in flames, festivities, copper, crystal, adorable, rosary of vermilion, sandal-paste, fragrance, jewel and gem to his right.

## \|\| 33.29-31 \|\|

*sugandhivāyorāghrāṇaṃ prāpa viprāśiṣaṃ śubhām \|\| 29 \|\|*

*ityevaṃ maṅgalaṃ jñātvā prayayau sa mudā'nvitaḥ \|*  
*astaṃ gate dinakare narmmadātīrasannidhau \|\| 30 \|\|*

*tatrākṣayavaṭaṃ divyaṃ dadarśa sumanoharam \|*  
*atyūrdhvaṃ vistṛtamatipuṇyāśramapadaṃ param \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. Besides the fragrant breeze was blowing which was inhaled by him and the Brahmanas blessed him. Thus finding the auspicious time Parashurama delightfully started the journey, by sun-set he reached the bank of Narmada and found the eternal banyan tree which was quite high and of gigantic proportions. He went close to the hermitage.

## \|\| 33.32-33 \|\|

*paulastyatapasaḥ sthānaṃ sugandhimarudanvitam \|*  
*kārtavīryyārjunābhyāśe tatra tasthau gaṇaiḥ saha \|\| 32 \|\|*

*suṣvāpa puṣpaśayyāyāṃ kiṅkaraiḥ parisevitaḥ \|*  
*nidrāṃ yayau pariśrāntaḥ paramānandasaṃgataḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. It happened to be the place for performing tapas by Pulastya. The fragrant breeze always blew there. Paras'urama halted at a place close to the place of Kartavirya, he slept on the bed of flowers and the attendants were serving him. Feeling fatigued, he went to sleep delightfully.

## \|\| 33.34 \|\|

*niśātīte ca sa bhṛguścāru svapnaṃ dadarśa ha \|*  
*na cintitaṃ yanmanasā vāyupittakaphaṃ vinā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. At the last leg of the night the witnessed a dream without the influence of cough, vdyu and pitta which was not thought over by anyone.

## \|\| 33.35-37 \|\|

*gajāśvaśailaprāsādagovṛkṣaphaliteṣu ca \|*  
*āruhyamāṇamātmānaṃ rudantaṃ kṛmibhakṣitam \|\| 35 \|\|*

*āruhyamāṇamātmānaṃ naukāyāṃ candanokṣitam \|*  
*dhṛtavantaṃ puṣpamālāṃ śobhitaṃ pītavāsasā \|\| 36 \|\|*

*viṇmūtrokṣitasarvāṃgaṃ vasāpūyasamanvitam \|*  
*vīṇāṃ varāṃ vādayantamātmānaṃ ca dadarśa ha \|\| 37 \|\|*

35-37. In that dream he found the elephants, horses, mountains, palaces, cows and fruits, climbing on the tree with him. He found the tree being eaten away by crying insects. He found himself boarded on a boat and all his limbs were decorated with sandal-paste, flower garlands and he was clad in yellow garments. He found himself plastered with refuse, urine, fat and puss, playing on vina.

## \|\| 33.38 \|\|

*vistīrṇapadmapattraiśca svaṃ dadarśa sarittaṭe \|*  
*dadhyājyamadhusaṃyuktaṃ bhuktavantaṃ ca pāyasam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He found curd having been placed on the wide-spread lotus leaves besides ghee, honey and the payasam being consumed by him. He also found himself chewing betel accepting blessing of the Brahmanas; accepting fruits and flowers and burning lamps.

## \|\| 33.39-40 \|\|

*bhuktavantaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ labhantaṃ brāhmaṇāśiṣam \|*  
*phalapuṣpapradīpaṃ ca paśyantaṃ svaṃ dadarśa ha \|\| 39 \|\|*

*paripakvaphalaṃ kṣīramuṣṇānnaṃ śarkarānvitam \|*  
*svastikaṃ bhuktavantaṃ svaṃ dadarśa ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. He consumed ripe fruits, payasam with sugar and hard food besides svastika. He found himself in this condition again and again.

## \|\| 33.41-43 \|\|

*jalaukasā vṛścikena mīnena bhujagena ca \|*  
*bhakṣitaṃ bhītamātmānaṃ palāyantaṃ dadarśa saḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*tato dadarśa cātmānaṃ maṇḍalaṃ candrasūryyayoḥ \|*  
*patiputravatīṃ nārīṃ paśyantaṃ sasmitaṃ dvijaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*suveṣayā kanyakā sa smitena dvijena ca \|*  
*dadarśa śliṣṭamātmānaṃ tuṣṭena parituṣṭayā \|\| 43 \|\|*

41-43. The leeches, scorpions, fish and snakes, were biting him and he found himself running in panic from them. Thereafter the same Brahmana, witnessed besides himself the sun, the moon and their regions, the ladies having their husbands and sons alive smiling serenely besides the smiling Brahmanas, he also found well-dressed girls and the Brahmanas who were smiling with satisfaction and embracing him.

## \|\| 33.44 \|\|

*phalitaṃ puṣpitaṃ vṛkṣaṃ devatāpratimāṃ nṛpam \|*  
*gajasthaṃ ca rathasthaṃ ca paśyantaṃ svaṃ dadarśa ha \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He saw the trees blossoming with fruits and flowers, images of the gods, the king and the elephant and he found himself mounted on a chariot.

## \|\| 33.45 \|\|

*pītavastraparīdhānāṃ ratnālaṅkārabhūṣitām \|*  
*viśantīṃ brāhmaṇo gehaṃ paśyantaṃ svaṃ dadarśa ha \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Wearing yellow garment, adorned with ornaments of gems, he found a Brahmana lady coming before him.

## \|\| 33.46 \|\|

*śaṃkhaṃ ca sphaṭikaṃ śvetamālāṃ muktāṃ ca candanam \|*  
*suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ ratnaṃ paśyantaṃ svaṃ dadarśa ha \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. He found himself looking at the conch, the crystal, white rosary, jewels, sandal-paste, gold, silver and the jewels.

## \|\| 33.47 \|\|

*gajaṃ vṛṣaṃ ca sarpaṃ ca śvetaṃ ca śvetacāmaram \|*  
*nīlotpalaṃ darpaṇaṃ ca bhārgavo vai dadarśa ha \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Bhargava then also saw the elephant, the bull, the snake, the white fly-whisks, blue lotus and the mirror.

## \|\| 33.48 \|\|

*rathasthaṃ navaratnāḍhyaṃ mālatīmālyabhūṣitam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ svaṃ bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Parashurama found himself seated on a gem-studded lion-throne in the dream, wearing fresh ornaments of gems, garlands of jasmine flowers.

## \|\| 33.49 \|\|

*padmaśreṇīṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ dadhi lājānghṛtaṃ madhu \|*  
*parṇacchatraṃ chatriṇaṃ ca bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Не also found the line of lotuses, vases filled with water, curd, fried paddy, ghee, honey and an umbrella of leaves on his head.

## \|\| 33.50 \|\|

*bakapaṃktiṃ haṃsapaṃktiṃ kanyāpaṃktiṃ vratānvitām \|*  
*pūjayanto ghaṭaṃ śubhraṃ bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. He found a line of skylarks, geese and the girls who had been performing vratas, adoring him holding kalasa in their hands. Parashurama saw this dream.

## \|\| 33.51 \|\|

*maṇḍapasthaṃ dvijagaṇaṃ pūjayantaṃ haraṃ harim \|*  
*jayo'stvityuktavantaṃ taṃ bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Adoring Shiva and Vishnu seated in Mandapa the Brahmana spoke to him, "You will be victorious". This was the dream witnessed by him.

## \|\| 33.52 \|\|

*sudhāvṛṣṭiṃ parṇavṛṣṭiṃ phalavṛṣṭiṃ ca śāśvatīm \|*  
*puṣpacandanavṛṣṭiṃ ca bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The rain of nectar and leaves besides fruits, flowers and sandal-paste was also witnessed by him.

## \|\| 33.53-54 \|\|

*sadyomāṃsaṃ jīvamatsyaṃ mayūraṃ śvetakhañjanam \|*  
*sarovaraṃ ca tīrthāni bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 53 \|\|*

*pārāvataṃ śukaṃ cāṣaṃ śaṃkhaṃ cillaṃ ca cātakam \|*  
*vyāghraṃ siṃhaṃ ca surabhīṃ bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. The fresh meat, the moving fish, the peacock, white partridge birds and the stream were witnessed by Parashurama in dream. He also saw the pigeons, parrots, Nilakantha, white kite, tiger, lion and cow in the dream.

## \|\| 33.55 \|\|

*gorocanāṃ haridrāṃ ca śukladhānyācalaṃ varam \|*  
*jvaladagniṃ tathā dūrvāṃ bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Bhrigu also witnessed Gorocana, turmeric, fire in flames, heaps of rice and Dtirvd-grass was witnessed by him in the dream.

## \|\| 33.56 \|\|

*devālayasamūhaṃ ca śivaliṃgaṃ ca pūjitam \|*  
*arcitāṃ mṛnmayīṃ śaivāṃ bhṛguḥ svapne dadarśa ha \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. He also found a group of temples, adorable Shivalinga, the image of Durga made in clay which was being adored by him. These were all seen by Parashurama in the dream.

## \|\| 33.57-59 \|\|

*yavagodhūmacūrṇānāṃ bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca \|*  
*bhṛgurdadarśa svapne ca bubhuje ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

*divyavastraparīdhānā ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitāḥ \|*  
*agamyāgamanaṃ svapne cakāra bhṛgunandanaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*dadarśa narttakīṃ veśyāṃ rudhiraṃ ca surāṃ papau \|*  
*rudhirokṣitasarvāṅgaḥ svapne ca bhṛgunandanaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

57-59. Не also saw the many stuffs made of wheat and barley flour and other eatables, which he continued consuming in the dream. Parashurama the son of Bhrigu found himself clad in divine garments adorned with gem-studded ornament and he also enjoyed the company of a prohibited lady. He also found a dancer, dancing in the dream and also enjoyed the blood and meat besides wine. He also found that his body was soaked in blood.

## \|\| 33.60 \|\|

*pakṣiṇāṃ pītavarṇānāṃ mānuṣāṇāṃ ca nārada \|*  
*māṃsāni bubhuje rāmo hṛṣṭaḥ svapne'ruṇodaye \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. О Narada, Parashurama saw in the dream at the time of rising of the sun, a yellow bird and human flesh.

## \|\| 33.61 \|\|

*akasmānnigaḍairbaddhaṃ kṣataṃ śastreṇa svaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā ca bubudhe prātaḥ samuttasthau hariṃ smaran \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. He then found himself in fetters and suddenly wounded. Witnessing such a dream Parashurama got up in the early morning.

## \|\| 33.62 \|\|

*atīva hṛṣṭaḥ svapnena prātaḥkṛtyaṃ cakāra saḥ \|*  
*manasā bubudhe sarvaṃ vijeṣyāmi ripuṃ dhruvam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Feeling delighted with the witnessing of the dreams, he completed the morning routine and he felt reassured in his mind that he would surely overcome the enemy.

## Chapter 34

## \|\| 34.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*sa prātarāhnikaṃ kṛtvā samālocya ca taiḥ saha \|*  
*dūtaṃ prasthāpayāmāsa kārttavīryyāśramaṃ bhṛguḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – After finishing the morning routine Parashurama summoned his people for consultation and sent a messenger to Kartavirya.

## \|\| 34.2 \|\|

*sa dūtaḥśīghramāgatya vasantaṃ rājasaṃsadi \|*  
*veṣṭitaṃ sacivaiḥ sārddhamuvāca nṛpatīśvaram \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The messengers went to Kartavirya who was surrounded by his courtiers and spoke to him.

## \|\| 34.3-4 \|\|

*rāmadūta uvāca \|*  
*narmmadātīrasānnidhye nyagrodhākṣayamūlake \|*  
*sa bhṛgurbhrātṛbhiḥ sārddhaṃ tatra tvaṃ gantumarhasi \|\| 3 \|\|*

*yuddhaṃ kuru mahārāja jātibhirjñātibhiḥ saha \|*  
*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kariṣyati mahīmiti \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. The messengers said – "O lord, Bhrigu has arrived on the bank of river Narmada and is currently resting under the banyan tree. Therefore, you go to him for a fight. He has resolved to rid the earth of the Kstriyas twenty one times."

## \|\| 34.5 \|\|

*ityuktvā rāmadūtaścāpyagacchadrāmasannidhim \|*  
*rājā vidhāya sannāhaṃ samaraṃ gantumudyataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Thus speaking the messengers of Parashurama returned to his master, on the other hand the king adorned himself with kavacha and got ready to go to the battle field.

## \|\| 34.6 \|\|

*gacchantaṃ samaraṃ dṛṣṭvā prāṇeśaṃ sā manoramā \|*  
*tameva vārayāmāsa vāsayāmāsa sannidhau \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Finding Kartavirya moving out for waging a war, his queen named Manorama prevented him from going. She called him before herself and embraced him.

## \|\| 34.7 \|\|

*rājā manoramāṃ dṛṣṭvā prasannavadanekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*tāmuvāca sabhāmadhye vākyaṃ mānasikaṃ mune \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О sage, finding Manorama there, the face of the king was delighted. He started speaking out his mind to her in the court itself.

## \|\| 34.8 \|\|

*kārttavīryyārjuna uvāca \|*  
*māmevāhvayate kānte jamadagnisuto mahān \|*  
*sa tiṣṭhannarmmadātīre raṇāya bhrātṛbhiḥ saha \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Kartavirya said – О damsel, Parashurama, the son of Jamadagni is challenging me who has arrived at the bed of the river Narmada together with all his brothers.

## \|\| 34.9 \|\|

*samprāpya śaṅkarācchastraṃ mantraṃ ca kavacaṃ hareḥ \|' trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kartumicchati medinīm \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. By achieving the weapons, mantra and kavacha from Shiva he has resolved to rid the earth of the Kshatriyas twenty one times.

## \|\| 34.10 \|\|

*āndolayanti me prāṇā manaḥ saṃkṣubhitaṃ muhuḥ \|*  
*śaśvatsphurati vāmāṅgaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ svapnaṃ śṛṇu priye \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Because of this, I fell panicky in my mind and my mind is disturbed; my left limbs are fluttering again and again. О dear one, I have seen a dream recently which I am going to speak out to you.

## \|\| 34.11-12 \|\|

*tailābhyaṅgitamātmānamapaśyaṃ gardabhopari \|*  
*auḍhapuṣpasya mālyaṃ ca bibhrataṃ raktacandanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

*raktavastraparīdhānaṃ lohālaṅkārabhūṣitam \|*  
*krīḍantaṃ ca hasantaṃ ca nirvāṇāṅgārarāśinā \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. I found myself soaked in oil, mounted on a donkey, wearing a garland of shoe flower, applying red sandal-paste on the body, wearing red garments and ornaments of iron. Besides I found myself playing on a heap of coal, playing and laughing at the same time.

## \|\| 34.13 \|\|

*bhasmācchannāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ japāpuṣpānvitāṃ sati \|*  
*rahitaṃ candrasūryyābhyāṃ rastasandhyānvitaṃ nabhaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Applying fire on my body wearing a garland of shoe flower, I found the sun and the moon absent from the sky, while the evening was becoming red.

## \|\| 34.14 \|\|

*muktakeśāṃ ca nṛtyantīṃ vidhavāṃ chinnanāsikām \|*  
*raktavastraparīdhānāmapaśyaṃ cāṭṭahāsinīm \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. I found the ladies with dishevelled hair, whose noses were cut-off, wearing red garments and the widows laughing loudly.

## \|\| 34.15 \|\|

*saśarāmagnirahitāṃ citāṃ bhasmasamanvitām \|*  
*bhasmavṛṣṭimasṛgvṛṣṭimagnivṛṣṭimapīśvari \|\| 15\|\|*

15\. O goddess, there were arrows without fire, the pyre was filled with ashes, the raining of ashes and the rain of blood and pyre.

## \|\| 34.16-17 \|\|

*pakva tālaphalākīrṇāṃ pṛthivīmasthisaṃyutām \|*  
*apaśyaṃ karparaughaṃ ca chinnakeśanakhānvitam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*parvataṃ lavaṇānāṃ ca rāśībhūtaṃ kapardakam \|*  
*cūrṇānāṃ caiva tailānāmadṛśaṃ kandaraṃ niśi \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. I found goddess earth infatuated with tala trees. The bones were spread over the surface of the earth, besides the skulls without hair and nails. I found the mountains of salt, heaps of small shells and caves filled with oil and powder, in the dream at night.

## \|\| 34.18-19 \|\|

*adṛśaṃ puṣpitaṃ vṛkṣamaśokakaravīrayoḥ \|*  
*tālavṛkṣaṃ ca phalitaṃ tatra caiva patatphalam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*svakarāpūrṇakalaśaḥ papāta ca babhañja ca \|*  
*ityapaśyaṃ ca gaganātsaṃpataccandramaṇḍalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. I also found the trees of Asoka, Nerium, Odorum, trees blossoming, the tala trees laden with fruits and the fruits falling from the trees. The vase filled with water had fallen from my hand and had broken, which was witnessed by me. I also found the moon and the stars falling from the sky.

## \|\| 34.20 \|\|

*apaśyamambarātsūryyamaṇḍalaṃ samampatadbhuvi \|*  
*ulkāpātaṃ dhūmaketuṃ grahaṇaṃ candrasūryyayoḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. I found the disk of the sun falling from the sky, the falling comets besides the eclipse of the sun and the moon.

## \|\| 34.21-22 \|\|

*vikṛtākārapuruṣaṃ vikaṭāsyaṃ digambaram \|*  
*āgacchantaṃ cāgratastamapaśyaṃ ca bhayānakam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*bālā dvādaśāvarṣīyā vastrabhūṣaṇabhūṣitā \|*  
*saṃruṣṭā yāti madgehādityapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. I found a person of gigantic proportion with a horrible look who was terribly hungry, naked and fearful in appearance approaching me. This is what I had seen in the dream.
