A twelve year old woman clad in all the garments adorned with all the ornaments left my place getting annoyed, such a dream had been witnessed by me during the night.

## \|\| 34.23 \|\|

*ājñāṃ tvaṃ dehi rājendra tvadgehādyāmi kānanam \|*  
*vadasi tvaṃ māmiti ca niśyapaśyamahaṃ śucā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. She was saying, "O king you permit me, I intend to leave your place and go to the forest. You tell me." Getting filled with grief I saw this dream during the night.

## \|\| 34.24 \|\|

*ruṣṭo vipro māṃ śapate saṃnyāsī ca tathā guruḥ \|*  
*bhittau puttalikāścitrā nṛtyantīśca dadarśa ha \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The Brahmanas, the ascetics and the teachers were also pronouncing curse on me getting annoyed. The figures painted on the walls were dancing. This is what I had seen.

## \|\| 34.25 \|\|

*cañcalānāṃ ca gṛdhrāṇāṃ kākānāṃ nikaraiḥ sadā \|*  
*pīḍitaṃ mahiṣāṇāṃ ca svamapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The unstable cows, vultures, clothes, herd of buffaloes were seen by me during the night.

## \|\| 34.26 \|\|

*pīḍitaṃ tailayantreṇa bhrāmitaṃ tailakāriṇā \|*  
*digambarānpāśahastānapaśyamahamīśvari \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. О goddess, I found the oil-man moving the oil-mill and various naked men were approaching me holding sticks in their hands.

## \|\| 34.27 \|\|

*nṛtyanti gāyakāḥ sarve gānaṃ gāyanti me gṛhe \|*  
*vivāhaṃ paramānandamityapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. I found all the singers of my house dancing. Then I found a marriage being performed delightfully in my house.

## \|\| 34.28 \|\|

*ramaṇaṃ kurvato lokānkeśākeśi ca kurvataḥ \|*  
*adṛśaṃ samaraṃ rātrau kākānāṃ ca śunāmapi \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The people were moving about holding the hair of one another. I also found crows and dogs during the night.

## \|\| 34.29 \|\|

*moṭakāni ca piṇḍāni śmaśānaṃ śavasaṃyutam \|*  
*raktavastraṃ śuklavastramapaśyaṃ niśi kāmini \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. О beautiful lady, I witness a pickaxe, pinda, a dead body in the cremation ground, besides red and white garments.

## \|\| 34.30 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāmbarā kṛṣṇavarṇā nagnā vai muktakeśinī \|*  
*vidhavā''śliṣyati ca māmapaśyaṃ niśi śobhane \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O beautiful one, I saw during the night a lady clad in black garments having a black complexion, appearing nude and having dishevelled hair. She was embracing me being a widow.

## \|\| 34.31-32 \|\|

*nāpito muṇḍate muṇḍaṃ śmaśruśreṇīṃ ca me priye \|*  
*vakṣaḥsthalaṃ ca nakharamityapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 31 \|\|*

*pādukā carmarajjūnāmapaśyaṃ rāśimulbaṇam \|*  
*cakraṃ bhramantaṃ bhūmau ca kulālasyeti sundari \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. O dear one, the barber was removing my beard and moustaches besides the hair of my chest and had the wounds of nails. О beautiful one, I found the potters wheel moving besides a string of leather in heaps.

## \|\| 34.33 \|\|

*vātyayā ghūrṇamānaṃ ca śuṣkavṛkṣaṃ tamutthitam \|*  
*pūrṇamānaṃ kabandhaṃ vai cāpaśyaṃ niśi suvrate \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О chaste lady, I found that with the movement of a strong wind, a fallen dry tree had stood erect and the trunk was moving without the head.

## \|\| 34.34 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde kārtavīryārjuna saṃnāho nāma catustriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О best of the lady, I found a well made garland of skulls which was moving with the terrific winds and whose tongue was protruding.

## \|\| 34.35 \|\|

*bhūtapretā muktakeśā vamantaśva hutāśanam \|*  
*māṃ bhīṣayanti satatamityapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. During the night I also saw that the goblins with dishevelled hair were inhaling fire flames and were frightening me at same time.

## \|\| 34.36 \|\|

*dagdhajīvaṃ dagdhavṛkṣaṃ vyādhigrastaṃ naraṃ param \|*  
*aṅgahīnaṃ ca vṛṣalamapyapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Burnt creatures, the burnt trees, the humans suffering from ailments besides the deformed Shudra woman also appeared before one.

## \|\| 34.37 \|\|

*gehaparvatavṛkṣāṇāṃ sahasā patanaṃ param \|*  
*muhurmuhurvajrapātamapyapaśyamahaṃ niśi \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. I also saw during the night, the mountains, the houses and trees suddenly falling with the attack of vaj'ra again and again.

## \|\| 34.38 \|\|

*kukkurāṇāṃ śṛgālānāṃ rodanaṃ ca muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*gṛhe gṛhe ca niyatamapaśyaṃ sarvato niśi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. I saw during the night, that in every house, the dogs and jackals were crying, while roaming about in all the directions.

## \|\| 34.39 \|\|

*adhaśśirastūrddhvapādaṃ muktakeśaṃ digambaram \|*  
*bhūmau bhramantaṃ gacchantaṃ cāpyapaśyamahaṃ naram \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. I also saw during the night heads placed on earth and the legs held in the sky. They had dishevelled hair and were roaming about naked.

## \|\| 34.40-41 \|\|

*vikṛtākāraśabdaṃ ca grāmādau deva rodanam \|*  
*prātaḥ śrutvaivāvabuddhaḥ ka upāyo vadādhunā \|\| 40 \|\|*

*nṛpatervacanaṃ śrutvā hṛdayena vidūyatā \|*  
*sagadgadaṃ ca rudatī tamuvāca nṛpeśvaram \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. Finding the people crying in the villages and those having deformed bodies besides the cries of the gods, woke me up in the morning. You tell me what is the remedy for this at the moment." On hearing the words of the king, Manorama spoke in a choked voice.

## \|\| 34.42-43 \|\|

*manoramovāca \|*  
*he nātha ramaṇaśreṣṭha śreṣṭha sarvamahībhṛtām \|*  
*prāṇātireka prāṇeśa śṛṇu vākyaṃ śubhāvaham \|\| 42 \|\|*

*nārāyaṇāṃśo bhagavāñjāmadagnyo mahābalī \|*  
*sṛṣṭisaṃharturīśasya śiṣyo'yaṃ jagataḥ prabhoḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. Manorama said – О best of the kings, О lord, you are the best among the kings. You are dearer to me than my life and are the lord of my life; you listen to me my beneficial words. Lord Jamadagni is quite valorous and happens to be the amsha of Narayana. He also happens to be the pupil of Shiva who is the destroyer of the universe.

## \|\| 34.44 \|\|

*triḥsaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kariṣyāmi mahīmiti \|*  
*pratijñā yasya rāmasya tena sārddhaṃ raṇaṃ tyaja \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He has taken a resolve to rid the earth of Ks astriya twenty-one times. Therefore, you discard the idea of fighting against Parashurama.

## \|\| 34.45 \|\|

*pāpinaṃ rāvaṇaṃ jitvā śūraṃ tvamapi manyase \|*  
*sa tvayā na jito nātha svapāpena parājitaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. You are considered to be a great warrior after defeating Ravana. In fact, О lord, you have not defeated him but he has been defeated because of his own sins.

## \|\| 34.46 \|\|

*yo na rakṣati dharmmaṃ ca tasya ko rakṣitā bhuvi \|*  
*sa naśyati svayaṃ mūḍho jīvannapi mṛto hi saḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Because the one who does not protect dharma, who can protect him on the earth? Such a person is himself destroyed by his own actions and seems to be dead while still alive.

## \|\| 34.47 \|\|

*śubhāśubhasya satataṃ sākṣī dharmmasya karmaṇaḥ \|*  
*ātmārāmaḥ sthitaḥ svānto mūḍhastvaṃ nahi paśyasi \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The one who is a witness to the good and bad deeds, the religious deeds, the soul of everyone and resides in the hearts of everyone, you are unable to realise his worth because of your ignorance.

## \|\| 34.48-49 \|\|

*putradārādikaṃ yadyatsarvaiśvaryyaṃ sudharmiṇām \|*  
*jalabudbudavatsarvamanityaṃ naśvaraṃ nṛpa \|\| 48 \|\|*

*saṃsāraṃ svapnasadṛśaṃ matvā santo'tra bhārate \|*  
*dhyāyanti satataṃ dharmmaṃ tapaḥ kurvanti bhaktitaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. О king, for the religious people, the son, the wife and all the pleasures are perishable like the bubbles of water; therefore the ascetics and noble persons considering the universe to be like a dream, always devote themselves to dharma and perform tapas with great devotion.

## \|\| 34.50-51 \|\|

*dattena dattaṃ yajjñānaṃ tatsarvaṃ vismṛtaṃ tvayā \|*  
*asti cedviprahiṃsāyāṃ kubuddhe tvanmanaḥ katham \|\| 50 \|\|*

*sukhārthe mṛgayāṃ gatvā tatropoṣya dvijāśrame \|*  
*bhuktvā miṣṭamapūrvaṃ ca hato vipro nirarthakam \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. You have forgotten the divine knowledge imparted to you by Dattatreya; otherwise how could you involve yourself in the killing of a Brahmana? You had gone for the sake of pleasure.

## \|\| 34.52 \|\|

*guru viprasurāṇāṃ ca yaḥ karoti parābhavam \|*  
*abhīṣṭadevastaṃ ruṣṭo vipattistasya sannidhau \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The one who insults the teacher, the Brahmana and the gods, the gods get annoyed with him and he is approached by misfortune pretty fast.

## \|\| 34.53 \|\|

*smaraṇaṃ kuru rājendra dattātreyapadāmbujam \|*  
*gurau bhaktiśca sarveṣāṃ sarvavighnavināśinī \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. О king, you remember the lotus-like feet of Dattatreya because of which the obstructions are removed by the reciting of the name of the teacher with devotion.

## \|\| 34.54 \|\|

*gurudevaṃ samabhyarcya taṃ bhṛguṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja \|*  
*vipre deve prasanne ca kṣattriyāṇāṃ nahi kṣatiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Adoring the same teacher you take refuge with Bhrigu. A Kshatriya never meets with any harm when he adores the gods and the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 34.55 \|\|

*viprasya kiṃkaro bhūpo vaiśyo bhūpasya bhūmipa \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ kiṅkarāḥ śūdrā brāhmaṇasya viśeṣataḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. О lord of the earth, the king happens to be the servant of the Brahmanas, Vaisya happens to be the servant of the king and the Shudra happens to be the servant of everyone and more so of a Brahmana.

## \|\| 34.56 \|\|

*ayaśaḥ śaraṇaṃ śaśvatkṣatriyasya ca kṣatriye \|*  
*mahadyaśastaccharaṇaṃ gurudevadvijeṣu ca \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. By taking refuge with the Kshatriya, he meets with disgrace but by taking refuge with the teacher, the gods and Brahmanas, one achieves great glory.

## \|\| 34.57-59 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaṃ bhaja rājendra garīyāṃsaṃ surādapi \|*  
*brāhmaṇe parituṣṭe ca santuṣṭāḥ sarvadevatāḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

*ityevamuktvā rājendraṃ kroḍe kṛtvā mahāsatī \|*  
*muhurmuhurmukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā vilalāpa ruroda ca \|\| 58 \|\|*

*kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭha mahārāja punarevamuvāca sā \|*  
*snānaṃ kuru mahārāja bhojayiṣyāmi vāñchitam \|\| 59 \|\|*

57-59. О best of the kings, the Brahmanas are considered to be better then the gods. Therefore, you serve them because at the pleasure of the Brahmanas all the gods also get pleased. Thus the immensely chaste lady made the king to understand everything and took him in her lap. She then started crying looking at his face, she again said, "O lord, you wait for a while; have a bath I shall serve you the food of your liking.

## \|\| 34.60 \|\|

*candanāgurukastūrīkarpūraiḥ kuṃkumairyutam \|*  
*anulepaṃ kariṣyāmi sarvāṃge tava sundara \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. О beautiful one, I shall adorn your body with sandal-paste, aguru, kastiirl, camphor, saffron.

## \|\| 34.61 \|\|

*kṣaṇaṃ siṃhāsane tiṣṭha kṣaṇaṃ vakṣasi me prabho \|*  
*sabhāyāṃ puṣparacite talpe paśyāmi śobhanam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. О lord, you take your seat on the lion-throne for a moment and be seated on my breast for a moment. I would like to see you on the flowery bed in the court.

## \|\| 34.62 \|\|

*śataputrādhikaḥ premṇā satīnāṃ vai patirnṛpa \|*  
*nirūpito bhagavatā vedeṣu hariṇā svayam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Because, О king, for the chaste ladies the husband is dearer to them than hundreds of their sons. This has been ordained by the lord himself in the Vedas.

## \|\| 34.63 \|\|

*manorastavacaḥ śrutvā rājā paramapaṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*bodhayāmāsa tāṃ rājñīṃ dadau pratyuttaraṃ punaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. On hearing the words of Manorama the most intelligent king tried to convince the queen and said to her.

## \|\| 34.64 \|\|

*kārttavīryyārjuna uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu kānte pravakṣyāmi śrutaṃ sarvaṃ tvayeritam \|*  
*śokārtānāṃ ca vacanaṃ na praśaṃsyaṃ sabhāsu ca \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Kartavirya said – О damsels, I have listened to all your words. The court does not take seriously the words of the people who are in grief.

## \|\| 34.65-66 \|\|

*sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhayaṃ śokaḥ kalahaḥ prītireva ca \|*  
*karma bhogārhakālena sarvaṃ bhavati sundari \|\| 65 \|\|*

*kālo dadāti rājatvaṃ kālo mṛtyuṃ punarbhavam \|*  
*kālaḥ sṛjati saṃsāraṃ kālaḥ saṃharate punaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

65-66. О beautiful one, the pleasure, the pain, fear, grief, quarrel and love, appear only according it is time above the time and to time alone that gives them their due importance. The time is death and rebirth. The time also creates the universe and also destroys it.

## \|\| 34.67-73 \|\|

*karoti pālanaṃ kālaḥ kālarūpī janārdanaḥ \|*  
*kālasya kālaḥ śrīkṛṣṇo vidhāturvidhireva ca \|\| 67 \|\|*

*saṃhartturvā'pi saṃharttā pātuḥ pātā ca karmakṛt \|*  
*sa karmaṇāṃ karmarūpī dadāti tapasāṃ phalam \|*  
*kaḥ kena hanyate jantuḥ karmaṇā vai vinā sati \|*  
*' \|\| 68 \|\|*

*sraṣṭā sṛjati sṛṣṭiṃ ca saṃharttā saṃharetpunaḥ \|*  
*pātā pāti ca bhūtāni yasyājñāṃ paripālayet \|\| 69 \|\|*

*yasyājñayā vāti vātaḥ santataṃ bhayavihvalaḥ \|*  
*śaśvatsañcarate mṛtyuḥ sūryyastapati santatam \|\| 70 \|\|*

*varṣatīndro dahatyagniḥ kālo bhramati bhītavat \|*  
*tiṣṭhanti sthāvarāḥ sarve caranti satataṃ carāḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

*vṛkṣāśca puṣpitāḥ kāle phalitaḥ pallavānvitāḥ \|*  
*śuṣyanti kālataḥ kāle varddhante ca tadājñayā \|\| 72 \|\|*

*āvirbhūtā tirobhūtā sṛṣṭireva tadājñayā \|*  
*tasyājñayā bhavetsarvaṃ na kiñcitsvecchayā nṛṇām \|\| 73 \|\|*

67-73. The time preserves it and Janardana himself is the form of time. Lord Krishna happens to be the death for the time, the creator of the creator, the killer of the killer, the preserver of the preserver and the one who performs the deeds. He alone becoming the form of the deeds provides the result of all the deeds. О chaste lady, who can kill anyone without the influence of deeds, by his command the creator creates the universe, the destroyer destroys it and the preserver preserves the universe. By whose command the wind getting terrified, always blows and death prevails everywhere and the sun bums, the god of death goes on roaming about getting everyone terrified, all the immovable things remain static and the movable things move on endlessly. The trees blossom with fruits and flowers and according to time they also dry up. They also grow up at the command of time, at the command of the time the universe appears and then disappears, the humans move according to the will of the time and nothing is possible on their own will.

## \|\| 34.74-75 \|\|

*nārāyaṇāṃśo bhagavāñjāmadagnyo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kariṣyati mahīmiti \|\| 74 \|\|*

*pratijñā viphalā tasya na bhavettu kadācana \|*  
*niścitaṃ tasya vadhyo'hamiti jānāmi suvrate \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. Lord Paras'urama is the arhsa of Narayana and the great warrior, he has already taken a vow to rid the earth of the Kshatriyas twenty-one times which cannot be otherwise. Therefore, О chaste lady, I am bound to meet my death at his hands and I am sure of this.

## \|\| 34.76 \|\|

*jñātvā sarvaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca śaraṇaṃ yāmi tatkatham \|*  
*pratiṣṭhitānāṃ cākīrttirmaraṇādatiricyate \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Being well aware of the future, how can I take refuge with him, because for the glorious people disgrace is worse than death.

## \|\| 34.77 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā rājendraḥ samaraṃ gantumudyataḥ \|*  
*vādyaṃ ca vādayāmāsa kārayāmāsa maṃgalam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. Thus speaking the king got ready for proceeding to the battle field. He started the playing of war-bands and also have the welfare song recited.

## \|\| 34.78-80 \|\|

*śatakoṭirnṛpāṇāṃ ca gajendrāṇāṃ trilakṣakam \|*  
*akṣauhiṇīnāṃ śatakaṃ mahābalaparākramam \|\| 78 \|\|*

*aśvānāṃ ca gajānāṃ ca padātīnāṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*asaṃkhyakaṃ rathānāṃ ca gṛhītvā gantumudyataḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

*babhūva stimitā sādhvī dṛṣṭvā taṃ gamanonmukham \|*  
*dhṛtavantaṃ ca sannāhamakṣayaṃ saśaraṃ dhanuḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

78-80. His army consisted of a hundred crore of kings, three lakhs of great kings accompanied by great warriors and hundred Aksauhinl army including elephants, horses, foot soldiers and innumerable chariots. He intended to move on with the entire army. But in the meantime Manorama wearing a smile on her face prevented him from moving forward and take away the infallible kavacha, the arrow and the bow.

## \|\| 34.81 \|\|

*krīḍāgāre kṣaṇaṃ tasthau kṛtvā kāntaṃ svavakṣasi \|*  
*paśyantī tanmukhāmbhojaṃ cucumba ca muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. She took him to the pleasure house and embraced him, kissing his face again and again.

## Chapter 35

## \|\| 35.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*manoramā prāṇanāthaṃ kṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā svavakṣasi \|*  
*bhaviṣyaṃ manasā cakre yadyatsvāmimukhācchutam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – Manorama embraced her husband for a moment and whatever she had heard from the mouth of her husband, she seriously considered its the meaning.

## \|\| 35.2-4 \|\|

*putrāṃśca purataḥ hṛtvā bāndhavāṃśca svakiṃkarān \|*  
*sasmāra sā haripadaṃ mene satyaṃ bhavenmune \|\| 2 \|\|*

*yogena bhittvā ṣaṭcakraṃ vāyuṃ saṃsthāpya mūrddhani \|*  
*brahmarandhrasthakamale sahasradalasaṃyute \|\| 3 \|\|*

*svāntamākṛṣya viṣayājjalabudbudasannibhāt \|*  
*saṃsthāpya baddhvā jñānena lolaṃ brahmaṇi niṣkale \|\| 4 \|\|*

2-4. О sage, thereafter she called for her relatives, sons and servants before her and considered the destiny's will which always prevails. She started reciting the name of the lord. With the yogic practices, she pierced through the Sat-cakra and established the air in Brahmdnda. Thereafter quick as a bubble of water she removed her mind from worldly pleasures and then concentrated her attention on the thousand pettaled lotus in Brahmarandhra. She then attracted the attention of her mind to the Brahmana and devoted her unstable mind to the divine knowledge making it stable.

## \|\| 35.5 \|\|

*dvividhaṃ karma saṃnyasya nirmūlamapunarbhavam \|*  
*tatra prāṇāṃśca tatyāja na ca prāṇādhikaṃ priyam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. She then discarded three types of deeds in order to destroy completely the deeds as well as rebirth. She even ended her life without ending her relationship with her husband.

## \|\| 35.6 \|\|

*sa rājā tāṃ mṛtāṃ dṛṣṭvā vilalāpa ruroda ca \|*  
*sannāhaṃ saṃparityajya kṛtvā vakṣasyuvāca tām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Finding her dead the king started lamenting and crying and throwing away the kavacha, he took her into his lap and said.

## \|\| 35.7 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*manorame samuttiṣṭha na yāsyāmi raṇājiram \|*  
*sacetanā māṃ paśyeti vilapantaṃ muhumurhuḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The king said – "О Manorama, get up I will not go to the battle-field now. You revive your senses and look at me." He thus kept on lamenting again and again.

## \|\| 35.8 \|\|

*manorame samuttiṣṭha mayā sārddhaṃ gṛhaṃ vraja \|*  
*na kariṣyāmi samaraṃ bhṛguṇā saha bhāmini \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Manorama, you get up and accompany me. О damsel, I shall not fight against Bhrigu now.

## \|\| 35.9 \|\|

*manorame samuttiṣṭha śrīśailaṃ vraja sundari \|*  
*tatra krīḍāṃ kariṣyāmi tvayā sārddhaṃ yathā purā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. О Manorama, О beautiful one, get up and accompany me to the mountain peak and let me enjoy the love-sport with you there.

## \|\| 35.10-11 \|\|

*manorame samuttiṣṭha vraja godāvarīṃ priye \|*  
*jalakrīḍāṃ kariṣyāmi tvayā sārdhaṃ yathā purā \|\| 10 \|\|*

*manorame samuttiṣṭha nandanaṃ vraja sundari \|*  
*puṣpabhadrānadītīre vihariṣyāmi nirjane \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. О Manorama, dear one, you get up, go to Godavari and let us have the water-sport there as usual. О Manorama, О beautiful one, you get up and let us go to Puspabhadra river and visit a secluded place in your company.

## \|\| 35.12 \|\|

*manorame samuttiṣṭha malayaṃ vraja sundari \|*  
*tvayā sārddhaṃ ramiṣye'haṃ tatra candanakānane \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О Manorama, О beautiful one, you get up and let us move to the Malaya mountain; let me enjoy your company in the forest of sandal-wood trees.

## \|\| 35.13 \|\|

*śītena gandhayuktena vāyunā surabhīkṛte \|*  
*bhramaradhvanisaṃyukte puṃskokila rutaśrite \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The place is filled with fragrance and cool breeze and the place is reverberated with the hissing sound of the black-wasps and the sweet sound of the cuckoo.

## \|\| 35.14 \|\|

*candanāgurukastūrīkuṃkumālepanaṃ kuru \|*  
*candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ paśya māṃ sasmite sati \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Reaching there you adorn my body with sandal-paste, aguru, kastUri, saffron, smilingly and look at the limbs of my body delightfully.

## \|\| 35.15 \|\|

*sudhātulyaṃ sumadhuraṃ vacanaṃ racaya priye \|*  
*kuṭilabhrūvikāraṃ ca kathaṃ na kuruṣe'dhunā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О dear one, you speak out sweet words like nectar, why are you not looking at me with side glances?"

## \|\| 35.16 \|\|

*nṛpasya rodanaṃ śrutvā vāgbabhūvāśarīriṇī \|*  
*sthirībhava mahārāja kuruṣe rodanaṃ katham \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Hearing the cries of the king there was a divine voice from the sky. "O king, be stable. Why are you crying?

## \|\| 35.17 \|\|

*tvaṃ mahājñānināṃ śreṣṭho dattātreyaprasādataḥ \|*  
*jalabudbudavatsarvaṃ saṃsāraṃ paśya śobhanam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. By the grace of Dattatreya you are the best of intellectuals and treat this universe like a bubble of water.

## \|\| 35.18 \|\|

*kamalāṃśā ca sā sādhvī jagāma kamalālayam \|*  
*tvameva gaccha vaikuṇṭhaṃ raṇaṃ kṛtvā raṇājire \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The chaste Kamala was the athsha of Lakshmi who has departed to her abode; you also proceed to the battle-field and achieve Vaikuntha while fighting there."

## \|\| 35.19-20 \|\|

*ityeva vacanaṃ śrutvā jahau śokaṃ narādhipaḥ \|*  
*tataścandanakāṣṭhena citāṃ divyāṃ cakāra ha \|\| 19 \|\|*

*saṃskārāgniṃ kārayitvā putradvārā dadāha tām \|*  
*nānāvidhāni ratnāni brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Hearing this the king shed away the grief and arranged for a pyre of sandal-wood and made his son to perform the last rights of his mother and distributed charity to the Brahmanas with delight.

## \|\| 35.21 \|\|

*nānāvidhāni dānāni vastrāṇi vividhāni ca \|*  
*manoramāyāḥ puṇyena brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. For the welfare of the departed soul of Manorama, the king distributed several types of riches including clothes to the Brahmanas with a delightful mind.

## \|\| 35.22-24 \|\|

*bhujyatāṃ bhujyatāṃ śaśvaddīyatāṃ dīyatāmiti \|*  
*śabdo babhūva sarvatra kārttavīryyāśrame mune \|\| 22 \|\|*

*koṣeṣu svādhikāreṣu sthitaṃ yadyaddhanaṃ tadā \|*  
*manoramāyāḥ puṇyena brāhmaṇebhyo dadau mudā \|\| 23 \|\|*

*rājā jagāma samaraṃ hṛdayena vidūyatā \|*  
*sārdhaṃ sainyasamūhaiśca vādyabhāṇḍairasaṃkhyakaiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

22-24. О sage, at that point of time the words of "consume more" or "bring more" were constantly heard. The king gave away in charity enormous riches whatever was available in his treasury. Thereafter, the king with a heavy heart accompanied by innumerable soldiers and war-bands moved towards the battle-field.

## \|\| 35.25 \|\|

*dadarśa maṅgalaṃ rājā puro vartmani vartmani \|*  
*yayau tathāpi samaraṃ nājagāma gṛhaṃ punaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. While moving on the journey he met with many inauspicious signs but disregarding them he kept on his forward march and never returned home.

## \|\| 35.26-46 \|\|

*muktakeśīṃ chinnanāsāṃ rudatīṃ ca digambarām \|*  
*kṛṣṇavastraparīdhānāmaparāṃ vidhavāmapi \|\| 26 \|\|*

*mukhaduṣṭāṃ yoniduṣṭāṃ vyādhiyuktāṃ ca kuṭṭinīm \|*  
*patiputravihīnāṃ ca ḍākinīṃ puṃścalī tathā \|\| 27 \|\|*

*kumbhakāraṃ tailakāraṃ vyādhaṃ sarpopajīvinam \|*  
*kucailamatirūkṣāṃgaṃ nagnaṃ kāṣāyavāsinam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*vasāvikrayiṇaṃ caiva kanyāvikrayiṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*citādagdhaṃ śavaṃ bhasma nirvāṇāṅgārameva ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

*sarpakṣataṃ naraṃ sarpaṃ godhāṃ ca śaśakaṃ viṣam \|*  
*śrāddhapākaṃ ca piṇḍaṃ ca moṭakaṃ ca tilāṃstathā \|\| 30 \|\|*

*devalaṃ vṛṣavāhaṃ ca śūdraśrāddhānnabhojinam \|*  
*śūdrānnapācakaṃ śūdrayājakaṃ grāmayājakam\_ā \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kuśaputtalikāṃ caiva śavadāhanakāriṇam \|*  
*śūnyakumbhaṃ bhagnakumbhaṃ tailaṃ lavaṇamasthi ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

*kārpāsaṃ kacchapaṃ cūrṇaṃ kukkuraṃ śabdakāriṇam \|*  
*dakṣiṇe ca sṛgālaṃ ca kurvantaṃ bhairavaṃ ravam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*kapardakaṃ ca kṣauraṃ ca cchinnakeśaṃ nakhaṃ malam \|*  
*kalahaṃ ca vilāpaṃ ca tathā tatkāriṇaṃ janam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde śaṅkarakavacaprakathanaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*mithyāsākṣya pradātāraṃ cauraṃ ca naraghātinam \|*  
*puṃścalīpatiputrau ca puṃścalyodanabhojinam \|\| 36 \|\|*

*devatāguruviprāṇāṃ vastuvittāpahāriṇam \|*  
*dattāpahāriṇaṃ dasyuṃ hiṃsakaṃ sūcakaṃ khalam \|\| 37 \|\|*

*pitṛmātṛviraktaṃ ca dvijāśvatthavighātinam \|*  
*satyaghnaṃ ca kṛtaghnaṃ ca sthāpyasyāpyapahāriṇam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*vipramitradrohamevaṃ kṣataṃ viśvāsaghātakam \|*  
*gurudevadvijānāṃ ca nindakaṃ svāṃgaghātakam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*jīvānāṃ ghātakaṃ caiva svāṃgahīnaṃ ca nirdayam \|*  
*vratopavāsahīnaṃ ca dīkṣāhīnaṃ napuṃsakam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*galitavyādhigātraṃ ca kāṇaṃ badhirameva ca \|*  
*pulkasaṃ chinnaliṅgaṃ ca surāmattaṃ surāṃ tathā \|\| 41 \|\|*

*kṣiptaṃ vamantaṃ rudhiraṃ mahiṣaṃ gardabhaṃ tathā \|*  
*mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ śleṣmāṇaṃ rūkṣiṇaṃ nṛkapālakam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*caṇḍavātaṃ raktavṛṣṭiṃ vādyaṃ vai vṛkṣapātanam \|*  
*vṛkaṃ ca sūkaraṃ gṛdhraṃ śyenaṃ kaṅkaṃ ca bhallukam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*pāśaṃ ca śuṣkakāṣṭhaṃ ca vāyasaṃ gandhakaṃ tathā \|\| 44 \|\|*

*pratigrāhibrāhmaṇaṃ ca tantramantropajīvinam \|*  
*vaidyaṃ ca raktapuṣpaṃ cāpyauṣadhaṃ tuṣameva ca \|\| 45 \|\|*

*kuvārttāṃ mṛtavārtāṃ ca vipraśāpaṃ ca dāruṇam \|*  
*durgandhivātaṃ duśśabdaṃ rājā'paśyatsa vartmani \|\| 46 \|\|*

26-46. He met with a naked widow with dishevelled hair and a severed nose and crying. Besides the wicked-faced, the degraded and ailing kuttinl, a woman without a child, Dakinis, an unchaste woman, a potter, an oil-man, a hunter, a snake-charmer, a man with dirty clothes and a bright body which was naked besides the one wearing ascetic garments, a seller of fat, a seller or daughter, a fire in which a body was burning, ashes, coal, a human bitten by snakes, a lizard, a rabbit, poison, food of sraddha, pinda, motaka, sheshamum, a temple of Shudra, a driver of bullocks, a performer of the sraddhas of the Shudras, a store-keeper of the Shudras, a performer of the yajha of Shudras, the one who performed yajnas from village to village, the one who burnt puppets in the cremation ground, an empty pitcher, a broken pitcher, oil, salt, bones, cofion, a tortoise, powder, barking dogs, a jackal howling on the right, small shells, a barber, nails, refuse, quarrelling people, lamenting people and the one who always spoke evil words, crying men, men in grief, men who always gave false evidence, a thief, a killer of humans, wicked women without husband and sons, the one who lived on the rice of a wicked woman, gods, teachers, the one who stole away the belongings of the Brahmanas, the one who usurped the things given away in charity, decoits, a terrorist, a back biter, a wicked person, the one who neglected his parents, the killers of the Brahmana and the pipala tree, a person who spoke falsehood, an ungrateful person, a usurper of the trust, the one who betrays Brahmanas and the friends, an injured person, a betrayer of the faith, the one who denounces the teacher, gods and Brahmanas, a destroyer of his own limbs, a killer of the creatures, deformed, cruel, the one deprived of the vrata and the vow, the one who did not give daksina, impotent, suffering from leprosy, a one-eyed person, deaf, Chandala, the one whose male organs are cut-off, a drunkard, a seller of wine, the one vomiting blood, a he-buffalo, a donkey, urine, refuse, cough, a person with shaven head, a terrific wind, a rain of blood, a man falling from a tree, a hyena, a pig, a vulture, an eagle, a white-kite, a bear, a bamboo, dry wood, a crow, sulphuric acid, a Brahmana the giver of charity, the one who lived on tantric practices, a physician, red flowers, medicine, straw, bad news, news of death, a terrific curse of Brahmana, a wind with foul smell, a horrible wound, a wind – these were the evil signs met by with king on the way.

## \|\| 35.47 \|\|

*manaśca kutsitaṃ prāṇāḥ kṣubhitāśca nirantaram \|*  
*vāmāṃgaspandanaṃ dehajāḍyaṃ rājño babhūva ha \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. All these made the mind of the king gloomy and he became upset, his body was fatigued and his left limbs started fluttering.

## \|\| 35.48 \|\|

*tathā'pi rājā niśśaṅko dadarśa samarāṅgaṇam \|*  
*sarvasainyasamāyuktaḥ praviveśa raṇājiram \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Inspite of all this, the king marched on towards the battle-field quite courageously. He entered the battle-field together with all his army.

## \|\| 35.49 \|\|

*avaruhya rathāttūrṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca purato bhṛgum \|*  
*nanāma daṇḍavadbhūmau rājendraiḥ saha bhaktitaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Finding Parashurama there, he descended from his chariot and he bowed in reverence to him together with all his sons.

## \|\| 35.50 \|\|

*āśiṣaṃ yuyuje rāmaḥ svargaṃ yāhīti vāñchitam \|*  
*teṣāṃ sahyaṃ tadbabhūvurdulaṃghyā brāhmaṇāśiṣaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Parasurama on his part blessed them saying, "You achieve your desired heaven." Thus the words of a Brahmana cannot be otherwise.

## \|\| 35.51-52 \|\|

*bhṛguṃ praṇamya rājendro rājendraiḥ saha tatkṣaṇāt \|*  
*āruroha rathaṃ tūrṇaṃ nānāyudhasamanvitam \|\| 51 \|\|*

*nānāprakāravādyaṃ ca dundubhiṃ murajādikam \|*  
*vādayāmāsa sahasā brāhmaṇebhyo dadau dhanam \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. The king assisted by many of the princes bowed in reverence to the king and hurriedly mounted on the chariot and made the war-bands and other musical instruments to be played upon. He gave away charities to the Brahmanas.

## \|\| 35.53 \|\|

*uvāca rāmo rājendraṃ rājendrāṇāṃ ca saṃsadi \|*  
*hitaṃ satyaṃ nītisāraṃ vākyaṃ vedavidāṃ varaḥ \|\| 53\|\|*

53\. Thereafter, Parashurama addressing the king in the assembly of kings spoke the beneficial words which were filled with welfare, were truthful and appropriate.

## \|\| 35.54-55 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu rājendra dharmiṣṭha candravaṃśasamudbhava \|*  
*viṣṇoraṃśasya śiṣyastvaṃ dattātreyasya dhīmataḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

*svayaṃ vidvāṃśca vedāṃśca śrutvā vedavido mukhāt \|*  
*kathaṃ durbuddhiradhunā sajjanānāṃ vihiṃsanā \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. Parashurama said – О king, you are quite a religious one; having been born in the solar race, you are arhs of Vishnu and are the pupil of great intellectual Dattatreya. You have heard the recitation of the Vedas from the mouth of those who were well-versed in the Vedas but how did you achieve this thought to kill the noble people?

## \|\| 35.56 \|\|

*tvaṃ pūrvamahano lobhānnirīhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ katham \|*  
*brāhmaṇī śokasantaptā bhartrā sārddhaṃ gatā satī \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Why did you kill an ascetic Brahmana getting overwhelmed grief? As a result of which, the chaste lady went to tne abode of her husband.

## \|\| 35.57-58 \|\|

*kiṃ bhaviṣyati te bhūpa paratraivānayorvadhāt \|*  
*sarvaṃ mithyaiva saṃsāraṃ padmapatre yathā jalam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*satkīrttiścātha duṣkīrtiḥ kathāmātrāvaśeṣitā \|*  
*viḍambanā vā kimato duṣkīrtteśca satāmaho \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. О king, what is the merit you could achieve by killing both of them? The entire universe is like the drops of water falling on a lotus leaf. In this universe a person meets only with grace and disgrace and the tales of his good and bad deeds are left behind. What is the use of earning disgrace by putting the noble people to disgrace?

## \|\| 35.59 \|\|

*kva gatā kapilā tvaṃ kva kva vivādo muniḥ kutaḥ \|*  
*yatkṛtaṃ viduṣā rājñā na kṛtaṃ hālikena tat \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Where has that Kapila gone? Where is the remorse that developed because of that and where has the ascetic gone? Thus evidentially the deeds performed by a king cannot even be performed by the one who ploughs the field.

## \|\| 35.60 \|\|

*tvāmupoṣitamīśaṃ hi dṛṣṭvā tāto hi dhārmikaḥ \|*  
*pāraṇāṃ kārayāmāsa dattaṃ tasya phalaṃ tvayā \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Finding you hungry my religious-minded father served you with food and you gave him the reward.

## \|\| 35.61 \|\|

*adhītaṃ vidhivaddattaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dine dine \|*  
*jagate yaśasā pūrṇamayaśo vārddhake katham \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. You have yourself studied the Vedas, served food to the Brahmana daily, which earned you the glory assured in the Vedas but why did you earn this disgrace in your old age?

## \|\| 35.62-63 \|\|

*dātā variṣṭho dharmmiṣṭho yaśasvī puṇyavānsudhīḥ \|*  
*kārttavīryārjunasamo na bhūto na bhaviṣyati \|\| 62 \|\|*

*purātanā vadantīti bandino dharaṇītale \|*  
*yo vikhyātaḥ purāṇeṣu tasya duṣkīrttirīdṛśī \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. Because no one could be as charitable, valorous, religious-minded, glorious, meritorious and intelligent as a king like Kartavirya in the past nor shall be in the future. These are the songs which the bards recite on the earth, the one who is well known is the Puranas, how could he meet with such a disgrace.

## \|\| 35.64 \|\|

*durvākyaṃ duḥsahaṃ rājaṃstīkṣṇāstrādapi jīvinām \|*  
*saṃkaṭe'pi satāṃ vaktrāddviruktirna vinirgatā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. О king, the harsh words of a person are worse than the sharp-edged weapons but inspite of any danger the noble people never utter harsh words.

## \|\| 35.65 \|\|

*na dadāmi dviruktiṃ te prakṛtaṃ kathayāmyaham \|*  
*uttaraṃ dehi rājendra mahyaṃ rājendrasaṃsadi \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. I don't want to use harsh words against you, but I am only speaking with reference to the context. Therefore, О king, you give me the answer in your court.

## \|\| 35.66-67 \|\|

*sūryyacandramanūnāṃ ca vaṃśajāḥ santi saṃsadi \|*  
*satyaṃ vada sabhāyāṃ ca śṛṇvantu pitaraḥ surāḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*śṛṇvantu sarve rājendrāḥ sadasadvaktumīśvarāḥ \|*  
*paśyanto hi samaṃ santaḥ pākṣikaṃ na vadanti ca \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. Because the descendants of the solar, lunar and Manu's races are present here, you speak out the truth in this assembly to enable your manes and the gods to listen to the truth besides other kings; the noble people always look at all the people similarly."

## \|\| 35.68 \|\|

*ityuktvā raiṇukeyaśca virarāma raṇasthale \|*  
*rājā bṛhaspatisamaḥ pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Thus speaking Parashurama kept quiet in the battle-field. Thereafter the king started speaking like Brihaspati in the battle-field.

## \|\| 35.69 \|\|

*kārttavīryyārjuna uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu rāma hareraṃśo haribhakto jitendriyaḥ \|*  
*śruto dharmo mukhādyeṣāṃ tvaṃ ca teṣāṃ gurorguruḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Kartavirya said – О Parashurama you are the arhsha of the lord besides being devoted to him and are the one who had controlled all his senses and the persons from whose mouth I have learnt about dharmas; you happen to be the teacher of the same teacher.

## \|\| 35.70 \|\|

*karmmaṇā vā'pyasadbudhyā karoti brahmabhāvanām \|*  
*svadharmanirataḥ śuddhastasmādbrāhmaṇa uccate \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The one who is born of the Brahmana, his mind is influenced by Brahman and he always gets devoted to the dharma. That is why he is called a Brahmana.

## \|\| 35.71 \|\|

*antarbahiśca mananātkuruta karmma nityaśaḥ \|*  
*maunī śaśvadvadetkāle yo vai sa munirucyate \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. The one who acts moving around and keeping mum and speaks only when it is necessary, he is called the muni.

## \|\| 35.72-73 \|\|

*svarṇe loṣṭe gṛhe'raṇye paṃke susnigdhacandane \|*  
*samatābhāvanā yasya sa yogī parikīrttitaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

*sarvajīveṣu yo viṣṇuṃ bhāvayetsamatā dhiyā \|*  
*harau karoti bhaktiṃ ca haribhaktaḥ sa ca smṛtaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

72-73. The one who has an equal feeling for gold, dust, house, forest, mud and sandal-paste, is called a yogi, the one who visualises Vishnu, equally in all the creatures and is always devoted to the lord, he is called a devotee.

## \|\| 35.74-75 \|\|

*tapodhanaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tapaḥ kalpataruryathā \|*  
*tapasyā kāmadhenuśca santataṃ tapasi spṛhā \|\| 74 \|\|*

*aiśvaryye kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca vāṇijye ca tathā viśām \|*  
*kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca tapasi spṛhā'tīvā'praśaṃsitā \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. The Brahmanas have only the tapas as their riches which serve them like kalpavrksa and their tapas is like Kamadhenu; therefore they always remain engaged in the tapas; the Brahmanas are highly esteemed. The riches are the belongings of Kshatriyas and the Vaisyas always remain engaged in business while the Stidras serve the Brahmana as well as the other people.

## \|\| 35.76 \|\|

*brāhmaṇānāṃ vivāde ca spṛhā'tīva vininditā \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The Ksatriyas are not so much devoted to performing tapas and if a Brahmana enters into any controversy he becomes deplorable.

## \|\| 35.77-78 \|\|

*rāgī rājasikaṃ kāryyaṃ kurute karmma rāgataḥ \|*  
*rāgāndho yo rājasikastena rājā prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

*rāgataḥ kāmadhenuśca mayā vai yācitā mune \|*  
*ko doṣa eka me jātaḥ kṣatriyasyānurāgiṇaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

77-78. The greedy person getting overcome by grief performs accordingly and involves himself in Rajoguna. He is, therefore, called a king. О sage, infatuated with greed I demanded Kamadhenu, therefore what is the wrong if a Ksatriya like me becomes greedy.

## \|\| 35.79 \|\|

*kutaḥ kasya munerasti kāmadhenustvayā vinā \|*  
*spṛhā raṇe vā bhoge vā yuṣmākaṃ ca vyatikramaḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Except you, who else is the sage possessing Kamadhenu, you have the opposite feeling about the battle-field and worldly pleasures?

## \|\| 35.80 \|\|

*triṃśadakṣauhiṇīṃ senāṃ rājendrāṇāṃ trikoṭikām \|*  
*nihatyāyāntamekaṃ māṃ na hantuṃ sahanaṃ mune \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. О sage, how can one tolerate a person who kills three aksauhini army and three crores of kings.

## \|\| 35.81 \|\|

*ātmānaṃ hantumāyāntamapi vedāṃgapāragam \|*  
*na doṣo hanane tasya na tena brahmahā'bhavam \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. Even if a person well-versed in the Vedas comes to kill me, there is no harm in destroying him. Therefore I do not happen to be the killer of a Brahmana.

## \|\| 35.82-84 \|\|

*prāyaścittaṃ hiṃsakānāṃ na vedeṣu nirūpitam \|*  
*vadhaḥ samucitasteṣāmityāha kamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

*pitrā te nihatā bhūpā mahābalaparākramāḥ \|*  
*idānīṃ rājaputrāśca śiśavo'tra samāgatāḥ \|\| 83 \|\|*

*triḥsaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kṛtsnāṃ kartuṃ mahīmiti \|*  
*tvayā kṛtā pratijñā yā tasyāstvaṃ pālanaṃ kuru \|\| 84 \|\|*

82-84. Because the Vedas do not prescribe for any repentance for the killing of the one who comes to kill you. It would be proper for anyone to kill such a person. This has been ordained by Brahma. Your father has killed many of the valorous kings possessing great prowess. The princes, who have come to face you are only the sons of those kings. Therefore, you fulfil your vow for ridding the earth of Kshatriyas twenty one times.

## \|\| 35.85 \|\|

*kṣatriyāṇāṃ raṇo dharmo raṇe mṛtyurna garhitaḥ \|*  
*raṇe svāhā brāhmaṇānāṃ loke vede viḍambanā \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Kshatriyas, dharmas is to fight; therefore their death in the battle-field is not denounced. It is also a fallacy for the Brahmanas to think of fighting which is neither accepted in the Vedas nor by the people.

## \|\| 35.86 \|\|

*tapodhanānāṃ viprāṇāṃ vāgbalānāṃ yuge yuge \|*  
*śāntiḥ svastyayanaṃ karma vipradharmo na saṃgaraḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Therefore such of the ascetic Brahmanas who are well-versed in the speech and the Vedas, have always to remain peaceful in all the yugas. They are devoted to the performing of the dharma of the Brahmana and do not take to fighting.

## \|\| 35.87-96 \|\|

*kṣatriyāṇāṃ balaṃ yuddhaṃ vyāpāraśca balaṃ viśām \|*  
*bhikṣā balaṃ bhikṣukāṇāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ viprasevanam \|\| 87 \|\|*

*harau bhaktirharerdāsyaṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ balaṃ hariḥ \|*  
*hiṃsā balaṃ khalānāṃ ca tapasyā ca tapasvinām \|\| 88 \|\|*

*balaṃ veṣaśca veśyānāṃ yoṣitāṃ yauvanaṃ balam \|*  
*balaṃ pratāpo bhūpānāṃ bālānāṃ rodanaṃ balam \|\| 89 \|\|*

*satāṃ satyaṃ balaṃ mithyā balamevāsatāṃ sadā \|*  
*anugānāmanugamaḥ svalpasvānāṃ ca sañcayaḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

*vidyā balaṃ paṇḍitānāṃ dhairyyaṃ sāhasināṃ balam \|\| 91 \|\|*

*dhanaṃ balaṃ ca dhanināṃ śucīnāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*balaṃ vivekaḥ śāntānāṃ guṇināṃ balamekatā \|\| 92 \|\|*

*guṇo balaṃ ca guṇināṃ caurāṇāṃ cauryyameva ca \|*  
*priyavākyaṃ ca kāpaṭyamadharmmaḥ puṃścalībalam \|\| 93 \|\|*

*hiṃsā ca hiṃsrajantūnāṃ satīnāṃ patisevanam \|*  
*varaśāpau surāṇāṃ ca śiṣyāṇāṃ gurusevanam \|\| 94 \|\|*

*balaṃ dharmo gṛhasthānāṃ bhṛtyānāṃ rājasevanam \|*  
*balaṃ stavaḥ stāvakānāṃ brahma ca brahmacāriṇām \|\| 95 \|\|*

*yatīnāṃ ca sadācāro nyāsaḥ saṃnyāsināṃ balam \|*  
*pāpaṃ balaṃ pātakināmaśaktānāṃ harirbalam \|\| 96 \|\|*

87-96. The valour of the Kshatriyas is in fighting, business is the strength of the Vaisyas, the ascetic's strength is taking to the alms. The strength of the Shudras lies less in the serving of the Brahmanas and the devotees of the lord getting engaged in the adoration of Hari. The Vaishnavas remain devoted to lord Vishnu and that is their strength. The wicked people remain engaged in terrific deeds, which happen to be their strength, the performing of tapas happens to be the strength of the ascetics; the wicked women have the strength as their self decoration. The ladies have the strength of youthfulness; the strength of the kings is their glory, the strength of the children is their cries, the strength of the noble people is truthfulness; the strength of the wicked people is falsehood, following in others' footsteps is the strength of the follower; the collecting of money is the strength of the poor; the learned people have the strength of courageous people; the riches happen to be the strength of the rich people as well as those who lead a purified life. Intelligence happens to be the strength of peaceful persons. The virtuous people have the strength of unity. Virtue is considered to be the strength of virtuous people. Theft is the strength of the thieves, deceitfulness happens to be the strength of the wicked people; boons happen to be the strength of the gods, besides the pronouncing of curses. Pupils have the strength in serving the teacher; house-holders have the strength of performing dharma, a servant's strength lies in serving the king; prayer happens to be the strength of those offering prayers, Brahmana happens to be the strength of the Brahmacaris; the у at is have the strength of performing noble deeds, recluses have the strength of disowning everything, sinners have the strength of performing sins and people having no strength have the strength of belief in the lord.

## \|\| 35.97-98 \|\|

*puṇyaṃ balaṃ puṇyavatāṃ prajānāṃ nṛpatirbalam \|*  
*phalaṃ balaṃ ca vṛkṣāṇāṃ jalajānāṃ jalaṃ balam \|\| 97 \|\|*

*jalaṃ balaṃ ca sasyānāṃ matsyānāṃ ca jalaṃ balam \|*  
*śāntirbalaṃ ca bhūpānāṃ viprāṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

97-98. Noble people have the strength of merits; the strength of the people happens to be the king, the strength of the tree lies in its fruits, the aquatic creatures have their strength in water, the cereals have their strength in water, the fish have the strength in water. Peace happens to be the strength of the king as well as the Brahmana.

## \|\| 35.99 \|\|

*vipraḥ śānto raṇodyogī naiva dṛṣṭo na ca śrutaḥ \|*  
*sthite nārāyaṇe deve babhūvānyaviparyyayaḥ \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. We have never come across a Brahmana who is desirous of all. All this happened when lord Narayana was present.

## \|\| 35.100-101 \|\|

*ityevamuktvā rājendro virarāma raṇājire \|*  
*tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sadyastūṣṇīṃ babhūva ha \|\| 100 \|\|*

*rāmasya bhrātaraḥ sarve tīkṣṇaśastrāsipāṇayaḥ \|*  
*ārebhire raṇaṃ karttuṃ mahāvīrāstadājñayā \|\| 101 \|\|*

100-101. Thus speaking in the battle-field, the king kept quiet and all other kings also kept quiet on hearing his words. Thereafter the valorous brothers of Parashurama started fighting holding in their hands sharp-edged weapons.

## \|\| 35.102-103 \|\|

*raṇonmukhāṃśca tāndṛṣṭvā matsyarājo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*samārebhe raṇaṃ kartuṃ maṅgalo maṅgalālayaḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

*śarajālena rājendro vārayāmāsa tānapi \|*  
*cicchiduḥ śarajālaṃ ca jamadagnisutāstadā \|\| 103 \|\|*

102-103. The great and valorous king of the Matsya-country who happened to be the most prosperous of all the properties, got ready for a fight in the battle-field. The king stopped the flow of arrows from the opponents with the shooting of his own arrow. The sons of Jamadagni then cut-off the arrows of the king.

## \|\| 35.104 \|\|

*rājā cikṣepa divyāstraṃ śatasūryyaprabhaṃ mune \|*  
*māheśvareṇa munayaścicchiduścaiva līlayā \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. О sage, the king used a divine weapon which was shining like a hundred suns which was neutralised by the sages with the use of Maheshvara-weapon playfully.

## \|\| 35.105-106 \|\|

*divyāstreṇaiva munayaścicchiduḥ saśaraṃ dhanuḥ \|*  
*rathaṃ ca sārathiṃ caiva rājñaḥ sannāhameva ca \|\| 105 \|\|*

*nyastaśastraṃ nṛpaṃ dṛṣṭvā munayo harṣavihvalāḥ \|*  
*dadhāra śūlinaḥ śūlaṃ matsyarājajighāṃsayā \|\| 106 \|\|*

105-106. Thereafter the sages, with the use of the divine weapons, cut-off the bow, the chariot, the charioteer and kavacha into pieces. Finding the king with weapons, the ascetics were delighted and they tried to use the weapons of Shiva to kill the king of Matsya.

## \|\| 35.107-111 \|\|

*śūlanikṣepasamaye vāgbabhūvāśarīriṇī \|*  
*śūlaṃ tyajata viprendrāḥ śivasyāvyarthameva ca \|\| 107 \|\|*

*śivasya kavacaṃ divyaṃ dattaṃ durvāsasā purā \|*  
*matsyarājagale'styetatsarvāvayavarakṣakam \|\| 108 \|\|*

*prāṇānāṃ ca pradātāraṃ kavacaṃ yācataṃ nṛpam \|*  
*tadā nikṣiptaśūlaṃ ca jaghāna nṛpatiściram \|\| 109 \|\|*

*tacchūlaṃ taṃ nṛpaṃ prāpya śatakhaṇḍaṃ gataṃ mune \|*  
*śrutvaivākāśavāṇīṃ ca śṛṅgī saṃnyāsaveṣakṛt \|\| 110 \|\|*

*yayāce kavacaṃ bhūpaṃ jamadagnisuto mahān \|*  
*rājā dadau ca kavacaṃ brahmāṇḍavijayaṃ param \|\| 111 \|\|*

107-111. At the time when they were about to shoot the trident of Shiva there was a divine voice from the sky, "O best of the Brahmanas this trident of Shiva never goes waste; therefore it should not be used now. The king was bestowed with a divine kavacha by the sage Durvasa in earlier times which is worn by him round his neck and protects all his limbs. First of all you ask for the kavacha from the king, only then should the trident be used. О sage, those people had already used the trident. Therefore, reaching before the king the trident was reduced to a hundred of pieces. On hearing the words of the divine voice, Srngi an ascetic who happened to be the son of Jamadagni took to the form of an ascetic and went to the king begging for the kavacha. The king gladly gave away the kavacha to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 35.112 \|\|

*gṛhītvā kavacaṃ tacca śūlenaiva jaghāna ha \|*  
*papāta matsyarājaśca śatacandrasamānanaḥ \|*  
*mahābaliṣṭho guṇavāṃścandravaṃśasamudbhavaḥ \|\| 112 \|\|*

112\. Thereafter getting the kavacha he again shot the trident. The king was hurt with the same and he fell down to the ground. He possessed the glory of a hundred moons, besides great prowess. He was virtuous and was born in the solar race.

## \|\| 35.113 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*śivasya kavacaṃ brūhi matsyarājena yaddhṛtam \|*  
*nārāyaṇa mahābhāga śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama \|\| 113 \|\|*

113\. Narada said – О Narayana, you kindly tell me the details about the kavacha of Shiva which was worn by the. king of Matsya. I am quite anxious about it.

## \|\| 35.114-115 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kavacaṃ śṛṇu viprendra śaṅkarasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*brahmāṇḍavijayaṃ nāma sarvāvayavarakṣaṇam \|\| 114 \|\|*

*purā durvāsasā dattaṃ matsyarājāya dhīmate \|*  
*dattvā ṣaḍakṣaraṃ mantraṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam \|\| 115 \|\|*

114-115. Narayana said – О Brahmana, the kavacha of Shiva is known as Brahmandavijaya and always protects the limbs of the body. I am going to tell you about the same; you please listen to it. In the earlier times Durvasa had imparted the knowledge of the same to Matsyaraja with the mantra of six letters which removes all sins.

## \|\| 35.116 \|\|

*sthite ca kavace dehe nāsti mṛtyuśca jīvinām \|*  
*astre śastre jale vahnau siddhiścennāsti saṃśayaḥ \|\| 116 \|\|*

116\. When the kavacha is worn on the body, the death never approaches him, the weapons, water and fire cannot affect him adversely.

## \|\| 35.117-118 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādbāṇaḥ śivatvaṃ prāpa līlayā \|*  
*babhūva śivatulyaśca yaddhṛtvā nandikeśvaraḥ \|\| 117 \|\|*

*vīraśreṣṭho vīrabhadraḥ sāmbo'bhūddhāraṇādyataḥ \|*  
*trailokyavijayī rājā hiraṇyakaśipuḥ svayam \|\| 118 \|\|*

117-118. By wearing it and reciting it Banasura gained the knowledge of Sivatva and Nandikesvara became like Siva himself. Samba became the best of the warriors and by wearing which Hiranyakasipu became the lord of the three worlds.

## \|\| 35.119 \|\|

*hiraṇyākṣaśca vijayī cābhavaddhāraṇāddhi saḥ \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanātsiddho durvāsā viśvapūjitaḥ \|\| 119 \|\|*

119\. By holding which Hiranyaksa was victorious and by reciting which Durvasa and achieved success and was adored by the universe.

## \|\| 35.120-121 \|\|

*jaigīṣavyo mahāyogī paṭhanāddhāraṇādyataḥ \|*  
*yaddhṛtvā vāmadevaśca devalaḥ pavanaḥ svayam \|*  
*agastyaśca pulastyaścāpyabhavadviśvapūjitaḥ \|\| 120 \|\|*

*oṃ namaḥ śivāyeti ca mastakaṃ me sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ namaḥ śivāyeti ca svāhā bhālaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 121 \|\|*

120-121. While reciting and holding which Jaiglsavya became a great ascetic besides Vamadeva, Devala, the wind-god himself. Agastya and Pulastya became adorable through out the universe. ‘ art ЯЯ: Riaid’ should protect my head.

## \|\| 35.122 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ śivāyeti svāhā netre sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hūm̐ śivāyeti namo me pātu nāsikām \|\| 122 \|\|*

122\. should protect both my eyes, should protect my nose.

## \|\| 35.123 \|\|

*oṃ namaḥ śivāya śāntāya svāhā kaṇṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hūm̐ saṃhārakartre svāhā karṇau sadā'vatu \|\| 123 \|\|*

123\. should protect my throat, should protect both my ears.

## \|\| 35.124 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ pañcavaktrāya svāhā dantaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ maheśāya svāhā cādharaṃ pātu me sadā \|\| 124 \|\|*

124\. should protect my teeth, should protect my lips.

## \|\| 35.125 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ trinetrāya svāhā keśānsadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ aiṃ mahādevāya svāhā vakṣaḥ sadā'vatu \|\| 125 \|\|*

125\. should protect my hair, should protect my chest.

## \|\| 35.126 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ maiṃ rudrāya svāhā nābhiṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ maiṃ śrīmīśvarāya svāhā pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 126 \|\|*

126\. should protect my navel.

## \|\| 35.127 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ mṛtyuñjayāya svāhā bhruvau bāhū sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hrīmīśānāya svāhā pārśvaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 127 \|\|*

127\. should protect my eyebrows. should protect my back.

## \|\| 35.128 \|\|

*oṃ hrīmīśvarāya svāhā codaraṃ pātu me sadā \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ mṛtyuñjayāya svāhā bāhū sadā'vatu \|\| 128 \|\|*

128\. should protect my belly. should protect my arms.

## \|\| 35.129 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīmīśvarāya svāhā pātu karau mama \|*  
*oṃ maheśvarāya rudrāya nitambaṃ pātu me sadā \|\| 129 \|\|*

129\. should protect my hands, should protect my buttocks.

## \|\| 35.130 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ bhūtanāthāya svāhā pādau sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ sarveśvarāya sarvāya svāhā pādau sadā'vatu \|\| 130 \|\|*

130\. should protect both my feet, should protect my feet.

## \|\| 35.131 \|\|

*prācyāṃ māṃ pātu bhūteśa āgneyyāṃ pātu śaṅkaraḥ \|*  
*dakṣiṇe pātu māṃ rudro nairṛtyāṃ sthāṇureva ca \|\| 131 \|\|*

131\. Bhutesa should protect from the eastern side, Sankara should protect the south-east direction, Rudra should protect the southern direction, Sthanu should protect the south-west direction.

## \|\| 35.132 \|\|

*paścime khaṇḍaparaśurvāyavyāṃ candraśekharaḥ \|*  
*uttare giriśaḥ pātu caiśānyāmīśvaraḥ svayam \|\| 132 \|\|*

132\. The west end direction should be protected by Khandaparas'u, the south-west direction should be protected by Chandrasekhara, the northern direction should be protected by Girlsa, the north-east direction should protected by Isvara.

## \|\| 35.133-134 \|\|

*ūrdhve mṛḍaḥ sadā pātu cādho mṛtyuñjayaḥ svayam \|*  
*jale sthale cāntarikṣe svapne jāgaraṇe sadā \|\| 133 \|\|*

*pinākī pātu māṃ prītyā bhaktaṃ savai bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 134 \|\|*

133-134. Mrda should protect the upper regions and Mrtyunjaya should protect the lower regions besides the water, the land, space, while sleeping or awake. A devotee like me should be protected by the Pinaka-holder. О son, thus have I explained to you the astonishing type of kavacha.

## \|\| 35.135 \|\|

*daśalakṣajapenaiva siddhirbhavati niścitam \|*  
*yadi syātsiddhakavaco rudratulyo bhaveddhruvam \|\| 135 \|\|*

135\. The one who recites it ten lakhs of times, surely meets with its success. The one who meets with the success of this kavacha, becomes like Rudra himself.

## \|\| 35.136 \|\|

*tava snehānmayā''khyātaṃ pravaktavyaṃ na kasyacit \|*  
*kavacaṃ kaṇvaśākhoktamatigopyaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 136 \|\|*

136\. Because of my affection for you, I have explained this kavacha to you. It should not be revealed by you to anyone. This has been explained in the Kanvasakha and is quite secret and inaccessible.

## \|\| 35.137 \|\|

*aśvamedhasahasrāṇi rājasūyaśatāni ca \|*  
*sarvāṇi kavacasyāsya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 137 \|\|*

137\. The merit of performing a thousand Asvamedha sacrifices or a hundred Rajasuya sacrifices and all the yajnas cannot compare even to the one sixteenth of the ray of the merit of this kavacha.

## \|\| 35.138 \|\|

*kavacasya prasādena jīvanmukto bhavennaraḥ \|*  
*sarvajñaḥ sarvasiddhīśo manoyāyī bhaveddhruvam \|\| 138 \|\|*

138\. By the grace of this kavacha, the humans are freed from the circle of birth and death, become all knowledgeable and lords of all the success and fortune and attaining the spread of the mind.

## \|\| 35.139 \|\|

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajedyaḥ śaṅkaraṃ prabhum \|*  
*śatalakṣaṃ prajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 139 \|\|*

139\. The one who practises this kavacha without proper knowledge cannot meet with success even after reciting it ten lakhs of times.

## Chapter 36

## \|\| 36.1-3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*matsyarāje nipatite rājā yuddhaviśāradaḥ \|*  
*rājendrānpreṣayāmāsa yuddhe śastraviśāradān \|\| 1 \|\|*

*bṛhadbalaṃ somadattaṃ vidarbhaṃ mithileśvaram \|*  
*niṣadhādhipatiṃ caiva magadhādhipatiṃ tathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*āyayuḥ samare yoddhuṃ jāmadagnyaṃ mahārathāḥ \|*  
*tritayākṣauhiṇībhiśca senābhiḥ saha nārada \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Narayana said – О Narada, after the killing of Matsyaraja, the king who was well-versed in the art of welfare and also the scripture on the subject deputed Brihadvala, Somadatta, Vidharbha, Mithileshvara, Nisidheshvara and Magadheshvara, who were the great warriors. All these kings came forward to fight against Parashurama the son of Jamadagni.

## \|\| 36.4-5 \|\|

*rāmasya bhrātaraḥ sarve vīrāstīkṣṇāstrapāṇayaḥ \|*  
*vārayāmāsurastraiśca tāneva raṇamūrddhani \|\| 4 \|\|*

*te vīrāḥ śarajālena divyāstreṇa prayatnataḥ \|*  
*vārayāmāsurekaikaṃ bhrātṛvargānbhṛgostathā \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Parashurama and all his brothers blocked their forward movement with the shooting of several arrows and weapons. The warriors too shot numerous arrows besides divine weapons. They also stopped the forward movement of the brother of Parashurama.

## \|\| 36.6 \|\|

*āyayau samare śīghraṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃśca parājitān \|*  
*pinākahastaḥ sa bhṛgurjvaladagniśikhopamaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Finding his brothers defeated Bhrigu held the Pinaka-bow which was emitting burning flames like fire and came forward in the battle-field.

## \|\| 36.7-9 \|\|

*cikṣepa nāgapāśaṃ ca jāmadagnyo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*ciccheda taṃ gāruḍena somadatto mahābalaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*bhṛguḥ śaṅkaraśūlena somadattaṃ jaghāna ha \|*  
*bṛhadbalaṃ ca gadayā vidarbhaṃ muṣṭibhistathā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*maithilaṃ mudgareṇaiva śaktyā vai naiṣadhaṃ tathā \|*  
*māgadhaṃ caraṇodghātairastrajālena sainikān \|\| 9 \|\|*

7-9. Thereafter the valorous Parashurama used Nagapasa and the immensely valorous Somadatta neutralised it with the use of Garudastra. Thereafter Bhrigu using the trident of Shiva killed Brihadvala with a blow of the club, Vidharbha with fists, Mithila, with Mudgara (huge club), Naisadha, with the use of shakti, Magadheshvara with kicks and all the army was destroyed with the use of weapons.

## \|\| 36.10 \|\|

*nihatya nikhilānbhūpānsaṃhārāgnisamo raṇe \|*  
*dudrāva kārttavīryaṃ ca jāmadagnyo mahābalaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Parashurama the great warrior like the fire of dissolution moved forward and killing all the kings rushed towards Kartavirya.

## \|\| 36.11 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā taṃ yoddhumāyāntaṃ rājānaśca mahārathāḥ \|*  
*āyayuḥ samaraṃ karttuṃ kārttavīryyaṃ nivāryya ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The great kings finding Bhrigu approaching them for a fight removing Kartavirya aside started this battle themselves.

## \|\| 36.12-13 \|\|

*kānyakubjāśca śataśaḥ saurāṣṭrāḥ śataśastathā \|*  
*rāṣṭrīyāḥ śataśaścaiva vīrendrāḥ śataśastathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

*saumyā vāṅgāśca śataśo mahārāṣṭrāstathā daśa \|*  
*tathā gurjarajātīyāḥ kaliṅgāḥ śataśastathā \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Out of them, a hundred belong to Kanyakubja, a hundred to Saurastra, a hundred to Rastrlya, a hundred valorous kings, a hundred noble kings, a hundred from king of Bengal, a thousand from Maharastra, a hundred from Gujarata and others happened to be the kings of Kalinga.

## \|\| 36.14 \|\|

*kṛtvā tu śarajālaṃ ca bhṛguściccheda tatkṣaṇam \|*  
*taṃ chittvā'bhyutthito rāmo nīhāramiva bhāskaraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. All of them created a net with shooting the arrow and covered Parashurama with it. Parashurama on his part cut-off all the arrows and emerged as if the sun emerges from a fog.

## \|\| 36.15-16 \|\|

*trirātraṃ yuyudhe rāmastaiḥ sārddhaṃ samarājire \|*  
*dvādaśākṣauhiṇīṃ senāṃ tathā ciccheda parśunā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*rambhāstambhasamūha ca yathā khaḍgena līlayā \|*  
*chittvā senāṃ bhūpavargaṃ jaghāna śivaśūlataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. For three nights Parashurama continued the battle with those kings and he emerged victorious like the sun who emerges out of the sky. He cut off all the soldiers like the cutting of the trunk of a banana tree and after doing so, he killed all the kings with the use of the trident of Shiva.

## \|\| 36.17 \|\|

*sarvāṃstānnihatāndṛṣṭvā sūryyavaṃśasamudbhavaḥ \|*  
*ājagāma sucandraśca lakṣa rājendrasaṃyutaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Finding them killed Suchandra who was born in the solar race came forward with a lakh of kings for a fight.

## \|\| 36.18 \|\|

*dvādaśākṣauhiṇībhiśca senābhiḥ saha saṃyuge \|*  
*kopena yuyudhe rāmaṃ siṃhaṃ siṃho yathā raṇe \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The king was accompanied by twelve aks auhinls of army. Parashurama fought there with great anger like a lion attacking other lions.

## \|\| 36.19 \|\|

*bhṛguḥ śaṅkaraśūlena nṛpalakṣaṃ nihatya ca \|*  
*dvādaśākṣauhiṇīṃ senāmahanvai parśunā balī \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The valorous Bhrigu then using the trident of Shiva killed the lakh of kings along with the twelve aksauhinis of army.

## \|\| 36.20-21 \|\|

*nihatya sarvāḥ senāśca sucandraṃ yuyudhe balī \|*  
*nāgāstraṃ prerayāmāsa nihṛtaṃ taṃ bhṛguḥ svayam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*nāgapāśaṃ ca ciccheda gāruḍena nṛpeśvaraḥ \|*  
*jahāsa ca bhṛguṃ rājā samare ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. After killing the army Parashurama started fighting with Suchandra. The valorous Bhrigu shot the Nagastra at king Suchandra which was neutralised by the king with the use of Garudastra. He then started attacking the son of Bhrigu again and again.

## \|\| 36.22 \|\|

*bhṛgurnārāyaṇāstraṃ ca cikṣepa raṇamūrdhani \|*  
*astaṃ yayau taṃ nihantuṃ śatasūryasamaprabham \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Finding Suchandra behaving thus, Bhrigu shot Narayanastra having the lustre of hundreds of suns.

## \|\| 36.23 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā'straṃ nṛpaśārdūlaścāvaruhya rathātkṣaṇāt \|*  
*nyastaśastraḥ prāṇamacca stutvā nārāyaṇaṃ śivam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The lion among the kings, finding Narayanastra approaching him, got down from his chariot and stood on the crown. He threw away all his weapons and bowed before it reciting the mantras of Shiva and Vishnu.

## \|\| 36.24 \|\|

*tameva praṇataṃ tyaktvā yayau nārāyaṇāntikam \|*  
*astrarājo bhagavato rāmaḥ saṃprāpa vismayam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Finding him going down, the Narayanastra left the king and went back to Narayana. This completely surprised Parashurama.

## \|\| 36.25-26 \|\|

*bhṛguḥ śaktiṃ ca musalaṃ tomaraṃ paṭṭiśaṃ tathā \|*  
*gadāṃ parśuṃ ca kopena cikṣipe tajjighāṃsayā \|\| 25 \|\|*

*jagrāha kālī tānsarvānsucandrasyandanasthitā \|*  
*cikṣepa śivaśūlaṃ sa nṛpamālyaṃ babhūva tat \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. Thereafter Bhrigu got enraged and shot for the killing of the king, sakti, mUsala, tomara, pattisa, club and battle-axe but Kali who was lodged on the chariot of Suchandra, caught hold of all the weapons in her hand. Thereafter, Parashurama made use of the trident of Shiva, which reached the king and adorned his neck like a garland.

## \|\| 36.27 \|\|

*dadarśa purato rāmo bhadrakālīṃ jagatprasūm \|*  
*vahantīṃ muṇḍamālāṃ ca vikaṭāsyāṃ bhayaṃkarīm \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Thereafter, Parashurama spotted Kali there, who was wearing the garland of skulls, having a terrific face and horrible appearance.

## \|\| 36.28 \|\|

*vihāya śastramastraṃ ca pinākaṃ ca bhṛgustadā \|*  
*tuṣṭāva tāṃ mahāmāyāṃ bhaktinamrātmakandharaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Bhrigu on his part throwing away all the weapons including Pinaka-bow stood in reverence to the goddess and started eulogising the goddess Mahamaya.

## \|\| 36.29 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*namaḥ śaṅkarakāntāyai sārāyai te namo namaḥ \|*  
*namo durgatināśinyai māyāyai te namo namaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Parashurama said – I bow in reverence to the wife of Shiva who is the essence of all; I bow in reverence to her again and again. She is the one who destroys misfortune and is known as Mahamaya, I bow in reverence to her.

## \|\| 36.30 \|\|

*namo namo jagaddhātryai jagatkartryai namonamaḥ \|*  
*namo'stu te jaganmātre kāraṇāyai namo namaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. She is the one who sustains the universe; I offer my salutation to her again and again. She is the creator of the universe. I bow in reverence to the mother of the universe and also the one who is the cause of the same, I bow in reverence to her.

## \|\| 36.31 \|\|

*prasīda jagatāṃ mātaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāriṇi \|*  
*tvatpādau śaraṇaṃ yāmi pratijñāṃ sārthikāṃ kuru \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O goddess, you are the destroyer of the universe and also its mother. Be pleased with me as I have come to take refuge under your feet; let me fulfil my vow.

## \|\| 36.32 \|\|

*tvayi me vimukhāyāṃ ca ko māṃ rakṣitumīśvaraḥ \|*  
*tvaṃ prasannā bhava śubhe māṃ bhaktaṃ bhaktavatsale \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. In case you turn your face against me, no one on earth can save me. Therefore, О pleasant one, the bestower of grace on devotees, you be pleased with me.

## \|\| 36.33 \|\|

*yuṣmābhiḥ śivaloke ca mahyaṃ datto varaḥ purā \|*  
*taṃ varaṃ saphalaṃ kartuṃ tvamarhasi varānane \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О beautiful faced goddess, in earlier times, you had bestowed me with a boon in the presence of Shiva; you kindly make it a success.

## \|\| 36.34-35 \|\|

*reṇukeyastavaṃ śrutvā prasannā'bhavadambikā \|*  
*mā bhairityevamuktvā tu tatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 34 \|\|*

*etadbhṛgukṛtaṃ stotraṃ bhaktiyuktaśca yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*mahābhayātsamuttīrṇaḥ sa bhavedeva līlayā \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Thereafter hearing the prayer of Parashurama, the son of Renuka, the goddess Ambika was pleased. There was a divine voice from the sky which declared that the king should not be afraid of anyone. The goddess at the same time disappeared from the scene. Whosoever recites this stotra composed by Bhrigu, will cross over all the dangers playfully.

## \|\| 36.36 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde bhṛgukārtavīryayuddhavarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaṭtriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He will be adored in all the three lokas and shall become the best. He will be adored in all the three lokas and will be victorious. He would become the best of the intellectuals and shall destroy all his enemies.

## \|\| 36.37 \|\|

*etasminnantare brahmā bhṛguṃ dharmabhṛtāṃ varam \|*  
*āgatya kathayāmāsa rahasyaṃ rāmameva ca \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. In the meantime Brahma arrived in front of the religious-minded Bhrigu and revealed the secret to him.

## \|\| 36.38-39 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*śṛṇu rāma mahābhāga rahasyaṃ pūrvameva ca \|*  
*sucandrajayahetuṃ ca pratijñāphalameva ca \|\| 38 \|\|*

*daśākṣarī mahāvidyā dattā durvāsasā purā \|*  
*sucandrāyaiva kavacaṃ bhadrakālyāḥ sudurlabham \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. Brahma said – "O virtuous one, О Parashurama, I am going to reveal a secret of the earlier times which can be helpful to you in becoming victorious over Suchandra. You please listen to me. In the earlier times, Suchandra was provided with a kavacha of ten letters of Durvasa for the adoration of Mahavidya and Bhadrakali.

## \|\| 36.40-41 \|\|

*kavacaṃ bhadrakālyāśca devānāṃ ca sudurlabham \|*  
*kavacaṃ tadgale yasya sarvaśatruvimardakam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*atīva pūjyaṃ śastaṃ ca trailokyajayakāraṇam \|*  
*tasminsthite ca kavace kastvaṃ jetumalaṃ bhuvi \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. The kavacha of Bhadrakali is beyond the reach of gods. It destroys all the enemies and is adorable in the three world. It could become the cause of conquering the three worlds. That kavacha has been tied around the neck of the king and till such time as the kavacha remains there, no one on earth can defeat the king.

## \|\| 36.42 \|\|

*bhṛgo gacchatu bhikṣārthaṃ karotu prārthanāṃ nṛpam \|*  
*sūryyavaṃśodbhavo rājā dātā paramadhārmikaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Therefore, О Bhrigu, you go and approach the king demanding for the kavacha. The king is born in the solar race and is quite religious minded and a giver of charities. He will give away the kavacha and the mantra besides other details on your demand.

## \|\| 36.43-44 \|\|

*prāṇāṃśca kavacaṃ mantraṃ sarvaṃ dāsyati niścitam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*bhṛguḥ saṃnyāsiveṣeṇa gatvā rājāntikaṃ mune \|*  
*bhikṣāṃ cakāra mantraṃ ca kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. О sage, Bhrigu then went to the king in the form of an ascetic and begged for the astonishing type of kavacha from the king.

## \|\| 36.45 \|\|

*rājā dadau ca tanmantraṃ kavacaṃ paramādarāt \|*  
*tataḥ śaṅkaraśūlena taṃ jaghāna nṛpaṃ bhṛguḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The king handed over gracefully the mantra and the kavacha to Bhrigu who ultimately killed the king with the use of the trident of Shiva.

## Chapter 37

## \|\| 37.1 \|\|

*. nārada uvāca \|*  
*kavacaṃ śrotumicchāmi tāṃ ca vidyāṃ daśākṣarīm \|*  
*nātha tvatto hi sarvajña bhadrakālyāśca sāmpratam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narada said – О lord and all knowledgeable one, I intend to learn about he Bhadrakali-kavacha and the knowledge of ten letters known as Dasaksari-vidya, you kindly tell me.

## \|\| 37.2 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu nārada vakṣyāmi mahāvidyāṃ daśākṣarīm \|*  
*gopanīyaṃ ca kavacaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Narayana said – О Narada, I am going to reveal the truth about the Dasaksari-vidya and the secret kavacha which are inaccessible in the world. You please listen to me.

## \|\| 37.3 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ kālikāyai svāheti ca daśākṣarīm \|*  
*durvāsā hi dadau rājñe puṣkare sūryyaparvaṇi \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. … this is called Dashaksari-vidya, the knowledge of which was imparted by Durvasa to the king at the time of a solar eclipse.

## \|\| 37.4 \|\|

*daśalakṣajapenaiva mantrasiddhiḥ kṛtā purā \|*  
*pañcalakṣajapenaiva paṭhankavacamuttamam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. By reciting it ten lakhs of times, he achieved success of this mantra in the earlier times. Thereafter reciting the same five lakhs of times, he met with success on the best of the kavacha.

## \|\| 37.5 \|\|

*babhūva siddhakavaco'pyayodhyāmājagāma saḥ \|*  
*kṛtsnāṃ hi pṛthivīṃ jigye kavacasya prabhāvataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Achieving success at the kavacha, he came to Ayodhya and with the influence of this kavacha, he became victorious over the entire universe.

## \|\| 37.6 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*śrutā daśākṣarī vidyā triṣu lokeṣu durlabhā \|*  
*adhunā śrotumicchāmi kavacaṃ brūhi me prabho \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Narada said – О lord, I have learnt about the inaccessible Dasaksari-vidya but I would like to know about the kavacha; you kindly impart the knowledge of the same to me.

## \|\| 37.7 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vakṣyāmi viprendra kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*nārāyaṇena yaddattaṃ kṛpayā śūline purā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Narayana said – О best of the Brahmanas, I am going to reveal to you the secret about the astonishing kavacha, the knowledge of which was bestowed gracefully by Narayana to Shiva.

## \|\| 37.8 \|\|

*tripurasya vadhe ghore śivasya vijayāya ca \|*  
*tadeva śūlinā dattaṃ purā durvāsase mune \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He overpowered Tripurasura with the application of the same. The knowledge of the same was imparted by Shiva to Durvasa in earlier times.

## \|\| 37.9 \|\|

*durvāsasā ca yaddattaṃ sucandrāya mahātmane \|*  
*atiguhyataraṃ tattvaṃ sarvamantraughavigraham \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Durvasa on his part imparted the knowledge of the same to Suchandra, which is extremely secret and is the form of all the mantras including the tattvas.

## \|\| 37.10 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ kālikāyai svāhā me pātu mastakam \|*  
*klīṃ kapālaṃ sadā pātu hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīmiti locane \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. should protect my head,should protect my forehead. should protect my eyes.

## \|\| 37.11 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ trilocane svāhā nāsikāṃ me sadā'vatu \|*  
*klīṃ kālike rakṣa rakṣa svāhā dantānsadā'vatu \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. should protect my nose,should protect my teeth.

## \|\| 37.12 \|\|

*klīṃ bhadrakālike svāhā pātu me'dharayugmakam \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ kālikāyai svāhā kaṇṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. should protect my lips, should protect my neck.

## \|\| 37.13 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ kālikāyai svāhā karṇayugmaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ krīṃ krīṃ klīṃ kālyai svāhā skandhaṃ pātu sadā mama \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. should always protect my shoulders.

## \|\| 37.14 \|\|

*oṃ krīṃ bhadrakālyai svāhā mama vakṣaḥ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ klīṃ kālikāyai svāhā mama nābhiṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. should protect my chest. should protect my navel.

## \|\| 37.15 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ kālikāyai svāhā mama pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*raktabījavināśinyai svāhā hasto sadā'vatu \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. should protect my back. should protect my hands.

## \|\| 37.16 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ muṇḍamālinyai svāhā pādau sadāvatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ cāmuṇḍāyai svāhā sarvāṅgaṃ me sadā'vatu \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. should protect my feet, should protect all my limbs.

## \|\| 37.17-18 \|\|

*prācyāṃ pātu mahākālī cāgneyyāṃ raktadantikā \|*  
*dakṣiṇe pātu cāmuṇḍā nairṛtyāṃ pātu kālikā \|\| 17 \|\|*

*śyāmā ca vāruṇe pātu vāyavyāṃ pātu caṇḍikā \|*  
*uttare vikaṭāsyā cāpyaiśānyāṃ sāṭṭahāsinī \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Mahakali should protect me from the northern side, the north-east side should be protected by Raktadantika, the western side should be protected by Camunda, the south-east should be protected by Kalika, the western side should be protected by Syama, the southern-western side should be protected by Chandika, the northern side should be protected by the goddess with deformed face and north-east should be protected by the roaring goddess.

## \|\| 37.19 \|\|

*pātūrdhvaṃ lolajihvā sā māyā''dyā pātvadhaḥ sadā \|*  
*jale sthale cāntarikṣe pātu viśvaprasūḥ sadā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The upper region should be protected by Lolajihva, the lower region should be protected by Maya. The region of water, earth and the sky should be protected by the mother of the universe.

## \|\| 37.20 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvamantraughavigraham \|*  
*sarveṣāṃ kavacānāṃ ca sārabhūtaṃ parātparam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. О son, I have therefore, revealed to you the truth about this kavacha which is the form of all the mantras and happens to be the essence of all the kavachas.

## \|\| 37.21-22 \|\|

*saptadvīpeśvaro rājā sucandro'sya prabhāvataḥ \|*  
*kavacasya prabhāvena māndhātā pṛthivīpatiḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*pracetā lomaśaścaiva yataḥ siddho babhūva ha \|*  
*yato hi yogināṃ śreṣṭhaḥ saubhariḥ pippalāyanaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. With the influence of the same kavacha, the king Suchandra has been ruling the seven continents. The sages like Praceta and Lomasa, met with success because of the same, besides Saubhari and Pippalayana became the great yogis.

## \|\| 37.23-24 \|\|

*yadi syātsiddhakavacaḥ sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet \|*  
*mahādānāni sarvāṇi tapāṃsyevaṃ vratāni va \|*  
*niścitaṃ kavacasyāsya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 23 \|\|*

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajetkālīṃ jagatprasūm \|*  
*śatalakṣaṃ prajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. The one who meets with success at this kavacha becomes the lord of the siddhas. All the great charities, tapas and vratas do not compare even with the one sixteenth part of this kavacha. The one who recites this kavacha without having a detailed knowledge about the same, does not meet with success in the world.

## Chapter 38

## \|\| 38.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*sucandre patite brahmanrājendrāṇāṃ śiromaṇau \|*  
*agamatpuṣkarākṣastu senātryakṣauhiṇīyutaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – О Brahmana, after the king, Suchandra met with his end in the battle-field, Puskaraksa came forward in the battle-field for waging a war. He happened to be the best of the kings and was accompanied by three aksauhini armies.

## \|\| 38.2 \|\|

*sūryyavaṃśodbhavo rājā sucandratanayo mahān \|*  
*mahālakṣmīsevakaśca lakṣmīvānsūryyasannibhaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The king who belonged to the solar dynasty, happened to be the eldest son of Suchandra, adored Maha-Lakshmi and had immense fortune and glory resembling the sun-rays.

## \|\| 38.3 \|\|

*mahālakṣmyāśca kavacaṃ gale yasya manoharam \|*  
*paramaiśvaryyasaṃyuktastrailokyavijayī tataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The pleasant kavacha of Maha-Lakshmi always adorned around his neck. He was lord of immense fortune and had conquered all the three worlds.

## \|\| 38.4-5 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhrātaraḥ sarve raiṇukeyasya dhīmataḥ \|*  
*āyayuḥ samaraṃ karttuṃ nānāśastrāstrapāṇayaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*rājendraḥ śarajālena cchedayāmāsa tāṃstathā \|*  
*cicchiduḥ śarajālaṃ ca te vīrāścaiva līlayā \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Finding him there, Parashurama's brothers came forward to fight with him holding several weapons in their hands and the prince covered all of them with his arrows and the great warrior also did not lag behind in destroying the net of the arrows.

## \|\| 38.6-7 \|\|

*cicchiduḥ syandanaṃ rājñaste vīrāḥ pañcabāṇataḥ \|*  
*sārathiṃ pañcabāṇena rathāśvaṃ daśabāṇataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*taddhanuḥ saptabāṇena tūrṇaṃ vai pañcabāṇataḥ \|*  
*cicchidustadbhrātṛvargānviprāḥ śaṅkaraśūlataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Thereafter, those warriors shot five arrows at the king, killing his charioteer with five arrows, the charioteer and horses with ten arrows, the bow with seven arrows and the quiver with five arrows. With the use of the trident of Shiva, they destroyed all the brothers.

## \|\| 38.8 \|\|

*te ca tryakṣauhiṇīṃ senānnijaghnuścāpi līlayā \|*  
*hantuṃ nṛpendraṃ te vīrāḥ śivaśūlaṃ nicikṣipuḥ \|*  
*gale babhūva tacchūlaṃ rājñaḥ puṣkaramālikā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The three aksuhini armies of the king were also playfully destroyed with the shooting of the arrow and the trident of Shiva, but the trident of Shiva touching the neck of the king became like a garland of flowers.

## \|\| 38.9 \|\|

*śaktiṃ ca parighaṃ caiva bhuśuṇḍīṃ mudgaraṃ tathā \|*  
*gadāṃ ca cikṣipurviprāḥ kopena jvaladagnayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Thereafter, getting enraged like the burning fire-flame, the Brahmanas shot shaktis, parighas, bhusundis, mudgaras and gadas.

## \|\| 38.10 \|\|

*tāni śastrāṇi cūrṇāni kṣmābhṛto dehayogataḥ \|*  
*vismitā bhrātaraḥ sarve bhṛgoreva mahāmune \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. О great sage, with the very touch of the body of the king, all the weapons were reduced to pieces. This surprised immensely the brothers of Bhrigu.

## \|\| 38.11 \|\|

*rathaṃ dhanuśca śastrāṇi cāstrāṇi vividhāni ca \|*  
*senāṃ prasthāpayāmāsa kārttavīryyārjunaḥ svayam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Thereafter Kartavliya himself deployed the army soldiers with chariots, bows and various types of other weapons.

## \|\| 38.12 \|\|

*rājā syandanamāruhya puṣkarākṣo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*cakāra śarajālaṃ ca mahāghorataraṃ mune \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О sage, the great warrior king Puskaraksa mounted on the chariot, immensely started showering arrows.

## \|\| 38.13 \|\|

*cicchiduḥ śarajālaṃ te vīrāḥ śastrāstrapāṇayaḥ \|*  
*rājā prasvāpanenaiva nidritāṃstāṃścakāra ha \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The warriors also faced them holding weapons in their hands, shooting arrows and destroyed the net of arrows. The king then cast a swoon on all of them and overpowered them with sleep.

## \|\| 38.14-16 \|\|

*bhrātṝṃśca nidritāndṛṣṭvā jāmadagnyo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*kṣatavikṣatasarvāṃgānbodhayāmāsa tattvataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*bodhayitvā tānnivāryya jagāma raṇamūrddhani \|*  
*cikṣepa parśuṃ kopena śīghraṃ rājajighāṃsayā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*chittvā rājñaḥ kirīṭaṃ ca parśurbhūmau papāta ha \|*  
*jagrāha paraśuṃ śīghraṃ jāmadagnyo mahābalaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

14-16. Thereafter Parashurama's brothers, who were the great warriors got wounded in the battle-field who were removed by him carefully from the battle-field. He therefore himself came forward holding a battle-axe in the hand. The hand of the king was cut-off who fell on the ground. Paras'urama caught hold of it.

## \|\| 38.17 \|\|

*tadā śaṅkaraśūlaṃ ca cikṣipe mantrapūrvakam \|*  
*nṛpasya kuṇḍalaṃ chittvā jagāma śivasannidhim \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Thereafter he used the trident of Shiva reciting the mantra, which cut off the kundalas of the king and went back to Shiva.

## \|\| 38.18 \|\|

*rājā nihantuṃ taṃ rāmaṃ śarajālaṃ cakāra ha \|*  
*ciccheda śarajālaṃ ca reṇukeyaśca līlayā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The king on the other hand shot arrows creating a net around Paras'urama but Bhrigu on his part shattered the same.

## \|\| 38.19 \|\|

*krameṇa rājā nānāstraṃ cikṣipe mantrapūrvakam \|*  
*tacciccheda krameṇaiva bhṛguḥ śastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The king however used many weapons which were destroyed by Parashurama the great archer.

## \|\| 38.20-21 \|\|

*bhṛguścikṣepa nānāstraṃ mahāsandhānapūrvakam \|*  
*tacciccheda mahārājaḥ sandhānenaiva līlayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

*rāmaścikṣepa sandhāya brahmāstraṃ mantrapūrvakam \|*  
*rājā nirvāpaṇaṃ cakre sandhānenaiva līlayā \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Bhrigu also used many types of weapons available with him, which were easily destroyed by the king, Paras'urama then thought of attacking the king with Brahmastra but the king on coming to know about the intention of Parashurama playfully destroyed the same.

## \|\| 38.22 \|\|

*sarvāṇyastrāṇi śastrāṇi rāmaḥ pāśupataṃ vinā \|*  
*cikṣepa kopavibhrāṃto bhūpaściccheda tāni ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Thereafter Parashurama was enraged and he shot the Pasupata arrow, besides several other weapons which were in turn neutralised by the king.

## \|\| 38.23 \|\|

*rāmaḥ stutvā śivaṃ natvā''dade pāśupataṃ mune \|*  
*nārāyaṇaśca bhagavānavocad viprarūpadhṛk \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. О sage, then Parasurama bowed in reverence to Shiva and held the Pasupata weapons again. At that very moment Narayana, taking to the form of a Brahmana came to him with a request.

## \|\| 38.24-25 \|\|

*brāhmaṇa uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ karoṣi bhṛgo vatsa tvamevaṃ jñānināṃ varaḥ \|*  
*naraṃ hantuṃ pāśupataṃ kopātkiṃ kṣipasi bhramāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

*viśvaṃ pāśupatenaiva bhavedbhasma ca satvaram \|*  
*sarvaghnaṃ syācchastramidaṃ vinā śrīkṛṣṇamīśvaram \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. The Brahmana said – О son, О Paras'urama, you are best among the intellectuals. What is all this being done by you. You are using the great Pasupata weapon just for the killing of the ordinary human. By doing so the entire universe would be reduced to ashes together with Shiva because everything else gets destroyed by its use except lord Krishna.

## \|\| 38.26 \|\|

*aho pāśupataṃ jetuṃ nālameva sudarśanam \|*  
*hareḥ sudarśanaṃ caiva sarvāstraparimardakam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Not only this, in order to over-power Pasupata, the Sudarsana-cakra of lord Krishna will be of no consequence. It destroy all the weapons and the enemies.
