## \|\| 38.27 \|\|

*khaṭvāṃginaḥ pāśupātaṃ harereva sudarśanam \|*  
*ete pradhāne sarveṣāmastrāṇāṃ ca jagattraye \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Therefore, the Pasupata of Shiva and Sudarsana-cakra of lord Krishna are believed to be the best of all the weapons.

## \|\| 38.28-29 \|\|

*tyaja pāśupataṃ brahmanmadīyaṃ vacanaṃ śṛṇu \|*  
*yathā jeṣyasi rājānaṃ puṣkarākṣaṃ mahābalam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*kārttavīryyamajetāraṃ yathā jeṣyasi sāmpratam \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ sāvadhānena tatsarvaṃ kathayāmi te \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Therefore, О Brahmana you place the Pas'upata weapon aside and listen to my words. I shall let you know the way to be victorious on Pusakaraksa. I am also going to tell you the way to become victorious over Kartavirya. You listen to me attentively.

## \|\| 38.30 \|\|

*mahālakṣmyāśca kavacaṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham \|*  
*bhaktyā ca puṣkarākṣeṇa dhṛtaṃ kaṇṭhe vidhānataḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The king Pusakaraksa is wearing around his right arm the inaccessible kavacha of Maha-Lakshmi.

## \|\| 38.31-32 \|\|

*paraṃ durgatināśinyāḥ kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*dhṛtaṃ ca dakṣiṇe bāhau puṣkarākṣasutena ca \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kavacasya prabhāveṇa viśvaṃ jetuṃ kṣamau ca tau \|*  
*ko jetā ca tribhuvane dehe ca kavace sthite \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The son of Puskaraksa has also tied the kavacha of goddess Durga around his right arm. With the influence of the kavachas both of them are competent enough to over-power the entire universe. Therefore till such time as the kavachas are worn by them, no one can defeat them.

## \|\| 38.33 \|\|

*ahaṃ yāsyāmi bhikṣārthaṃ sannidhāne tayormune \|*  
*kariṣyāmi ca tadbhikṣāṃ pratijñā saphalā'stu te \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. О sage, I am therefore proceeding to both of them begging for the kavachas after which you will meet with success."

## \|\| 38.34 \|\|

*brāhmaṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā rāmaḥ saṃtrastamānasaḥ \|*  
*uvāca brāhmaṇaṃ vṛddhaṃ hṛdayena vidūyatā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. On hearing the words of the Brahmana the mind of Parashurama feit panicky and with a painful heart, he spoke to the Brahmana.

## \|\| 38.35 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*na jānāmi mahāprājña kastvaṃ brāhmaṇarūpadhṛk \|*  
*śīghraṃ ca brūhi māṃ mūḍhaṃ tadā gaccha nṛpāntikam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Parasurama said – О great intellectual, I am not aware, as to who you are and why you have appeared in the form of an old Brahmana. First of all you enlighten a foolish person like me on this and then proceed to the king."

## \|\| 38.36 \|\|

*jāmadagnyavacaḥ śrutvā prahasya brāhmaṇaḥ svayam \|*  
*uktvā cāhaṃ viṣṇuriti yayau bhikṣitumīśvaraḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. On hearing the words of Jamadagni, Brahmana smiled and said – "I am Vishnu." Thereafter the lord Vishnu went for begging alms.

## \|\| 38.37 \|\|

*gatvā tayoḥ sannidhānaṃ yayāce kavace ca tau \|*  
*dadatustau ca kavace viṣṇave viṣṇumāyayā \|*  
*gṛhītvā kavace viṣṇurvaikuṇṭhaṃ nirjagāma saḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Approaching both of them he begged for the kavachas. Both of them influenced by the illusion of Vishnu handed over their respective kavachas to him and lord Vishnu went back to Vishnuloka carrying both of them with him.

## \|\| 38.38-39 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*mahālakṣmyāśca kavacaṃ kena dattaṃ mahāmune \|*  
*puṣkarākṣāya bhūpāya śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ mama \|\| 38 \|\|*

*kavacaṃ cāpi durgāyāḥ puṣkarākṣasutāya ca \|*  
*durllabhaṃ kena vā dattaṃ tadbhavānvaktumarhasi \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. Narada said – О great sage, who had bestowed the kavacha of Maha-Lakshmi? I am anxious to know about it. Who gave the invaluable kavacha of Durga to Puskaraksa? You tell me all this.

## \|\| 38.40 \|\|

*kavacaṃ cāpi kimbhūtaṃ tayorvā tasya kiṃ phalam \|*  
*mantrau tu kiṃprakārau ca tanme brūhi jagadguro \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. You kindly tell me, О teacher of the universe, all about both the kavachas and the relevant mantras and the result thereof.

## \|\| 38.41-42 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*dattaṃ sanatkumāreṇa puṣkarākṣāya dhīmate \|*  
*mahālakṣmyāśca kavacaṃ mantraścāpi daśākṣaraḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*stavanaṃ cāpi gopyaṃ vai proktaṃ taccaritaṃ ca yat \|*  
*dhyānaṃ ca sāmavedoktaṃ pūjāṃ caiva manoharām \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. Narayana said – The kavacha of Maha-Lakshmi has the ten letter mantra, secret stotra, her life story as brought out in the Samaveda, the dhyanam and the method of adoration which was revealed to Puskaraksa by Sanatkumara.

## \|\| 38.43 \|\|

*durgāyāścāpi kavacaṃ dattaṃ durvāsasā purā \|*  
*stavanaṃ cāpi gopyaṃ ca mantraścāpi daśākṣaraḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. In the earlier times the Durga-kavacha was endowed by Durvasa to the king and the secret stotra and the ten letter mantra.

## \|\| 38.44 \|\|

*paścācchroṣyasi tatsarvaṃ devyāśca paramādbhutam \|*  
*mahāyuddhasamārambhe dattaṃ prārthanayā ca yat \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The extremely astonishing kavacha of the goddess Durga shall be told to you later, which was handed over to the kings at the start of the great war.

## \|\| 38.45 \|\|

*mahālakṣmyāśca mantraṃ ca śṛṇu taṃ kathayāmi te \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ kamalavāsinyai svāheti paramādbhutam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Currently I am revealing the mantra of Maha-Lakshmi to you ... This is the best of the mantras.

## \|\| 38.46 \|\|

*dhyānaṃ ca sāmavedoktaṃ śṛṇu pūjāvidhiṃ mune \|*  
*dattaṃ tasmai kumāreṇa puṣkarākṣāya dhīmate \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. О sage, the dhyanam, the method of adoration, whatever was revealed by Sanatkumara to Pus karaksa is being retold by me. You please listen to it.

## \|\| 38.47-49 \|\|

*sahasradalapadmasthāṃ padmanābhapriyāṃ satīm \|*  
*padmālayāṃ padmavaktrāṃ padmapatrābhalocanām \|\| 47 \|\|*

*padmapuṣpapriyāṃ padmapuṣpatalpādhiśāyinīm \|*  
*padminīpadmahastāṃ ca padmamālāvibhūṣitām \|\| 48 \|\|*

*padmabhūṣaṇabhūṣāḍhyāṃ padmaśobhāvivarddhanīm \|*  
*padmāṭavīṃ prapaśyantīṃ sasmitāṃ tāṃ bhaje mudā \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. Maha-Lakshmi, the beloved of lord Vishnu, is lodged on the lotus of a thousand petals. She is chaste, lotus-faced, having the eyes like lotus flowers and she is like the lotus flowers. She sleeps on the bed of lotus flowers and holds the lotus stalk in her hand. She wears garlands of lotus flowers and is also adorned with the ornaments of lotus flowers. She increases the glory of lotus flowers, looks at the forest of lotus flowers and she is the one who wears a serene smile on her face. I serve her with devotion.

## \|\| 38.50-51 \|\|

*candanāṣṭadale padme padmapuṣpeṇa pūjayet \|*  
*gaṇaṃ sampūjya dattvā caivopacārāṃśca ṣoḍaśa \|\| 50 \|\|*

*tataḥ stutvā ca praṇametsādhako bhaktipūrvakam \|*  
*kavacaṃ śrūyatāṃ brahmansarvasāraṃ vadāmi te \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. One should write it on the lotus with ten petals and should adorn her with lotus flowers; her attendants should also be adored and the offerings of sixteen types should be made to her, after making prayers. Thereafter, the devotees should bow before her with devotion. (O Brahmana, now you listen from me about the essence of all the kavachas from me.

## \|\| 38.52-53 \|\|

*śṛṇu viprendra padmāyāḥ kavacaṃ paramaṃ śubham \|*  
*padmanābhena yaddattaṃ brahmaṇe nābhipadmake \|\| 52 \|\|*

*samprāpya kavacaṃ brahmā tatpadme sasṛje jagat \|*  
*padmālayāprasādena salakṣmīko babhūva saḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

52-53. Narayana said – О best of the Brahmana, this is the best kavacha of Lakshmi which was bestowed by lord Padmanabha on Brahma who emerged seated on a lotus from the navel of Vis nu. Brahma after receiving the kavacha started the creation of the universe seated on the lotus and with the grace of Lakshmi, he achieved all the riches.

## \|\| 38.54-55 \|\|

*padmālayāvaraṃ prāpya pādmaśca jagatāṃ prabhuḥ \|*  
*pādyena padmakalpe ca kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 54 \|\|*

*dattaṃ sanatkumārāya priyaputrāya dhīmate \|*  
*kumāreṇa ca yaddattaṃ puṣkarāya ca nārada \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. After receiving a boon from Lakshmi, Brahma, the lord of the universe then imparted the knowledge of the kavacha to his intelligent son Sanatkumara. О Narada, the same kavacha was bestowed by Sanatkumara to Puskaraksa.

## \|\| 38.56 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā pāṭhanād brahmā savasiddheśvaro mahān \|*  
*paramaiśvaryyasaṃyuktaḥ sarvasampatsamanvitaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. By wearing and reciting the same Brahma became the great lord and was bestowed with all the fortunes and riches.

## \|\| 38.57 \|\|

*yaddatvā ca dhanādhyakṣaḥ kuberaśca dhanādhipaḥ \|*  
*svāyambhuvo manuḥ śrīmānpaṭhanāddhāraṇādyataḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. By wearing this, Kubera became the lord of riches and wealth and by wearing this Manu became Svayambhuva.

## \|\| 38.58-59 \|\|

*priyavratottānapādau lakṣmīvantau yato mune \|*  
*pṛthuḥ pṛthvīpatiḥ sadyo hyabhavadvāraṇādyataḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*kavacasya prasādena svayaṃ dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*dharmmaśca karmmaṇāṃ sākṣī pātā yasya prasādataḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

58-59. О sage, by wearing this Priyavrata and Uttanapada gained all the riches and the king Prthu became the king of his kingdom.

## \|\| 38.60 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā dakṣiṇe bāhau viṣṇuḥ kṣīrodaśāyitaḥ \|*  
*bhaktyā vidhatte kaṇṭhe ca śeṣo nārāyaṇāṃśakaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. With the grace of this kavacha Daksha became Prajapati. By the grace of this kavacha, dharma became the witness of all and by wearing it around the right arm, Vishnu became the dweller of the ocean of milk and with the grace of Narayana, Shesha became devoted to the lord.

## \|\| 38.61-62 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā vāmanaṃ lebhe kaśyapaśca prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*sarvadevādhipaḥ śrīmānmahendro dhāraṇādyataḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

*rājā marutto bhagavānabhavaddhāraṇādyataḥ \|*  
*trailokyādhipatiḥ śrīmānnahuṣo yasya dhāraṇāt \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. By wearing it, Kashyapa became Prajapati and achieved Vamana as a son. Mahendra also became the lord of the gods with the same.

## \|\| 38.63 \|\|

*viśvaṃ vijigye khaṭvāṅgaḥ paṭhanāddhāraṇādyataḥ \|*  
*mucukundo yataḥ śrīmānmāndhātṛtanayo mahān \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. By wearing it Maruta became the lord; the great king Nahusa became the lord of the three worlds. By reciting it or wearing it, the king Khat vanga conquered the entire universe and Mucukunda the son of king Mandhata became the great lord.

## \|\| 38.64-65 \|\|

*sarvasampatpadasyāsya kavacasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca bṛhatī devī padmālayā svayam \|\| 64 \|\|*

*dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|*  
*puṇyabījaṃ ca mahatāṃ kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. The kavacha which bestows all the fortune and riches has Prajapati as its Rsi, Brihatl as the metre, Laksrnl herself is the goddess and one should resolve to achieve dharma, artha, каща and moksa with this mantra. This is quite an astonishing kavacha and is the sacred seed for becoming glorious.

## \|\| 38.66 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ kamalavāsinyai svāhā me pātu mastakam \|*  
*śrīṃ me pātu kapālaṃ ca locane śrīṃ śriyai namaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. should protect my forehead. should protect my head. should protect my eyes.

## \|\| 38.67 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ śriyai svāheti ca karṇayugmaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ klīṃ mahālakṣyai svāhā me pātu nāsikām \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. should protect my ears, should protect my nose.

## \|\| 38.68 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ padmālayāyai ca svāhā dantānsadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ kṛṣṇapriyāyai ca dantarandhraṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. should protect ёmy teeth, should protect nostrils.

## \|\| 38.69 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ nārāyaṇeśāyai mama kaṇṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ śrīṃ keśavakāntāyai mama skandhaṃ sadā'vatu \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. should protect my neck. should protect my shoulders.

## \|\| 38.70 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ padmanivāsinyai svāhā nābhiṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ saṃsāramātre mama vakṣaḥ sadā'vatu \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. should protect my navel, should protect my chest.

## \|\| 38.71 \|\|

*oṃ śrīṃ moṃ kṛṣṇakāntāyai svāhā pṛṣṭhaṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ śriyai svāhā ca mama hastau sadā'vatu \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. should protect my back, should protect my hands.

## \|\| 38.72 \|\|

*oṃ śrīnivāsakāntāyai mama padau sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ śriyai svāhā sarvāṅgaṃ me sadā'vatu \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. should protect my feet. should protect all my limbs.

## \|\| 38.73-74 \|\|

*prācyāṃ pātu mahālakṣmīrāgneyyāṃ kamalālayā \|*  
*padmā māṃ dakṣiṇe pātu nairṛtyāṃ śrīrharipriyā \|\| 73 \|\|*

*padmālayā paścime māṃ vāyavyāṃ pātu sā svayam \|*  
*uttare kamalā pātu caiśānyāṃ sindhukanyakā \|\| 74 \|\|*

73-74. The eastern direction should be protected by Maha-Lakshmi, south-east by Kamalalaya, southern direction by Padma, south-west by Haripriya, western direction by Padmalaya, north-west should be protected by the goddess herself. The northern region should be protected by Kamala, the north-east by Sindhu-kanya.

## \|\| 38.75 \|\|

*nārāyaṇī ca pātūrdhvamadho viṣṇupriyā'vatu \|*  
*santataṃ sarvataḥ pātu viṣṇuprāṇādhikā mama \|\| 75\|\|*

75\. The upper region should be protected by Narayani and the lower region by Vishnupriya. Vishnupranadhika protects all directions.

## \|\| 38.76 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvamantraughavigraham \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyapradaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. O son, thus I have explained to you the kavacha which bestows the fortune and is the best of all the mantras, being the astonishing one.

## \|\| 38.77 \|\|

*suvarṇaparvataṃ dattvā merutulyaṃ dvijātaye \|*  
*yatphalaṃ labhate dharmī kavacena tato'dhikam \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. The merit one gets by giving in charity heaps of gold measuring the Meru mountain will be of no consequence as compared to the merit one derives by reciting this kavacha.

## \|\| 38.78 \|\|

*gurumabhyarcya vidhivatkavacaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|*  
*kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe bāhau sa śrīmānpratijanmani \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. One should wear this kavacha round the neck or around the right arm, adoring the teacher in a proper manner and by doing so one becomes lord of Lakshmi in all one's births.

## \|\| 38.79 \|\|

*asti lakṣmīrgṛhe tasya niścalā śatapūruṣam \|*  
*devendraiścāsurendraiśca so'vadhyo niścitaṃ bhavet \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. The goddess of riches resides in his house up to hundred generations. He remains infallible from the gods and the demons.

## \|\| 38.80 \|\|

*sa sarvapuṇyavāndhīmānsarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ \|*  
*sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu yasyedaṃ kavacaṃ gale \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. The one who wears the kavacha around his neck always becomes virtuous, intelligent, a performer of all the yajnas and earns the merit of taking a bath in all the sacred places.

## \|\| 38.81 \|\|

*yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ lobhamohabhayairapi \|*  
*gurubhaktāya śiṣyāya śaraṇyāya prakāśayet \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. Therefore, it should not be parted with because of greed, passion and fear to anyone else. It should be given to a person who is devoted to his teacher and takes refuge under him.

## \|\| 38.82 \|\|

*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā japellakṣmīṃ jagatprabhum \|*  
*koṭisaṃkhyaṃ prajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. The one who adores Lakshmi the mother of the universe, without knowing folly well about it, cannot succeed with this mantra even after reciting it crores of times.

## Chapter 39

## \|\| 39.1 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*kavacaṃ kathitaṃ brahmanpadmāyāśca manoharam \|*  
*paraṃ durgatināśinyāḥ kavacaṃ kathaya prabho \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narada said – О lord, you have apprised me of the pleasant kavacha of goddess Padma; now you kindly bestow the knowledge of the kavacha of Durga who is known as Durgatinasini.

## \|\| 39.2 \|\|

*padmākṣaprāṇatulyaṃ ca jīvanaṃ balakāraṇam \|*  
*kavacānāṃ ca yatsāraṃ durgāsevanakāraṇam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. This was the life of king Padmaksa, his strength and the essence of all the kavachas. This kavacha happens to be the main source of the adoration Durga.

## \|\| 39.3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu nārada vakṣyāmi durgāyāḥ kavavaṃ śubham \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇenaiva yaddattaṃ goloke brahmaṇe purā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Narayana said – О Narada, I bestow the knowledge of the auspicious kavacha of Durga, the knowledge of which was imparted in the Goloka by lord Krishna to Brahma in the earlier times.

## \|\| 39.4 \|\|

*brahmā tripurasaṃgrāme śaṃkarāya dadau purā \|*  
*jaghāna tripuraṃ rudro yaddhṛtvā bhaktipūrvakam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. At the time of the battle with Tripurasura, Brahma gave this kavacha to Shiva which was worn by him with devotion, as a result of which Tripura was killed by him.

## \|\| 39.5 \|\|

*haro dadau gautamāya padmākṣāya ca gautamaḥ \|*  
*yato babhūva padmākṣaḥ saptadvīpeśvaro jayī \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Thereafter Shiva imparted its knowledge to Gautama and Gautama ultimately gave it to Padmaksa, as a result of which the king became victorious over all the seven islands.

## \|\| 39.6 \|\|

*yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādbrahmā jñānavāñchaktimānbhuvi \|*  
*śivo babhūva sarvajño yogināṃ ca gururyataḥ \|*  
*śivatulyo gautamaśca babhūva munisattamaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. By wearing and reciting this, Brahma became adorable on earth besides becoming intellectual and valorous. Shiva became all knowledgeable besides being the teacher and Gautama the best of the sages became equal to Shiva.

## \|\| 39.7-8 \|\|

*brahmāṇḍavijayasyāsya kavacasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*ṛṣiśchandaśca gāyatrī devī durgatināśinī \|\| 7 \|\|*

*brahmāṇḍavijaye caiva viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ \|*  
*puṇyatīrthaṃ ca mahatāṃ kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. This kavacha is known as the conqueror of the globe and Prajapati happens to be the Rsi of this kavacha, Gayatri is the metre, Durgatinasinl is the goddess and one should take a vow for this kavacha for conquering the universe. This is quite an astonishing kavacha and happens to be the sacred place for great people.

## \|\| 39.9 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ durgatināśinyai svāhā me pātu mastakam \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ me pātu kapālaṃ cāpyoṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ pātu locane \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. should protect my forehead. should protect my head. should protect both my eyes.

## \|\| 39.10 \|\|

*pātu me karṇayugmaṃ cāpyoṃ durgāyai namaḥ sadā \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ śrīmiti nāsāṃ me sadā pātu ca sarvataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. should protect my ears. should protect my nose from all the sides.

## \|\| 39.11 \|\|

*hrīṃ śrīṃ krūmiti dantāṃśca pātu klīmoṣṭhayugmakam \|*  
*klīṃ klīṃ klīṃ pātu kaṇṭhaṃ ca durge rakṣatu gaṇḍake \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. should protect my teeth, should protect both my lips, should protect my throat. should protect my cheeks.

## \|\| 39.12 \|\|

*skandhaṃ mahākāli durge svāhā pātu nirantaram \|*  
*vakṣo vipadvināśinyai svāhā me pātu sarvataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. should protect my shoulders. should protect my chest from all sides.

## \|\| 39.13 \|\|

*durge durge rakṣa pārśvau svāhā nābhiṃ sadā'vatu \|*  
*durge durge dehi rakṣāṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ me pātu sarvataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. should protect my back. should protect my navel. should protect my back.

## \|\| 39.14 \|\|

*oṃ hrīṃ durgāyai svāhā ca hastau pādau sadā'vatu \|*  
*oṃ hrīṃ durgāyai svāhā ca sarvāṃgaṃ me sadā'vatu \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. should protect my hands and feet, should protect all my limbs.

## \|\| 39.15-16 \|\|

*prācyāṃ pātu mahāmāyā cāgneyyāṃ pātu kālikā \|*  
*dakṣiṇe dakṣakanyā ca nairṛtyāṃ śivasundarī \|\| 15 \|\|*

*paścime pārvatī pātu vārāhī vāruṇe sadā \|*  
*kuberamātā kauberyyāmaiśānyāmīśvarī sadā \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. The goddess Mahamaya protects the eastern direction and Kalika the south-eastern direction, Dakshakanya protects the southern direction, south-west is protected my Shivasundari. The western direction is protected my Parvati, the north-west direction by Varahl. Northern direction is protected my the mother of Kubera, while Ishvari protects the north-east.

## \|\| 39.17 \|\|

*ūrdhvaṃ nārāyaṇī pātu tvambikā'dhaḥ sadā'vatu \|*  
*jñānaṃ jñānapradā pātu svapne nidrā sadā'vatu \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The goddess Narayani protects the upper region while the lower region is protected by Ambika, who possesses all knowledge and bestows the same. She should protect when one is in sleep or awake.

## \|\| 39.18 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ vatsa sarvamantraughavigraham \|*  
*brahmāṇḍavijayaṃ nāma kavacaṃ paramādbhutam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. О son, thus I have revealed to you the knowledge of the astonishing Brahmandavijaya-kavacha which is the essence of all the kavachas.

## \|\| 39.19 \|\|

*susnātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu yatphalam \|*  
*sarvavratopavāse ca tatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The merit one achieves by’having a bath at all the holy places, performing all the yajnas, vratas besides fasting, the same merit is achieved by a person while adoring this mantra.

## \|\| 39.20-22 \|\|

*gurumabhyarcya vidhivadvastrālaṃkāracandanaiḥ \|*  
*kaṇṭhe vā dakṣiṇe bāhau kavacaṃ dhārayettu yaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*sa ca trailokyavijayī sarvaśatrupramardakaḥ \|*  
*idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajeddurgatināśinīm \|\| 21 \|\|*

*śatalakṣaṃ prajapto'pi na mantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. The one who gives away in charity various types of clothes, ornaments and sandal-paste, to his teacher and ties this kavacha on the right arm, becomes victorious over all the three worlds and all his enemies are destroyed; the one who adores the goddess Durga without fully being aware of the kavacha, cannot be successful in his mission even after reciting it for a hundred lakhs of times.

## \|\| 39.23 \|\|

*kavacaṃ kāṇvaśākhoktamuktaṃ nārada siddhidam \|*  
*yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ gopanīyaṃ sudurlabham \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. O Narada, this kavacha which is revealed in the Kanvasakha of the Samaveda provides success and is quite secret in nature, besides being inaccessible. Therefore its knowledge could not be imparted to everyone.

## Chapter 40

## \|\| 40.1-3 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*taṃ gṛhītvā tadā viṣṇau vaikuṇṭhaṃ ca gate sati \|*  
*saputraṃ ca sahasrākṣaṃ jaghāna bhṛgunandanaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kṛtvā yuddhaṃ tu saptāhaṃ brahmāstreṇa prayatnataḥ \|*  
*rājā kavacahīno'pi saputraśca papāta ha \|\| 2 \|\|*

*patite tu sahasrākṣe kārtavīryyārjunaḥ svayam \|*  
*ājagāma mahāvīro dvilakṣākṣauhiṇīyutaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

1-3. Narayana said – When the kavachas were carried to Vaikuntha by Vishnu, Paras'urama the son of Bhrigu then killed Sahasraksa. Having been deprived of the kavacha, the king continued to fight for a week with Brahmastra but was destroyed with all his sons. At the fall of Sahasraksa, the valorous Karttivirya himself came foreword with two lakhs aksauhinl army.

## \|\| 40.4 \|\|

*suvarṇarathamāruhya ratnasāraparicchadam \|*  
*nānāstraṃ paritaḥ kṛtvā tasthau samaramūrddhani \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He mounted on a golden chariot. He was clad in costumes studded with the best of gems and was surrounded by several of the weapons all round him. Thus having been assured protection from all the sides, he marched foreword in the battle-field.

## \|\| 40.5-6 \|\|

*samare taṃ paraśurāmo rājendraṃ ca dadarśa ha \|*  
*ratnālaṃkārabhūṣāḍhyai rājendrāṇāṃ ca koṭibhiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*ratnātapatrabhūṣāḍhyaṃ ratnālaṃkārabhūṣitam \|*  
*candanokṣitasarvāṃgaṃ sasmitaṃ sumanoharam \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Parashurama then saw the king in the battlefield, who was adorned with innumerable ornaments studded with gems and equated the glory of crores of Indras. He had a chatra of gems and was adorned with gem-studded ornaments. All his limbs were plastered with sandal-paste. He looked quite pleasant while smiling.

## \|\| 40.7 \|\|

*rājā dṛṣṭvā munīndraṃ tamavaruhma rathādaho \|*  
*praṇamya rathamāruhya tasthau nṛpagaṇaiḥ saha \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Having a look at sage Parashurama, the king got down from the chariot and bowed down in reverence to him with all the kings. Thereafter, he went back to his chariot with the kings and took his seat there.

## \|\| 40.8 \|\|

*dadau śubhāśiṣaṃ tasmai rāmaśca samayocitam \|*  
*provāca ca gatārthaṃ taṃ svargaṃ gaccheti sānugaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Parashurama blessed the king and spoke appropriate words which suited the time, "You proceed to heaven with your attendants.”

## \|\| 40.9 \|\|

*ubhayoḥ senayoryuddhamabhavattatra nārada \|*  
*palāyitā rāmaśiṣyā bhrātaraśca mahābalāḥ \|*  
*kṣatavikṣatasarvāṅgāḥ kārttavīryyaprapīḍitāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O Narada, thereafter both the armies fought with each other in which the pupils and brothers of Paras'urama were shattered by Kartavirya who fled away frohi the battle-field.

## \|\| 40.10 \|\|

*nṛpasya śarajālena rāmaḥ śastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*na dadarśa svasainyaṃ ca rājasainyaṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. With the shooting of innumerable arrows, Paras'urama was unable to look at his army as well as the relatives.

## \|\| 40.11 \|\|

*cikṣepa rāmaścāgneyaṃ babhūvāgnimayaṃ raṇe \|*  
*nirvāpayāmāsa rājā vāruṇenaiva līlayā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Thereafter Parashurama shot the fire arrows in the battle-field, as a result of which, everything was in flames in the battle-field. The king on his part shot the Varuna-arrow, which extinguished the fire making the atmosphere peaceful.

## \|\| 40.12 \|\|

*cikṣepa rāmo gāndharvaṃ śailasarpasamanvitam \|*  
*vāyavyena mahārājaḥ prerayāmāsa līlayā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Parashurama then shot Gandharvastra which emitted mountains and snakes which was neutralised by the king by shooting the wind arrow.

## \|\| 40.13 \|\|

*cikṣepa rāmo nāgāstraṃ durnivāryyaṃ bhayaṃkaram \|*  
*gāruḍena mahārājaḥ prerayāmāsa līlayā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Parasurama then shot Nagastra which was quite terrific but the king neutralised it by shooting Garudastra which destroyed the serpent without much effort.

## \|\| 40.14 \|\|

*māheśvaraṃ ca bhagavāṃścikṣepa bhṛgunandanaḥ \|*  
*nirvāpayāmāsa rājā vaiṣṇavāstreṇa līlayā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Parashurama the son of Bhrigu then used the weapon of Maheshvara which was neutralised by the king, by using the Vaishnava weapon.

## \|\| 40.15 \|\|

*brahmāstraṃ cikṣipe rāmo nṛpanāśāya nārada \|*  
*brahmāstreṇa ca śāntaṃ tatprāṇanirvāpaṇaṃ raṇe \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О Narada, thereafter Paras'urama used Brahmastra for the destruction of the king. The king also used the same type of Brahmastra neutralising the one shot by Parashurama.

## \|\| 40.16 \|\|

*dattadattaṃ ca yacchūlamavyarthaṃ mantrapūrvakam \|*  
*jagrāha rājā paraśurāmanāśāya saṃyuge \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The king at that point of time shot the trident bestowed to him by Dattatreya for killing his enemy which was always successful in its mission.

## \|\| 40.17-18 \|\|

*śūlaṃ dadarśa rāmaśca śatasūryyasamaprabham \|*  
*pralayāgriśikhodriktaṃ durnivāryaṃ surairapi \|\| 17 \|\|*

*papāta śūlaṃ samare rāmasyopari nārada \|*  
*mūrcchāmavāpa sa bhṛguḥ papāta ca hariṃ smaran \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Parashurama then saw the trident emitting lustre of crores of suns which was extremely glorious and emitted fire like the fire of dissolution. Even the gods were unable to face it. О Narada, the trident fell over Parashurama as a result of which he fell down fainted.

## \|\| 40.19 \|\|

*patite tu tadā rāme sarve devā bhayākulāḥ \|*  
*ājagmuḥ samaraṃ tatra brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. With the fall of Paras'urama, all the gods became restive. At that point of time Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva also arrived in the battle-field.

## \|\| 40.20 \|\|

*śaṅkaraśca mahājñānī mahājñānena līlayā \|*  
*brāhmaṇaṃ jīvayāmāsa tūrṇaṃ nārāyaṇājñayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. At the instance of Narayana the valorous Shiva brought back the Brahmana to life with this illusion.

## \|\| 40.21 \|\|

*bhṛguśca cetanāṃ prāpya dadarśa purataḥ surān \|*  
*praṇanāma paraṃ bhaktyā lajjānamrātmakandharaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. After regaining consciousness Bhrigu found all the three gods before him and he bowed in reverence to them.

## \|\| 40.22 \|\|

*rājā dṛṣṭvā sureśāṃśca bhaktinamrātmakandharaḥ \|*  
*praṇamya śirasā mūrdhnā tuṣṭāva ca sureśvarān \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Finding them there, the king bowed his head in devotion and offered his salutation to all of them, besides eulogising them.

## \|\| 40.23-24 \|\|

*tatrājagāma bhagavāndattātreyo raṇasthalam \|*  
*śiṣyarakṣānimittena kṛpālurbhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*bhṛguḥ pāśupatāstraṃ ca so'grahītkopasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*dattadattena dṛṣṭena babhūva stambhito bhṛguḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. In the meantime lord Dattatreya also reached in the battle-field for the protection of his devotee. Thereafter Paras'urama getting enraged shot the Pasupata weapon but at the same time Bhrigu was surprised at the sight of Dattatreya.

## \|\| 40.25 \|\|

*dadarśa stambhito rāmo rājānaṃ raṇamūrddhani \|*  
*nānāpārṣadayuktena kṛṣṇenā''rakṣitaṃ raṇe \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Inspite of getting surprised Parashurama looked at the king in the battle-field, who was protected in the battle-field by lord Krishna and many of his courtiers.

## \|\| 40.26 \|\|

*sudarśanaṃ prajvalantaṃ bhramaṇaṃ kurvatā sadā \|*  
*sasmitena stutenaiva brahmaviṣṇumaheśvaraiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Lord Krishna was protecting him wielding his Sudarsana-cakra. All the gods including Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva were offering prayers to him smilingly.

## \|\| 40.27 \|\|

*gopālaśatayuktena gopaveṣavidhāriṇā \|*  
*navīnajaladābhena vaṃśīhastena gāyatā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. He was surrounded by hundreds of cowherds and was clad in the costumes of cowherds, possessing the complexion of new clouds and holding a flute in his hand. Lord Krishna was playing on the flute.

## \|\| 40.28-31 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra vāgbabhūvāśarīriṇī \|*  
*dattena dattaṃ kavacaṃ kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*rājño'sti dakṣiṇe bāhau sadratnaguṭikānvitam \|*  
*gṛhītakavace śambhau bhikṣayā yogināṃ gurau \|\| 29 \|\|*

*tadā hantuṃ nṛpaṃ śakto bhṛguśceti ca nārada \|*  
*śrutvā'śarīriṇīṃ vāṇīṃ śaṅkaro dvijarūpadhṛk \|\| 30 \|\|*

*bhikṣāṃ kṛtvā tu kavacamānīya ca nṛpasya ca \|*  
*śambhunā bhṛgave dattaṃ kṛṣṇasya kavacaṃ ca yat \|\| 31 \|\|*

28-31. In the meantime a divine voice from the sky was heard that the king possessed the kavacha of Krishna which was handed over to him by Dattatreya and he was wearing it in the casket of gem bound over the right arm. Therefore Shiva the teacher of the yogis should bet it from him. Parashurama would be able to kill the king only thereafter. О Narada on hearing this divine voice, Shiva took to the form of a Brahmana and went to the king and begged for the kavacha of Krishna which he had received from the lord and gave it ultimately to Bhrigu.

## \|\| 40.32 \|\|

*etasminnantare devā jagmuḥ svasthānamuttamam \|*  
*pratyuvācāpi paraśurāmo vai samare nṛpam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Thereafter the gods dispersed to their respective abodes and then Parashurama again said to the king.

## \|\| 40.33 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*rājendrottiṣṭha samaraṃ kuru sāhasapūrvakam \|*  
*kālabhede jayo nṛṇāṃ kālabhede parājayaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Parasurama said – "O Indra among the kings, get up and fight with me quite courageously. The humans face victory or defeat according to the time.

## \|\| 40.34 \|\|

*adhītaṃ vidhivaddattaṃ kṛtsnā pṛthvī suśāsitā \|*  
*samyakkṛtaśca saṃgrāmastvayā'haṃ mūrcchito 'dhunā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Because I have carefully studied myself the scriptures and also made my pupils do so, I have ruled the entire universe pretty well and have fought also pretty well but you made me fainted.

## \|\| 40.35 \|\|

*jitāḥ sarve ca rājendrā līlayā rāvaṇo jitaḥ \|*  
*jito'haṃ dattaśūlena śambhunā jīvitaḥ punaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. You also playfully became victorious indulging the Brahmanas but still I have been defeated with the trident given to you by Dattatreya. Shiva then arrived and brought me back to life."

## \|\| 40.36 \|\|

*rāmasya vacanaṃ śrutvā rājā paramadhārmikaḥ \|*  
*mūrdhnā praṇamya taṃ bhaktyā yathārthoktimuvāca ha \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. On hearing the words of Parashurama, the religious minded king bowed to him with devotion and spoke to him the appropriate words.

## \|\| 40.37 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*kimadhītaṃ tathā dattaṃ kā vā pṛthvī suśāsitā \|*  
*gatāḥ katividhā bhūpā mādṛśā dharaṇītale \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The king said – What have you studied? What have you given away in charity? Which is the earth ruled by you and how many of the kings like me have been killed by you?

## \|\| 40.38-39 \|\|

*buddhistejo vikramaśca vividhā raṇamantraṇā \|*  
*śrīraiśvaryaṃ tathā jñānaṃ dānaśaktiśca laukikam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*ācāro vinayo vidyā pratiṣṭhā paramaṃ tapaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ manoramāsaṃge gatameva mama prabho \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. О lord, my wisdom, glory, prowess, various types of battle art, wealth, fortune, knowledge, capacity for giving charity, universal glory, conduct, discipline, knowledge, position, great tapas and everything has been lost with Manorama.

## \|\| 40.40-41 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte tṛtīye gaṇeśakhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde bhṛgoḥ kailāsagamanopadeśo nāma catvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*ābālyātsaṃginī śaśvacchayane bhojane raṇe \|*  
*tāṃ vinā prāṇahīno'haṃ viṣahīno yathoragaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. She was dearer to me than my life, quite chaste and was born out of the amsa of Kamala. The wife in the yajnas becomes affectionate like mothers and at the time of love sports she becomes a good companion besides sleeping, taking food, in the battle-field and in childhood she always remains with him. Therefore without her I have become a serpent without poison.

## \|\| 40.42 \|\|

*tvayā na dṛṣṭaṃ yuddhaṃ me pureyaṃ śocanā sthitā \|*  
*dvitīyā śocanā vipra hato'haṃ brāhmaṇena ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. О Brahmana you had never seen me fight earlier, this is my first grief and my second grief is that I am being defeated by a Brahmana.

## \|\| 40.43 \|\|

*kāle siṃhaḥ sṛgālaṃ ca sṛgālaḥ siṃhameva ca \|*  
*kāle vyāghraṃ hanti mṛgo gajendraṃ hariṇastathā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Though according to the times a lion kills a jackal and a jackal kills a lion. At certain times, a stag kills a tiger and a buck kills an elephant.

## \|\| 40.44 \|\|

*mahiṣaṃ makṣikā kāle garuḍaṃ ca tathoragaḥ \|*  
*kiṅkaraḥ stauti rājendraṃ kāle rājā ca kiṃkaram \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. At times a fly kills a he-buffalo and similarly a snake kills a Garuda. Ordinarily the king is adored by his servants but at times the king has also to adore the servant.

## \|\| 40.45 \|\|

*indraṃ ca mānavaḥ kāle kāle brahmā mariṣyati \|*  
*tirobhūtvā sā prakṛtiḥ kāle śrīkṛṣṇavigrahe \|\| 45\|\|*

45\. At times a human kills Indra and in due course of time Brahma also has to meet with his death; at times Prakriti also gets merged into the body of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 40.46 \|\|

*mariṣyanti surāḥ sarve trilokasthāścarācarāḥ \|*  
*sarve kāle layaṃ yānti kālo hi duratikramaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. All the gods will have to meet with their end, all the movable and immovable riches of the world have to meet with their end in due course of time. Therefore the time is indeed horrible and cannot be averted.

## \|\| 40.47 \|\|

*kālasya kālaḥ śrīkṛṣṇaḥ sraṣṭuḥ sraṣṭā yathecchayā \|*  
*saṃhartā caiva saṃhartuḥ pātuḥ pātā parātparaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. But lord Krishna by his own sweet will is the death for the lord of death, is the creator of the creators, destroyer of the destroyers and preserver of the preservers besides being beyond all of them.

## \|\| 40.48 \|\|

*mahāsthūlātsthūlatamaḥ sūkṣmātsūkṣmatamaḥ kṛśaḥ \|*  
*paramāṇuparaḥ kālaḥ kālassyātkālabhedakaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. He is harder than the hardest, smaller than the smallest, lifeless, extremely small, death for the god of death and creates difference in time.

## \|\| 40.49 \|\|

*yasya lomāni viśvāni sa pumāṃśca mahāvirāṭ \|*  
*tejasā ṣoḍaśāṃśaśca kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. There are innumerable globes which happen to project only one sixteenth of the tejas of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 40.50-51 \|\|

*tataḥ kṣudravirāḍ jātaḥ sarveṣāṃ kāraṇaṃ param \|*  
*yaḥ sraṣṭā ca svayaṃ brahmā yannābhikamalodbhavaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*nābheḥ kamaladaṇḍasya yo'ntaṃ na prāpa yatnataḥ \|*  
*bhramaṇāllakṣavarṣaṃ ca tataḥ svasthānasaṃsthitaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. The pure Virat emerged from him who is the cause of all in the universe. Brahma himself who is the creator of the universe emerged out of his navel but inspite of making strenuous efforts Brahma couldn't find the end of the lotus stalk. He wandered for a lakh of years in search of it and ultimately returned to his original place.

## \|\| 40.52 \|\|

*tapaścakre tatastatra lakṣavarṣaṃ ca vāyubhuk \|*  
*tato dadarśa golokaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ ca sapārṣadam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Thereafter inhaling air he performed tapas for a lakh of years and ultimately he achieved Goloka and had an audience with lord Krishna.

## \|\| 40.53-54 \|\|

*gopagopīparivṛtaṃ dvibhujaṃ muralīdharam \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca rādhāvakṣasthalasthitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā'nujñāṃ gṛhītvā ca praṇamya ca punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*īśvarecchāṃ ca vijñāya sraṣṭuṃ sṛṣṭiṃ mano dadhe \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. Brahma then found lord Krishna resting his head on the breasts of Radha, seated over a gem-studded lion-throne, having two arms and surrounded by cowherds and cowherdesses. He offered salutations to him again and again. He getting permission from the lord and knowing his minjd, started creating the universe.

## \|\| 40.55 \|\|

*yaḥ śivaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃharttā sa ca sraṣṭurlalāṭajaḥ \|*  
*viṣṇuḥ pātā kṣudravirāṭ śvetadvīpanivāsakṛt \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Shiva who destroyed the universe appeared from the head of Brahma and Vishnu who resides in the Shveta-dvipa is known as small Virat.

## \|\| 40.56 \|\|

*sṛṣṭikāraṇabhūtāśca brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|*  
*santi viśveṣu sarveṣu śrīkṛṣṇasya kalodbhavāḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva who emerged from the rays of lord Krishna happens to be the cause of the creation of universe.

## \|\| 40.57-58 \|\|

*te'pi devāḥ prākṛtikāḥ prākṛtaśca mahāvirāṭ \|*  
*sarvaprasūtiḥ prakṛtiḥ śrīkṛṣṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

*na śaktaḥ parameśo'pi tāṃ śaktiṃ prakṛtiṃ vinā \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ vidhātuṃ māyeśo na sṛṣṭirmāyayā vinā \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. All the gods have emerged from Prakriti; even Parameshvara is unable to resort to creation without the strength of Prakriti. He happens to be the lord of illusion and no creation is possible without him.

## \|\| 40.59 \|\|

*sā ca kṛṣṇe tirobhūya sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārake \|*  
*sā''virbhūtā sṛṣṭikāle sā ca nityā maheśvarī \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Lord Krishna who is the creator of the universe absorbs Prakriti ultimately in his body at the time of dissolution. At the time of recreation, she emerges again. The same goddess Prakriti is eternal.

## \|\| 40.60 \|\|

*kulālaśca kaṭaṃ kartuṃ yathā'śakto mṛdaṃ vinā \|*  
*svarṇaṃ vinā svarṇakāraḥ kuṇḍalaṃ kartumakṣamaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. As a potter is unable to make a pot without clay or a gold-smith can never make ornaments without gold, similarly there can be no creation without illusion.

## \|\| 40.61 \|\|

*sā ca śaktiḥ sṛṣṭikāle pañcadhā ceśvarecchayā \|*  
*rādhā padmā ca sāvitrī durgā devī sarasvatī \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The Prakriti who is the form of shakti with the will of the lord, turned herself as Radha, Padma, Savitri, Durga, Sarasvati. She is divided into these five forms.

## \|\| 40.62 \|\|

*prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*prāṇādhikapriyatamā sā rādhā parikīrttitā \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. She is called Radha because, she is dearer to lord Krishna than his life and is quite dear to him.

## \|\| 40.63 \|\|

*aiśvaryyādhiṣṭhātṛdevī sarvamaṅgalakāriṇī \|*  
*paramānandarūpā ca sā lakṣmīḥ parikīrttitā \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. She is the goddess of fortune and bestows all the welfare and is blissful, as a result of which, she is called Lakshmi.

## \|\| 40.64 \|\|

*vidyādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā parameśasya durlabhā \|*  
*yā mātā vedaśāstrāṇāṃ sā sāvitrī prakīrtitā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The one who is the great goddess of knowledge and the strength of the lord, beside being the creator of the Vedas, is known as Savitri.

## \|\| 40.65 \|\|

*buddhyadhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā sarvaśaktisvarūpiṇī \|*  
*sarvajñānātmikā sarvā sā durgā durganāśinī \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The one who is the goddess of wisdom and prowess, possessing all the knowledge and the form of all, besides being destroyer of the misfortune, is known as Durga.

## \|\| 40.66 \|\|

*vāgadhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā śāstrajñānapradā sadā \|*  
*kṛṣṇakaṇṭhodbhavā sā syādyā ca devī sarasvatī \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. The goddess who controls the speech and bestows the wisdom in the scriptures, has been born out of the throat of lord Krishna and is known as Sarasvati.

## \|\| 40.67 \|\|

*pañcadhā''dau svayaṃ devī mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī \|*  
*tataḥ sṛṣṭikrameṇaiva bahudhā kalayā ca sā \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The great goddess also known as MiilaPrakriti is divided into five forms and thereafter in the process of creation, she appears in many forms.

## \|\| 40.68 \|\|

*yoṣitaḥ prakṛteraṃśāḥ pumāṃsaḥ puruṣasya ca \|*  
*māyayā sṛṣṭi kāle ca tadvinā na bhavedbhavaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Therefore all the ladies of the universe are born out of the ariisa of Prakriti and the men are born out of the amsa of Purusa because during the time of creation without Maya no creation is possible.

## \|\| 40.69 \|\|

*sṛṣṭiśca prativiśveṣu brahmanbrahmodbhavā sadā \|*  
*pātā viṣṇuśca saṃharttā śivaḥ śaśvacchivapradaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. О Brahma in every globe, Brahma happens to be the creator of the universe while Vishnu remains the preserver and Shiva always becomes the destroyer.

## \|\| 40.70 \|\|

*dattadattaṃ jñānamidaṃ rāma mahyaṃ ca puṣkare \|*  
*dīkṣākāle ca māghyāṃ ca munipravarasannidhau \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. О Parashurama, this knowledge was bestowed on me by DattStreya at Puskara on the full moon day of the month of Magha.

## \|\| 40.71 \|\|

*ityuktvā kārttavīryyaśca rāmaṃ natvā ca sasmitaḥ \|*  
*āruroha rathaṃ śīghraṃ gṛhītvā saśaraṃ dhanuḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Thus speaking to Parashurama, Karttivirya offered salutations to him and holding his bow and arrow, at once mounted the chariot.

## \|\| 40.72 \|\|

*rāmastato rājasainyaṃ brahmāstreṇa jaghāna ha \|*  
*nṛpaṃ pāśupatenaiva līlayā śrīhariṃ smaran \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. Soon thereafter, Parashurama with the use of Brahmastra destroyed the army of the king and reciting the name of Shrihari in his mind killed the king with the use of Pasupatastra.

## \|\| 40.73 \|\|

*evaṃ trissaptakṛtvaśca krameṇa ca vasundharām \|*  
*rāmaścakāra nirbhūpāṃ līlayā ca śivaṃ smaran \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. Thus Parashurama reciting the name of Shiva rid the earth of the Kshatriyas twenty one times quite easily.

## \|\| 40.74 \|\|

*garbhasthaṃ māturaṅkasthaṃ śiśuṃ vṛddhaṃ ca madhyamam \|*  
*jaghāna kṣatriyaṃ rāmaḥ pratijñāpālanāya vai \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. For the fulfilment of his vow he killed even the infants being carried by the mothers in their wombs, besides the old as well as the young Ksatriyas.

## \|\| 40.75 \|\|

*kārttavīryyaśca golokaṃ tvagamatkṛṣṇasannidhim \|*  
*jagāma toṣātparaśurāmaśca śrīhariṃ smaran \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. After his death Karttivirya reached Goloka and met lord Krishna there. Parashurama on the other hand continued reciting the name of Hari and left the place.

## \|\| 40.76 \|\|

*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ mahīṃ dṛṣṭvā maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*ramaṇaṃ tena paraśurāmaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca kātaram \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Maheshvara ridding the earth of Kshatriyas twenty one times and because of his holding the battle-axe on his shoulders, he came to be known by the name of Parashurama subsequently.

## \|\| 40.77-78 \|\|

*devāśca munayo devyaḥ siddhagandharvakinnarāḥ \|*  
*sarve cakruḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ rāmamūrdhni ca nārada \|\| 77 \|\|*

*svarge dundubhayo nedurharṣaśabdo babhūva ha \|*  
*yaśasā caiva paraśurāmasyāpūritaṃ jagat \|\| 78 \|\|*

77-78. О Narada, the gods, the sages, goddesses and siddhas, Gandharvas and Kinnaras, showered flowers on Parashurama. The musical instruments were played upon including the Dundubhls in the heaven, the gods felt immensely delighted. The glory of Parashurama was spread all over the universe.

## \|\| 40.79 \|\|

*brahmā bhṛguśca śukraśca vālmīkiścyavanastathā \|*  
*jamadagnirbrahmalokādājagāma praharṣitaḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Thereafter Brahma, Bhrigu, Sukra, Valmlki, Cyavana and Jamadagni delightfully went to Goloka.

## \|\| 40.80 \|\|

*pulakāñcitasarvāṅgāḥ sānandāśca samanvitāḥ \|*  
*dūrvāpuṣpakarāḥ sarve kurvanto maṃgalāśiṣaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. All the people were feeling emotional and tears were coming out of their eyes gleefully. They were offering blessings to him holding Durva-grass and the flowers in their hands, uttering the words of blessing and welfare.

## \|\| 40.81-82 \|\|

*praṇanāma ca tānrāmo daṇḍavatpatito bhuvi \|*  
*kroḍe cakāra brahmā''dau kramāttāteti saṃvadan \|\| 81 \|\|*

*tamuvācātha paraśurāmaṃ brahmā jagadguruḥ \|*  
*vedasāraṃ nītiyutaṃ pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|\| 82 \|\|*

81-82. Finding them there, Parashurama prostrated on the ground and also expressed his reverence for them. He was carried by Brahma in his lap and expressed his delight. Brahma the teacher of the Vedas then spoke to him the appropriate words which bestowed pleasure.

## \|\| 40.83-84 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*śṛṇu rāma pravakṣyāmi sarvasampatkaraṃ param \|*  
*kāṇvaśākhoktavacanaṃ satyaṃ vai sarvasammatam \|\| 83 \|\|*

*pūjyānāmeva sarveṣāmiṣṭaḥ pūjyatamaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*janako janmadānācca pālanācca pitā smṛtaḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

83-84. Brahma said – О Parashurama, I am going to speak to you the words which are quite appropriate, bestow all the fortunes, are the best, truthful and respected by all. You please listen to them. The family god happens to be the most adorable one of all the gods. Someone is termed as the father who happens to be the cause of one's birth and another is known as the father because he brings up a child.

## \|\| 40.85 \|\|

*garīyāñjanmadātuśca so'nnadātā pitā mune \|*  
*vinā'nnaṃ naśvaro deho nityaṃ ca piturudbhavaḥ \|\| 85\|\|*

85\. О sage, the father who brings up the child is considered to be better than the one who becomes the cause of the birth because without food the body perishes. It is not enough to be born from a father.

## \|\| 40.86 \|\|

*tayoḥ śataguṇaṃ mātā pūjyā mānyā ca vanditā \|*  
*garbhadhāraṇapoṣābhyāṃ saiva proktā garīyasī \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Out of these the mother happens to be a hundred times more adorable. Because of her holding the child in the womb and by bringing up the child, she is considered to be the best.

## \|\| 40.87 \|\|

*tebhyaḥ śataguṇaṃ pūjyo'bhīṣṭadevaḥ śrutau śrutaḥ \|*  
*jñānavidyāmantradātā'bhīṣṭadevātparo guruḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. Out of these the gods are a hundred times more adorable. This has been ordained by the Vedas. The teacher who imparts wisdom, knowledge and mantra is better than the family gods.

## \|\| 40.88-89 \|\|

*guruvadguruputraśca gurupatnī tato'dhikā \|*  
*deve ruṣṭe gurū rakṣedgurau ruṣṭe na kaścana \|\| 88 \|\|*

*gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*gurureva paraṃ brahma brāhmaṇebhyaḥ priyaḥ paraḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

88-89. The son of the teacher who happens to be like the teacher is also adorable and the wife of the teacher is much more adorable because when the gods get annoyed the teacher protects a person and when the teacher is annoyed no one can protect him. A Guru happens to be Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva besides being Parabrahman and is dearer than Brahmana.

## \|\| 40.90 \|\|

*gururjñānaṃ dadātyeva jñānaṃ ca haribhaktidam \|*  
*haribhaktipradātā yaḥ ko vā bandhustataḥ param \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. Guru imparts wisdom and knowledge, himself for the lord. The one who bestows the devotion of the lord, no other relative can equate him.

## \|\| 40.91-92 \|\|

*ajñānatimirācchanno jñānadīpaṃ yato labhet \|*  
*labdhvā ca nirmalaṃ paśyetko vā bandhustataḥ paraḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

*gurudattaṃ sumantraṃ ca japtvā jñānaṃ tato labhet \|*  
*sarvajñatvācca siddhiṃ ca ko vā bandhustato'dhikaḥ \|\| 92 \|\|*

91-92. A person who is engrossed in darkness, attains the light of wisdom from the teacher and ultimately attains wisdom and success in many fields. Therefore no one could be a relative better than the teacher.

## \|\| 40.93 \|\|

*sukhaṃ jayati sarvatra vidyayā gurudattayā \|*  
*yayā pūjyo'pi jagati ko vā bandhustato'dhikaḥ \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. The knowledge imparted by the teacher makes one to achieve success in various fields pleasantly. He is adored in the universe. The same knowledge, therefore, could be a better relative than the teacher.

## \|\| 40.94 \|\|

*vidyāndho vā dhanāndho vā yo mūḍho na yajedgurum \|*  
*brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. A foolish person getting intoxicated with knowledge and riches does not adore his teacher and he therefore earns the sin of Brahmahatya. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 40.95 \|\|

*daridraṃ patitaṃ kṣudraṃ narabudyā bhajedgurum \|*  
*tīrthasnāto'pi na śucirnādhikārī ca karmasu \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. The person who serves the Guru inspite of his being a pauper, the degraded one as an ordinary person, his taking bath at the sacred places cannot purify him nor can he become entitled to the performing of noble deeds.

## \|\| 40.96 \|\|

*abhīṣṭadevaḥ śrīkṛṣṇo guruste śaṅkaraḥ svayam \|*  
*śaraṇaṃ gaccha he putra devapūjyatamaṃ gurum \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Lord Krishna happens to be your family god and Shiva happens to be your teacher. Therefore, О son, you go to take refuge under your respectable teacher who is more adorable than other gods.

## \|\| 40.97 \|\|

*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpā tvayā pṛthvī kṛtā yataḥ \|*  
*prāptā tvayā harerbhaktistaṃ śivaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. By his grace you have been able to rid the earth of the Kshatriyas, twenty one times, because of whom you have achieved the devotion of the lord, you go and take refuge under the same lord Shiva.

## \|\| 40.98 \|\|

*śivāṃ ca śivarūpaṃ ca śivadaṃ śivakāraṇam \|*  
*śivārādhyaṃ śivaṃ śāntaṃ guruṃ tvaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. You go and take refuge under lord Shiva who happens to be the lord of Parvati, is quite blissful, bestower of bliss and the cause of welfare besides being adored by Parvati.

## \|\| 40.99 \|\|

*golokanātho bhagavānaṃśena śivarūpadhṛk \|*  
*ya iṣṭadevaḥ sa gurustameva śaraṇaṃ vraja \|\| 99 \|\|*

99\. Shri Krishna the lord of Goloka takes to the form of Shiva with his arhsa. The family god happens to be the teacher, therefore, you go and take refuge under him.

## \|\| 40.100 \|\|

*ātmā kṛṣṇaḥ śivo jñānaṃ mano'haṃ sarvajīviṣu \|*  
*prāṇā viṣṇossā prakṛtissarvaśaktiyutā suta \|\| 100 \|\|*

100\. О son, lord Krishna happens to be the soul of everyone; Shiva happens to be the store of knowledge; I myself am the mind; Vishnu is the life and Prakriti appears as the strength in everyone.

## \|\| 40.101-103 \|\|

*jñānadaṃ jñānarūpaṃ ca jñānabījaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*mṛtyuñjayaṃ kālakālaṃ taṃ guruṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja \|\| 101 \|\|*

*brahmajyotissvarūpaṃ taṃ bhaktānugrahavigraham \|*  
*śaraṇaṃ vraja sarvajñaṃ bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 102 \|\|*

*prakṛtirlakṣavarṣaṃ ca tapastaptvā yamīśvaram \|*  
*kāntaṃ priyapatiṃ lebhe taṃ guruṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja \|\| 103 \|\|*

101-103. He happens to be bestower of knowledge, the form of knowledge, the seed of knowledge, eternal, the one who has conquered death and is death for the death. You, therefore, take refuge under him. He takes to many forms inspite of his being the form of Brahmana, in order to shower his grace on his devotees. He is all knowledgeable, eternal and as such you go and take refuge under him. Prakriti (Parvati) achieved him in the form of husband after performing tapas for a lakh of years. You go and take refuge under the teacher like this.

## \|\| 40.104 \|\|

*ityuktvā munibhiḥ sārddhaṃ jagāma kamalodbhavaḥ \|*  
*rāmaśca gantuṃ kailāsaṃ manaścakre ca nārada \|\| 104 \|\|*

104\. О Narada, thus speaking Brahma accompanied by the sages and Parashurama resolved to proceed to Kailasa.

## Chapter 41

## \|\| 41.1-2 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*hareśca kavacaṃ dhṛtvā kṛtvā niḥ kṣatriyāṃ mahīm \|*  
*rāmo jagāma kailāsaṃ namaskartuṃ śivaṃ gurum \|\| 1 \|\|*

*gurupatnīṃ śivāmambāṃ draṣṭuṃ gurusutau ca tau \|*  
*guṇairnārāyaṇasamau kārttikeyagaṇeśvarau \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narayana said – Paras'urama wore the kavacha and rid the earth of Kshatriyas. Thereafter he went to Shiva, his teacher for offering salutations to him, besides Parvati the wife of his teacher. He went to Kailasa and also met Karttikeya and Ganesha, the sons of the teacher, who were like Narayana himself in virtues.

## \|\| 41.3-6 \|\|

*manoyāyī mahātmā sa bhṛgussaṃprāpya tatkṣaṇam \|*  
*dadarśa parvataṃ ramyamatīva sumanoharam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśairmaṇibhiḥ sumanoharaiḥ \|*  
*suvarṇabhūmisadṛśai rājamārgairvirājitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*sindūrāruṇavarṇaiśca veṣṭitaṃ maṇivedibhiḥ \|*  
*saṃyuktaṃ muktānikaraiḥ pūritaṃ maṇimaṇḍapaiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*yakṣāṇāmālayairdivyaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ śatakoṭibhiḥ \|*  
*kapāṭastambhasopānaiḥ śobhitairmaṇinirmitaiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

3-6. Parasurama the son of Bhrigu who could travel with the speed of thought, at once reached Kailasa and found the beautiful city which was shinning like transparent crystal gems and had the roads shining like gold. It was decorated with many mandapas studded with rubies, pearls and valuables gems. It had a hundred crores of divine houses of the Yaksas. Those houses had the doors of gems and the pillars and steps were also decorated with gems.

## \|\| 41.7-14 \|\|

*suvarṇakalaśairdivyai rājataiḥ śvetacāmaraiḥ \|*  
*ratnakāñcanapūrṇaiśca yakṣendragaṇaveṣṭitaiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣāḍhyairdīpitaiḥ sundarīgaṇaiḥ \|*  
*bālikābhirbālakaiśca citraputtalikākaraiḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*krīḍadbhiḥ sasmitaiḥ śaśvatsvacchandaṃ ca virājitaiḥ \|*  
*pārijātadrumagaṇaiḥ svarṇadītīranīrajaiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*ākīrṇaṃ puṣpajālaiśca puṣpitaiśca sugandhibhiḥ \|*  
*kalpavṛkṣāśritaiḥ siddhaiḥ kāmadhenupuraskṛtaiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*siddhavidyāsu nipuṇaiḥ puṇyavadbhirniṣevitam \|*  
*trilakṣayojanācchāyairvaṭavṛkṣairathākṣayaiḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*śatayojanavistīrṇaiḥ śataskandhasamanvitaiḥ \|*  
*asaṃkhyaśākhāni karairasaṃkhyaphalasaṃyutaiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*nānāpakṣigaṇākīrṇaiḥ sumanoharaśabditaiḥ \|*  
*kampitaiḥ śītavātena maṇḍitaṃ ca sugandhinā \|\| 13 \|\|*

*puṣpodyānasahasreṇa sarasāṃ ca śatena ca \|*  
*siddhendrālayalakṣaiśca maṇiratnavikārajaiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

7-14. It had the divine kalasas made of gold and silver beside the white fly-whisks. It had heaps of gem and gold. The Yaksas were adorned with heaps of ornaments studded with gems. The same was the condition with beautiful Yaksis. The children were carrying small dolls in their hands and played with them happily. There were trees of Parijata flowers on the bank of the divine river Ganga, bunches of fragrant flowers were found everywhere. The siddhas were resting under the shades of kalpavrksas. There were many of Kamadhenu cows and the people well-versed in divine knowledge. There was an eternal banyan tree having a height of three lakhs of yojanas and was spread in an area of a hundred yojanas. It had a hundred trunks, innumerable branches and was filled with many fruits. It created quite a pleasant sound and provided abode to innumerable birds. The fragrant cool breeze made the leaves to move. The city had a thousand orchards, a hundred streams and a lakh of abodes of siddhas which were built with gems and jewels.

## \|\| 41.15-16 \|\|

*rāmaśca dṛṣṭvā nagaramatisaṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*dadarśa purato ramyaṃ śrīyuktaṃ śaṃkarālayam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*suvarṇamūlyaśatakairmaṇibhiḥ svarṇavarṇakaiḥ \|*  
*khacitaṃ ratnasāraiśca racitaṃ viśvakarmaṇā \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. Witnessing the city of such a great devotee, the mind of Parashurama felt delighted; thereafter he found the abode of lord Shiva which was quite graceful and filled with all the riches. It was built by Vishvakarma using gold and gems.

## \|\| 41.17 \|\|

*tripañcayojanocchrāyaṃ caturyojanavistṛtam \|*  
*caturasraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ prākāraṃ sumanoharam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. It had the height of fifteen yojanas and the width of four yojanas besides being square and quite charming to look at, having been surrounded by beautiful boundary walls.

## \|\| 41.18 \|\|

*dvāraṃ ratnakapāṭena nānā citrānvitena ca \|*  
*maṇīndravedibhiryuktaṃ maṇistambhavirājitaiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. There were several types of paintings and the doors were made of gems. Its pillars and pedestals were decorated with jewels.

## \|\| 41.19-22 \|\|

*taddakṣiṇe vṛṣendraṃ ca vāme siṃhaṃ ca nārada \|*  
*nandīśvaraṃ mahākālaṃ piṅgalākṣaṃ bhayaṃkaram \|\| 19 \|\|*

*viśālākṣaṃ ca bāṇaṃ ca virūpākṣaṃ mahābalam \|*  
*vikaṭākṣaṃ bhāskarākṣaṃ raktākṣaṃ vikaṭodaram \|\| 20 \|\|*

*saṃhārabhairavaṃ kālabhairavaṃ ca bhayaṅkaram \|*  
*rurubhairavamīśābhaṃ mahābhairavameva ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇāṅgabhairavaṃ caiva krodhabhairavamulbaṇam \|*  
*kapālabhairavaṃ caiva rudrabhairavameva ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

19-22. О Narada, Nandi was lodged to Shiva's right. To the left were lodged the lion the lord of Nandi, Mahakala, the terrific Pingalaksa, Vishalaksa, Bana; then the valorous Virapaksa, Vikataksa, Bhaskaraksa then Raktaksa, Vikat odara, Samharabhairava, the terrific Kalabhairava, Rurubhairava, Isabha, Mahabhairava, Krishnangabhairava, Krodha-bhairava, Ulbana, Kapalabhairava and Rudrabhairava.

## \|\| 41.23-24 \|\|

*siddheṃdrādīnrudragaṇānvidyādharasuguhyakān \|*  
*bhūtānpretānpiśācāṃśca kūṣmāṇḍānbrahmarākṣasān \|\| 23 \|\|*

*vetālāndānavāṃścaiva yogīndrāṃśca jaṭādharān \|*  
*yakṣānkimpuruṣāṃścaiva kinnarāṃśca dadarśa ha \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. Thereafter Rudras like Siddhendra, the Rudras, Vidyadharas, Guhyakas, the goblins, the Pretas, Pisacas then Kiismandas, Brahmaraksas, Vetalas, Danavas then, the ones having matted locks of hair on the heads, the yogis, the Yaksas, Kiihpurusas and Kinnaras.

## \|\| 41.25 \|\|

*tāndṛṣṭvā nandikeśājñāṃ gṛhītvā bhṛgunandanaḥ \|*  
*tānsambhāṣyābhyantaraṃ ca jagāmānandasaṃplutaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After looking at them Parashurama the son of Bhrigu, getting permission from Nandikeshvara entered the premises delightfully talking to others.

## \|\| 41.26-29 \|\|

*ratnendrasārakhacitaṃ dadarśa śatamandiram \|*  
*amūlyaratnakalaśairjvaladbhiśca virājitam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*amūlyaratnaracitairmuktānirmmaladarpaṇaiḥ \|*  
*hīrasāravikāraiśca kapāṭaiśca virājitam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*gorocanābhirmaṇibhiryutaṃ stambhasahasrakaiḥ \|*  
*maṇisāravikāraiśca sopānaiḥ pariśobhitam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*dadarśābhyantaraṃ dvāraṃ nānācitraiśca citritam \|*  
*māṇikyamuktāgrathitairmālājālairvirājitam \|\| 29 \|\|*

26-29. He found several of the temples there which were studded with the essence of the gems and had innumerable vases made of gems there were several mirrors which were made of gems and jewels. The doors were also decorated with gems and jewels. Besides there were hundreds of pillars which were decorated with Gorocana and jewels, beside the steps which were similarly decorated. He then found the inner-gate which had beautiful paintings, painted on it, besides the strings of pearls and gems hanging on the same.

## \|\| 41.30 \|\|

*dadarśa kārttikeyaṃ ca vāme dakṣe gaṇeśvaram \|*  
*vīrabhadraṃ mahākāyaṃ śivatulyaparākramam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. He found Karttikeya seated to the left and the Nandikeshvara of gigantic size seated to the right besides Virabhadra who was as valorous as Shiva himself.

## \|\| 41.31 \|\|

*pradhānapārṣadagaṇānkṣetrapālāṃśca nārada \|*  
*ratnasiṃhāsanasthāṃśca ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitān \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. О Narada, he found many of the courtiers and ksetrapalas who were all seated on the gem-studded lion-thrones and were adored with gem-studded ornaments.

## \|\| 41.32 \|\|

*tānsambhāṣya bhṛguḥ śīghraṃ mahābalaparākramaḥ \|*  
*parśuhastassa paraśurāmo gantuṃ samudyataḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Holding the battle-axe in his hand, the immensely valorous Parashurama got ready to go to them for a talk.

## \|\| 41.33 \|\|

*gacchantaṃ taṃ gaṇeśaśca kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭhetyuvāca ha \|*  
*nidrito nidrayā yukto mahādevo'dhuneti ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Finding him entering the palace of Shiva, Ganesha said, "You stop for a while." Lord Shiva is sleeping at the moment.

## \|\| 41.34 \|\|

*īśvarājñāṃ gṛhītvā'hamatrāgatya kṣaṇāntare \|*  
*tvayā sārddhaṃ gamiṣyāmi bhrātastiṣṭhātra sāmpratam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O brother, I shall jut now go to him and get his permission in a moment. Thereafter, I shall accompany you to him."

## \|\| 41.35 \|\|

*gaṇeśavākyaṃ paraśurāmaśśrutvā mahābalaḥ \|*  
*bṛhaspatisamo vaktā pravaktumupacakrame \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. On hearing the words of Ganesha, Paras'urSma who was speaker a like Brihaspati started speaking to him.

## Chapter 42

## \|\| 42.1 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*yāsyāmyantaḥpuraṃ bhrātaḥ praṇāmaṃ kartumīśvaram \|*  
*praṇamya mātaraṃ bhaktyā yāsyāmi tvaritaṃ gṛham \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Parashurama said – О brother, with my mind filled with devotion, I am entering into the inner apartment to offer my salutation to mother PSrvati and lord Shiva. Thereafter, I shall return in no time.

## \|\| 42.2-8 \|\|

*trissaptakṛtvo nirbhūpāṃ kṛtvā pṛthvīṃ ca līlayā \|*  
*kārttavīryyaḥ sucandraśca hato yasya prasādataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*nānāvidyā yato labdhā nānāśastraṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*taṃ guruṃ jagatāṃ nāthaṃ draṣṭumicchāmi sāmpratam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ caiva bhaktānugrahavigraham \|*  
*satyaṃ satyasvarūpaṃ ca brahma jyotiḥ sanātanam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*svecchāmayaṃ dayāsindhuṃ dīnabandhuṃ munīśvaram \|*  
*ātmārāmaṃ pūrṇakāmaṃ vyaktāvyaktaṃ parātparam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*parāparāṇāṃ sraṣṭāraṃ puruhūtaṃ puraskṛtam \|*  
*purāṇaṃ paramātmānamīśānaṃ tvādimavyayam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*sarvamaṅgalamāṅgalyaṃ sarvamaṃgalakāraṇam \|*  
*sarvamagaladaṃ śāntaṃ sarvaiśvaryyapradaṃ varam \|\| 7 \|\|*

*āśutoṣaṃ prasannāsyaṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalam \|*  
*bhaktābhayapradaṃ bhaktavatsalaṃ samadarśanam \|\| 8 \|\|*

2-8. Because by their grace I rid the earth of the Ks atriyas twenty one times, besides killing the great kings like Kartavirya and Suchandra and from whom I achieved the divine knowledge and the inaccessible weapons, I want to have an audience with such a great lord of the universe, who happens to be my teacher as well. He is visible as well as invisible, besides being the one who takes to the definite form for the sake of his devotees, truthful, Brahmana, the eternal flame, ever-lasting, truthful, the one who moves at will, ocean of mercy, the one who provides welfare to the down-trodden, the great ascetic, the one who always roams about with the soul, the one who fulfils the wishes of all, who is visible as well as invisible, beyond everyone, who creates the universe, adored and eulogised by many, the form of Purana, the great soul, Tsana, the eternal form, indestructible, welfare of all the welfare, the one who bestows prosperity, the peaceful one, the one who provides with all the fortune, the best of all, the one who gets pleased quickly, the one having a smiling face, the one who loves the people who take refuge under him, the one who provides protection to his devotees, the lover of the devotees, the one who looks at all with a compassionate glance.

## \|\| 42.9 \|\|

*itthaṃ paraśurāmo'sthāduktvā gaṇapateḥ puraḥ \|*  
*vācā madhurayā tatra samuvāca gaṇeśvaraḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. After thus speaking Parashurama stood before Ganapati. Thereafter Ganapati the leader of the ganas started speaking to Parashurama in a sweet voice.

## \|\| 42.10 \|\|

*kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭha kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭha śṛṇu bhrātaridaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*rahaḥsthalasthito naiva draṣṭavyaḥ strīyutaḥ pumān \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Ganeshvara said – О brother, you kindly wait for a moment and listen to me. One should not look at a man and a woman when they are alone.

## \|\| 42.11 \|\|

*strīsaṃyuktaṃ ca puruṣaṃ yaḥ paśyati narādhamaḥ \|*  
*karoti rasabhaṅgaṃ vā kālasūtraṃ vrajeddhruvam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Because only a degraded person looks at the couple engaged in love-sports or disturbs their pleasure. The one who does so surely has to fall in the hell named MlasHtra.

## \|\| 42.12-14 \|\|

*tatra tiṣṭhati pāpīyānyāvacanadradivākarau \|*  
*viśeṣataśca pitaraṃ guruṃ bhūpatiṃ dvija \|\| 12 \|\|*

*rahaḥ suratasaṃsaktaṃ nahi paśyedvicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*kāmataḥ kopato vā'pi yaḥ paśyetsuratonmukham \|\| 13 \|\|*

*strīvicchedo bhavettasya dhruvaṃ saptasu janmasu \|*  
*śroṇīvakṣasthalaṃ vaktraṃ yaḥ paśyati parastriyaḥ \|*  
*kāmato'pi vimūḍhaśca so'ndho bhavati niścitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

12-14. O Brahmana such a sinner has to remain in hell as long as the sun and the moon last. Particularly the people with wisdom should not look at the father, the teacher, the king and the Brahmana engaged in conjugal pleasures in lonely places, because the one who is infatuated with passion or anger looks at the people engaged in conjugal pleasure, such a person has to face separation from his wife for seven births. The one who castes a voluptuous eye at others' wives, her breasts, the pelvic region or the face, such a great fool surely becomes blind. '

## \|\| 42.15 \|\|

*gaṇeśasya vacaḥ śrutvā prahasya bhṛgunandanaḥ \|*  
*tamuvāca mahākopānniṣṭhuraṃ vacanaṃ mune \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O sage, on hearing the words of Ganesha Parashurama got enraged. He then smiled and uttered harsh words in anger.

## \|\| 42.16 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*aho śrutaṃ kiṃ vacanamapūrvaṃ nītisaṃyutam \|*  
*idamevamaho naivaṃ śrutamīśvaravaktrataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Parashurama said -I have heard today the most appropriate and the unprecedented word because I had never heard such words from the mouth of the lord.

## \|\| 42.17 \|\|

*śrutaṃ śrutau vākyamidaṃ kāmināṃ ca vikāriṇām \|*  
*nirvikārasya ca śiśorna doṣaḥ kaścideva hi \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Because such punishment has been prescribed for the passionate persons or those with a deformed mind have not been heard of in the Vedas. This has been heard by me that the child having a spotless mind does not attract any sin. Therefore, О brother, I am entering the inner apartment. О child, what can you do. Don’t stop me from moving forward.

## \|\| 42.18-22 \|\|

*yāsyāmyantaḥpuraṃ bhrātastava kiṃ tiṣṭha bālaka \|*  
*yathādṛṣṭi kariṣyāmi matkāryyaṃ samayocitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*tavaiva tāto mātā cetyevaṃ naiva nirūpitam \|*  
*jagatāṃ pitarau tau ca pārvatīparameśvarau \|\| 19 \|\|*

*pārvatī strī pumāñchambhuriti kairna nirūpitaḥ \|*  
*sarvarūpaḥ śaṅkaraśca sarvārūpā ca pārvatī \|\| 20 \|\|*

*guṇātītasya kā krīḍā tadbhaṃgo vā kuto vibho \|*  
*krīḍā lajjā bhītibhaṃgo grāmyasyaiva na ceśituḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*standhayaṃ ca māṃ dṛṣṭvā pitrorllajjā kuto bhavet \|*  
*lajjāyāśca kuto lajjā lajjeśasya ca sā kutaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

18-22. I am reaching the place. I shall act according to the prevailing situation. They are not your parents alone, because those Parvati and Parameshvara happen to be the parents of the universe. It is never believed that Parvati is the woman and Shiva is the man. Shiva has the universal form and the same is the case with Parvati. О virtuous one, the one who is beyond virtues, how can he jndulge in the love-sport and how could the same be disturbed. The love-sport, shame and fear, are meant for ordinary people and not for the lord.

## \|\| 42.23-24 \|\|

*lajjā lajjāṃ kimāpnoti tāpaṃ kiṃ vā hutāśanaḥ \|*  
*śītaṃ śaityamaho bhrātarnidāgho dāhameva ca \|\| 23 \|\|*

*bhītirbhītimavāpnoti mṛtyormṛtyurbibheti kim \|*  
*kuto jvaro jvaraṃ hanti vyādhiṃ vyādhiśca jīryati \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. How can the parent keep shy by looking at an infant who feeds on the breasts. Will the lord of same, can attract the shame. Can the shame achieve the shame or can the fire achieve the warmth. О brother, can the cold get the cold, can the fire get the burning sensation, can the fear get the fear or can the death face the death? In fact can the fever destroy the fever; can the ailment destroy the ailments.

## \|\| 42.25 \|\|

*saṃharttā nāpi saṃharttuḥ kālaḥ kālādbibheti kim \|*  
*sraṣṭāraṃ sṛjate sraṣṭā pātā kiṃ pāti te mate \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Can the killer be afraid of the killer and can the god of death be afraid of death? Can the creator according the your opinion, create the creator and can the preserver preserve the preserver?

## \|\| 42.26-28 \|\|

*kṣutkṣudhaṃ samavāpnoti tṛṣṇā tṛṣṇāṃ prayāti kim \|*  
*nidrā nidrāṃ ca śobhāṃ śrīḥ śāntiḥ śāṃtiṃ ca te mate \|\| 26 \|\|*

*puṣṭiḥ puṣṭiṃ kimāpnoti tuṣṭistuṣṭiṃ kṣamā kṣamām \|*  
*ātmanaḥ paramātmā'sti śaktiḥ śaktyā bibheti kim \|\| 27 \|\|*

*kāmakrodhau lobhamohau svātmanaite na bādhitāḥ \|*  
*dayā na baddhā dayayā necchā baddhecchayā prabho \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. Can the hunger satisfy the hunger and the lust satisfy the lust? According to your opinion can the sleep get the sleep, the glory get the glory, the satisfaction get the satisfaction, can the pleasure get the pleasure and the mercy get the mercy and can the soul be afraid of the great soul or can the strength be afraid of the strength?

## \|\| 42.29 \|\|

*jñānabuddhyoḥ ko vikāro jarāṃ no bādhate jarā \|*  
*cintā na cintayā grastā cakṣuścakṣurna paśyati \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. О lord, the lust, the anger, the passion and the grief cannot be destroyed by themselves, the mercy cannot be connected with mercy nor the can the passion be linked with passion.

## \|\| 42.30 \|\|

*harṣo mudaṃ kiṃ prāpnoti śokaṃ śoko na bādhate \|*  
*kā vipattirvipatteśca sampattiḥ sampadaḥ kutaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Can the divine intelligence develop any blemish? The old age cannot be destroyed by the old age, the worries cannot be removed by worries; one eye cannot look at the other eye.

## \|\| 42.31 \|\|

*medhāyā dhāraṇā śaktiḥ smṛtervā smaraṇaṃ kutaḥ \|*  
*na dagdhaḥ svapratāpena vivasvāniti sammataḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Can the pleasure get pleased and cannot the grief destroy the grief, cannot the misfortune remove the misfortune and cannot the fortune attract fortune.

## \|\| 42.32 \|\|

*viparītamato bhrātastvayaivācarito'dhunā \|*  
*na śruto'yaṃ gurumukhānna dṛṣṭo na śrutau śrutaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The memory cannot remember the memory and the son can never be destroyed with his own deeds. This is believed by everyone.

## \|\| 42.33-34 \|\|

*ityuktvā cāpi paraśurāmaśśaśvatprahasya saḥ \|*  
*śīghraṃ gantuṃ manaścakraṃ tadguhābhyantaraṃ mudā \|\| 33 \|\|*

*tacca rāmavacaḥ śrutvā jitakrodho gaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*śuddhasattvasvarūpaśca prahasya tamuvāca ha \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. О brother, currently you have conducted yourself in opposite directions. I have heard this neither from the mouth of my teacher nor is it provided in the Vedas. Thus speaking Parashurama continued laughing and tried to forcibly enter the house.

## \|\| 42.35-36 \|\|

*gaṇapatiruvāca \|*  
*ajñānatimirācchanno jñānaṃ prāpnoti tadvidaḥ \|*  
*piturbhrāturmukhājjñānaṃ durlabhaṃ bhāgyavām̐llabhet \|\| 35 \|\|*

*śrutaṃ jñānaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ ca jñānināmapi durlabham \|*  
*kiñcinme tvaṃ manda buddheḥ śṛṇu bhrātarnivedanam \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. Listening to the words of Parashurama, Ganesha overcoming his anger and maintaining the peaceful form, kept on smiling and said, "A person who is infatuated with darkness and is devoid of the knowledge can achieve wisdom from the one who possesses the same but the inaccessible knowledge comes only from the mouth of the father and the brother.

## \|\| 42.37 \|\|

*yo nirguṇaḥ sa nirliptaḥ śaktibhirna hi saṃyutaḥ \|*  
*sisṛkṣurāśritaśśaktyā nirguṇaḥ saguṇo bhavet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. О brother, I have heard about the inaccessible knowledge which is meant for the intellectuals only. Therefore you kindly listen to a few words of an unwise person like me.

## \|\| 42.38 \|\|

*yāvanti ca śarīrāṇi bhogārhāṇi mahāmune \|*  
*prākṛtāni ca sarvāṇi vinā śrīkṛṣṇavigraham \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He who is Nirguna, does not involve himself and does not remain with shakti. The creator has to remain dependent on shakti. Therefore sometimes, the formless appears in a definite form.

## \|\| 42.39 \|\|

*dhyāyanti yoginastaṃ ca śuddhajyotissvarūpiṇam \|*  
*hastapādādirahitaṃ nirguṇaṃ prakṛteḥ param \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. О great sage, barring the body of lord Krishna all other bodies have to enjoy all the pleasures or displeasures of life and that is quite natural.

## \|\| 42.40 \|\|

*vaiṣṇavāste namasyanti bhaktānugrahakārakam \|*  
*kuto babhūva tajjyotiraho tejasvino vinā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Because of that the yogis meditate upon the formless form of the lord which is spotless, illumining without hand and feet and is beyond Prakriti.

## \|\| 42.41 \|\|

*jyotirabhyantare nityaṃ śarīraṃ śyāmasundaram \|*  
*dvibhujaṃ muralīhastaṃ sasmitaṃ pītavāsasam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The one who bestows his grace on his devotees is called Vishnu and people adore him, because there could be no flame without the tejas.

## \|\| 42.42-43 \|\|

*atīvāmūlyasadratnabhūṣaṇaiśca vibhūṣitam \|*  
*jyotirabhyantare mūrtiṃ paśyanti kṛpayā vibhoḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde gaṇeśaparaśurāmasaṃvādo nāma dvicatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. The beautiful dark-complexioned body always remains inside the flame which is always beautiful and eternal. He has two arms and holds a flute in his hands, wears a serene smile on his face and pltambara as the lower garment and is adorned with valuable gem-studded ornaments. The yogis conceive him as all-pervading and visualise this form of the lord in the eternal flame.

## \|\| 42.44 \|\|

*yadā sṛṣṭyunmukhaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sasṛje prakṛtiṃ mudā \|*  
*tadyonau hyarpitaṃ vīryaṃ vīryyāḍḍimbho babhūva ha \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. They engage themselves with the grace of the lord for his slavehood. All the yogic practices and the tapas do not equate to one-sixteenth part to the slavehood of the lord.

## \|\| 42.45-46 \|\|

*divyena lakṣavarṣeṇa garbhāḍḍimbho vinirgataḥ \|*  
*tadā babhūva niśśvāsastato vāyurbabhūva ha \|\| 45 \|\|*

*niśśvāsena samaṃ bhrātarmukhabindurvinirgataḥ \|*  
*tato babhūva sahasā jalarāśirhareḥ puraḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Whenever lord Krishna desired for creation, he delightfully created Prakriti and implanted the semen in her womb. The semen created an egg which remained in the womb of Prakriti for a lakh of years and ultimately it came out. Then, there was a deep sigh and the wind was created.

## \|\| 42.47 \|\|

*tajjale ca sthito ḍimbho divyavarṣāṇi lakṣakam \|*  
*tato babhūva sahasā viśvādhāro mahāvirāṭ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. О brother, with the deep sigh a drop emerged from the mouth as a result of which the water appeared in the very presence of the lord.

## \|\| 42.48 \|\|

*yāvanti gātralomāni tasya santi mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*brahmāṇḍāni ca tāvanti vidyamānāni niścitam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The egg was consigned into the water for a lakh of years and Mahavirat suddenly emerged out of the same becoming the base of the universe.

## \|\| 42.49 \|\|

*tatraiva pratividhyaṇḍe brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|*  
*devāśca munayaścaiva vidyamānāścarācarāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. All the hair-pits on the body of Virat and a globe remain enshrined in each one of his hair-pits.

## \|\| 42.50-54 \|\|

*mahāvirāḍāśrayaśca sarvasya ca janasya ca \|*  
*niśśvāsavāyurbhagavānbabhūva śrīharermune \|\| 50 \|\|*

*mahāviṣṇuśca kalayā tataḥ kṣudravirāḍabhūt \|*  
*tannābhikamale brahmā śaṃkarastallāṭajaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

*viṣṇustadaṃśaḥ pātā yaḥ śvetadvīpanivāsakṛt \|*  
*evaṃ te pratividhyaṇḍe brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

*svayaṃ ca svāṃśakalayā nānāmūrttidharo hariḥ \|*  
*tadā'bhavacca saguṇaḥ sarvaśaktiyutastadā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*kathaṃ lajjādirahitaḥ sa ca svacchāmayo mahān \|*  
*sarvadā sarvabhogārhaḥ sarvaśaktisamanvitaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

50-54. In every globe, Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva besides the gods, the sages and moveable and immovable creatures always exist. Mahavirat happens to be the refuge of everyone. О sage, with the inhaling of air, the wind was turned into the god and by one of the rays Maha-Vishnu was created. From him the small Virat (Vishnu) emerged from whose navel Brahma emerged and from Brahma’s forehead emerged Shiva. Vishnu was born out of the amsa of Rama who dwells in the Shveta-dvipa and is the protector of the universe. Therefore, in each and every globe Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva always reside. The lord himself appeared in various forms because of his own rays when he turns himself into a definite shape. Thereafter, he was called omnipotent, possessing all the virtues with definite forms because he moves at will being a great lord and how could he be freed from the same? He is all-powerful and therefore he can indulge in all the worldly pleasures.

## \|\| 42.55-56 \|\|

*lajjā nāstyeva lajjāyā mato'yaṃ sarvasammataḥ \|*  
*yā ca lajjāvatī devī tasya lajjā kuto gatā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*sarvaśaktimatī durgā prakṛtyā sā ca śailajā \|*  
*tasyā lajjādayaḥ santi sarvadā sarvasammatāḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

55-56. Though one does not feel shy because of the same, it is well-known that the goddess who is full of shyness disappears. Goddess Durga is all-powerful but currently she is born out of Himalaya. She was the form of Prakriti and as such the shyness is always enshrined in her. It is quite well-known.

## \|\| 42.57-58 \|\|

*pañcadhā prakṛtiryā ca śrīkṛṣṇasya babhūva ha \|*  
*rādhā padmā ca sāvitrī durgā devī sarasvatī \|\| 57 \|\|*

*prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*prāṇādhikā priyā sā ca rādhā''ste tasya vakṣasi \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. The Prakriti of lord Krishna is known in five forms as Radha, Padma, Savitri, Durga and Sarasvati. Out of these five, Radha happens to be the dearest beloved of lord Krishna who always resides in his heart.

## \|\| 42.59-60 \|\|

*vidyādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā sāvitrī brahmaṇaḥ priyā \|*  
*lakṣmīrnārāyaṇasyaiva sarvasampatsvarūpiṇī \|\| 59 \|\|*

*sarasvatī dvidhā bhūtvā kṛṣṇasya mukha nirgatā \|*  
*sāvitrī brahmaṇaḥ kāntā svayaṃ nārāyaṇasya ca \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. The great goddess of learning known as Savitri became the spouse of Brahma and Lakshmi the goddess of all the riches became the spouse of Narayana. Sarasvati after emerging out of the mouth of lord Krishna was divided into two forms, one of them was known as Savitri and became the spouse of Brahma while Sarasvati became the beloved of Narayana.

## \|\| 42.61 \|\|

*buddhyadhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā jñānasūḥ śaktisaṃyutā \|*  
*sā durgā śūlinaḥ kāntā tasyā lajjā kuto gatā \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The goddess of wisdom and the strength was known as Durga who became the spouse of Shiva. Therefore, where shall the shyness of these goddesses disappear.

## \|\| 42.62 \|\|

*prakṛtiḥ pañcadhā bhrātargoloke ca babhūva ha \|*  
*imāḥ pradhānāḥ kalayā babhūvurnaikadhā yataḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. О brother, Prakriti took to these five forms in Goloka itself and they are well-known in the universe and they appeared again and again.

## \|\| 42.63 \|\|

*viprendra nityaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ brahmāṇḍātparamucyate \|*  
*avināśi sthalaṃ śaśvallaye prākṛtike dhruvam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. О best of the Brahmanas, the eternal Vaikunt ha is the best place in the globe, it remains intact even during the time of dissolution.

## \|\| 42.64-65 \|\|

*tatra nārāyaṇo devaḥ kṛṣṇārddhāṃśaścaturbhujaḥ \|*  
*vanamālī pītavāsāḥ śaktyā vai padmayā saha \|\| 64 \|\|*

*svayaṃ kṛṣṇaśca goloke dvibhujaḥ śyāmasundaraḥ \|*  
*sasmito muralīhasto rādhāvakṣaḥsthalasthitaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

64-65. Vishnu resides in Vaikuntha as a part of lord Krishna having four arms wearing a garland of forest flowers and a yellow lower garment. The dark-complexioned lord Krishna having two arms remains in Goloka and plays on the flute wearing a serene smile on his face, living in the heart of Radha.

## \|\| 42.66-67 \|\|

*śaśvadgogopagopībhiḥ saṃyukto goparūpadhṛk \|*  
*paripūrṇatamaḥ śrīmānnirguṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*svecchāmayaḥ svatantrastu paramānandarūpadhṛk \|*  
*surāḥ kalodbhavā yasya ṣoḍaśāṃśo mahāvirāṭ \|\| 67 \|\|*

66-67. He is always surrounded by cowherds and cowherdesses appearing in the form of a cowherd. He is complete in all respects, full of fortune, formless, beyond Prakriti, moves at will, independent and blissful. All the gods appear from his rays and Mahavirat happens to be the one-sixteenth part of his rays.

## \|\| 42.68 \|\|

*yato bhavanti viśvāni sthūlasūkṣmādikāni ca \|*  
*punastatra pralīyanta evameva muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The stout and the small are all born from him and they ultimately merge in him. This process is repeated again and again.

## \|\| 42.69 \|\|
