*goloka ūrdhvavaikuṇṭhātpañcāśatkoṭiyojanaḥ \|*  
*nāsti lokastadūrdhvaṃ ca nāsti kṛṣṇātparaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The Goloka is located at a distance of five crores yojana above Vaikuntha and above that, there is no other loka. There is no other lord greater than lord Krishna.

## \|\| 42.70 \|\|

*idaṃ śrutaṃ śambhuvaktrānmayā te kathitaṃ dvija \|*  
*kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭhādhunā bhrātarīśvaraḥ suratonmukhaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. O Brahmana I had heard the whole of it from the mouth of lord Shiva which I have told you about. Therefore, О brother, you please wait here for a moment because both Shiva and Parvati are currently engaged in conjugal pleasures.

## Chapter 43

## \|\| 43.1-2 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*gaṇeśavacanaṃ śrutvā sa tadā rāgataḥ sudhīḥ \|*  
*parśuhastassa vai rāmo nirbhayo gantumudyataḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*gaṇeśvarastadā dṛṣṭvā śīghramutthāya yatnataḥ \|*  
*vārayāmāsa sa prītyā cakāra vinayaṃ punaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

1-2. Narayana said – On hearing the words of Ganesha, the wise Parashurama holding the battle-axe in his hand tried to force his entry into the house. Finding Parashurama so behaving, Ganesha got up at once and making great effort tried to stop Parashurama, making several requests.

## \|\| 43.3-5 \|\|

*rāmastaṃ prerayāmāsa huṃ kṛtvā tu punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*babhūva ca tatastatra vāgyuddhaṃ hastakarṣaṇaiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*parśunikṣepaṇaṃ kartuṃ manaścakre bhṛgustadā \|*  
*hāhā kṛtvā kārtikeyo bodhayāmāsa saṃsadi \|\| 4 \|\|*

*avyarthamastraṃ he bhrātarguruputre kathaṃ kṣipeḥ \|*  
*guruvadguruputraṃ ca mā bhavānhantumarhati \|\| 5\|\|*

3-5. But Parashurama challenged him again and again, as a result of which a controversy developed besides the scuffle between the two. Bhrigu at that point of time intended to attack Ganesha with battle-axe which created panic in the court. Thereafter Karttikeya said, "O brother, why are you attacking with this infallible weapon on the son of the teacher. The son of the teacher cannot be killed like the teacher himself.

## \|\| 43.6-7 \|\|

*parśuṃ kṣipantaṃ kupitaṃ raktapadmadalekṣaṇam \|*  
*gaṇeśo rodhayāmāsa nivarttasvetyuvāca tam \|\| 6 \|\|*

*punargaṇeśaṃ rāmaśca prerayāmāsa kopataḥ \|*  
*papāta purato vegānmānahīno gajānanaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. Wielding the battle-axe Parashurama was enraged and his eyes became red like the red-lotus. Inspite of that, Ganesha stopped him and said, ;,You better return." But Parashurama getting enraged challenged Ganesha again. Finding thus humiliated Brahmana rushed forward and stood before Parashurama.

## \|\| 43.8-9 \|\|

*gajānanaḥ samutthāya dharmaṃ kṛtvā tu sakṣiṇam \|*  
*punastaṃ bodhayāmāsa jitakrodhaḥ śivātmajaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*nivartasva nivarttasvetyuccāryya ca punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*praveśane te kā śaktirīśvarājñāṃ vinā prabho \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. Ganesha became attentive citing dharma as the witness. Ganesha who had over-powered his anger, again tried to convince Parashurama again and again and said, "O lord you go back." Without the permission of the lord you don’t have the strength to enter the inner apartment.

## \|\| 43.10 \|\|

*mama bhrātā tvamatithirvidyāsambandhato dhruvam \|*  
*īśvarapriyaśiṣyaśca soḍhaṃ vai tena hetunā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. You are my brother in relation to the bestowing of knowledge to you Shiva. You are a guest and are the dear pupil of the lord. Therefore, I am tolerating all your misbehaviour.

## \|\| 43.11 \|\|

*na hyahaṃ kārttavīryyaśca bhūpāste kṣudrajantavaḥ \|*  
*ato vipra na jānāsi māṃ ca viśveśvarātmajam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. О Brahmana, I am neither Kartavirya nor any petty king, killed by you in the battle-field. Evidently you are not aware of the birth of the son of the lord.

## \|\| 43.12 \|\|

*kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭha nivarttasva samare brāhmaṇātithe \|*  
*kṣaṇāntare tvayā sārddhaṃ yāsyāmīśvarasannidhim \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О Brahmana you are a guest, you wait for a while; go back and I shall accompany you to Shiva in a moment.

## \|\| 43.13 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*herambavacanaṃ śrutvā prajahāsa punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*parśuṃ kṣeptuṃ manaścakre praṇamya hariśaṅkarau \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Narayana said – On hearing the words of Ganesha, Bhrigu laughed at him again and again and bowing before Hari and Shiva in his mind he resolved to make use of the battle-axe.

## \|\| 43.14-17 \|\|

*parśuṃ kṣipantaṃ kopena taṃ ca rāmaṃ gajānanaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā mumūrṣuṃ deveśo dharmaṃ kṛtvā tu sākṣiṇam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*yogena varddhayāmāsa śuṇḍāṃ tāṃ koṭiyojanām \|*  
*yogīndrastatra santiṣṭhanbhrāmayitvā punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*śatadhā veṣṭayitvā tu bhrāmayitvā tu tatra vai \|*  
*ūrddhvamuttolya vegena kṣudrāhiṃ garuḍo yathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

*saptadvīpāṃśca śailāṃśca meruṃ cākhilasāgarān \|*  
*kṣaṇena darśayāmāsa rāmaṃ yogaparāhatam \|\| 17 \|\|*

14-17. Ganesha realised that Paras'urama intends to attack him with battle-axe. Therefore citing dharma as his witness, he extended his trunk to crores of yojanas in length and encircling Parashurama, he lifted him up like Garuda lifting a serpent. Thus entwining Parashurama in his trunk, he removed Parashurama around all the seven continents, the mountains, Meru and all the oceans in a moment.

## \|\| 43.18 \|\|

*hastapādādyanāthaṃ taṃ jaḍaṃ sarvāṃgakampitam \|*  
*punastaṃ bhrāmayāmāsa darpitaṃ darpanāśanaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thereafter, Ganesha who destroys the arrogance, making Parashurama helpless in feet as well as arm, all his limbs started trembling and Ganesha again took him round and round the globe.

## \|\| 43.19-21 \|\|

*bhūrlokaṃ ca bhuvarlokaṃ svarlokaṃ ca sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*janolokaṃ tapolokaṃ dhruvalokaṃ ca tatparam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*punastatra bhrāmayitvā brahamāṇḍāṭūdhvamuttamam \|*  
*satyalokaṃ brahmalokaṃ śruvalokaṃ ca tatparam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*gaurīlokaṃ śambhulokaṃ darśayāmāsa nārada \|*  
*darśayitvā tu vidhyaṇḍaṃ sa papau sapta sāgarān \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. Thereafter Ganesha carried Paras'urama round the earth, Bhuloka, Bhuvarloka, Svarloka, Janoloka and Tapoloka, playfully. After taking him round these places, then he carried him to Satyaloka, Brahmaloka, Dhruvaloka, Gauriloka and Shivaloka. Thereafter showing him round the globe he took him round all the oceans.

## \|\| 43.22 \|\|

*punarudgiraṇaṃ cakre sanakramakarodakam \|*  
*tatra taṃ pātayāmāsa gambhīre sāgarodake \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He then threw out all the aquatic animals like crocodiles and others and threw him in the oceanic water.

## \|\| 43.23-24 \|\|

*mumūrṣuṃ taṃ santarantaṃ punarjagrāha līlayā \|*  
*punastatra bhrāmayitvā brahmāṇḍādūrddhvamapyamum \|\| 23 \|\|*

*vaikuṇṭhaṃ darśayāmāsa salakṣmīkaṃ caturbhujam \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. Parasurama started floating in the oceanic water, where he was about to die but he again picked him up from there and moving him round and round, the globe, he lifted him up to Vaikunt ha and produced him before lord Vishnu and Lakshmi.

## \|\| 43.25-28 \|\|

*kṣaṇaṃ tatra bhrāmayitvā yogīndro yogamāyayā \|*  
*punaḥ karaṃ ca yogena varddhayāmāsa līlayā \|\| 25 \|\|*

*golokaṃ darśayāmāsa virajāṃ ca nadīśvarīm \|*  
*vṛndāvanaṃ śṛṅgaśataṃ śailendraṃ rāsamaṇḍalam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*gopīgopādibhiḥ sārddhaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ śyāmasundaram \|*  
*dvibhujaṃ muralīhastaṃ sasmitaṃ sumanoharam \|\| 27 \|\|*

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|*  
*tejasā koṭisūryyābhaṃ rādhāvakṣaḥsthalasthitam \|\| 28 \|\|*

25-28. Thus Ganesha, the lord of the yogis with the application of illusion extending his trunk carried him to Goloka, where the river Viraja was flowing. He then carried him through Vrindavana, Himalaya having a hundred peaks, Rasamandala and produced him before lord Krishna surrounded by the cowherds and cowherdesses. He was having two arms and holding a flute in his hands, wearing a serene smile on the face. He was seated on a gem-studded lion-throne and was adorned with all the ornaments, bearing the lustre of crores of suns, resting his head on the breasts of Radha.

## \|\| 43.29-30 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ darśayitvā praṇamayya punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*kṣaṇena lambamānaṃ ca bhrāmayitvā punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇaṃ ceṣṭadevaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam \|*  
*bhrūṇahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ bhṛgordūraṃ cakāra ha \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Having an audience with lord Krishna he bowed in reverence to him again and again and then he extended his trunk. Taking Paras'urama round and round he produced him before lord Krishna, as a result of which Parashurama was relieved of all the sins committed by him, because an audience with the lord destroys all the sins.

## \|\| 43.31-32 \|\|

*na bhavedyātanā naṣṭā vinā bhogena pāpajā \|*  
*svalpāṃ ca bubhuje rāmo gatā'nyā kṛṣṇadarśanāt \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kṣaṇena cetanāṃ prāpya bhuvi vegātpapāta ha \|*  
*babhūva dūrībhūtaṃ ca gaṇeśastambhanaṃ bhṛgoḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. The result that emerged from sin can never be destroyed without facing the same. Parashurama had already faced the result of some of his misdeeds and the result of the remaining sins vanished with the audience with lord Krishna. He, therefore, regained consciousness in a moment and at once he reached the earth and was relieved of the miseries caused to him by Ganesha.

## \|\| 43.33 \|\|

*sasmāra kavacaṃ stotraṃ gurudattaṃ sudurlabham \|*  
*abhīṣṭadevaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ guruṃ śambhuṃ jagadgurum \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Thereafter, Parashurama adored the inaccessible kavacha bestowed to him by his teacher, besides the stotra of lord Krishna reciting the name of lord Shiva who happens to be the teacher of the universe.

## \|\| 43.34 \|\|

*cikṣepa parśumavyarthaṃ śivatulyaṃ ca tejasā \|*  
*grīṣmamadhyāhnamārtaṇḍaprabhāśataguṇaṃ mune \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. О sage, thereafter he thought of using the infallible battle-axe which was like Shiva and dazzling like the shine of the mid-day sun of the summer season.

## \|\| 43.35 \|\|

*pituravyarthamastraṃ ca dṛṣṭvā gaṇapatiḥ svayam \|*  
*jagrāha vāmadantena nāstraṃ vyarthaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Ganapati on his part witnessed the weapon which was granted by Shiva himself to Parashurama and face the attack on the left tusks. He did not make it infractuous.

## \|\| 43.36 \|\|

*nipātya parśurvegena cchittvā dantaṃ samūlakam \|*  
*jagāma rāmahastaṃ ca mahādevabalena ca \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The battle-axe shot by Paras'urama severed the left tusk of Ganapati with the force of Mahadeva and again went back to Parasurama.

## \|\| 43.37 \|\|

*hāheti śabdamākāśe devāścakrurmahābhiyā \|*  
*vīrabhadraḥ kārttikeyaḥ kṣetrapālāśca pārṣadāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Finding this the gods in the sky, Virabhadra, Karttikeya and other attendants of Shiva beside Ksetra-palas felt panicky.

## \|\| 43.38 \|\|

*papāta bhūmau dantaśca saraktaḥ śabdayaṃstadā \|*  
*papāta gairikāyukto yathā sphaṭikaparvataḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Like the mountain of crystal of red ochre, the tusk soaked in blood making a thundering sound fell on the ground.

## \|\| 43.39 \|\|

*śabdena mahatā vipra cakampe pṛthivī bhiyā \|*  
*kailāsasthā janāḥ sarve mūrcchāmāpuḥ kṣaṇaṃ bhiyā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. О dear one the deafening sound created by its fall made the earth to shake and the dwellers of Kailasa fainted with panic in a moment.

## \|\| 43.40 \|\|

*nidrā babhañja tatkāle nidreśasya jagatprabhoḥ \|*  
*ājagāma bahiḥ śambhuḥ pārvatyā saha sambhramāt \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Thereafter Shiva, the lord of the universe woke up with Parvati and came out of the chamber.

## \|\| 43.41 \|\|

*puro dadarśa herambaṃ lohitāsyaṃ kṣatena tam \|*  
*bhagnadantaṃ jitakrodhaṃ sasmitaṃ lajjitaṃ mune \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. О sage, they saw Ganesha with a broken tusk, he had already over-come the anger and was smiling with shyness.

## \|\| 43.42-43 \|\|

*papraccha pārvatī śīghraṃ skandaṃ kimiti putraka \|*  
*sa ca tāṃ kathayāmāsa vārtāṃ paurvāparīṃ bhiyā \|\| 42 \|\|*

*cukopa durgā kṛpayā ruroda ca muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*uvāca śambhoḥ purataḥ putraṃ kṛtvā svavakṣasi \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. Parvati at once asked Skanda, "O son what was happened?" He narrated the entire story to Parvati. At that point of time Durga was enraged and she started crying again and again feeling merciful carrying her son in her lap.

## \|\| 43.44 \|\|

*sambodhya śambhuṃ śokena bhiyā vinayapūrvakam \|*  
*uvāca praṇatā sādhvī praṇatārtiharaṃ patim \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Because of grief and fear she addressed lord Shiva. The chaste lady meekly spoke to her husband who always removed the miseries of his devotees.

## Chapter 44

## \|\| 44.1-4 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*sarve jānanti jagati durgāṃ śaṅkarakiṃkarīm \|*  
*apekṣā rahitā dāsī tasyā vai jīvanaṃ vṛthā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*īśvarasya samāḥ sarvāstṛṇaparvatajātayaḥ \|*  
*dāsīputrasya śiṣyasya doṣaḥ kasyeti ca prabho \|\| 2 \|\|*

*vicāraṃ kartumucitaṃ tvaṃ ca dharmavidāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*vīrabhadraḥ kārttikeyaḥ pārṣadāḥ santi sākṣiṇaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*sākṣye mithyāṃ ko vadedvā dvāveṣāṃ bhrātarau samau \|*  
*sākṣye same śatrumitre satāṃ dharmanirūpaṇe \|\| 4 \|\|*

1-4. Parvati said – Everyone in the universe knows that Durga happens to be the slave of Shiva but when the slave has no importance in the household of the lord, the same becomes of no consequence. О lord, in the abode of Shiva all the creatures from a straw to the mountain are treated equally. Then who is at fault? My son or your devotee? You are the best in those well-versed in the dharma; therefore, you kindly think over it. Virabhadra, Karttikeya and all other courtiers are witnesses to the event, though both Ganesha and Karttikeya are brothers but who can speak falsehood at the time of giving witness, because at the time of giving evidence according to dharma both friends and foes are considered to be equal.

## \|\| 44.5-6 \|\|

*sākṣī sabhāyāṃ yatsākṣyaṃ jānannapyanyathā vadet \|*  
*kāmataḥ krodhato vā'pi lobhena ca bhayena ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

*sa yāti kumbhīpākaṃ ca nipātya śatapūruṣam \|*  
*taiśca sārddhaṃ vasettatra yāvaccandradivākaram \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. In the court, if a witness knowingly, influenced by passion, anger and fear, gives false evidence, he is driven to the kumbhipaka hell with his hundred generations and has to remain there till the life of the sun and moon lasts.

## \|\| 44.7 \|\|

*ahaṃ vibodhituṃ śaktā yannirṇetrī dvayorapi \|*  
*tathā'pi tava sākṣāttu mamājñā ninditā śrutau \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Though I am unable to take a decision on both of them, still it will not be proper for me to do so when you are there, because in these circumstances any opinion of mine will be deplorable in the Vedas.

## \|\| 44.8 \|\|

*kiṃkarāṇāṃ prabhā tatra nṛpe vasati saṃsadi \|*  
*udite bhāskare pṛthvyāṃ khadyoto hi yathā prabho \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O lord, when the king is present in the court, all the courtiers appear like fire-flies before the sun.

## \|\| 44.9-11 \|\|

*suciraṃ tapasā prāptaṃ tvadīyaṃ caraṇāmbujam \|*  
*parityāgabhayenaiva santataṃ bhītayā mayā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*yatkiṃcitkopaśokābhyāmuktaṃ mohena tatparam \|*  
*tatkṣamasva jagannātha putrasnehācca dāruṇāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

*tvayā yadi parityaktā tadā putreṇa tena kim \|*  
*sādhvyāḥ sadvaṃśajāyāśca śataputrādhikaḥ patiḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

9-11. I have achieved the grace of your feet after performing tapas for a long time. I am, therefore, always apprehensive, lest I might be disowned. Therefore, О lord of the universe, whatever I have spoken in anger or out of affection of my son, you kindly forgive me for the same. Because in case you disown me, then what shall I do with the sons. Because for the chaste lady the husband is more important than hundreds of sons.

## \|\| 44.12 \|\|

*asadvaṃśaprasūtā yā duśśīlā jñānavarjitā \|*  
*svāminaṃ manyate nāsau pitrordoṣeṇa kutsitā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The ladies born in the low caste or those who are wicked and unwise, do not show due respect to the husband because of the defect in their approach because of their parents.

## \|\| 44.13-14 \|\|

*kutsitaṃ patitaṃ mūḍhaṃ daridraṃ rogiṇaṃ jaḍam \|*  
*kulajā viṣṇutulyaṃ ca kāntaṃ paśyati santatam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*hutāśano vā sūryyo vā sarvatejasvināṃ varaḥ \|*  
*pativratātejasaśca kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. The denounce, the fallen, foolish, poor, those suffering from ailment or the deaf and dumb husband who is always considered by a chaste lady like Vishnu. Therefore, fire and even the sun cannot compare with the one-sixteenth ray of the chaste lady.

## \|\| 44.15 \|\|

*mahādānāni puṇyāni vratānyanaśanāni ca \|*  
*tapāṃsi patisevāyāḥ kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. All the great charities, merits, vratas and fasting or the performing of tapas cannot be compared with the sixteenth part of the lady serving her husband with devotion.

## \|\| 44.16 \|\|

*putrovā'pi pitā vā'pi bāndhavo'tha sahodaraḥ \|*  
*yoṣitāṃ kulajātānāṃ na kaścitsvāminaḥ samaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. For the chaste lady, the son, father, brother and real brother cannot be equated with the husband.

## \|\| 44.17 \|\|

*ityuktvā svāminaṃ durgā dadarśa purato bhṛgum \|*  
*śambhoḥ padābjaṃ sevantaṃ nirbhayaṃ tamuvāca ha \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Thus speaking to the lord, Durga looked at Bhrigu who was serving at the lotus-like feet of lord Shiva and was fearless. She said to him.

## \|\| 44.18 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*aho rāma mahābhāga brahmavaṃśyo'si paṇḍitaḥ \|*  
*putro'si jamadagneśca śiṣyo vai yogināṃ guroḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Parvati said – О lord Paras'urama, you are born in a В rah man a family and are quite well-read. You are the son of Jamadagni and are the pupil of the teacher of the yogis.

## \|\| 44.19 \|\|

*mātā te reṇukā sādhvī padmāṃśā satkulodbhavā \|*  
*mātāmaho vaiṣṇavaśca mātulaśca tato'dhikaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Renuka happens to be your mother who was quite a chaste lady born out of the arhsa of Kamala (Lakshmi), your maternal grandfather was a great Vaishnava and your maternal uncle happens to be superior to him in devotion.

## \|\| 44.20 \|\|

*tvaṃ ca reṇukabhūpasya manuvaṃśodbhavasya ca \|*  
*dauhitro mātulaḥ sādhuḥ śūro viṣṇupadāśrayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. You are the son of the daughter of Renuka of the Manu family, your maternal uncle is quite a noble person besides being valorous and always remains devoted to the feet of lord Vishnu.

## \|\| 44.21 \|\|

*kasya doṣeṇa durddharṣastvaṃ na jāne'pyaśuddhadhīḥ \|*  
*yeṣāṃ doṣairjano duṣṭastamṛte śuddhamānasaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. I am unable to understand the cause of your losing wisdom in such a way. The blame by which one becomes wicked and without that blame one gets purified (has to be taken into account).

## \|\| 44.22 \|\|

*amoghaṃ prāpya parśuṃ ca guruṃ ca karuṇānidhim \|*  
*parīkṣāṃ kṣattriye kṛtvā babhūvāsya sute punaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. After receiving the infallible battle-axe from the teacher who happens to be extremely merciful, you used the same initially on the Ks atriyas and now you have used it on the son of the teacher.

## \|\| 44.23-24 \|\|

*gurave dakṣiṇādānamucitaṃ ca śrutau śrutam \|*  
*bhagno dantastatsutasya cchindhi mastakamapyaho \|\| 23 \|\|*

*gaṇeśvaraṃ raṇe jitvā sthitaścedāvayoḥ puraḥ \|*  
*sa tvaṃ labdhvā''śiṣo loke pūjito'bhūrjagattraye \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. Giving such a type of daksina the teacher has been well rewarded by you. Presently you have only cut off the tusk of the son of the teacher, now you serve his head also. After defeating Ganeshvara in the battle-field you will present yourself in the world as victorious and will also earn blessings from us.

## \|\| 44.25 \|\|

*parśunā'moghavīryyeṇa śaṅkarasya vareṇa ca \|*  
*hantuṃ śaktaḥ sṛgālaśca siṃhaṃ śārdūlamākhubhuk \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. You are not aware that by getting the infallible weapons from Shiva like the battle-axe and receiving a boon from him, a jackal can kill a lion and a mouse can kill even a tiger.

## \|\| 44.26 \|\|

*tvadvidhaṃ lakṣakoṭiṃ ca hantuṃ śakto gaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*jitendriyāṇāṃ pravaro nahi hanti ca makṣikām \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Ganesha is competent to kill lakhs and crores of persons like you but the one who has overpowered his organs of senses, will never try to kill even a fly.

## \|\| 44.27 \|\|

*tejasā kṛṣṇatulyo'yaṃ kṛṣṇāṃśaśca gaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*deśaścānye kṛṣṇakalāḥ pūjā'sya puratastataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The leader of the ganas is compared with lord Krishna in lustre and happens to be his athsha alone. All other gods happen to be his rays. That is why he is adored first of all.

## \|\| 44.28 \|\|

*vrataprabhāvataḥ prāptaḥ śaṅkarasya vareṇa ca \|*  
*śokenātikaṭhoreṇa nahi sampadvinā''padam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. With the influence of vrata, the boon of Shiva and after performing great tapas I had achieved him as my husband because no one can achieve pleasure without pain.

## \|\| 44.29 \|\|

*ityuktvā pārvatī roṣāttaṃ rāmaṃ hantumudyatā \|*  
*rāmaḥ sasmāra taṃ kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya manasā gurum \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Thus speaking, Parvati got ready to pronounce a curse on Parashurama suffering from mental agony. Finding this, Parashurama feeling panicky at heart, started reciting the name of the lord Krishna in his mind, bowing before the teacher in reverence.

## \|\| 44.30-33 \|\|

*etasminnantare durgā dadarśa purato dvijam \|*  
*atīva vāmanaṃ bālaṃ sūryyakoṭisamaprabham \|\| 30 \|\|*

*śukladantaṃ śuklavāsaṃ śuklayajñopavītinam \|*  
*daṇḍinaṃ chatriṇaṃ caiva suprabhaṃ tilakojjvalam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*dadhataṃ tulasīmālāṃ sasmitaṃ sumanoharam \|*  
*ratnakeyūravalayaṃ ratnamālāvibhūṣitam \|\| 32 \|\|*

*ratnanūpurapādaṃ ca sadratnamukuṭojjvalam \|*  
*ratnakuṇḍalayugmāḍhyagaṇḍasthalavirājitam \|\| 33 \|\|*

30-33. In the meantime Durga saw a dwarfish boy coming to her having the lustre of crores of suns, having white teeth, clad in white garment and yajnopavita, holding a staff and an umbrella in his hands. His forehead was painted with bright Tilakam and he wore the rosary of Tulasi. He had a serene smile on his face and looked quite pleasant. He was adorned with armlets of gems besides wristlets and the rosary of gems, he had anklets of gems and his feet were quite charming. He was wearing a beautiful crown on his head and gem-studded kundalas appeared in his ears.

## \|\| 44.34 \|\|

*sthiramudrāṃ darśayantaṃ bhaktavāmakareṇa ca \|*  
*dakṣiṇe'bhayamudrāṃ ca bhakteśaṃ bhaktavatsalam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Не displayed Sthira-mudra with his left hand and Abhaya-mudra with his right hand to his devotees. He happens to be the lord of his devotees.

## \|\| 44.35 \|\|

*bālikābālakagaṇairnāgaraiḥ sasmitairyutam \|*  
*kailāsavāsibhiḥ sarvairāvṛddhairīkṣitaṃ mudā \|\| 35\|\|*

35\. Wearing a serene smile on his face, he was surrounded by the boys and girls of the town. All the dwellers of Kailasa, a group of young and old stared at him.

## \|\| 44.36-37 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā sambhramācchambhuḥ sabhṛtyaḥ sahaputrakaḥ \|*  
*mūrdhnā bhaktyā prāṇamacca durgāyai daṇḍavadbhuvi \|\| 36 \|\|*

*āśiṣaṃ pradadau bālaḥ sarvebhyo vāñchitapradām \|*  
*taṃ dṛṣṭvā bālakāḥ sarve mahāścaryyaṃ yayurbhiyā \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. At the sight of the lord, Shiva at once offered his salutation to him with devotion, together with his attendants and sons. Durga also prostrated before him; thereafter the boy blessed all those present there for the fulfilment of their desires. All the children of the town left the place looking at the boy with surprise.

## \|\| 44.38-40 \|\|

*dattvā tasmai śivo bhaktyā tūpacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*pūjāṃ cakāra śrutyuktāṃ paripūrṇatamasya ca \|\| 38 \|\|*

*tuṣṭāva kāṇvaśākhoktastotreṇa natakandharaḥ \|*  
*pulakāṅkitasarvāṃgo bhagavantaṃ sanātanam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*ratnasiṃhāsanasthaṃ ca prāvocacchaṅkaraḥ svayam \|*  
*atīva tejasā'tyantaṃ pracchannākṛtimeva ca \|\| 40 \|\|*

38-40. Lord Shiva then adore,d the boy with his mind filled with devotion making sixteen types of offerings to him. Getting emotional, he adored him lowering his head before him, offering prayer to him at the same time. Thereafter lord Shiva seated on the gem-studded lion-throne spoke to the boy who possessed immense lustre.

## \|\| 44.41 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*ātmārāmeṣu kuśalapraśno'tīva viḍambanam \|*  
*te śaśvatkuśalādhārāḥ kuśalāḥ kuśalapradāḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Shiva said – It is just a fallacy to ask about the welfare of those who always roam about in the soul because they are themselves the base of the welfare, the form of the welfare and the bestowers of the welfare.

## \|\| 44.42-43 \|\|

*ayane saphalaṃ janma jīvitaṃ ca sujīvitam \|*  
*prāptastvamatithibrahmankṛṣṇasevāphalodayāt \|\| 42 \|\|*

*paripūrṇatamaḥ kṛṣṇo lokanistārahetave \|*  
*puṇyakṣetre hi kalayā bhārate ca kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. O Brahmana, my birth has met with success today. My life has become glorious because of your arrival here with the grace of lord Krishna. You are complete in all respects, besides being the abode of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 44.44 \|\|

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde gaṇeśastotrakathanaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The one who adores the guest, the gods get pleased with him. When the guest is pleased even the god is pleased.

## \|\| 44.45-46 \|\|

*snānena sarvatīrtheṣu sarvadānena yatphalam \|*  
*sarvavratopavāsena sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣayā \|\| 45 \|\|*

*sarvaistapobhirvividhairnityairnaimittikādibhiḥ \|*  
*tadevātithisevāyāḥ kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. The merit earned by taking a bath in all the holy places, performing all the charities, performing all the vratas and yajnas and performing all other religious ceremonies and the tapas, do not compare with one sixteenth part of serving a guest.

## \|\| 44.47 \|\|

*atithiryasya bhagnāśo yāti ruṣṭaśca mandirāt \|*  
*koṭijanmārjitaṃ puṇyaṃ tasya naśyati niścitam \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. If a guest returns disappointed from a householder, the merit earned by him during crores of births in the past, is destroyed.

## \|\| 44.48-53 \|\|

*strīgoghnaśca kṛtaghnaśca brahmaghno gurutalpagaḥ \|*  
*pitṛmātṛgurūṇāṃ ca nindako naraghātakaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*sandhyāhīnaḥ svaghātī ca satyaghno harinindakaḥ \|*  
*brahmasvasthāpyahārī ca mithyāsākṣyapradāyakaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*mitradrohī kṛtaghnaśca vṛṣavāhaśca sūpakṛt \|*  
*śavadāhī grāmayājī brāhmaṇo vṛṣalīpatiḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*śūdraśrāddhānnabhojī ca śūdraśrāddheṣu bhojakaḥ \|*  
*kanyāvikrayakārī ca śrīharernāmavikrayī \|\| 51 \|\|*

*lākṣāmāṃsatilānāṃ ca lavaṇasya tilasya ca \|*  
*vikretā brāhmaṇaścaiva turagāṇāṃ gavāṃ tathā \|\| 52 \|\|*

*ekādaśīkṛṣṇasevāhīno vipraśca bhārate \|*  
*ete mahāpātakinastriṣu lokeṣu ninditāḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

48-53. The one who kills his wife and the cow, the ungrateful person, the killer of a Brahmana, the one enjoying the company of the wife of the teacher, one who denounces his father, mother and teacher, killer of human beings, the one who is deprived of the sandhya-vandana, the one who commits suicide, the killer of truthfulness, the denouncer of lord Hari, the killer of Brahmanas, the thief of wealth, the one who gives false evidence, the one who betrays his friend, the ungrateful one, the one who loads the bulls, the store-keeper, the one who bums the dead bodies, the one who performs pUja from village to village, the Brahmana husband of a wicked woman, the one who performs shraddha of the Shudras and takes food with them, the seller of daughters, the seller of the name of god, of wax, meat, sea-same and salt, the one Who sells away the cows and horses, the Brahmana who does not perform Ekadas'l vrata and the one who is deprived of the devotion of lord Krishna are considered to be great sinners in the universe and are therefore denounced everywhere.

## \|\| 44.54 \|\|

*kālasūtre ca narake patanti brahmaṇāṃ śatam \|*  
*etebhyo'pyadhikaḥ so'pi yaścātithiparāṅmukhaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. All of them fall into the kalamtra hell and remain there up to the age of Brahma. The position of those who make the guest to return disappointed is much verse.

## \|\| 44.55 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śaṅkarasya vacaḥ śrutvā santuṣṭaḥ śrīhariḥ svayam \|*  
*meghagambhīrayā vācā tamuvāca jagatpatiḥ \|\| 55\|\|*

55\. Narayana said – On hearing the words of Shiva, the lord was pleased and thereafter, the lord of the universe spoke thundering like the clouds.

## \|\| 44.56 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*śvetadvīpādāgato'haṃ jñātvā kolāhalaṃ ca vaḥ \|*  
*asya rāmasya rakṣārthaṃ kṛṣṇabhaktasya sāmpratam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Vishnu said – Listening to the uproar, I have arrived here from Shveta-dvipa in order to protect Parashurama the devotee of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 44.57 \|\|

*naiteṣāṃ kṛṣṇabhaktānāmaśubhaṃ vidyate kvacit \|*  
*rakṣāmi tāṃścakrahasto gurumanyaṃ vinā śiva \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. О Shiva, the one who is devoted to lord Krishna has never to face miseries anywhere, I protect him always holding a disk in my hand, barring the one who betrays his teacher.

## \|\| 44.58 \|\|

*nāhaṃ pātā gurau ruṣṭe balavadguruhelanam \|*  
*tatparaḥ pātakī nāsti sevāhīno gurośca yaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Because I cannot protect a person who annoys his own teacher, the denouncing of the teacher is a terrific sin. There is no other greater sinner than the one who neglects the adoration of the teacher.

## \|\| 44.59 \|\|

*mānyaḥ pūjyaśca sarvebhyaḥ sarveṣāṃ janako bhavet \|*  
*aho yasya prasādena sarvānpaśyati mānavaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. By his grace a human is in a position to look at everything, he is adorable by all and respectable by all.

## \|\| 44.60-61 \|\|

*janako janmadānācca pālanācca pitā nṛṇām \|*  
*tato vistārakaraṇātkalayā sa prajāpatiḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

*pituḥ śataguṇaṃ mātā poṣaṇādgarbhadhāraṇāt \|*  
*vandyā pūjyā ca mānyā ca prasūssyādvai vasundharā \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. One becomes the father because of giving birth to a human being or becomes the father because of feeding the child and with the expansion is called Prajapati. The mother is a hundred times more adorable than the father because she carries the child in her womb and feeds it. She is more adorable and respectable. Not only this, she happens to be the form of the earth.

## \|\| 44.62 \|\|

*mātuḥ śataguṇaṃ vandyaḥ pūjyo mānyo'nnadāyakaḥ \|*  
*yadvinā naśvaro deho viṣṇuśca kalayā'nnadaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The giver of food is a hundred times more adorable and respectable than the mother because the body perishes without food, the giver of food actually is considered to be the form of lord Vis nu.

## \|\| 44.63 \|\|

*annadātuḥ śataguṇo'bhīṣṭadevaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*gurustasmācchataguṇo vidyāmantrapradāyakaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. The family god is more adorable than the giver of food and the teacher is a hundred times more adorable than the family god who bestows knowledge and mantra.

## \|\| 44.64 \|\|

*ajñānatimirācchannaṃ jñānadīpena cakṣuṣā \|*  
*yaḥ sarvārthaṃ darśayati tatparo naiva bāndhavaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. A teacher provides the knowledge of lamp to a person who roams in the darkness of ignorance and makes him realise the truth; therefore there is no other brother greater than him.

## \|\| 44.65 \|\|

*gurudattena mantreṇa tapaseṣṭasukhaṃ labhet \|*  
*sarvajñatvaṃ sarvasiddhiṃ tatparo naiva bāndhavaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. After adoring with the mantra provided by the guru, a person achieves the desired pleasure, becomes all knowledgeable and achieves all success; there are, therefore, no other relatives greater than the teacher.

## \|\| 44.66 \|\|

*sarvaṃ jayati sarvatra vidyayā gurudattayā \|*  
*tasmātpūjyo hi jagati ko vā bandhustato'dhikaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. A person becomes victorious with the use of arms from the knowledge achieved by him from his teacher, therefore, there are no other relatives more respectable than the teacher in the universe.

## \|\| 44.67 \|\|

*vidyāndho vā dhanāndho vā yo mūḍho na bhajedgurum \|*  
*brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpaiḥ sa lipto nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. Therefore, blinded with knowledge or riches, if a foolish fellow does not serve the teacher, he earns the sin of Brahmahatya. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 44.68 \|\|

*daridraṃ patitaṃ kṣudraṃ narabuddhyā''caredgurum \|*  
*tīrthasnāto'pi na śucirnādhikārī ca karmasu \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Therefore if a person adores a teacher who has become a pauper, has fallen or becomes degraded, he cannot be purified even after taking baths in all the sacred places nor does he get entitled to performing all the religious ceremonies.

## \|\| 44.69-71 \|\|

*pitaraṃ mātaraṃ bhāryyāṃ gurupatnīṃ guruṃ param \|*  
*yo na puṣṇāti kāpaṭyātsa mahāpātakī śiva \|\| 69 \|\|*

*gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*gurureva paraṃ brahma gururbhāskararūpakaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*guruścandrastathendraśca vāyuśca varuṇo'nalaḥ \|*  
*sarvarūpo hi bhagavānparamātmā svayaṃ guruḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

69-71. О Shiva, he who does not maintain his own father, mother, wife, teacher, deceitfully, earns a very great sin. Because the teacher happens to be Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva besides being the eternal Brahmana and the form of Shrya. Guru also is the form of the moon, Indra the wind-god, Varuna and Agni. The teacher happens to be the form of everything and the great soul.

## \|\| 44.72-74 \|\|

*nāsti vedātparaṃ śāstraṃ nahi kṛṣṇātparaḥ suraḥ \|*  
*nāsti gaṅgāsamaṃ tīrthaṃ na puṣpaṃ tulasīdalāt \|\| 72 \|\|*

*nāsti kṣamāvatī bhūmeḥ putrānnāstyaparaḥ priyaḥ \|*  
*na ca daivātparā śaktirnaikādaśyāḥ paraṃ vratam \|\| 73 \|\|*

*śālagrāmātparo yantro na kṣetraṃ bhāratātparam \|*  
*paraṃ puṇyasthalānāṃ ca puṇyaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ yathā \|\| 74 \|\|*

72-74. There is no other scripture greater than the Vedas and no other god greater than lord Krishna. There is no other holy place greater than the Ganga. There is no other leaf greater than the Tulasi leaf. There is no other greater in forgiveness than the earth and there is on one else dearer than the son. There is no other strength greater than the destiny and no other vrata greater than Ekadashi. There is no other yantra greater than Sdlagmma and there is no other place more sacred than the land of Bharata and out of other sacred places, there is no other place more auspicious than Vrindavana.

## \|\| 44.75 \|\|

*mokṣadānāṃ yathā kāśī vaiṣṇavānāṃ yathā śivaḥ \|*  
*na pārvatyāḥ parā sādhvī na gaṇeśātparo vaśī \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. There is no other place providing redemption better than KasI and among the Vaishnavas, there is no other devotee better than Shiva himself. There is no chaste lady better than Parvati on earth and there is no other god more self-controlled than Ganesha.

## \|\| 44.76-77 \|\|

*na ca vidyāsamo bandhurnāsti kaścidguroḥ paraḥ \|*  
*vidyādātuḥ putradārau tatsamau nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

*gurustriyāṃ ca putre cāpyabhavadrāmahelanam \|*  
*paraṃ sammārjanaṃ kartumāgato'haṃ tavālayam \|\| 77 \|\|*

76-77. There is no relative greater than the learnings and there is no well-wisher greater than the teacher, the wife of the teacher and the teacher's sons are also comparable with the same. There is no doubt about it. Parashurama has insulted the wife and the son of the teacher, I have arrived here in order to remove the same.

## \|\| 44.78 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*ityevamuktvā śambhuṃ ca durgāṃ sambodhya nārada \|*  
*uvāca bhagavāṃstatra satyasāraṃ paraṃ vacaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Narayana said – О Narada, thus speaking addressing Shiva and Durga, the lord spoke truthful and the best of the words.

## \|\| 44.79 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi madīyaṃ vacanaṃ śubham \|*  
*nītiyuktaṃ vedasāraṃ pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. Vishnu said – О goddess, I am speaking to you so auspicious words which are quite appropriate and are the essence of the Vedas and would be pleasant in result.

## \|\| 44.80-81 \|\|

*yathā te gajavaktraśca kārttikeyaśca pārvati \|*  
*tathā paraśurāmaśca bhārgavo nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

*nāstyeṣu snehabhedaśca tava vā śaṃkarasya ca \|*  
*vicāryya sarvaṃ sarvajñe kuru mātaryathocitam \|\| 81 \|\|*

80-81. O Parvati, as you had the sons like Ganesha and Karttikeya, similar is the case with Parashurama, there is no doubt about it. О all knowledgeable mother, you do not have any difference in the love for him, nor does Shiva has so. Therefore you think over it and do whatever you consider best.

## \|\| 44.82 \|\|

*putreṇa sārddhaṃ putrasya vivādo daivadoṣataḥ \|*  
*daivaṃ hantuṃ ko hi śakto daivaṃ ca balavattaram \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. If there has been a controversy between the two sons, it has developed due to the move of the destiny. Who can avert the mover of the destiny which is all powerful?

## \|\| 44.83-84 \|\|

*putrābhidhānaṃ vedeṣu paśya vatse varānane \|*  
*ekadanta iti khyātaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam \|\| 83 \|\|*

*putranāmāṣṭakaṃ stotraṃ sāmavedoktamīśvari \|*  
*śṛṇuṣvāvahitaṃ mātaḥ sarvavighnaharaṃ param \|\| 84 \|\|*

83-84. О daughter, О damsel, you search in the Vedas and you will find that your son is known with the epithet of Ekadanta (single tusk). He is well known with the same epithet; therefore, О goddess, О mother, you kindly listen to the stotra of Ganesha which has been inscribed in the Samaveda.

## \|\| 44.85 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*gaṇeśamekadantaṃ ca herambaṃ vighnanāyakam \|*  
*lambodaraṃ śūrpakarṇaṃ gajavaktraṃ guhāgrajam \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Vishnu said – He is known in the Samaveda as Ganesha, Ekadanta, Heramba, Vighnanayaka, Lambodara, Surpakama (the one having the ears like winnowing basket), Gajanana, Guhagraja. These are the eight names given in the scriptures.

## \|\| 44.86 \|\|

*aṣṭākhyārthaṃ ca putrasya śṛṇu mātarharapriye \|*  
*stotrāṇāṃ sārabhūtaṃ ca sarvavighnaharaṃ param \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. О beloved of loved Shiva, О mother, you listen from me to the meaning of the eight names of Ganesha which happen to be the gist of the stotra and remove all obstructions.

## \|\| 44.87 \|\|

*jñānārthavācako gaśca ṇaśca nirvāṇavācakaḥ \|*  
*tayorīśaṃ paraṃ brahma gaṇeśaṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. … stands for knowledge, stands for redemption. The one who is the lord of both of them is known as Ganesha. I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 44.88 \|\|

*ekaśabdaḥ pradhānārtho dantaśca balavācakaḥ \|*  
*balaṃ pradhānaṃ sarvasmādekadantaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. … stands for principal ... stands for strength. Therefore the one who is the foremost of the valorous persons is known as Ekadanta, I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 44.89-90 \|\|

*dīnārthavācako heśca rambaḥ pālakavācakaḥ \|*  
*pālakaṃ dīnalokānāṃ herambaṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 89 \|\|*

*vipattivācako vighno nāyakaḥ khaṇḍanārthakaḥ \|*  
*vipatkhaṇḍanakāraṃ taṃ praṇame vighnanāyakam \|\| 90 \|\|*

89-90. … stands for the poor and stands for protector, therefore the one who protects the poor in the universe, is known as Heramba. … stands for obstruction or misfortune and stands for the one who removes it. Therefore the one who removes misfortunes is known as Vighnanayaka and I adore him.

## \|\| 44.91 \|\|

*viṣṇudattaiśca naivedyairyasya lambaṃ purodaram \|*  
*pitrā dattaiśca vividhairvande lambodaraṃ ca tam \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. The one whose belly became protruding because of the excessive eating of the food provided by lord Vishnu or his own father, is known by the name of Lambodara. I bow in reverence to him.

## \|\| 44.92 \|\|

*śūrpākārau ca yatkarṇau vighnavāraṇakārakau \|*  
*sampaddau jñānarūpau ca śūrpakarṇaṃ namāmyaham \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. The one whose ears are as large as the winnowing baskets for the removal of obstructions, he has been designated as Surpakama and also bestows riches and learning. I bow in reverence to the god.

## \|\| 44.93 \|\|

*viṣṇuprasādaṃ muninā dattaṃ yanmūrdhni puṣpakam \|*  
*tadgajendramukhaṃ kāntaṃ gajavaktraṃ namāmyaham \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. The one on whose head the garland of Vishnu was placed by Indra, I bow in reverence to the same Gajendara having the face of an elephant.

## \|\| 44.94 \|\|

*guhasyāgre ca jāto'yamāvirbhūto harālaye \|*  
*vande guhāgrajaṃ devaṃ sarvadevāgrapūjitam \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. He was born in the house of Shiva earlier than Skanda. Therefore he is known as the elder brother of Skanda and I adore him as such.

## \|\| 44.95 \|\|

*etannāmāṣṭakaṃ durge nānāśaktiyutaṃ param \|*  
*etannāmāṣṭakaṃ stotraṃ nānārthasahitaṃ śubham \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. О Durga, these eight verses comprise of many saktis and different meanings and is a devotional stotra for Ganesha.

## \|\| 44.96 \|\|

*trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhennityaṃ sa sukhī sarvato jayī \|*  
*tato vighnāḥ palāyante vainateyādyathoragāḥ \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. The one who recites this stotra thrice a day, enjoys all the worldly pleasures and becomes victorious in the universe like Garuda who always overcomes the serpents.

## \|\| 44.97 \|\|

*gaṇeśvaraprasādena mahājñānī bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*putrārthī labhate putraṃ bhāryyārthī kuśalāṃ striyam \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. With the grace of Ganeshvara he attains all knowledge and learning, the person desirous of a son gets the son, a person desirous of a wife gets a virtuous wife.

## \|\| 44.98 \|\|

*mahājaḍaḥ kavīndraśca vidyāvāṃśca bhaveddhruvam \|*  
*putrastvaṃ paśya vede ca tathā kopaṃ ca no kuru \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. The immensely foolish person becomes a great poet and achieves all the learnings; therefore, О daughter, you look into the Vedas and shed away your anger.

## Chapter 45

## \|\| 45.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*pārvatīṃ bodhayitvā tu viṣṇū rāmamuvāca ha \|*  
*hitaṃ sāraṃ nītisāraṃ pariṇāmasukhāvaham \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narayana said – Thus making Parvati understand the reality, Vishnu spoke to Parashurama the words which were beneficial, truthful, appropriate and could result in pleasure, ultimately.

## \|\| 45.2 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*rāma tvamadhunā satyamaparādhī śrutermmate \|*  
*kopātkṛtvā dantabhaṅgaṃ gaṇeśasya sthito'śive \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Vishnu said – О Parashurama, according to the Vedas, you are truly a culprit because inspite of Shiva remaining present on the scene you have broken the tusk of Ganesha.

## \|\| 45.3 \|\|

*stotreṇaiva mayoktena stutvā gaṇapatiṃ param \|*  
*kāṇvaśākhoktavidhinā stuhi durgāṃ jagatprasūm \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Therefore, you recite the prayer according to Kanvasakha in favour of Ganapati and Durga, the mother of the universe.

## \|\| 45.4 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇasya parā śaktirbuddhirūpā jagatprabhoḥ \|*  
*asyāṃ ca tava ruṣṭāyāṃ hatā buddhirbhaviṣyati \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Because she happens to be the extreme intelligence of lord Krishna and in case of her getting annoyed with you, you will be deprived of all your wisdom.

## \|\| 45.5 \|\|

*sarvaśaktisvarūpeyamanayā śaktimajjagat \|*  
*anayā śaktimānkṛṣṇo nirguṇaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. She is all powerful and represents the strength of the universe; even lord Krishna becomes powerful because of her strength alone.

## \|\| 45.6 \|\|

*sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ na śaktaśca brahmā śaktyā'nayā vinā \|*  
*vayamasyāṃ prasūtāśca brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Even Brahma is unable to resort to his creation without her strength. All of us including Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva have emerged out of her.

## \|\| 45.7 \|\|

*surasaṃghe'suragraste kāle ghoratare dvija \|*  
*tejastu sarvadevānāmāvirbhūtā purā satī \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. О Brahmana, at the time when the gods were over-powered by the demons, she emerged out of the tejas of all the gods.

## \|\| 45.8 \|\|

*kṛṣṇājñayā'surānhatvā dattvā tebhyaḥ padaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*dakṣapatnyāṃ janiṃ lebhe dakṣasya tapasā purā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. She destroyed the demons at the command of lord Krishna and restored the gods to their original status. Because of the tapas of Daksha, she was born as his daughter from his wife.

## \|\| 45.9 \|\|

*bhāryyā bhūtvā śaṃkarasya punaḥ patyuśca nindayā \|*  
*dehaṃ tyaktvā śailapatnyāṃ janiṃ lebhe purā satī \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. She became the spouse of Shiva and because of the denouncement of her husband, she ended her body born out of Daksha. Thereafter she was born of the wife of Himalaya.

## \|\| 45.10 \|\|

*śaṃkarastapasā labdho yogīndrāṇāṃ gurorguruḥ \|*  
*labdho gaṇapatiḥ putraḥ kṛṣṇāṃśaḥ kṛṣṇasevayā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. She then performed tapas for a long time and achieved Shiva, the teacher of the great teachers as her husband and after serving Shiva, she achieved Ganapati as her son who happens to be the ariisha of Krishna.

## \|\| 45.11 \|\|

*yaṃ dhyāyasyeva nityaṃ kiṃ taṃ na jānāsi bālaka \|*  
*sa eva bhagavānkṛṣṇaścāṃśena girijāsutaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O child, the one who is always adored by you, are you not aware of her? The same lord Krishna has appeared as the son of Girija.

## \|\| 45.12-13 \|\|

*kṛtāñjalirnato bhūtvā stuhi durgāṃ śivapriyām \|*  
*śivāṃ śivapradāṃ śaivāṃ śivabījāṃ śiveśvarīm \|\| 12 \|\|*

*śivāyāḥ stotrarājena purā śūlikṛtena vai \|*  
*tripurasya vadhe ghore brahmaṇā preritena ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Therefore you offer prayers to Durga, the beloved of Shiva, meekly with folded hands. Shiva is the bestower of welfare, devoted to Shiva, the form of the seed of Shiva and the goddess of Shiva, you adore Parvati by reciting the stotra composed by Shiva which was composed by him at the time of the killing of Tripura under the influence of Brahma.

## \|\| 45.14-15 \|\|

*ityuktvā śrīpadaṃ śīghraṃ jagāma śrīniketanam \|*  
*gate harau hariṃ smṛtvā rāmastāṃ stotumudyataḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*stotreṇa viṣṇudattena sarvavighnahareṇa ca \|*  
*dharmmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ kāraṇena ca nārada \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. О Narada, thus speaking that dwarfish boy at once left for Vishnuloka. After the departure of the lord, reciting the name of the lord, Parashurama started offering prayers to Parvati which were given to him by Vishnu and could remove all obstructions and was the cause of dharma, artha, kama and moksa.

## \|\| 45.16-17 \|\|

*kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā snātvā gaṅgodake śubhe \|*  
*guruṃ praṇamya bhakteśaṃ dhṛtvā dhaute ca vāsasī \|\| 16 \|\|*

*ācamya natvā mūrdhnā tāṃ bhaktinamrātmakandharaḥ \|*  
*pulakāñcitasarvāṃgaścānandāśrusamanvitaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. After taking a bath with the auspicious water of the Ganga and bowing his head, he offered salutation to the teacher of the gods and then clad himself in two washed clothes and performing йсатапа, he bowed in reverence getting emotional and his eyes were filled with tears. He prostrated before him and started adoring the goddess.

## \|\| 45.18 \|\|

*paraśurāma uvāca \|*  
*śrīkṛṣṇasya ca goloke paripūrṇatamasya ca \|*  
*āvirbhūtā vigrahataḥ purā sṛṣṭyunmukhasya ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Parashurama said – In the earlier times, in the Goloka when he was emerging out of body of lord Krishna at the time of creation you appeared on earth.

## \|\| 45.19-20 \|\|

*sūryyakoṭiprabhāyuktā vastrālaṅkārabhūṣitā \|*  
*vahniśuddhāṃśukādhānā sasmitā sumanoharā \|\| 19 \|\|*

*navayauvanasampannā sindūrāruṇyaśobhitā \|*  
*lalitaṃ kabarībhāraṃ mālatīmālyamaṇḍitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. You have the lustre of crores of suns and are adorned with the best of ornaments and costumes. You are clad in the garments purified by the fire and wear a serene smile on your face; you are quite pleasant, youthful and decorated with vermilion. You are wearing the garland of jasmine flowers and you wear a beautiful hair-do on the head.

## \|\| 45.21-23 \|\|

*aho'nirvacanīyā tvaṃ cārumūrttiṃ ca bibhratī \|*  
*mokṣapradā mumukṣūṇāṃ mahāviṣṇorvidhiḥ svayam \|\| 21 \|\|*

*mumoha kṣaṇamātreṇa dṛṣṭvā tvāṃ sarvamohinīm \|*  
*bālaissaṃbhūya sahasā sasmitā dhāvitā purā \|\| 22 \|\|*

*sadbhiḥ khyātā tena rādhā mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī \|*  
*kṛṣṇastāṃ sahasā bhīto vīryyādhānaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 23 \|\|*

21-23. You have a beautiful figure, which is adorable. You provide salvation to the truth seekers. Your immense beauty attracts lord Maha-Vishnu and Brahma in an instant. At that point of time you start running with the children wearing a smile on your face. That is why the noble people achieve you in the form of Radha, though you happen to be the supreme goddess and MiilaPrakriti. Lord Krishna too getting afraid planted his seed in your womb.

## \|\| 45.24 \|\|

*tato ḍimbhaṃ mahajjajñe tato jāto mahāvirāṭ \|*  
*yasyaiva lomakūpeṣu brahmāṇḍānyakhilāni ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. As a result of this a great egg emerged out of your body and Mahavirat was born out of it in whose hair-pits the innumerable globes are enshrined.

## \|\| 45.25 \|\|

*rādhāratikrameṇaiva tanniśvāso babhūva ha \|*  
*sa niśśvāso mahāvāyuḥ sa virāḍ viśvadhārakaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Lord Krishna while enjoying the dance with Radha took a deep sigh which resulted in the creation of the great wind and also Virat which became the base of the universe.

## \|\| 45.26 \|\|

*bhayagharmmajalenaiva pupluve viśvagolakam \|*  
*sa virāḍ viśvanilayo jalarāśirbabhūva ha \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. At that point of time out of sweat of the Virat, the water emerged. Ultimately the same Virat was converted into the vast expanse of water.

## \|\| 45.27-32 \|\|

*tatastvaṃ pañcadhābhūya pañca mūrtīśca bibhratī \|*  
*prāṇādhiṣṭhātṛmūrtiryā kṛṣṇasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇaprāṇādhikāṃ rādhāṃ tāṃ vadanti purāvidaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*vedādhiṣṭhātṛmūrtiryā vedaśāstraprasūrapi \|*  
*tāṃ sāvitrīṃ śuddharūpāṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*aiśvaryyādhiṣṭhātṛmūrtiḥ śāntistvaṃ śāntarūpiṇī \|*  
*lakṣmīṃ vadanti santastāṃ śuddhāṃ sattvasvarūpiṇīm \|\| 29 \|\|*

*rāgādhiṣṭhātṛdevī yā śuklamūrtissatāṃ prasūḥ \|*  
*sarasvatīṃ tāṃ śāstrajñāṃ śāstrajñāḥ pravadantyaho \|\| 30 \|\|*

*buddhirvidyā sarvaśakteryā mūrtiradhidevatā \|*  
*sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalyā sarvamaṅgalarūpiṇī \|\| 31 \|\|*

*sarvamaṅgalabījasya śivasya nilaye'dhunā \|\| 32 \|\|*

27-32. Thereafter you emerged in five forms. The one who happens to be the beloved and life-like of lord Krishna and is dearer to him than his own life, is called Radha by the ancient people. The one who is the great goddess of the Vedas and the creator of the Vedas, such an intellectual and auspicious goddess is known as Savitri. Being the goddess of all the fortunes and riches, you are quite peaceful in nature and because of that nature you are called Lakshmi, the goddess who is white in complexion, is the mother of the noble people; being the goddess of learning she is called Sarasvati. The one who is the goddess of intelligence and learning, besides being the strength of the universe and who also provides prosperity to all is called Sarvamangala. You bestow all the welfare and are the cause for welfare being the form of prosperity.

## \|\| 45.33-35 \|\|

*śive śivāsvarūpā tvaṃ lakṣmīrnārāyaṇāntike \|*  
*sarasvatī ca sāvitrī vedasūrbrahmaṇaḥ priyā \|\| 33 \|\|*

*rādhā rāseśvarasyaiva paripūrṇatamasya ca \|*  
*paramānandarūpasya paramānandarūpiṇī \|\| 34 \|\|*

*tvatkalāṃśāṃśakalayā devānāmapi yoṣitaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

33-35. Pleasantly you are the seed of all the prosperity in the universe, you are in fact the spouse of Shiva known as Parvati and you happen to be Lakshmi with Narayana and with Brahma you happen to be Sarasvati and Savitri the mother of the Vedas. You happen to be Radha of Krishna who is complete in all respects, blissful and bestower of the bliss. All the wives of the gods emerged out of your rays alone.

## \|\| 45.36-37 \|\|

*tvaṃ vidyā yoṣitaḥ sarvāssarveṣāṃ bījarūpiṇī \|*  
*chāyā sūryyasya candrasya rohiṇī sarva mohinī \|\| 36 \|\|*

*śacī śakrasya kāmasya kāminī ratirīśvarī \|*  
*varuṇānī jaleśasya vāyoḥ strī prāṇavallabhā \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. You are the goddess of learning and the seed for all the ladies. You happen to be the shadow of the sun and Robin! for the moon who attracts everyone. You happen to be IndranI of Indra, Rati of Kamadeva, VarunanI of Varuna, the beloved of the wind-god.

## \|\| 45.38-41 \|\|

*vahneḥ priyā hi svāhā ca kuberasya ca sundarī \|*  
*yamasya tu suśīlā ca nairṛtasya ca kaiṭabhī \|\| 38 \|\|*

*aiśānī syācchaśikalā śatarūpā manoḥ priyā \|*  
*devahūtiḥ kardamasya vasiṣṭhasyāpyarundhatī \|\| 39 \|\|*

*lopāmudrā'pyagastyasya devamātā'ditistathā \|*  
*ahalyā gautamasyāpi sarvādhārā vasundharā \|\| 40 \|\|*

*gaṅgā ca tulasī cāpi pṛthivyāṃ yā saridvarā \|*  
*etāḥ sarvāśca yā hyanyā sarvāstvatkalayā'mbike \|\| 41 \|\|*

38-41. You happen to be Svaha, the beloved of Agni, Sundari of Kubera, Susila of Yama, KaitabI of Nairirta, Sasikala of Sankara, dear Satampa of Manu, Devahuti of Kardama, Arundhatl of Vasis tha, Lopamudra of Agastya, Aditi the mother of gods, Ahalya the Gautama besides being Vasundhara the best of all. О Ambika, all the sacred rivers like Ganga and Tulasi have emerged from your rays, together with other rivers.

## \|\| 45.42-45 \|\|

*gṛhalakṣmī gṛhe nṝṇāṃ rājalakṣmīśca rājasu \|*  
*tapasvināṃ tapasyā tvaṃ gāyatrī brāhmaṇasya ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

*satāṃ sattvasvarūpā tvamasatāṃ kalahāṅkurā \|*  
*jyotīrūpā nirguṇasya śaktistvaṃ saguṇasya ca \|\| 43 \|\|*

*sūryye prabhāsvarūpā tvaṃ dāhikā ca hutāśane \|*  
*jale śaityasvarūpā ca śobhārūpā niśākare \|\| 44 \|\|*

*iti śrībrahmavaivartte mahāpurāṇe tṛtīye gaṇapatikhaṇḍe nāradanārāyaṇasaṃvāde paraśurāmakṛtadurgāstotraṃ nāma pañcacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

42-45. You are the GrhaLakshmi of the house-holder, Raja-Lakshmi of the kings, the meditation of the tapasvis, Gayatri of Brahmanas, truthfulness of the noble people, quarrelsomeness of the wicked people, the flames of Nirguna, the strength of Saguna, the lustre of the sun, the power of burning of the fire, coolness in water, glory in the moon, fragrance in the earth, the sound in the sky, hunger and thirst among the teachers, you happen to be thus the strength of all.

## \|\| 45.46 \|\|

*sarvabījasvarūpā tvaṃ saṃsāre sārarūpiṇī \|*  
*smṛtirmedhā ca buddhirvā jñānaśaktirvipaścitām \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. You happen to be the form of the seed in the universe, the memory of the wild people besides being intelligence and the divine knowledge.

## \|\| 45.47 \|\|

*kṛṣṇena vidyā yā dattā sarvajñāna prasūḥ śubhā \|*  
*śūline kṛpayā sā tvaṃ yayā mṛtyuñjayaḥ śivaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Lord Krishna imparted the divine knowledge to Shiva, as a result of which the latter became victorious over the death. You respect the same.

## \|\| 45.48 \|\|

*sṛṣṭipālanasaṃhāraśaktayastrividhāśca yāḥ \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ sā tvameva namo'stu te \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. You happen to be the strength of Brahma, Vishnu and Shiva for creation, preservation and destruction. I bow in reverence to you.

## \|\| 45.49 \|\|

*madhukaiṭabhabhītyā ca trasto dhātā prakampitaḥ \|*  
*stutvā muktaśca yāṃ devīṃ tāṃ mūrdhnā praṇamāmyaham \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Brahma was terrified because of the demons Madhu and Kaitabha. He started trembling and it is the goddess who relieved him of the danger and the One who was offered prayer by him, you happen to be the same. I offer my salutation to you.

## \|\| 45.50 \|\|

*madhukaiṭabhayoryuddhe trātā'sau viṣṇurīśvarīm \|*  
*babhūva śaktimānstutvā tvāṃ durgāṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The goddess for whom lord Vishnu prayed at the time of danger from Madhukaitabha, you happen to be the same, I offer my salutations to you.

## \|\| 45.51 \|\|

*tripurasya mahāyuddhe sarathe patite śive \|*  
*yāṃ tuṣṭuvuḥ surāḥ sarve tāṃ durgāṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The goddess who is adored by the gods after the chariot of Shiva fell at the time of the war with Tripura, I offer my salutation to the same goddess, Vishnu himself turned into the form of a bull and lifted up Shiva. Thereafter Shiva offered prayers to the goddess and killed Tripura, I offer my salutation to the same goddess.

## \|\| 45.52 \|\|

*viṣṇunā vṛṣarūpeṇa svayaṃ śambhuḥ samutthitaḥ \|*  
*jaghāna tripuraṃ stutvā tāṃ durgāṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. With your command the wind blows, the sun always bums, Indra pours the rain and the fire bums, I offer my salutation to the same Durga. At the command of whom KSla, the god of death, always roams about, I offer my salutation to the same Durga. At your command the creator creates the universe, Vishnu preserves it and Shiva destroys it, I offer my salutation to the same goddess Durga.

## \|\| 45.53-55 \|\|

*yadājñayā vāti vātaḥ sūryastapati santatam \|*  
*varṣatīndro dahatyagnistāṃ durgāṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 53 \|\|*

*yadājñayā hi kālaśca śaśvadbhramati vegataḥ \|*  
*mṛtyuścarati jantūnāṃ tāṃ durgāṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 54 \|\|*

*sraṣṭā sṛjati sṛṣṭiṃ ca pātā pāti yadājñayā \|*  
*saṃharttā saṃharetkāle tāṃ durgāṃ praṇamāmyaham \|\| 55 \|\|*

53-55. Lord Krishna, is the form of the flame and is invisible but he is unable to resort to the creation without the goddess. I, therefore, bow in reverence to the same goddess.

## \|\| 45.56 \|\|

*jyotissvarūpo bhagavāñchrīkṛṣṇo nirguṇaḥ svayam \|*  
*yayā vinā na śaktaśca sṛṣṭiṃ kartuṃ namāmi tām \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. О mother of the universe, protect me and forgive me my sins. The mother never gets annoyed in case the children commit mischief.

## \|\| 45.57 \|\|

*rakṣa rakṣa jaganmātaraparādhaṃ kṣamasva me \|*  
*śiśūnāmaparādhena kuto mātā hi kupyati \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Thus speaking and bowing in reverence to the goddess, Parashurama started crying. The goddess Durga was instantaneously pleased with him and granted him the boon.

## \|\| 45.58 \|\|

*uktvā paraśurāmaśceti natvā tāṃ ruroda ha \|*  
*tuṣṭā durgā saṃbhrameṇa cābhayaṃ ca varaṃ dadau \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. She said, "O son you will be eternal. О son be composed and by the grace of all you will be victorious always in future.

## \|\| 45.59-60 \|\|

*amaro bhava he putra vatsa susthiratāṃ vraja \|*  
*sarvaprasādātsarvatra jayo'stu tava santatam \|\| 59 \|\|*

*sarvāntarātmā bhagavāṃstuṣṭassyātsantataṃ hariḥ \|*  
*bhaktirbhavatu te kṛṣṇe śivade ca śive gurau \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. Let the universal lord be pleased with you always and you remain devoted to lord Krishna and lord Shiva who happens to be the bestower of welfare and your teacher.

## \|\| 45.61 \|\|

*iṣṭadeve gurau yasya bhaktirbhavati śāśvatī \|*  
*taṃ hantuṃ nahi śaktā vā ruṣṭā vā sarvadevatāḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Because to the one who is devoted to his teacher and the family god, no one can do any harm.

## \|\| 45.62 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇasya ca bhaktastvaṃ śiṣyo vai śaṃkarasya ca \|*  
*gurupatnīṃ stauṣi yasmātkastvāṃ hantumiheśvaraḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Becoming the devotee of lord Krishna and the pupil of Shiva, you are adoring the wife of the teacher, therefore who on earth can dare to kill you?

## \|\| 45.63 \|\|

*aho na kṛṣṇabhaktānāmaśubhaṃ vidyate kvacit \|*  
*anyadeveṣu ye bhaktā na bhaktā vā niraṅkuśāḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. The devotees of lord never have to face any unpleasantness. Those who are attached to other gods, are either not devoted to me or are independent.

## \|\| 45.64 \|\|

*candramā balavāṃstuṣṭo yeṣāṃ bhāgyavatāṃ bhṛgo \|*  
*teṣāṃ tārāgaṇā ruṣṭāḥ kiṃ kurvaṃti ca durbalāḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. О son of Bhrigu, the fortunate people with whom the moon gets pleased but the stars get annoyed, then what harm can the weak starts bring on him?

## \|\| 45.65 \|\|

*yasmai tuṣṭaḥ pālayati naradevo mahānsukhī \|*  
*tasya kiṃ vā kariṣyanti ruṣṭā bhṛtyāśca durbalāḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The one who is protected by the king wholeheartedly, enjoys the pleasure always. Even if the servants get annoyed with him they cannot do any harm to him.

## \|\| 45.66 \|\|

*ityuktvā pārvatī tuṣṭā dattvā rāmāya cāśiṣam \|*  
*jagāmāntaḥpuraṃ tūrṇaṃ harṣaśabdo babhūva ha \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Thus speaking Parvati delightfully blessed Parashurama and retired to her inner apartment. Thereafter, there was rejoicing everywhere.

## \|\| 45.67 \|\|

*stotraṃ vai kāṇvaśākhoktaṃ pūjākāle ca yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*yātrākāle tathā prātarvācchitārthaṃ labheddhruvam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. This happens to be the prayer from the Kanvasakha and whosoever recites the same at the time of puja, at the time of journey or daily in the morning, surely gets his desires fulfilled.

## \|\| 45.68-74 \|\|

*putrārthī labhate putraṃ kanyārthī kanyakāṃ labhet \|*  
*vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ prajārthī cāpnuyātprajāḥ \|*  
*bhraṣṭarājyo labhedrājyaṃ naṣṭavitto dhanaṃ labhet \|\| 68 \|\|*

*yasya ruṣṭo gururdevo rājā vā bāndhavo'thavā \|*  
*tasmai tuṣṭaśca varadaḥ stotrarājaprasādataḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

*dasyugrastaḥ phaṇigrastaśśatrugrasto bhayānakaḥ \|*  
*vyādhigrasto bhavenmuktaḥ stotrasmaraṇamātrataḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*rājadvāre śmaśāne ca kārāgāre ca bandhane \|*  
*jalarāśau nimagnaśca muktastatsmṛtimātrataḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

*svāmibhede putrabhede mitrabhede ca dāruṇe \|*  
*stotrasmaraṇamātreṇa vāñchitārthaṃ labheddhruvam \|\| 72 \|\|*

*kṛtvā haviṣyaṃ varṣaṃ ca stotrarājaṃ śṛṇoti yā \|*  
*bhaktyā durgāṃ ca sampūjya mahāvandhyā prasūyate \|\| 73 \|\|*

*labhate sā divyaputraṃ jñāninaṃ cirajīvinam \|*  
*asaubhāgyā ca saubhāgyaṃ ṣaṇmāsaśravaṇāllabhet \|\| 74 \|\|*

68-74. The one who is desirous of a son will get a son, the one who is desirous of a daughter will get a daughter, the one who is desirous of learning will get learning, the one who is desirous of the people will get the people, the one who has lost his kingdom will get it back and the one who has lost his riches will get them back, the one, with whom the teacher, the king or the relatives get annoyed, he will make them all pleased with the reciting of the stotra. The one who is surrounded by thieves, bitten by a snake, surrounded by the enemies suffering from terrific disease, will get himself relieved with the reciting of the stotra. One gets freed from the royal court, the cremation ground, prison and fetters, besides the deep waters. All the desires of a person are fulfilled with the reciting of this stotra, inspite of opposition from the husband, son or the friends, such of the ladies who consuming the left-over of the yajna for a year and recite this stotra regularly or listens to it, performing the puja of Durga, they inspite of being completely barren get a child. She gets a well-read and a divine son having long life.

## \|\| 45.75 \|\|

*navamāsaṃ kākavandhyā mṛtavatsā ca bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*stotrarājaṃ yā śṛṇoti sā putraṃ labhate dhruvam \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. The one who listens to it for six months, earns all the fortunes by the grace of Durga. The one who is totally barren and the one who gives birth to dead infants, gets a son if she recites the stotra or listens to it, for nine months.

## \|\| 45.76 \|\|

*kanyāmātā putrahīnā pañcamāsaṃ śṛṇoti yā \|*  
*ghaṭe sampūjya durgāṃ ca sā putraṃ labhate dhruvam \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The one who listens to the best of the stotra surely achieves the son, the woman who gives birth only to daughters or the one having no son at all, if she listens to this stotra for five months and performs pUja of Durga consecrating the vase, she surely achieves a son.

## Chapter 46

## \|\| 46.1 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*stutvā tāṃ parśurāmo'sau harṣasaṃphullamānasaḥ \|*  
*stotreṇa hariṇoktena sa tuṣṭāva gaṇādhipam \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Narada said – Parashurama adored Parvati delightfully and thereafter he also adored Ganesha by reciting this stotra provided to him by the lord.

## \|\| 46.2 \|\|

*pūjāṃ cakāra bhaktyā ca naivedyairvividhairapi \|*  
*dhūpairdīpaiśca gandhaiśca puṣpaiśca tulasīṃ vinā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. He also offered various types of eatables with devotion including essence, lamp, fragrance and flowers.

## \|\| 46.3 \|\|

*sampūjya bhrātaraṃ bhaktyā sa rāmaḥ śaṃkarājñayā \|*  
*gurupatnīṃ guruṃ natvā gamanaṃ kartumudyataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. With the permission of Shiva, Parashurama adored Parvati and Ganesha besides Shiva, his teacher and went back to his abode.

## \|\| 46.4-5 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*pūjāṃ bhagavataścakre rāmo gaṇapateryadā \|*  
*naivadyairvividhaiḥ puṣpaistulasīṃ ca vinā katham \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tulasī sarvapuṣpāṇāṃ mānyā dhanyā manoharā \|*  
*kathaṃ pūjāṃ sārabhūtāṃ na gṛhṇāti gaṇeśvaraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. Narada said – Parashurama offered puja to Ganesha with all the eatables, flowers, etc., but how could his adoration be accepted without the Tulasi leaves because of all the flowers, Tulasi is considered to be the best; then why Tulasi leaves are not acceptable by Ganesha?

## \|\| 46.6 \|\|

*nārāyaṇa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu nārada vakṣye'hamitihāsaṃ purātanam \|*  
*brahmakalpasya vṛttāntaṃ nigūḍhaṃ ca manoharam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Narayana said – О Narada, I am going to tell you an ancient story which is contained as an ancient secret. You listen to it.

## \|\| 46.7-8 \|\|

*ekadā tulasī devī prodbhinnanavayauvanā \|*  
*tīrthaṃ bhramantī tapasā nārāyaṇaparāyaṇā \|\| 7 \|\|*

*dadarśa gaṅgātīre sā gaṇeśaṃ yauvanānvitam \|*  
*atīva sundaraṃ śuddhaṃ sasmitaṃ pītavāsasam \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. Once Tulasi in her youthful age, performing tapas and reciting the name of Narayana was visiting holy places. Thereafter the youthful Ganesha spotted her on the bank of the Ganga. He was quite beautiful, having a spotless mind, wearing a serene smile on his face and was clad in a yellow lower garment.

## \|\| 46.9 \|\|

*candanokṣitasarvāṃgaṃ ratnabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|*  
*dhyāyantaṃ kṛṣṇapādābjaṃ janmamṛtyujarāpaham \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. All his limbs were plastered with sandal-paste and he was wearing gem-studded ornaments on his body and devoted his mind to the lotus-like feet of lord Kpsna, who happens to be the remover of birth, death and old age.

## \|\| 46.10 \|\|

*jitendriyāṇāṃ pravaraṃ yogīndrāṇāṃ gurorgurum \|*  
*surūpahāryyaṃ niṣkāmaṃ sakāmā tamuvāca ha \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. He happens to be the best of those who had controlled the organs of senses, a great yogi, teacher of the teachers but the passionate Tulasi looked at him and said.

## \|\| 46.11 \|\|

*tulasyuvāca \|*  
*aho dhyāyasi kiṃ deva śāntarūpa gajānana \|*  
*kathaṃ lambodaro deho gajavaktraṃ kathaṃ tava \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Tulasi said – О elephant-faced god, whom are you adoring with a peaceful mind? How could you achieve the big belly and the face of an elephant?

## \|\| 46.12 \|\|

*ekadantaḥ kathaṃ vaktre vadāmutra ca kāraṇam \|*  
*tyaja dhyānaṃ mahābhāga sāyaṃkāla upasthitaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. О virtuous one, why are you having only one tusk on your face, you tell me the reason for it? It is getting evening time and you stop performing dhyanam.

## \|\| 46.13 \|\|

*ityuktvā tulasī devī prajahāsa punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*paraṃ cetasi dagdhā sā kāmabāṇaiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus speaking the goddess Tulasi was laughing again and again but she was burning in passion from inside.

## \|\| 46.14 \|\|

*gaṇeśasya pradhānāṃge dattvā kiñcijjalaṃ mune \|*  
*jaghāna tarjanyagreṇa niṣpandaṃ kṛṣṇamānasam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then she dropped some water on Ganesha and pushed him with the tip of her forefinger. Ganesha was engrossed in the devotion of lord Krishna.

## \|\| 46.15 \|\|

*babhūva dhyānabhagnaṃ ca tasya nārada cetanam \|*  
*duḥkhaṃ ca dhyānabhedena tadvicchedo hi śokadaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. О Narada, this disturbed his meditation and he felt painful because being disturbed in one's meditation is quite painful.

## \|\| 46.16 \|\|

*dhyānaṃ tyaktvā hariṃ smṛtvā cāpaśyatkāminīṃ puraḥ \|*  
*navayauvanasampannāṃ sasmitāṃ kāmapīḍitām \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Having been disturbed in meditation on Hari, he opened his eyes and found a beautiful damsel standing before him who was quite youthful and was smiling besides being infatuated with passion.

## \|\| 46.17 \|\|

*lambodaraśca tāṃ dṛṣṭvā paraṃ vinayapūrvakam \|*  
*uvāca sasmitaḥ śāntaḥ śāntāṃ kāmāturāṃ vaśī \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Lord Ganesha who had controlled his senses smilingly and with a peaceful mind spoke looking at her, who was filled with passion.

## \|\| 46.18-19 \|\|

*gaṇeśvara uvāca \|*  
*kā tvaṃ vatse kasya kanyā mātarmā brūhi kiṃ śubhe \|*  
*pāpado'śubhadaḥ śaśvaddhyānabhaṃgastapasvinām \|\| 18 \|\|*

*kṛṣṇaḥ karotu kalyāṇaṃ hantu vighnaṃ kṛpānidhiḥ \|*  
*taddhyānabhaṃgajāddoṣānnāśubhaṃ syāttu te śubhe \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Ganeshvara said – О damsel, who are you? Who is your father? О mother, О auspicious one, you tell me. It is sinful to disturb a person performing the tapas which results in evil. О virtuous one, let lord Krishna bestow welfare on you and also remove all obstructions. Let you be free of the sin of disturbing my meditation.

## \|\| 46.20 \|\|

*gaṇeśavacanaṃ śrutvā tamuvāca smarāturā \|*  
*sasmitaṃ sakaṭākṣaṃ ca devaṃ madhurayā girā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Oh hearing the words of Ganesha, the passionate Tulasi laughed and casting side-glances she spoke in a sweet voice.

## \|\| 46.21 \|\|

*tulasyuvāca \|*  
*dharmātmajasya kanyā'hamaprauḍhā ca tapasvinī \|*  
*tapasyā me svāmino'rthe tvaṃ svāmī bhava me prabho \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Tulasi said – "O lord, I am the daughter of Dharmaputra. I am youthful and am an ascetic, I am performing tapas for achieving a husband; therefore you be my husband."

## \|\| 46.22 \|\|

*tulasīvacanaṃ śrutvā gaṇeśaḥ śrīhariṃ smaran \|*  
*tāmuvāca mahāprājñaḥ prājñīṃ madhurayā girā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. On hearing the words of Tulasi, the intelligent Ganesha while reciting the name of the lord spoke to the intelligent damsel.

## \|\| 46.23 \|\|

*gaṇeśa uvāca \|*  
*he mātarnāsti me vācchā ghore dāraparigrahe \|*  
*dāragraho hi duḥkhāya na sukhāya kadācana \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Ganesha said – О mother, it is terrible for me to accept a spouse. I am, therefore not interested in the same. After marriage no one gets pleasure but one is sure to get pain.

## \|\| 46.24 \|\|

*haribhaktervyavāyaśca tapasyānāśakārakaḥ \|*  
*mokṣadvārakapāṭaśca bhavabandhanapāśakaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. By doing so one’s devotion towards the lord is interrupted and the tapas is disturbed. The door to salvation is closed and a person gets entangled in the worldly fetters.

## \|\| 46.25-26 \|\|

*garbhavāsakaraḥ śaśvattattvajñānanikṛntakaḥ \|*  
*saṃśayānāṃ samārambho yastyājyo vṛṣalairapi \|\| 25 \|\|*

*geho'yaṃ kāraṇānāṃ ca sarvamāyākaraṇḍakam \|*  
*sāhasānāṃ samūhaśca doṣāṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. One has to face pregnancy and ultimate loss of the knowledge of tattvas resulting in an increase of doubts. Therefore even the Shudras should not indulge themselves in the same. This is a storehouse of pride and a box of illusion. It is a bundle of courage and a heap of sins.

## \|\| 46.27 \|\|

*nivartasva mahābhāge paśyānyaṃ kāmukaṃ patim \|*  
*kāmukenaiva kāmukyāḥ saṃgamo guṇavānbhaveta \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Therefore, О virtuous one, you return and search for another passionate husband. Because only a passionate one can be the husband of a passionate lady and their union is always beneficial."

## \|\| 46.28-29 \|\|

*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā kopātsā taṃ śaśāpa ha \|*  
*dārāste bhavitā'sādhvī gaṇeśvara na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*ityākarṇya suraśreṣṭhastāṃ śaśāpa śivātmajaḥ \|*  
*devi tvamasuragrastā bhaviṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. On hearing these words she pronounced a curse on him saying, "O Ganeshvara, you will get a wicked woman, there is no doubt about it." On hearing this Ganesha also pronounced a curse on her saying, "goddess, you will be over-powered by a demon. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 46.30-31 \|\|

*tatpaścānmahatāṃ śāpādvṛkṣastvaṃ bhaviteti ca \|*  
*mahātapasvītyuktvā tāṃ virarāma ca nārada \|\| 30 \|\|*

*śāpaṃ śrutvā tu tulasī sā ruroda punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva ca suraśreṣṭhaṃ sa prasanna uvāca tām \|\| 31 \|\|*

30-31. Thereafter with the curse of elders you will have to be turned into the form of a tree." Thus speaking the immensely intelligent Ganesha kept quiet. On hearing the curse Tulasi weeping again and again, started offering prayer to lord Ganesha. Lord Ganesha was pleased with her and said.

## \|\| 46.32 \|\|

*gaṇeśvara uvāca \|*  
*puṣpāṇāṃ sārabhūtā tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi manorame \|*  
*kalāṃśena mahābhāge svayaṃ nārāyaṇapriyā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Ganeshvara said – О pleasant one, you will be the best of flowers known as Tulasi. О virtuous one, because of the rays of lord Vishnu, you will become the beloved of Narayana.

## \|\| 46.33 \|\|

*priyā tvaṃ sarvadevānāṃ śrīkṛṣṇasya viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*pūjā vimuktidā nṝṇāṃ mayā bhogyā na nityaśaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. You will be loved by all and particularly you would be beloved of lord Krishna; you will always remain auspicious and shall bestow salvation on the people but I shall never accept your leaves.

## \|\| 46.34-35 \|\|

*ityuktvā tāṃ suraśreṣṭho jagāma tapase punaḥ \|*  
*harerārādhanavyagro badarīsannidhiṃ yayau \|\| 34 \|\|*

*jagāma tulasī devī hṛdayena vidūyatā \|*  
*nirāhārā tapaścakre puṣkare lakṣavarṣakam \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Lord Ganesha thus speaking and because of his devotion towards the lord, went to Badrikasrama. Tulasi on the other hand feeling painful at heart went to the Puskara-ksetra. She performed tapas therefore a lakh of years without consuming anything.

## \|\| 46.36-37 \|\|

*paścānmunīndraśāpena gaṇeśasya ca nārada \|*  
*sā priyā śaṃkhacūḍasya babhūva suciraṃ mune \|\| 36 \|\|*

*tataḥ śaṅkaraśūlena sa mamārāsureśvaraḥ \|*  
*sā kalāṃśena vṛkṣatvaṃ yayau nārāyaṇapriyā \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. О Narada, О sage, because of the curse of Ganesha, the best of the sages, Tulasi remained the wife of SamkhacQda for a long time. Thereafter, Samkhacuda was killed with the trident of Shiva. Tulasi became devoted to Narayana and after due course of time she was turned into a tree.

## \|\| 46.38 \|\|

*kathitaścetihāsaste śruto dharmamukhātpurā \|*  
*mokṣapradaśca sāraśca purāṇena prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. I have heard this from the mouth of Dharma, the story which I have narrated to you and is well known in the Puranas, which provides salvation.

## \|\| 46.39 \|\|

*tataḥ paraśurāmo'sau jagāma tapase vanam \|*  
*praṇamya śaṅkaraṃ durgāṃ saṃpūjya ca gaṇeśvaram \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thereafter, Parashurama adoring Ganesha, Shiva and Durga offered his adoration to them and performed tapas.

## \|\| 46.40 \|\|

*pūjito vanditaḥ sarvaiḥ surendramunipuṅgavaiḥ \|*  
*pārvatīśivasānnidhye sukhaṃ tasthau gaṇeśvaraḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Ganesha also having been adored by the gods and the sages started living with Parvati and Shiva comfortably.

## \|\| 46.41-44 \|\|

*idaṃ gaṇapateḥ khaṇḍaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sa rājasūyayajñasya phalamāpnoti niścitam \|\| 41 \|\|*

*aputro labhate putraṃ śrīgaṇeśaprasādataḥ \|*  
*dhīraṃ vīraṃ ca dhaninaṃ guṇinaṃ cirajīvinam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*yaśasvinaṃ putriṇaṃ ca vidvāṃsaṃ sukavīśvaram \|*  
*jitendriyāṇāṃ pravaraṃ dātāraṃ sarvasampadām \|\| 43 \|\|*

*suśīlaṃ ca sadācāraṃ praśaṃsyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ labhet \|*  
*ahiṃsakaṃ dayāluṃ ca tattvajñānaviśāradam \|\| 44 \|\|*

41-44. Thus, he who listens to the Ganapati Khanda attentively, surely achieves the merit of performing the RajasQya-yajna. With the grace of Ganesha, the one having on son, gets a son, who is always valorous, patient, rich, virtuous, having a long life and glorious, intelligent, a poet and the best of those who control their sense organs. He gets immense riches, gives immense riches in charity, is quite noble, praise-worthy, is a Vaishnava, merciful and possesses the knowledge of tattvas.

## \|\| 46.45-46 \|\|

*bhaktyā gaṇeśaṃ saṃpūjya vastrālaṅkāracandanaiḥ \|*  
*śrutvā gaṇapateḥ khaṇḍaṃ mahāvandhyā prasūyate \|\| 45\|\|*

*mṛtavatsā kākavandhyā brahmanputraṃ labheddhruvam \|*  
*adūṣyadūṣaṇaparā śuddhā caiva labhetsutam \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Adoring Ganesha offering with devotion, the garment and ornaments one should listen to the Ganapati Khanda and whosoever does so, even the totally barren one gets a son. О Brahmana, the lady who gives birth to dead children or is totally barren gets a son. The lady who blames other ladies with unblemished character is relieved of his sin and gets a son.

## \|\| 46.47 \|\|

*saṃpūrṇaṃ brahmavaivartaṃ śrutvā yallabhate phalam \|*  
*tatphalaṃ labhate martyaḥ śrutvedaṃ khaṇḍamuttamam \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The merit one earns by listening to the entire Brahmavaivarta Purana, the same merit is achieved by a person by listening to the Ganapati-khanda.

## \|\| 46.48-50 \|\|

*vāñchāṃ kṛtvā tu manasi śṛṇoti paramāsthitaḥ \|*  
*tasmai dadāti sarveṣṭaṃ suraśreṣṭho gaṇeśvaraḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*śrutvā gaṇapateḥ khaṇḍaṃ vighnanāśāya yatnataḥ \|*  
*svarṇayajñopavītaṃ ca śvetacchatraṃ ca mālyakam \|\| 49 \|\|*

*pradīyate vācakāya svastikaṃ tilalaḍḍukān \|*  
*paripakvaphalānyeva deśakālodbhavāni ca \|\| 50 \|\|*

48-50. The one who listens to the same keeping some desires in his mind, is bestowed with the fulfilment of the desires by lord Ganesha. On listening to the Ganapati Khanda one should give away in charity the yajnopavlta of gold, a white umbrella, a rosary, sea-same, sweet balls and the fruits of the season. The one who recites the same for the removal of obstruction (his obstructions will be removed).
